### [1] [Perturbation responses on topological synchrony in simplicial Kuramoto model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29166)
*Abhijeet Kumar,Palash Kumar Pal,Dibakar Ghosh*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文通过Hodge分解（Hodge decomposition）研究单纯复形上的Kuramoto模型（simplicial Kuramoto model），证明了在临界耦合强度之上，精确与余精确（exact和coexact）扇区可收敛到不动点，从而给出了拓扑障碍条件下同步（synchronization）的广义定义，并揭示了系统脆弱性与调和扇区维数之间的超线性标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同步概念仅适用于欧几里得空间中的动力学单元，而当动力学变量定义在更高维单纯形上时，下层单纯复形的拓扑结构会带来新的约束；非平凡的拓扑环会产生不受耦合影响的调和子空间，导致全局同步态无法达到。因此需要借助Hodge分解来理解并克服这一拓扑障碍。

Method: 在单纯形Kuramoto动力学上应用Hodge分解，将系统状态分解为精确、余精确与调和（harmonic）三个扇区，并分别对各扇区的动力学进行分析；对两个非调和扇区在临界耦合强度以上的不动点状态进行解析推导，并利用带第一个Betti数（first Betti number）变化的三角化环面进行数值表征，研究系统的鲁棒性与脆弱性。

Result: 在临界耦合强度之上，精确与余精确扇区均存在固定的不动点态，并已解析推导；排除漂移且无相互作用的调和分量后，扰动响应由加权拉普拉斯算子的谱决定，恢复出与标准Kuramoto情形类似的Kirchhoff指数依赖关系；调和扇区是非耗散的，系统脆弱性随该拓扑子空间维数增大而增加，数值上发现脆弱性与调和扇区维数之间存在超线性标度关系。

Conclusion: Hodge分解为单纯形Kuramoto模型提供了一种广义同步概念：非调和分量收敛到固定状态即可视为同步。尽管调和分量无法被抑制并持续漂移，但其维数直接决定系统的脆弱程度，这一发现为理解高维拓扑系统上的同步现象提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Synchronization is conventionally understood as the emergence of a phase-locked collective state among interacting dynamical units. However, extending this notion to systems whose dynamical variables are defined on higher-dimensional simplices introduces fundamentally new constraints arising from the topology of the underlying simplicial complex. In the simplicial Kuramoto model, nontrivial topological cycles give rise to a higher dimensional harmonic subspace that is unaffected by the coupling and can therefore drift indefinitely, preventing a globally synchronized state. To resolve this we employ Hodge decomposition on simplicial Kuramoto dynamics and investigated the components. Despite this topological obstruction, we show that the simplicial Kuramoto model admits fixed-point states in the exact and coexact sectors of the Hodge decomposition above critical coupling strengths, which we derive analytically for both sectors. This decomposition provides a generalized notion of synchronization in which the non-harmonic components converge to fixed states. We further investigate the robustness of these fixed-point states to external perturbations. Excluding the drifting and non-interacting harmonic component, the perturbation response is governed by the spectrum of weighted Laplacian and recovers a Kirchhoff index dependence analogous to standard Kuramoto case. In contrast, the harmonic sector is non-dissipative, and consequently the fragility of the system increases with the dimension of this topological subspace. We characterize this effect numerically using triangulated tori with varying first Betti number and find a superlinear scaling relation between system fragility and the dimension of the harmonic sector.

</details>

### [2] [Exact Haar Statistics of Planar $k$-Purity in Multipartite Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28914)
*Kourosh Mirsohi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入"平面k-纯度"（planar k-purity）作为环形量子态中所有k位区间约化态最大混合这一约束的忠实代价函数，并通过四副本计算（four-replica calculation）推导出Haar随机纯态下区间联合纯度的完整循环协方差核（cyclic covariance kernel）及解析方差公式，提供了多体纠缠的几何感知基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管平面k-均匀态（planar k-uniform states）在环形排布上具有重要的最大混合约化约束，但目前缺乏对该约束的定量度量，导致无法刻画多体纠缠中几何关联子系统（geometrically related subsystems）的集体涨落特性。

Method: 定义平面k-纯度为环上所有k位区间平均纯度，作为该同步约束的忠实代价函数；通过四副本计算推导两个区间联合纯度仅依赖于其重叠度的协方差核；利用位点因子化置换表示（site-factorized permutation representation）计算所有原始矩和偏度，并通过固定种子模拟验证不同情形下的理论结果。

Result: 在平衡qubit情形下，方差在n为偶数和奇数时分别渐近于20/(3nN²)和6/(nN²)；非平衡qubit与qutrit情形及n≤10的平衡qubit模拟均验证了奇偶校验公式和有限尺寸偏度；在n=10时平面纯度的方差约为绝对平衡纯度的2.83倍。

Conclusion: 子系统发生率（subsystem incidence）虽然对每个单切口边际分布不可见，但控制了几何相关切口的集体涨落；结合普适可达性（universal attainability）和平衡态下线性数量的区间约束，该结果为AME存在区域之外的多体纠缠提供了几何感知的基准。

Abstract: Planar $k$-uniform states have maximally mixed reductions on every $k$-site interval of a ring. We introduce planar $k$-purity---the mean purity of those intervals---as a faithful cost function for this simultaneous constraint. For a Haar-random pure state of $n$ parties with local dimension $p$, the joint purity of two intervals depends only on their overlap. An exhaustive four-replica calculation therefore yields the complete cyclic covariance kernel and a closed variance for every $1\le k\le\lfloor n/2\rfloor$. In the balanced qubit case, $k=\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ and $N=2^n$, the variance is asymptotic to $20/(3nN^2)$ for even $n$ and $6/(nN^2)$ for odd $n$. A site-factorized permutation representation gives all raw moments and permits exact finite-sum evaluation of the skewness. Fixed-seed simulations validate nonbalanced qubit and qutrit cases, while balanced-qubit simulations through $n=10$ validate the parity formulas and finite-size skewness. Balanced planar and absolute balanced purity have the same Haar mean but different fluctuations; the planar variance is approximately $2.83$ times larger at $n=10$. Thus subsystem incidence, although invisible to every one-cut marginal distribution, controls the collective fluctuations of geometrically related cuts. Together with universal attainability and a linear number of interval constraints at balance, this provides a geometry-aware benchmark for multipartite entanglement beyond AME existence regimes.

</details>


### [3] [Quantum Natural Gradient on Quotient Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29350)
*Zeyu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子自然梯度（quantum natural gradient, QNG）框架中系统处理参数化量子电路中保态冗余导致的量子Fisher信息矩阵奇异性问题，提出了商流形上的QNG公式化、circuit-to-orbit传递原理、表示论下的根分辨Fisher尺度分析，并给出了有限样本实现中规范去除与软模正则化的分离策略及去极化场景下的信噪比结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化量子电路常含有保态冗余，使得即使物理态流形正常（regular），量子Fisher信息矩阵（QFIM）仍是奇异的。这种奇异性阻碍了QNG在量子计算与量子机器学习中的直接应用，因此需要从流形结构层面给出系统的数学处理。

Method: 作者将QNG定义在参数商空间（parameter quotient）上，证明当冗余穷尽Fisher核时，Moore–Penrose更新是最小范数的水平提升（minimum-norm horizontal lift）。提出circuit-to-orbit传递原理以分离内禀态可区分度与电路坐标畸变；运用表示论（representation theory）在最高权轨道（highest-weight flag orbits）上导出根分辨的Fisher尺度，对Slater与费米高斯流形给出各向同性内禀度量。在余极小嵌入（cominuscule embeddings）上推导内禀保真度QNG的标量方程及Lie收缩步长的局部三阶与稳定性边界；在有限样本情形下将精确规范移除与物理软模正则化分离，并给出结构投影下的零累积规范漂移及置信控制软模规则。

Result: 在η=2处Lie收缩步长局部达到三阶精度，稳定性边界为η=4；有限样本实现中结构投影实现零累积规范漂移；去极化（depolarization）下逆Fisher缩放虽恢复确定性尺度但会放大涨落，无法恢复丢失的更新信噪比；冗余Slater/Givens电路实验验证了传递定律与预测的有限样本权衡。

Conclusion: 本文为含保态冗余的量子电路中的QNG提供了完备的流形与表示论框架，明确了QNG更新的几何意义与有限样本行为，揭示了去极化场景下信噪比无法恢复的固有限制，为实际量子优化中处理Fisher矩阵奇异性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Parametrized quantum circuits often contain state-preserving redundancies that make the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) singular even when the physical state manifold is regular. We formulate quantum natural gradient (QNG) on the resulting parameter quotient and prove that, when the prescribed redundancy exhausts the Fisher kernel, the Moore--Penrose update is the minimum-norm horizontal lift of the quotient Riemannian gradient. A circuit-to-orbit transfer principle separates intrinsic state distinguishability from circuit-coordinate distortion and gives the exact condition under which a circuit realizes an intrinsic orbit-QNG direction. Representation theory then yields root-resolved Fisher scales on highest-weight flag orbits and isotropic intrinsic metrics for Slater and fermionic-Gaussian manifolds. On cominuscule embeddings, intrinsic fidelity QNG conserves principal-defect ratios and reduces to one scalar equation; Lie-retracted steps are locally cubic at $η=2$, with stability boundary $η=4$. For finite-shot implementations, we separate exact gauge removal from regularization of physical soft modes, obtain zero cumulative gauge drift under structural projection, and derive confidence-controlled soft-mode rules. Under depolarization, inverse-Fisher scaling restores deterministic scale only by amplifying fluctuations and therefore cannot recover a lost update signal-to-noise ratio. A redundant Slater/Givens circuit verifies the transfer law and the predicted finite-shot tradeoffs.

</details>


### [4] [Convex conservation of von Neumann entropy implies unitarity or antiunitarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29373)
*Nicolas G. Underwood,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对有限维希尔伯特空间中的密度矩阵空间，提出了一个与卡迪森定理（Kadison's theorem）类似的定理，将原来的可逆性（invertibility）要求替换为熵守恒（entropy conservation）条件，从而论证量子力学中的可逆性假设可被熵守恒原理所取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡迪森定理表明所有可逆的凸变换均为幺正变换或反幺正变换，但可逆性假设在量子力学中可能过于严格。作者希望探究能否用更物理化的条件——熵守恒——替代这一数学上的可逆性要求。

Method: 针对有限维希尔伯特空间，作者提出将卡迪森定理中的可逆性条件替换为熵守恒条件，并论证在该条件下所有保持熵的凸变换同样只能是幺正（unitary）或反幺正（antiunitary）变换。

Result: 在有限维希尔伯特空间中，建立了以熵守恒取代可逆性的类似定理，说明熵守恒条件足以刻画密度矩阵空间上允许的凸变换。

Conclusion: 量子力学中的可逆性假设可以被熵守恒原理替代，这一观点可能为量子理论的基础设定提供新的视角。

Abstract: On the space of density matrices $D(H)$, Kadison's theorem establishes that all invertible convex transformations $K:D(H)\to D(H)$ are unitarity or antiunitary. For a finite Hilbert space, we propose a similar theorem which replaces the invertibility requirement with that of entropy conservation. Thus, we argue, the supposition of reversibility in quantum mechanics may be replaced with a principle of entropy conservation.

</details>


### [5] [A statistical model for quantum spin and photon number states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.13535)
*Sam Powers,Guangpeng Xu,Herbert Fotso,Tim Thomay,Dejan Stojkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二元符号序列的基础量子本体论模型，将量子理论中的概率还原为对更基础的"本体态"（ontic states）的计数，使用有限循环群ℤ₂、集合论与组合学构建形式体系，并通过半角定律将计数映射为旋转角。该模型在大序列极限下与量子力学精确一致，且不违反贝尔不等式、Kochen-Specker定理等不可行定理，并提出了可用于桌面实验检验的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在寻找量子理论更基础的还原论解释——将量子概率还原为对更基础本体态的简单计数。核心动机是构建一个无需自由参数、完全自洽的形式体系，用以解释量子理论中出现的概率，同时避免与已知不可行定理（no-go theorems）冲突。

Method: 方法以二元符号序列（0和1）作为构建块，将量子态解释为事件网络（event networks）。作者利用有限循环群ℤ₂={0,1}、基本集合论和组合学建立自洽的计数形式体系，并推导出将抽象计数映射为物理旋转角的半角定律 tan(θ_ab/2)=B̃_map/Ã_map，该映射由连续旋转的合成性质唯一确定。模型随后推广至包含光束分束器的光子数态光学系统。

Result: 模型对任意自旋粒子与两个旋转Stern-Gerlach探测器相互作用的概率分布计算，在大序列长度n的极限下与非相对论量子力学精确一致，有限n修正以O(1/n)衰减，且无任何自由或拟合参数。有限n的残余偏差不会导致贝尔不等式、Kochen-Specker定理或PBR定理的违反。利用高精度光学实验进展，作者进一步提出了可行的桌面实验检验方案。

Conclusion: 研究表明，量子理论中的概率可被还原为更基础二元序列的计数，从而为量子理论提供一种自洽的还原论基础。该模型无需自由参数即与量子力学在大序列极限下吻合，并保有实验可检验的有限n修正，为未来研究提供了理论与实验方向。

Abstract: The most irreducible way to represent information is a sequence of two symbols. In this paper, we construct quantum states using this basic building block. Specifically, we show that the probabilities that arise in quantum theory can be reduced to counting more fundamental ontic states, which we interpret as event networks and model using sequences of 0's and 1's. A completely self contained formalism is developed for the purpose of organizing and counting these ontic states, which employs the finite cyclic group $\mathbb{Z}_2 = \{0, 1\}$, basic set theory, and combinatorics. This formalism is then used to calculate probability distributions associated with particles of arbitrary spin interacting with sequences of two rotated Stern-Gerlach detectors. A central ingredient of this construction is the rule which converts the abstract counts labelling an operation into the physical angle of rotation it represents. We show that this rule is not linear in the counts, but is instead fixed by the half-angle law $\tan(θ_{ab}/2)=\tilde{B}_{map}/\tilde{A}_{map}$, which is the unique assignment consistent with the composition of successive rotations. These calculations are compared with the predictions of non-relativistic quantum mechanics and shown to agree exactly in the limit of large sequence length $n$, with finite $n$ corrections which vanish as $O(1/n)$ and with no free or fitted parameters. The residual deviation at finite $n$ does not lead to violations of relevant no-go theorems, such as Bell's inequalities, the Kochen-Specker theorem, or the PBR theorem. The proposed model is then extended to an optical system involving photon number states passing through a beam splitter. Leveraging recent advancements in high precision experiments on these systems, we then propose a means of testing the new model using a tabletop experiment.

</details>


### [6] [Analytical diagonalization of the open-boundary bosonic Kitaev chain: An asymmetric plane-wave ansatz approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29684)
*Ning Wu,Wen-Long You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对开放边界条件下的玻色子Kitaev链（bosonic Kitaev chain），提出了一种纯代数的求解方案。该方案完全基于标准玻色子Bogoliubov变换，通过反对称平面波拟设（asymmetric plane-wave ansatz）解析求解2N×2N非厄米(asssociated matrix)，并利用玻色子对易关系解决简并度，最终构造出N个物理Bogoliubov准粒子算符并显式得到哈密顿量的对角形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子Kitaev链在开放边界条件下因其在驱动-耗散系统中的实现以及有趣的非厄米边界物理而受到关注，但现有求解方法依赖位置-动量表示中的局部压缩变换(local squeezing transformation)。本文旨在提供一种纯代数的替代求解方案，完全基于标准玻色子Bogoliubov变换，以简化并推广该模型的求解方法。

Method: 对N位点链，提出具有不等左行与右行动量（unequal left- and right-moving momenta）的反对称平面波拟设，用于解析求解相应的2N×2N非厄米"关联矩阵"(associated matrix)。左本征值问题给出N个不同的本征值，每个本征值均为二重简并；通过仔细利用玻色子对易关系解析这些简并度，构造N个物理Bogoliubov准粒子算符。最终显式给出哈密顿量的对角形式，并证明其与原哈密顿量等价。

Result: 该方法成功构建了N个物理Bogoliubov准粒子算符，且构造过程中揭示了非唯一性，在特殊情况下恰好与原始方法中局部压缩变换的自由度完全对应。哈密顿量的对角形式被显式得到并证明与原哈密顿量等价。所提出的反对称平面波拟设和简并解析技术可推广至其他玻色子配对(bosonic pairing)系统，包括具有非均匀配对或跳跃项的体系。

Conclusion: 本文提出的纯代数求解方案为玻色子Kitaev链提供了新的解析框架，且该方法具有通用性，可扩展到更广泛的含非均匀参数（inhomogeneous pairing or hopping）的玻色子配对系统。

Abstract: The bosonic Kitaev chain under open boundary conditions has attracted recent attention due to its realization in driven-dissipative systems and its intriguing non-Hermitian boundary physics. The model is known to be solvable via local squeezing transformations in the position-momentum representation. In this paper, we present an alternative, purely algebraic solution that relies entirely on the standard bosonic Bogoliubov transformation. For an $N$-site chain, we propose an asymmetric plane-wave ansatz with unequal left- and right-moving momenta to analytically solve the associated $2N\times 2N$ non-Hermitian ``associated matrix". The left eigenvalue problem yields $N$ distinct eigenvalues, each of which is twofold degenerate. By carefully resolving these degeneracies using the bosonic commutation relations, we construct the $N$ physical Bogoliubov quasiparticle operators. The construction reveals non-uniquenesses that in special cases exactly mirror the freedom in the local squeezing transformations of the original approach. The diagonal form of the Hamiltonian is obtained explicitly and is shown to be equivalent to the original Hamiltonian. The proposed asymmetric plane-wave ansatz and degeneracy-resolution technique are not limited to the present model and can be generalized to other bosonic pairing systems, including those with inhomogeneous pairing or hopping terms.

</details>


### [7] [Spin-spin effects from non-relativistic limit of Dirac-Pauli-Maxwell Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28904)
*Radmehr Fathi,Nima Khosravi*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻色子量子纠错码（如GKP码）基于平直空间中的平移对称性，而本研究旨在探索在更高亏格的黎曼曲面上是否可能存在类似的稳定子码结构，以及其代数性质与GKP码有何本质差异。

Method: 作者在亏格为二的黎曼曲面上研究玻色子量子纠错码，将码字显式构造为自守形式，并解析地生成全权重范围内码空间的完整塔结构。同时，通过对稳定子群的代数性质（可均性）进行分析，证明了针对亏格大于一的稳定子群的不可能性定理。

Result: 该协议直接生成一个位于周期边界条件下零能量本征空间内的连续矩阵积态流形，其跨度随系统尺寸呈指数增长，且在奇数系统尺寸下覆盖整个零动量零能量本征空间。此外，利用与该流形物理结构（关联于局部本征基）相关的性质，还可以推导出非零动量零模的解析表达式，以及在 E=±√3 能量处的多项式数量精确疤痕。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于局部无约束哈密顿量特征分解的方法是解析构造约束系统中平移不变EZMs的有效工具，能够系统性地获取零能量本征空间的完整结构，并为量子疤痕的解析推导提供新途径。

Abstract: Kinetically constrained systems, such as those representing Rydberg atom arrays in the blockade regime, have gathered considerable attention due to the presence of atypical eigenstates in their spectrum. The latter manifests itself through the presence of quantum many-body scars as well as unusually large zero-mode (ZM) subspaces which contain analytically tractable eigenstates with various entanglement scalings. In particular, some of the latter are excitable zero-modes (EZMs), meaning that they can be promoted to a non-zero energy for open boundary conditions. In this work, I present an efficient protocol for finding analytical expressions for translation-invariant EZMs in constrained systems, based on the eigendecomposition of the local unconstrained Hamiltonian. I demonstrate the power of this method on a decorated Rydberg chain. In that model, my protocol directly produces a continuous matrix-product-state manifold located entirely in the zero-energy eigenspace for periodic boundary conditions. The span of that manifold grows exponentially with the system, and for odd system sizes it covers the entire zero-momentum eigenspace with zero energy. I then show how the physical structure of the manifold, which is tied to the local eigenbasis, also allows one to derive analytical expressions for ZMs at non-zero momentum and for a polynomial number of exact scars at $E=\pm\sqrt{3}$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出倾向性直通（propensity straight-through，PST）估计器，通过利用连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的仿射状态更新，精确求导一步条件均值敏感性，从而在不依赖温度或Gumbel松弛的情况下，实现大规模梯度式机器学习与精确随机轨迹的整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）是应用、物理和生物科学中建模离散随机动力学的核心工具，但其与现代梯度式机器学习的集成受限于Gillespie型模拟算法中固有的硬类别事件选择（hard categorical event selection），导致梯度无法直接反向传播。

Conclusion: PST通过微分精确条件均值而非松弛样本，提供了一条无需温度和Gumbel噪声的路径，实现了通过精确随机轨迹进行可扩展的梯度式学习，兼具高精度与快速收敛优势。

Abstract: Continuous-time Markov chains (CTMCs) provide the backbone for modeling discrete stochastic dynamics across applied, physical, and biological sciences. Their integration with modern gradient-based machine learning, however, is limited by the hard categorical event selection intrinsic to Gillespie-type simulation algorithms. We exploit the affine state update to obtain the exact one-step conditional-mean sensitivity by differentiating normalized reaction propensities. We pair this backward rule with exact forward trajectories to define the propensity straight-through (PST) estimator. At the trajectory level, we show that one-step sensitivities composed across events can depart from the exact multistep sensitivity. We derive the resulting per-step discrepancy in closed form and prove that it vanishes identically for affine downstream dependence. PST matches the accuracy of Gumbel-Softmax straight-through across all benchmarks: reversible dimerization (0.06% error), a genetic oscillator (1.7% error), a 50-task repressilator suite (0.17% median error), and patch-clamp ion-channel recordings ($R^2$ = 0.988). Under matched settings, PST converges 3.0-fold faster on the oscillator and 2.1-fold faster on the ion channel. At deep-learning scale, PST trains a 203,796-parameter stochastic reaction network with hard sampling, reaching 98.22% MNIST digit classification accuracy. By differentiating an exact conditional mean rather than a relaxed sample, PST offers a temperature- and Gumbel-free path to scalable gradient-based learning through exact stochastic trajectories.

Method: 采用离子-光子符合测量（ion-photon coincidence）方法，分离出keV He弹丸穿过不同样品系统后的出射电荷态（exit charge states），将光子与发射它的特定离子关联起来；基于此提取电荷态分辨的光子产额，并对符合方法的原始数据进行必要的修正分析。

Result: 结果显示，He弹丸的电子激发在很大程度上与样品材料无关，而是随弹丸动能升高而显著增强；光子产额与最初为束箔光谱学（beam foil spectroscopy）开发的经典激发模型高度吻合，将该模型的适用范围扩展到更高光子能量和不同样品材料。

Conclusion: 该研究将束箔光谱学激发模型的适用性拓展至更高的光子能量范围及多样化的样品材料，同时提供了对符合测量方法及其数据修正的详细分析，证明该方法在基础研究和材料分析中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Investigating the electronic excitations caused by light keV ions in matter contributes to a better understanding of materials modification such as space weathering or plasma-wall interactions in fusion reactors as well as improved materials analysis methods such as low or medium energy ion scattering. This study investigates the dependence of specific excitations in keV He projectiles, the most common projectile employed in these analytical methods, on the kinetic energy of the ion. We report on photon emission at energies >10eV emitted by projectiles excited upon transmission through different sample systems. We separate exit charge states and report on measurements of ion-photon pairs in coincidence, i.e. we can link a photon to the specific ion emitting it. From these measurements we extracted charge state resolved photon yields and obtained a deep insight into the electronic excitation occurring. Our results show that the electronic excitation of the helium projectile is largely material independent and rising strongly with the kinetic energy of the projectile. The photon yields are in good agreement with a common excitation model initially developed for beam foil spectroscopy. We hence extend the validity of this model to higher photon energies and different sample materials. We further provide a detailed analysis of the employed coincidence approach and necessary corrections to the raw data, as the methodology has a broad applicability in fundamental research and materials analysis.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用商用台式SAXS/WAXS仪器，采用8 keV低能X射线源和定制集成的真空几何装置，对硅(111)和(100)晶片采集特征TDS图样。随后通过全局优化算法从这些台式图像中提取IFCs，并以含统计噪声的模拟数据为基准评估拟合精度。

Result: 台式仪器获得的TDS图样包含足够的信息用于提取IFCs，拟合精度与从含统计噪声的模拟数据中获得的结果相当。该方法能够获取整个布里渊区（Brillouin Zone）的声子信息，这是传统实验室技术（如一阶拉曼光谱）无法提供的。

Abstract: Momentum-resolved phonon spectroscopy is essential for understanding the thermal, electronic, and structural properties of materials, yet it traditionally necessitates access to large-scale synchrotron or neutron facilities. We report a paradigm shift by demonstrating the accurate measurement and quantitative analysis of Thermal Diffuse Scattering (TDS) patterns using a commercially available benchtop Small/Wide-Angle X-ray Scattering (SAXS/WAXS) instrument. By utilizing a low-energy (8 keV) X-ray source and a custom-integrated vacuum geometry, we acquired characteristic TDS patterns in silicon (111) and (100) slabs. We demonstrate that these benchtop images contain sufficient information to extract interatomic force constants (IFCs) through global optimization algorithms, achieving a fitting accuracy comparable to those obtained from simulated data with statistical noise. Crucially, this capability provides access to phonon information across the entire Brillouin Zone, a critical component of lattice dynamics unavailable via standard laboratory techniques like first-order Raman spectroscopy. This low-barrier approach makes synchrotron-level lattice dynamics characterization accessible to virtually any research laboratory, enabling new avenues for the study of intrinsic strain and anisotropic lattice dynamics in emerging material systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 强电子-空穴关联可与非平凡能带拓扑完全兼容，激子的束缚强度与空间特征仍强烈依赖于底层带边电子结构与晶体对称性；一维纳米线作为强维度约束极限，进一步加剧了激子束缚并推动光学响应进入紫外波段。

Abstract: The interplay between dimensionality, band-edge electronic structure, and electron-hole interactions governs the optical response of low-dimensional tellurium, yet the microscopic origin of its excitonic behavior remains largely unexplored. Our results reveal a strong dependence of the excitonic response on band-edge dispersion, crystal symmetry, and dimensional confinement. $α$-tellurene exhibits comparatively weak and spatially extended electron-hole correlations, whereas the SOC-induced quasi-flat band-edge states of $β$-tellurene give rise to a strongly bound and anisotropic near-infrared exciton. This demonstrates that strong electron-hole correlations are fully compatible with nontrivial band topology, while the binding strength and spatial character of the exciton remain strongly dependent on the underlying band-edge electronic structure and crystal symmetry. The one-dimensional helical nanowire represents the strong-confinement limit, exhibiting a direct high exciton binding energy and a pronounced shift of the optical response toward the ultraviolet.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解理面在建模多组分化合物表面时往往会切断强键合的多面体结构，导致产生非物理的表面能，因此需要一种能够保留最优配位环境的表面建模方法。

Abstract: When modeling surfaces of multinary compounds, conventional cleavage planes often cut through strongly bonded polyhedra, resulting in unphysical surface energies. Here, we introduce SALAMI (Symmetric Atomic Layers for Arbitrary Multinary Interfaces), a Python package that generates symmetric, charge-neutral, dipole-free, and low-energy slab models for multinary compounds. SALAMI performs combinatorial searches to selectively remove surface atoms and generate corrugated terminations that preserve optimal coordination environments. We applied this workflow to all symmetrically inequivalent crystallographic orientations with Miller indices up to 2 for two prototypical structures: the solid-state electrolyte Li3PS4 and the transparent conducting oxide ZnSb2O6. Density functional theory calculations reveal that Li3PS4 must preserve all PS4 units to achieve the minimum surface energy. For ZnSb2O6, low-energy surfaces are achieved by partial undercoordination of surface Sb atoms to SbO5 or SbO4 from the bulk SbO6, depending on the surface orientations. Compared to surface models generated with unconstrained coordination, applying constraints to achieve optimal local coordination environments significantly lowers surface energies, shrinking the volume of the predicted Wulff shape by approximately 20%. Our results demonstrate that meticulous control of local coordination environments is necessary for accurately predicting the surface energetics of multinary compounds.

</details>

*Johannes Krotz,Anton Lüders*

TL;DR: 本文通过解析方法研究刚性磁转子阵列在匹配磁性基底上滑动时产生的磁摩擦（magnetic friction），利用准静态能量景观将参数平面划分为不同室（chamber），识别出全局交替磁矩排列（moment alignment）的高耗散动力学状态，并推导出小间隙与大间隙区域的集体磁摩擦主导修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性耦合的阻尼转动自由度在相对滑动激发下可产生摩擦损失，将摩擦与磁矩动力学联系起来。由于这类磁性摩擦的动力学机制尚缺乏解析理解，需要建立简化模型以揭示其基本规律。

Result: 发现由层间距（layer separation）和时间张成的参数平面可分为不同室，每个室内能量景观结构不变。据此识别不同动力状态的层间距区间，并恢复一种磁矩排列全局交替且伴随峰值耗散的动力学区域。此外，推导了小间隙和大间隙区域集体磁摩擦的领先非平庸阶，交替区域中摩擦可通过追踪室边界处从不稳定到稳定临界点的能量跳跃来计算。

Conclusion: 该解析结果为由滑动转子阵列中的动力学和耗散现象提供了理论基础。由于简化模型具有尺度不变性（scale-free），所得结论可迁移至从微观到宏观的广泛长度尺度。

Abstract: Magnetically coupled damped rotational degrees of freedom can give rise to tribological loss when excited by a relative sliding motion, linking friction to the dynamics of magnetic moments. We analytically investigate such magnetic friction by studying a recently introduced simplified model for a rigid magnetic rotor array sliding over a commensurate magnetic substrate. Here, the array rotors can rotate about an axis perpendicular to the sliding direction and are damped by microscopic shaft friction, while the substrate magnets have a fixed in-plane orientation. The simplified model reduces the collective rotor dynamics of this setup to a set of coupled nonlinear differential equations, which can be studied by the corresponding energy landscape in the quasi-static regime. We find that the parameter plane spanned by layer separation and time can be divided into distinct chambers, in which the structure of the energy landscape remains invariant. Using these chambers, we identify the layer separation intervals corresponding to different dynamical states and recover the emergence of a regime where the moment alignment alternates globally while exhibiting peak dissipation. Additionally, we derive the leading nontrivial orders of the collective magnetic friction in the regimes of small and large gaps between the array and the substrate. For the alternating regime, we find that friction can be computed by tracking energy jumps from unstable to stable critical points at the chamber boundaries. These results provide an analytical foundation for the observed dynamics and dissipation in sliding rotor arrays. Because the simplified model is scale-free, our conclusions transfer across a broad range of microscopic and macroscopic length scales.

</details>


### [28] [Giant staggered Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya vectors emerged in synthetic antiferromagnets with spatially alternating stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28953)

Conclusion: 结构无序的存在构成了实验鉴定自旋液体材料的关键障碍，需要在区分玻璃态模拟行为与真实自旋液体性质方面保持谨慎。

Abstract: In this report, we have shown that, in an otherwise geometrically frustrated lattice, the underlying interactions compete to stabilize different magnetic phases. This competition is further modified by the presence of inherent structural disorder in a frustrated two-dimensional lattice. Furthermore, due to the local nature of the additional structural disorder, the majority of the samples evolve toward glassy dynamics, which can not only mimic spin-liquid-like behavior but also make it difficult to identify genuine spin-liquid candidates experimentally.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 研究表明，三角形-三角形图案接触在较高载荷下因承载接触点数量少，导致最大局部接触压力超过超润滑涂层的强度而失效。而方形-三角形图案界面增加了承载接触点并将其组织为连续接触线，在沿特定方向滑动时能够在显著更高的载荷下维持超润滑，同时对表面缺陷也具有一定容忍度。

Abstract: Ultrafast control of magnetization through lattice excitation provides a route to manipulating magnetic order on short timescales, yet the role of transient strain in driving magnetization dynamics remains poorly understood. Abstract: This work provides an overview of the different strategies that can be used to evaluate the performance of AI models and agents based on large language models (LLMs) for materials synthesis. We discuss evaluation strategies spanning knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, tool-use benchmarks, and closed loop benchmarks involving the interaction with experimental systems or realistic virtual tools. We use atomic layer deposition (ALD) as a case study, emphasizing how existing approaches in the literature both build from general approaches used beyond materials science and can be generalized to other materials synthesis techniques. Finally, we provide a practical evaluation framework to evaluate LLMs in the context of materials synthesis

Method: 采用协变方法（covariant treatment），将原本定义在三维欧氏空间中的刚度相关张量通过拉回运算（pullback）映射到界面流形（interface manifold）上，并与曲率张量进行缩并，从而推导出驱动力的分量形式。

The latter is expressible via contraction of the stiffness tensor and interface curvature tensor. Practical computations involving tensors necessarily rely on their individual components. In this work, an explicit component-wise expression is derived for the contraction of the stiffness and curvature tensors, valid in arbitrary coordinate systems. Within this formulation, to get the driving force, the curvature tensor is contracted with the pullback of a stiffness-related tensor - originally defined in three-dimensional Euclidean space - onto the interface manifold. This covariant treatment establishes a physically consistent foundation for three-dimensional computational models involving interface stiffness.

*Stephanie M. Ribet,Mohsen Danaie,Willem P. M. de Kleijne,Arthur R. C. McCray,Frederick Allars,Christopher S. Allen,Colin Ophus,Georgios Varnavides*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了在高分辨率迭代叠层衍射成像（iterative ptychography）中，能否用小会聚角代替高会聚角电子探针。研究显示，当会聚角小到衍射盘不重叠时，重构结果存在本质歧义性（ambiguity），尽管晶格间距和取向总能正确恢复，但重建物体并非唯一解，且增加探针位置重叠无法消除此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高分辨率迭代叠层衍射成像依赖高会聚角、良好对准的电子探针。作者希望探索使用小会聚角的可能性，以放宽对探针校正器（probe corrector）的需求，并支持低加速电压或去激发的物镜（如洛伦兹模式，Lorentz mode）下的实验。

Method: 通过实验和模拟相结合的方法，在会聚角小到衍射盘（diffracted disk）互不重叠的条件下进行叠层衍射重构，并系统性考察多种因素（如探针位置实空间重叠、非晶衬底、纯相位物体约束、热振动引入）对重构唯一性的影响。

Result: 当会聚角足够小时，布拉格盘（Bragg disk）之间缺乏干涉，导致每个反射缺失相位信息，重构结果出现简并（degeneracy）：晶格的刚性平移和物体对比度反转产生相同的实验数据与正演模型误差。增加实空间重叠无法消除此简并；非晶衬底虽可提供缺失的相位关系，但这种相位恢复（phasing）很脆弱。在固定剂量下，无论仅约束物体为纯相位物体还是在前向模型中引入热运动，单独任一措施都足以导致解的非唯一性。

Conclusion: 小会聚角叠层衍射的重构在小干重叠阈值（overlap threshold）时是歧义的，且由于晶格间距和取向总能正确恢复，这种现象具有迷惑性。唯一的诊断方法是多次从不同初始点重复重构以检测非唯一性。

Abstract: High-resolution iterative ptychography typically relies on a well-aligned, high-convergence-angle electron probe. Here we explore whether it can instead be performed at small convergence angles, relaxing the need for probe correctors and enabling experiments at low accelerating voltages or with a de-excited objective lens, as in Lorentz mode. Through experiments and simulations, we show that once the convergence angle is small enough that no diffracted disks overlap, the resulting reconstruction is ambiguous, posing a significant challenge for robust interpretation of results. This challenge arises because of the lack of interference between Bragg disks in the recorded diffraction pattern intensity, leading to no phase information for each reflection. Reconstructions with these data lead to degenerate objects in which rigid translations of the lattice and reversals of contrast of the object produce the same error between experimental data and the ptychography forward model. Increasing the real-space overlap between probe positions does not lift this degeneracy. An amorphous substrate can supply the missing phase relationships by giving the Bragg beams support in the gaps between disks. This phasing is fragile, however, and survives only where the forward model matches the experiment. At fixed dose, either constraining the object to be a pure phase object or introducing thermal motion into the forward model is enough on its own to make the solution non-unique, highlighting why our experimental reconstructions below the overlap threshold are ambiguous despite ample dose and real-space redundancy. Most troublingly, the lattice spacing and orientation are always recovered correctly, so a non-unique reconstruction looks convincing and can be diagnosed only by repeating the reconstruction from different starting points.

</details>

Result: 筛选得到三个有前景的候选材料：NaCaInN₂、NaSrInN₂和K₄ZnP₂。这些候选材料均具有热力学（亚）稳定性，展现出适合光伏应用的直接（或近直接）带隙，并对多种类型的点缺陷具有抵抗性。

Conclusion: 该第一性原理驱动的工作流程及所鉴定的候选材料有望推动新型光伏材料的发展，并重新激发对磷族化物探索的兴趣。

Abstract: Developing novel and efficient materials that are beyond silicon for photovoltaic (PV) applications is required to meet the upcoming energy needs of our societies. To identify novel candidate materials that can act as PVs, we use first principles calculations to perform a systematic screening of the pnictide chemical space (i.e., nitrides and phosphides). Specifically, we explore three different ternary and quaternary pnictide classes, namely, ABCX$_2$, BB'B"X$_2$, and A$_4$BX$_2$ (A = Li, Na, or K; B, B', B" = Ca, Sr, Mg, or Zn; C = Al, Ga, or In; X = N or P), leading to a set of 104 possible pnictide compositions. Based on our evaluations of ground state structures, 0 K thermodynamic stabilities, electronic structures, carrier effective masses, dynamic stabilities, and intrinsic point defect formation energies, we arrive at three promising candidates, namely, NaCaInN$_2$, NaSrInN$_2$, and K$_4$ZnP$_2$. Notably, all the identified candidates are thermodynamically (meta)stable, exhibit direct (or nearest direct) band gaps that are optimal for PV applications, and are resistant to forming several types of point defects. We hope that our first principles driven workflow and the identified candidates will advance the development of novel PV materials and reinvigorate interest in the exploration of pnictides.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Dark excitons play a central role in the nonequilibrium dynamics of two-dimensional semiconductors, but remain difficult to characterize. In this work we show that transient-absorption experiments, with probe pulses tuned in the exciton-exciton transitions energy range (exc-tr-abs), can be used to detect excitations from any populated dark excitonic state, including symmetry-forbidden, spin-forbidden, and finite momentum ones. We develop a fully ab initio scheme, on top of $GW$+BSE, for computing exc-tr-abs spectra from arbitrary populated exciton distributions. The different classes of dark excitons are included on an equal footing, by evaluating exciton-exciton dipole matrix elements in a locally smooth gauge, which accounts for both intra-band and inter-band contributions. As a test case, we consider monolayer MoS$_2$, for which experimental data are available. The exc-tr-abs signal emerges from a sum of contributions from four different valleys in the exciton dispersion, namely $Γ$, $K$, $M$, and $Q$ valleys. The interpretation of the final signal is substantially different from the one based on transitions at $\mathbf{q}=Γ$ alone. The exciton-exciton dipoles show similar intensity among these valleys, while their relative weight is mostly dictated by the initial excitonic population. State- and spin-resolved analyses show that the main peaks can be described as $1s \rightarrow 2p$ and $1s \rightarrow 3p$ transitions from A and B excitons, but with a superposition of transitions from both spin-flip and spin-conserving excitons across the valleys, and with contributions averaged over different momenta within the individual valleys.

We find that TOMs have an important contribution in spin-compensated systems, since the spin-orbit coupling induced orbital moments are nearly compensated. Conclusion: 该理论为位移型铁电体中非平衡极性晶格动力学与超快电子非线性光学之间建立了微观联系，尤其在缺乏低能载流子动力学的绝缘性铁电体中具有重要意义。

Abstract: Optical second-harmonic generation (SHG) is a powerful probe of ferroelectric order, yet its microscopic origin and dynamical control are often understood primarily from symmetry considerations rather than from the underlying electronic processes. Here, we develop a microscopic theory of terahertz-controlled optical SHG in displacive ferroelectrics, establishing a direct connection between THz-driven polar lattice distortions and the resulting electronic nonlinear optical response. Starting from a complete Bloch-band representation, we show that an inversion-breaking lattice distortion reconstructs electronic Bloch wave functions, modifies optical dipole matrix elements, and activates nonlinear optical pathways that are forbidden in the centrosymmetric structure. We derive the second-order susceptibility in terms of the distortion-induced reconstruction of electronic states and optical transition matrix elements, and demonstrate that the electronic SHG susceptibility is linear in the polar distortion, $χ^{(2)}({\bf Q})\propto{\bf Q}$, leading to an SHG intensity quadratic in the inversion-breaking order parameter. When the polar mode is coherently driven by a terahertz electric field, the resulting time-dependent lattice distortion dynamically reconstructs the electronic states and thereby modulates the optical SHG response, with $I_{2ω}(t)\propto|{\bf Q}(t)|^2$ to leading order. This framework distinguishes the THz-driven lattice dynamics from the electronic interband processes responsible for optical SHG, which is particularly important in insulating ferroelectrics where low-energy carrier dynamics are absent. Our theory thus provides a microscopic bridge between nonequilibrium polar lattice dynamics and ultrafast electronic nonlinear optics.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (AlₓGa₁₋ₓ)₂O₃合金及Al₂O₃作为宽带隙半导体在功率电子器件中具有重要应用前景，但可控n型掺杂是其面临的核心瓶颈。此前针对氧位点施主杂质的系统研究尚不充分，卤素杂质（F和Cl）的掺杂行为及其在不同相结构和合金成分下的演变规律缺乏第一性原理层面的深入理解。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，系统考察F和Cl在Ga₂O₃和Al₂O₃单斜相与刚玉相中的替位（substitutional）和间隙（interstitial）构型，计算其结构性质、形成能（formation energy）和电荷态跃迁能级（charge-state transition levels），建立不同原子构型与电荷态的相对稳定性图像。

Abstract: We present a systematic first-principles investigation of halide impurities (F and Cl) in Ga$_2$O$_3$ and Al$_2$O$_3$, considering both monoclinic and corundum phases. Our study of the structural properties, formation energies, and charge-state transition levels establishes the relative stability of different atomic configurations and charge states. We find that F and Cl on oxygen sites act as shallow donors in Ga$_2$O$_3$ in both the monoclinic and corundum phases. However, their behavior differs substantially as the band gap increases with greater Al compositions. Fluorine is prone to $DX$-center formation with increased Al composition, leading to self-compensation at 38% Al concentration in monoclinic (Al$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$)$_2$O$_3$ and 70% Al concentration in corundum (Al$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$)$_2$O$_3$. Chlorine is more resistant to $DX$-center formation: in monoclinic (Al$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$)$_2$O$_3$, Cl$_\mathrm{O}$ on the lowest-energy oxygen site shows an onset of $DX$ behavior at 50% alloy composition, while in corundum (Al$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$)$_2$O$_3$ this onset for Cl$_\mathrm{O}$ occurs only at Al concentrations as high as 84%. We also study F and Cl interstitials, finding that they act as compensating centers but also exhibit migration barriers that are low enough for them to be removed by post-growth annealing. Surprisingly, in both monoclinic and corundum Al$_2$O$_3$, Cl$_\mathrm{O}$ exhibits a relatively shallow transition level located at $\sim$0.48 eV below the conduction-band minimum, much shallower than F$_\mathrm{O}$ and other donor candidates. These remarkable results identify Cl as an unusually promising donor candidate in (Al$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$)$_2$O$_3$ alloys and even pure Al$_2$O$_3$, although high formation energies and compensation will render observation of true $n$-type conductivity in Al$_2$O$_3$ difficult.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁轴材料作为新兴的铁性（ferroic）材料类别，对杂散场完全鲁棒，非常适合数据存储等非易失性应用；然而铁轴态的检测一直困难重重，因为铁轴性不直接表现为可测量的宏观电极化或磁化，现有探测手段主要局限于光学方法。因此需要建立更普适、更直接的铁轴有序性探测途径。

Method: 作者通过理论分析确立了铁轴有序性与多种非常规响应（如自旋霍尔效应、磁热电效应、法拉第效应）之间的普遍对应关系，覆盖线性与非线性输运、光学及平衡态现象；并针对代表性材料开展了第一性原理（first-principles）计算以验证铁轴金属中的非常规输运探测方案。

Result: 研究表明，铁轴有序性总是直接耦合到这些非常规响应，可用于区分铁轴畴（ferroaxial domains），从而提供铁轴有序性的有效探针；第一性原理计算进一步证实了铁轴金属中非常规输运作为自然探测手段的可行性。

Conclusion: 铁轴性主要通过非常规材料响应而非可直接测量的序参量来显现，这一发现揭示了铁性有序的一种根本不同的表现方式，拓展了非易失性铁性调控与柔性器件设计的可能性。

Abstract: Ferroaxial materials are an emerging class of ferroic materials that, in contrast to ferromagnets and ferroelectrics, are fully robust against stray fields, making them ideally suited for data storage and other nonvolatile applications. However, detection of the ferroaxial state remains challenging, as ferroaxiality does not manifest directly through a measurable macroscopic electric polarization or magnetization, and probes are mostly limited to optical methods. Here, we theoretically establish that ferroaxial order universally generates unconventional components of responses to external stimuli, such as the spin Hall effect, magneto-Seebeck effect, or Faraday effect, across a broad range of linear and nonlinear transport, optical and equilibrium phenomena. These unconventional responses are always directly coupled to ferroaxial order and can distinguish ferroaxial domains, providing probes of ferroaxial order. This general connection also reveals a largely unexplored class of ferroaxial metals, in which unconventional transport provides a natural probe of ferroaxial order, as demonstrated by our first-principles calculations for representative materials. Our results reveal a fundamentally different way for ferroic order to manifest -- ferroaxiality primarily shows unconventional material responses rather than the directly measurable order parameters, broadening the possibilities of nonvolatile ferroic control and flexible device design.

</details>

Here, we report on photoluminescence lines in the vicinity of the M-center and investigate their properties, including their dependence on temperature, implantation fluence, implantation isotope, and annealing temperature. Three emission lines are observed that are blue-shifted by 1.1, 2.8 and 3.6 meV relative to the 761 meV zero phonon line of the M center, where the 2.8 meV line exhibits negative thermal quenching and is therefore proposed to originate from a second excited state of the M-center. The remaining blue-shifted emission lines, together with two additional red-shifted (4.7 and 6.4 meV) emission lines display normal thermal quenching, and are unaffected by an isotope shift of the implanted carbon atom ($^{12}$C versus $^{13}$C implantation) and implantation fluence.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨透射电子显微镜（HRTEM）中能量损失区域的相干晶格衬度扩展机制尚不明确，需要建立一个能够预测干涉可见度边界并与传统噪声来源相区分的物理模型。

Abstract: The extension of coherent lattice contrast into the energy loss region in high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HRTEM) is described by a pulse-like electron-sample interaction in the energy/time uncertainty limit. It generates a wave packet by electron self-interference in any coherent-inelastic scattering event with energy loss. The width of this wave packet is characterized by a self-coherence length ls(ΔE) that is predictable because an intrinsic decoherence phase around one radian is set by the expectation value for phase fluctuations. In this case the visibility of interference contrast from a crystalline sample with lattice parameter a is limited by a Rayleigh-like transfer factor P(ls, a) in the self-coherently illuminated sample area. The model is verified by energy-filtered HRTEM images of hexagonal BN and identifies energy-loss-induced phase noise as a single-electron visibility limit distinct from resolution limitations caused by ensemble-coherence or counting-statistical noise.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: From linear response theory, we derive the equation of motion for Landau-damped magnon quasi-particles in absence of spin-orbit coupling. The derivation is based on a minimal set of assumptions, namely that the transverse magnetic susceptibility is diagonalized by one collective eigenmode per magnetic atom in the unit cell, and that the spectrum of each collective eigenmode is dominated by a single magnon resonance. The resulting equation of motion leads to a natural definition of the exchange interaction as the restoring force acting on the collective mode magnetization near equilibrium. In addition to magnetic exchange, the magnon equation of motion also contains a damping term and a dispersive quasi-particle mass, both originating from the electron-magnon coupling. The effect of these terms is illustrated for a prototypical itinerant ferromagnet, where they redshift the magnon dispersion and reduce the magnon lifetime as the magnon enters the Stoner continuum. By further assuming wave vector independence of the collective magnon subspace, the magnon equation of motion becomes atomistic with well defined magnetic sites. For real materials, one can approximate the magnon coupling constants using an arbitrary level of band theory for the dynamic transverse magnetic susceptibility.

</details>

Abstract: Porous triboelectric materials offer significant potential for enhancing the performance of triboelectric nanogenerators, yet their practical application is limited by structural instability and humidity-induced performance degradation. Hydrophilic MOF fillers comprising HKUST-1 crystals are incorporated within the porous matrix to reinforce the membrane structure and regulate moisture effects. In addition, HKUST-1 mitigates humidity-induced charge dissipation by confining water molecules within its framework, giving enhanced triboelectric output stability and reduced performance degradation under increasing relative humidity compared to the neat porous system. This work demonstrates a strategy that integrates self-foamed porous structures, sustainable polymer design, and functional filler reinforcement to engineer mechanically robust and environmentally stable triboelectric systems.

Method: 利用扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）和电子能量损失谱（EELS）解析界面原子构型，以此为输入进行密度泛函（density-functional）计算；角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）确认能带结构后，用紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）拟合并计算自旋与轨道埃德尔斯坦响应；最后通过谐波磁输运（harmonic magnetotransport）进行实验验证。

Abstract: The orbital Edelstein effect converts an electric field into a non-equilibrium orbital polarization, opening new opportunities for orbitronics. Although signatures of the orbital Edelstein effect have been reported, its microscopic mechanisms and quantitative validation remain underexplored. Harmonic magnetotransport indicates that a $\sim$20 \% contribution from the orbital Edelstein response is necessary to describe the magnitude and anisotropy of the effect. TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理（first principles）出发，结合杂化泛函能带结构和完全相对论性电子-声子相互作用，对体锗（bulk germanium）的电子输运和自旋弛豫进行了无经验参数的定量预测，在100–400 K范围内与实验密切吻合。研究识别了主导自旋弛豫的微观机制，并证明压缩双轴应变可通过应变诱导的价带劈裂和自旋混合抑制，将空穴自旋寿命提高最多两个数量级，为Ge-on-Si器件中长寿命空穴自旋的工程化设计提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锗（germanium）是半导体自旋量子比特（spin qubit）和自旋电子学（spintronics）的领先平台，但其电子和空穴自旋动力学目前主要依赖现象学模型理解，缺乏从微观第一性原理出发的系统性预测。

Method: 采用杂化泛函（hybrid-functional）计算能带结构，并结合完全相对论性电子-声子相互作用来描述散射过程；通过按能谷（valley）和声子模（phonon mode）分解自旋翻转散射，解析自旋弛豫的微观机制；同时对压缩双轴应变（compressive biaxial strain）下空穴自旋寿命的影响进行了系统评估。

Result: 在100–400 K温度范围内，无经验参数预测的载流子迁移率、速度-电场曲线以及电子和空穴自旋弛豫时间均与实验密切一致。自旋弛豫与动量弛豫虽均由电子-声子散射介导，但受控于不同过程；5%压缩双轴应变可通过价带劈裂和自旋混合抑制，将空穴自旋寿命提升最多两个数量级。

Conclusion: 工作确立了体锗中自旋弛豫的微观散射机制，并揭示了应变作为实用手段来调控Ge-on-Si器件中长寿命空穴自旋的潜力，为量子技术中自旋比特的工程化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Germanium is a leading platform for semiconductor spin qubits and spintronics. Yet its electron and hole spin dynamics remain understood primarily through phenomenological models. Here, we predict electronic transport and spin relaxation in bulk Ge entirely from first principles, combining hybrid-functional band structures with fully relativistic electron-phonon ($e$-ph) interactions. Without empirical parameters, we predict carrier mobilities, velocity-field curves, and electron and hole spin relaxation times in close agreement with experiments over 100$-$400 K. By resolving spin-flip scattering by valley and phonon mode, we identify the microscopic mechanisms governing spin relaxation and show that spin and momentum relaxation, although both mediated by $e$-ph scattering, are controlled by distinct processes. We further show that compressive biaxial strain enhances the hole spin lifetime by up to two orders of magnitude at 5% strain, through strain-induced valence-band splitting and suppressed spin mixing. This mechanism is directly relevant to Ge-on-Si devices, where strain provides a practical route to engineering long-lived hole spins for quantum technologies.

</details>

### [72] [Nijenhuis torsion and Frölicher-Nijenhuis brackets of recursion operators via their full-fledged forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29964)
*Petr Vojcak*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于全展形式（full-fledged forms）计算递推算子（recursion operators）的Nijenhuis挠率（Nijenhuis torsion）和Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号的新方法，无需借助覆盖（covering）中非局部对称的映射表示，可直接从定义公式计算。该方法适用于多独立变量（如四维）的微分方程，并发现了一个此前文献中未出现的四维universal hierarchy方程的新递推算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将递推算子表示为给定覆盖中非局部对称的影射（shadows）之间的映射，计算Nijenhuis挠率需要额外的数学构造，在处理高度非局部或多维情形时存在困难。作者旨在通过全展形式直接计算Nijenhuis挠率和Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号，从而更有效地研究递推算子的遗传性（hereditary）和相容性（compatibility）性质。

Method: 基于Jahnová和Vojčák（2024）引入的全展形式来表示递推算子，直接从其定义公式计算Nijenhuis挠率，不依赖额外的数学构造。同一框架还可用于计算Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号并直接验证递推算子的相容性。方法通过四独立变量微分方程的多个全展递推算子实例加以展示。

Result: 文中给出了四个独立变量下微分方程的多个全展递推算子示例，包括一个此前未见文献的四维universal hierarchy方程的新递推算子。该算子的影射成分在标准传统形式中似乎没有合理的表示，但作者仍能直接证明其Nijenhuis挠率为零。对于所有考虑的示例，对应的Frölicher-Nijenhuis括号也均为零，确认了相应递推算子对的相容性。

Conclusion: 全展形式为研究递推算子的遗传性和相容性提供了一个有效框架，特别适用于高度非局部和/或多维的情形，而这些情形用现有方法难以处理。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to computing the Nijenhuis torsion and Frölicher-Nijenhuis brackets of recursion operators for symmetries, based on their full-fledged forms introduced by Jahnová and Vojčák (2024). In contrast to the conventional approach, which represents recursion operators as maps between shadows of nonlocal symmetries within a given covering, our method allows the Nijenhuis torsion to be computed directly from its defining formula, without requiring any additional mathematical constructions. The same framework can also be used to compute the Frölicher-Nijenhuis bracket of recursion operators and to verify their compatibility directly in their full-fledged forms. The procedure is illustrated by several examples of full-fledged recursion operators for symmetries of differential equations in four independent variables, including a new recursion operator for the four-dimensional universal hierarchy equation that, to the best of our knowledge, has not previously appeared in the literature. A notable feature of this new full-fledged recursion operator is that its shadow component does not appear to admit a reasonable representation in any of the standard conventional forms. Nevertheless, our approach allows us to prove directly that the Nijenhuis torsion of this operator vanishes. For the examples considered, the corresponding Frölicher-Nijenhuis brackets also vanish, confirming the compatibility of the respective pairs of recursion operators. This demonstrates that full-fledged forms provide an effective framework for studying the hereditary and compatibility properties of recursion operators, including highly nonlocal and/or multidimensional cases that are difficult to handle by existing methods.

</details>

### [73] [Emergent hydrodynamic response and dynamical backreaction: Magnon bound-state propagation in a Bose-Hubbard fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28719)
*Andrés N. Cáliz,Arnau Riera,Enrique Rico,João Barata,Marcin Pł odzień*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维Bose-Hubbard介质对沿吸引性XXZ链传播的双磁振子束缚态（two-magnon bound state）的非平衡响应。通过推导长波长流体力学描述（hydrodynamic description），并与矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）模拟对比，揭示了伴随运动近场形变和限制在声锥内的延迟密度波，并验证了流体力学在探针反作用于介质时的定量适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解磁性系统中局域激发（如双磁振子束缚态）在介质中传播时引发的非平衡响应，是对超越平衡态物理的重要一步。摘要暗示该研究旨在建立一种无需拟合参数、可由平衡态性质预测动力学行为的理论框架，并检验其在强耦合反作用下的精确性。

Method: 采用Holstein型位移耦合使磁振子密度同时作为局域化学势微扰和玻色子源。在长波长极限下推导密度和相位涨落的流体力学理论，并将其与全耦合动力学的矩阵乘积态数值模拟结果进行对比测试。

Result: 在Mott区，形变保持局域化；在可压缩区，两个相向运动的波前脱离束缚态并以准经典声速（由平衡态密度得到，无拟合参数）传播。耦合还产生动态反作用，使束缚态减速并加宽其磁振子密度轮廓。理论与模拟的对比界定了流体力学在探针反作用于介质时的定量适用边界。

Conclusion: 该流体力学理论能够定量描述双磁振子束缚态在XXZ链中的非平衡响应，其预言的声速可从平衡态密度推导而无需拟合参数。但探针的动力学反作用限制了流体力学描述的有效性范围，需要在Mott区与可压缩区分别评估其精度。

Abstract: We study the nonequilibrium response of a one-dimensional Bose-Hubbard medium to a two-magnon bound state propagating along an attractive XXZ chain. A Holstein-type displacement coupling makes the magnon density act both as a local chemical-potential perturbation and as a source of bosons. We derive a long-wavelength hydrodynamic description of the density and phase fluctuations and test it against matrix-product-state simulations of the full coupled dynamics. The theory predicts a comoving near-field deformation together with retarded density waves confined to a sound cone. In the Mott regime, the deformation remains localized around the moving pair. In the compressible regime, two counterpropagating fronts detach and approach the semiclassical sound velocity, recovered from the equilibrium density with no fitted parameters. The coupling also induces a dynamical backreaction that slows the bound state and broadens its magnon-density profile. The comparison delimits where hydrodynamics stays quantitative once the probe reacts back on the medium.

</details>


### [74] [Regularization of Vortex Core Size in Photon BECs due to Harmonic Trap: An Analytical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28862)
*Joshua Krauß,Tabitha Bates,Francisco Ednilson Alves dos Santos,Axel Pelster*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究光子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（photon Bose–Einstein condensate）中量子涡旋（quantized vortex）的尺寸问题，通过谐振子势（harmonic confinement）将涡旋尺寸正则化到实验可测尺度，并利用投影优化方法（projection optimization method）在开放耗散系统中获得近似的动力单涡旋解，进而通过线性稳定性分析揭示相互作用、泵浦与损耗可驱动系统进入不稳定区间，从而在根本上区分封闭与开放耗散系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中，光子-光子相互作用极弱，均匀系统中治疗长度（healing length）超过凝聚体本身可实验实现的尺寸，使得涡旋难以直接观测。作者意图通过外部的谐振子约束来解决这一尺度障碍，并进一步理解开放耗散体系（open-dissipative system）中涡旋的动力学特性。

Method: 作者采用复Gross–Pitaevskii方程（complex Gross–Pitaevskii equation）对凝聚体进行建模，并引入外部谐振子势（harmonic confinement）以正则化涡旋尺寸。单涡旋近似解通过最近提出的投影优化方法获得，该方法无需预设凝聚体相位形式，将闭系统的变分法推广至开放耗散系统。涡旋核尺寸的定义基于增益与损耗的竞争所决定的径向光子流，并经过线性稳定性分析检验系统的稳定性。

Result: 研究表明，谐振子约束可将涡旋尺寸正则化到实验上可实现的尺度，与泵浦的染料填充微腔（pumped dye-filled microcavity）标准实验装置吻合。径向光子流为驱动-耗散系统所特有，由此导出的涡旋核尺寸定义在物理基础上复现了启发式闭系统的定义。线性稳定性分析显示，相互作用、泵浦和损耗均可使系统进入不稳定区间。

Conclusion: 这项工作在开放耗散系统中给出了基于物理基础的涡旋核尺寸定义，证明了闭系统与开放耗散系统在稳定性行为上存在根本差异，为在实验中观测光子凝聚体中的量子涡旋提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantized vortices are a hallmark of superfluidity. However, in a photon Bose--Einstein condensate, the photon-photon interaction is so weak that in a homogeneous system the healing length exceeds the experimentally achievable size of the condensate itself. Here we show that a harmonic confinement regularizes the vortex size to experimentally achievable scales. Moreover, such an external confinement closely resembles the standard experimental setup of a pumped dye-filled microcavity. We model the condensate via a complex Gross--Pitaevskii equation and obtain an approximate dynamical single-vortex solution by applying the recently proposed projection optimization method. The latter generalizes the variational approach of closed systems to open-dissipative systems without assuming a specific form for the condensate phase. The radial photon flow, which is characteristic for driven-dissipative systems, yields a definition of the vortex core size based on the competition of gain and loss. However, this condition reproduces the heuristic closed system definition now based on physical grounds. A subsequent linear stability analysis shows that interaction, as well as pumping and loss can drive the system to an unstable regime. In this way one can fundamentally distinguish between closed and open-dissipative systems.

</details>


### [75] [Mass-gap functional determinant approach for mobile Fermi polarons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30539)
*Emilio Ramos Rodríguez,Eugen Dizer,Xin Chen,Richard Schmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文将原本仅适用于静态杂质的函数行列式方法（functional determinant approach, FDA）扩展到有限质量杂质的情形，利用费米极化子（Fermi polaron）的质量隙（mass-gap）描述实现了对移动杂质多体谱和Ramsey动力学的精确求解，并提供了一种透明且计算高效的量子动力学描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的函数行列式方法此前只能处理静态杂质（static impurities），而实际实验中杂质往往具有有限质量，需要一种能够处理移动杂质（mobile impurities）的可解析方法。

Method: 采用费米极化子的质量隙模型（mass-gap description），利用该模型二次型结构实现对多体谱和Ramsey动力学的精确求解；通过质量隙函数行列式方法（mass-gap FDA）在无限重杂质和有限质量杂质两种极限情形之间进行平滑插值。

Result: 质量隙FDA在无限重杂质对应的费米边奇异性（Fermi-edge singularity）与有限质量杂质出现的准粒子权重（quasiparticle weight）之间实现了平滑过渡，验证了该框架的准确性与普适性。

Conclusion: 质量隙FDA为描述移动杂质的量子动力学提供了一种透明且计算高效的框架，拓展了函数行列式方法的适用边界。

Abstract: We extend the functional determinant approach (FDA), previously restricted to static impurities, to the case of finite-mass impurities by using the mass-gap description of Fermi polarons [Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 193401 (2025)]. The quadratic structure of the mass-gap model enables the exact evaluation of many-body spectra and Ramsey dynamics for mobile impurities. We show that this mass-gap FDA smoothly interpolates between the Fermi-edge singularity for infinitely heavy impurities and the emergence of quasiparticle weight for finite impurity mass. Our results demonstrate that the mass-gap FDA provides a transparent and computationally efficient framework to describe the quantum dynamics of mobile impurities.

</details>


### [76] [Static-Field Shielding of Bosonic Molecules: Evaporation to Degeneracy and Self-Bound Droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31116)
*Jongheum Jung,Laura Futamura,Matteo Ciardi,Jason Rosenberg,Youssef Aziz Alaoui,Yukai Lu,Thomas Pohl,Waseem S. Bakr*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究在玻色子极性分子（bosonic polar molecules）$^{23}$Na$^{87}$Rb上实现了静电场Förster屏蔽，将两体损失抑制了四个数量级，并在束缚态-自由态电场窗口内同时抑制三体损失，从而实现高效蒸发冷却，使相空间密度提升两个数量级，达到简并态，并观察到自束缚液滴的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷分子的强可调偶极相互作用使其成为量子多体物理的强大平台，但通过蒸发冷却达到简并态需要抑制非弹性碰撞。此前微波碰撞屏蔽已能制备简并费米气体和玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，而静电场屏蔽仅适用于费米子物种。本工作的动机是将静电场屏蔽扩展至更易发生非弹性损失的玻色子极性分子体系。

Method: 采用静电场Förster屏蔽方案（static-field Förster shielding），在一特定电场窗口内抑制两体和三体非弹性损失，对$^{23}$Na$^{87}$Rb玻色子极性分子实施蒸发冷却。通过测量相空间密度（phase-space density）的变化评估蒸发效率，并结合路径积分蒙特卡洛（Path Integral Monte Carlo）模拟对观测液滴的形态进行数值分析。

Result: 两体损失被抑制多达四个数量级；在束缚态-自由态电场窗口内三体损失也受到强烈抑制，实现蒸发效率2.09(9)，相空间密度提升两个数量级，达到7200(1000)个分子的简并态。蒸发结束时在广泛电场强度范围内观察到自束缚液滴，可源于简并或非简并母气；PIMC模拟表明液滴呈丝状结构，与实验观测的液滴形成温度吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该工作确立Förster屏蔽作为制备玻色子极性分子简并气体和自束缚液滴的单一场（single-field）途径，拓展了静电场屏蔽在玻色子分子体系中的应用范围。

Abstract: The strong, tunable dipolar interactions of ultracold molecules make them a powerful platform for quantum many-body physics, but reaching degeneracy by evaporative cooling requires suppressing inelastic collisions. Microwave collisional shielding has enabled the preparation of degenerate Fermi gases and Bose-Einstein condensates of polar molecules, whereas static electric field shielding has been limited to fermionic species, which are less prone to inelastic loss. We demonstrate static-field Förster shielding of bosonic $^{23}$Na$^{87}$Rb molecules, suppressing two-body loss by up to four orders of magnitude. Within a bound-state-free electric-field window, three-body loss is also strongly suppressed, enabling efficient evaporation. Evaporating with an efficiency of 2.09(9), we increase the phase-space density of the gas by two orders of magnitude, reaching degeneracy with 7200(1000) molecules. We observe self-bound droplets at the end of evaporation over a wide range of field strengths, emerging from either degenerate or non-degenerate parent gases. We perform Path Integral Monte Carlo simulations, which suggest that the observed droplets are filamentary in nature, and find good agreement with the experimentally observed droplet formation temperatures. Our results establish Förster shielding as a single-field route to prepare degenerate gases and self-bound droplets of bosonic polar molecules.

</details>

### [77] [Development and characterization of a wingless ICPC HPGe detector with thin amorphous-Ge contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31123)
*S. A. Panamaldeniya,K. M. Dong,D. M. Mei*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种采用薄非晶锗（amorphous-germanium, a-Ge）接触的20克无翅片倒置同轴点接触（inverted coaxial point-contact, ICPC）高纯锗探测器，在77 K下实现皮安级漏电流、约440 V耗尽电压、良好能量分辨率（59.5 keV处1.75 keV FWHM，662 keV处3.19 keV FWHM），以及0.496 pF的绝对电容，证明了亚皮法级电容稳定运行的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高纯锗探测器需要薄的非晶锗接触层以实现双极电荷阻挡和表面钝化，同时最小化接触相关的非活性厚度。此前原型使用了保护翅片结构，本文旨在通过去除该翅片、采用无翅片几何设计，进一步简化器件结构并验证其性能。

Method: 采用USD生长、区熔纯化的高纯锗晶体，制备了20.0克的无翅片薄接触p型倒置同轴点接触探测器。在77 K下测量泄漏电流和耗尽电压，利用伽马射线谱学评估能量分辨率，通过标定电荷注入法（calibrated charge-injection method）测定绝对电容，并与SolidStateDetectors.jl静电建模结果对比；用241Am源置于探测器孔内测试近表面相互作用响应。

Result: 探测器在77 K下表现出皮安级漏电流和约440 V工作耗尽电压；伽马能谱在59.5 keV和662 keV处分别获得1.75 keV和3.19 keV FWHM的能量分辨率；标定电荷注入法测得绝对电容为0.496 pF（方法级相对不确定度6–8%），与静电建模得到的0.488 pF一致；241Am源置于孔内时观察到源相关过剩电流和位于3558 keV附近的宽泛退化α连续谱，表明对近表面相互作用具有敏感性。

Conclusion: 核主结论是成功制备了20克无翅片薄接触ICPC原型探测器，实现了亚皮法级电容的稳定运行。由于源封装、中间材料和近表面电荷收集响应未被独立表征，该连续谱不用于推断独特的死层厚度或能量损失机制，这一限制有待后续研究。

Abstract: High-purity germanium (HPGe) detectors with thin amorphous-germanium (a-Ge) contacts can provide bipolar charge blocking and surface passivation while minimizing contact-related inactive thickness. We report the fabrication and characterization of a p-type inverted coaxial point-contact (ICPC) HPGe detector using thin a-Ge contacts in a wingless geometry that avoids the protective wing structure used in our previous prototype. The detector was fabricated from a USD-grown, zone-refined HPGe crystal and has a mass of 20.0~g. At 77~K, it exhibited picoampere-scale leakage current and an operational depletion voltage of approximately 440~V. Gamma-ray spectroscopy yielded energy resolutions of 1.75~keV FWHM at 59.5~keV and 3.19~keV FWHM at 662~keV. The calibrated charge-injection method gave an absolute capacitance of 0.496~pF, with an estimated method-level relative uncertainty of 6--8\%, consistent with the 0.488~pF value obtained from electrostatic modeling with \texttt{SolidStateDetectors.jl}. With a $^{241}$Am source positioned inside the detector bore, the detector showed a source-correlated excess current and a broad degraded-alpha continuum centered near 3558~keV, demonstrating sensitivity to near-surface interactions. Because the source encapsulation, intervening materials, and near-surface charge-collection response were not independently characterized, the continuum is not used to infer a unique dead-layer thickness or energy-loss mechanism. The principal result is successful fabrication and stable sub-pF operation of a 20-g wingless thin-contact ICPC prototype.

Abstract: Models where an axion-like inflaton is coupled to a $U(1)$ gauge field are theoretically well motivated and can display a rich phenomenology. In this paper we present an analytical study of the onset of the strong backreaction regime. Our formalism relies on a Laplace transform to convert a complicated integro-differential equation into the search of the poles of an analytic function. We confirm the existence, previously observed using a completely different formalism, of a stable region of strong backreaction. Abstract: Gravitational waves offer a unique opportunity to solve the Hubble tension. In order to do so, we have to extract as much information as possible from cross correlating gravitational wave events (used as "dark sirens") with incomplete galaxy catalogs. Traditional methods assume a uniform in comoving volume distribution for galaxies missing from the catalogs, which neglects the fact that galaxies tend to cluster, leading to less precise and possibly biased posteriors. In this paper, we introduce a new method for accounting for galaxy clustering when dealing with an incomplete galaxy catalog, by adding back galaxies to the incomplete catalog and distributing them in pixels according to the correlation function $ξ(r)$. We find that our method drastically improves on traditional ones when the galaxy catalog is only $1\%-10\%$ complete, leading to posteriors that are between 2 and 4 times as precise, depending on the completeness fraction, without introducing any biases. Our method produces results comparable to traditional methods at extremely low catalog completeness fractions (<0.5\%) or for very high uncertainties in the recovered luminosity distance and sky localization of the gravitational wave events.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者从遍历性偏差的定义出发，推导其在大体积极限下按R^{-3/2}衰减所要求的高斯随机场功率谱条件；进而将分析推广到有限体积R的情形，对曲率扰动、密度扰动和速度扰动三种情形分别量化该偏差的大小，并评估R趋于无穷的极限对宇宙学观测的相关性。

Result: 研究发现R→∞的极限对宇宙学观测几乎无实际意义，因为观测尺度和关联范围已延伸至可观测宇宙的尺寸。在大尺度上，遍历性偏差对曲率、密度和速度扰动三类情形均显著。对于观测上最相关的密度扰动，相对偏差在分离尺度r=177 Mpc时首次超过100%，并且在所有r>560 Mpc的尺度上始终大于100%。微波背景辐射（CMB）温度扰动在大角尺度上也存在大偏差，但由于其分析不涉及体积平均，对观测无实际影响。

Conclusion: 当比较单位体积内大尺度结构的系综平均与体积平均时，必须考虑遍历性偏差的影响。CMB温度扰动的大尺度偏差虽大但因其分析不使用体积平均，故不影响观测结论。

Abstract: We consider the difference between ensemble and volume average in cosmology. It is known that for sufficiently weak long-range correlations the root mean square of the difference, which we call ergodicity bias, decays like $R^{-3/2}$ in the limit of large volume $R^3$. We calculate the condition this imposes on the power spectrum of a Gaussian random field. We quantify the bias for finite $R$, and show that the $R\to\infty$ limit is of little relevance for cosmological observations when the measured scales and correlations extend to the size of the observable universe. We consider curvature, density, and velocity perturbations. On large scales the bias is important in all three cases. For the density perturbations, which are observationally the most relevant, the relative bias first exceeds 100% at the separation $r=177$ Mpc, and is larger than 100% for all $r>560$ Mpc. It should be taken into account when comparing ensemble and volume averages for large-scale structure. The bias is also large for the cosmic microwave background temperature perturbations on large angular scales, but this is not relevant for observations, as their analysis does not involve volume averaging.

Conclusion: 该共振效应具有可观测的引力波信号特征，共振频率与增长率可映射到当前及未来引力波探测器的观测范围，为探测早期宇宙手性磁流体动力学提供可能途径。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the resonant interaction between stochastic gravitational waves (GWs) and chiral Alfvén waves in a magnetized chiral plasma in the early Universe. Starting from covariant chiral magnetohydrodynamics coupled to linearized gravity, we derive a closed system of equations for the coupled chiral-Alfvén velocity and magnetic-field perturbations. Both analytics and numerics show a parametric resonance at the sum frequency of the two CVE-split branches, with an instability band that widens as the GW strain grows. This CVE-to-Alfvén ratio is not a free parameter. In the early Universe, it is fixed by the Standard Model's relativistic degrees of freedom. Including the plasma's backreaction on the GW makes the energy exchange nonlinear, breaking the usual single-frequency Manley-Rowe picture. For a self-consistent choice of parameters, where backreaction is only a small correction, the unsuppressed resonance still drives the system to a finite-time blow-up within a few Hubble times. We then work out the resonant frequencies and growth rates involved, mapping out what current and future gravitational-wave detectors could observe.

### [83] [ABRR Summation Formulas for Relative Extremal Projectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30245)
*Jonas T. Hartwig*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将对极值投影子（extremal projector）的递归公式推广到由Conley和Sepanski引入的相对极值投影子（relative extremal projector）情形。当Levi子代数取为Cartan子代数时，恢复经典的ABRR递归；并给出了递归解的紧凑显式表达，适用于无限维反梯度Lie超代数、Kac-Moody代数、基本经典Lie超代数及有限维约化Lie代数等广泛范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khoroshkin在2004年证明了极值投影子与动力学扭曲（dynamical twist）的等价性，其中ABRR方程导出了极值投影子系数的递归公式，该公式在表示论和Mickelsson-Zhelobenko约化代数中具有广泛而统一的适用性。本文的动机是将这一强大的递归方法推广到更一般的相对极值投影子，以覆盖更广泛的代数类别。

Method: 将ABRR递归公式推广至由Conley和Sepanski（2003）引入的相对极值投影子。在Levi子代数取为Cartan子代数的特殊情形下，该推广自然地退化还原为通常的ABRR递归。此外，作者给出了该递归解的紧凑且显式的表达式，并验证该框架涵盖各类Lie超代数和约化Lie代数。

Result: 成功地建立了相对极值投影子的广义递归公式，并获得了递归解的紧致显式形式。所构建的框架统一涵盖了无限维反梯度Lie超代数（contragredient Lie superalgebras）、Kac-Moody代数、基本经典Lie超代数以及有限维约化Lie代数等多种代数结构。

Conclusion: 本文的推广将ABRR递归的适用范围从通常的极值投影子扩展到相对极值投影子，在更广泛的代数类别（包括超代数和无限维代数的情形）中保持了递归方法的一致性和适用性，为该领域后续研究与实际应用提供了统一工具。

Abstract: In 2004, Khoroshkin proved that the extremal projector is equivalent to the dynamical twist. The Arnaudon-Buffenoir-Ragoucy-Roche (ABRR) equation, satisfied by the dynamical twist, yields a recursive formula for the coefficients in the extremal projector. The resulting summation formula is uniform and powerful in its applications to representation theory and Mickelsson-Zhelobenko reduction algebras. In this paper we generalize this recursive formula to the relative extremal projector, introduced by Conley and Sepanski in 2003. When the Levi subalgebra is the Cartan subalgebra, we recover the usual ABRR recursion. We also find a compact and explicit expression for the solution to the recursion. Our setting includes infinite-dimensional contragredient Lie superalgebras, Kac-Moody algebras, basic classical Lie superalgebras, and finite-dimensional reductive Lie algebras.

</details>


### [84] [GIT for root stacks and 3d mirror symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30350)
*Swapnil Garg,Ruoxi Li,Yuji Okitani*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文基于Bodzenta-Donovan的窗口理论（window theory）结果，将根层（root stack）构造范畴化，并利用3d镜像对称（3d mirror symmetry）的B-side拉回与A-side前推等价性，推导出2n周期的2项半正交分解（semiorthogonal decomposition），同时将构造推广至新的GIT问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bodzenta-Donovan已证明根层的导出范畴具有2n周期的2项半正交分解，本文旨在将此代数结果纳入更广泛的几何与范畴框架中理解，并通过3d镜像对称与范畴表示论工具提供统一的解释及新推广。

Method: 定义根层构造的范畴推广，并解释为Gammage-Hilburn-Mazel-Gee 3d镜像对称等价B-side上的拉回（pullback）；利用Ben-Zvi-Francis-Nadler与Ben-Zvi-Nadler-Preygel的范畴表示论结果分析该拉回作用在半正交分解上的效果；证明该拉回等价于A-side上perverse schobers的前推（pushforward），从而通过对A-side语言Lagrangian骨架（Lagrangian skeleton）的简单分解得到周期性；并将Bodzenta-Donovan构造适配到新的GIT问题，证明互素m<n情形下Coh(√[m]{X/D})嵌入Coh(√[n]{X/D})。

Result: 建立了根层构造的范畴化版本，将其与3d镜像对称的B-side拉回对应；证明该拉回等价于A-side上perverse schobers的前推，并由A-side Lagrangian骨架的分解推导出2n周期性；在新的GIT问题下构造了互素嵌入Coh(√[m]{X/D})→Coh(√[n]{X/D})，并证明了所得2项半正交分解的2n周期性。

Conclusion: 通过3d镜像对称与范畴表示论的视角，对根层导出范畴的2n周期半正交分解给出了统一的范畴化解释，并将该构造推广至更一般的GIT设置，进一步拓展了窗口理论的应用范围。

Abstract: Using window theory, Bodzenta--Donovan showed that the derived category of a root stack $\sqrt[n]{X/D}$ has a $2n$-periodic $2$-term semiorthogonal decomposition. We define a categorical generalization of the root stack construction and interpret this as a pullback on the B-side of the 3d mirror symmetry equivalence of Gammage--Hilburn--Mazel-Gee. We analyze this pullback using categorical representation theoretic results of Ben-Zvi--Francis--Nadler and Ben-Zvi--Nadler--Preygel applied to SODs. We also show that the pullback is equivalent to a pushforward of perverse schobers on the A-side, allowing us to deduce periodicity from a simple decomposition of an A-side Lagrangian skeleton. Additionally, we adapt the construction of Bodzenta--Donovan to a new GIT problem, which yields an embedding of Coh($\sqrt[m]{X/D}$) into Coh($\sqrt[n]{X/D}$) for $m<n$ coprime, and prove $2n$-periodicity of the resulting $2$-term SOD.

</details>

### [85] [Inspiralling Binary merger parameter reconstruction with lunar and satellite Laser Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28753)
*Miguel Vanvlasselaer*

TL;DR: 本文首次从合成测距数据中重构并合大质量黑洞双星的啁啾质量（chirp mass）与光度距离（luminosity distance），发现单基线（如月球激光测距，LLR）下啁啾质量可按SNR幂律收敛恢复，但光度距离与天空方位角存在严重简并；双卫星测距构型（GUEST）可打破该简并，恢复光度距离的SNR依赖收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 假设地球—卫星双星遵循理想无扰开普勒运动，作者建立从测距观测量扰动中重构啁啾质量和光度距离的框架，并以月球激光测距（Lunar Laser Ranging, LLR）和双卫星测距构型（GUEST）为具体案例进行数值分析。

In contrast, the luminosity distance suffers from a severe, SNR-independent degeneracy with the sky-orientation angles in the LLR configuration, yielding fractional biases and uncertainties of tens to over a hundred percent regardless of signal strength. The two-satellite GUEST configuration breaks this degeneracy and restores a convergent, SNR-dependent recovery of $D_L$ comparable in scaling to that of the chirp mass. These results demonstrate that multi-baseline ranging architectures are essential for extracting luminosity-distance information from ranging-based gravitational wave searches, while chirp-mass measurements remain reliably accessible even with a single ranging baseline such as the Earth--Moon system.

Abstract: We show that for static & spherically symmetric black hole geometries, the angular components of the Brown-York tensor on a finite radius boundary can be interpreted as the product of the Tolman-Ehrenfest temperature and a quasi-local entropy. Thus, the behaviour of this entropy provides explicit verification of the covariant entropy conjecture.

Abstract: The existence of quantum gravity is the most important question in theoretical physics. Besides the theoretical development in this direction, looking for its low-energy consequences can provide clues. The spin-$2$ nature of its propagator. In this Letter, we calculate what the unique feature of the spin-$2$ graviton is in the interaction of two spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ particles. This unique feature shows itself in intertwining the spin and momentum of two particles simultaneously as a dipole-dipole interaction. This cannot happen for any spin-$0$ or spin-$1$ propagator, at least at leading order, which scales as $\frac{1}{r^3}$ in real space. We re-calculate the results using an independent approach, i.e., the Foldy-Wouthuysen transformation, and reach the same results. Due to simultaneous coupling of both particles' spins and momenta, a four-partite entanglement framework is suggested to observe this effect.

</details>

*Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh,Mark Essa Sukaiti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出紧致化谱离散化（compactified spectral discretisation）可能产生稳定的负虚轴（NIA）特征值梯子，但有限矩阵收敛并不能确立准正规极点（quasinormal pole）。通过对轴向史瓦西（Schwarzschild）扰动的分析，发现端点C^k正则性对于4α>k无选择性，C2连续谱问题在α≈15–32区间不可能具有离散NIA谱。尽管任意精度笔型计算揭示了68点C1梯子，但参数原理（argument-principle）计算在两条延拓带内给出零绕数，拒绝了全部68个候选极点。然而，同一阻尼控制可恢复史瓦西准正规模（QNM），说明精确的有限笔型特征值仍可能无法通过不变的Jost/Evans极点判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧致化谱离散化方法在数值上可能产生稳定的负虚轴特征值梯子，但这类有限矩阵收敛并不能证明这些特征值对应于真实存在的准正规极点，需要严格甄别数值假象与物理极点。

Method: 通过入出射因子分解（ingoing–outgoing factorisation）分析不希望的地平线解与无穷远解；使用切比雪夫网格（Chebyshev-grid）精确公式计算根-网格最大半径；采用任意精度笔型方程（arbitrary-precision pencil）计算C1与C2梯子；利用Jost行列式（Jost determinant）在离割扫描与参数原理计算中对候选极点进行甄别；并以相同阻尼控制的对照计算恢复已知的史瓦西准正规模。
