Conclusion: 精确的有限笔型特征值可能无法满足不变的Jost/Evans极点判据，因此不能仅凭谱离散化的数值收敛判定准正规极点存在性；表示依赖的节点（具有Keldysh权重）是否集体收敛到史瓦西割响应与Price尾仍属开放问题。

Abstract: Compactified spectral discretisations may yield stable negative-imaginary-axis (NIA) eigenvalue ladders, but finite-matrix convergence does not establish quasinormal poles. For axial Schwarzschild perturbations, ingoing--outgoing factorisation leaves an unwanted horizon solution behaving as $t^{4α}$ at $Ω=-iα$, and a $C^\infty$-flat unwanted infinity solution. Thus endpoint $C^k$ regularity is nonselective for $4α>k$, so the $C^2$ continuum problem cannot have a discrete NIA spectrum over $α\simeq15$--32. Exact Chebyshev-grid formulas give roots-grid maximum radius $O(n^{2p})$ for $x\sim C(1-y)^{-p}$, explaining C1, $x=2/(1-y)$, versus C2, $x=4/(1-y)^2$. Arbitrary-precision pencils nevertheless reveal a reproducible 68-point C1 ladder, while C2 reorganises it. Both physical lateral Jost determinants remain stably nonzero at every C1 frequency. A 0.05-spaced on-cut scan finds no zero, and refined argument-principle calculations give zero winding in both continuation strips. This rejects all 68 candidates, although the mesh and strip evidence is not interval-certified. Same-damping controls recover Schwarzschild QNMs $n=60,100,130$, with $|\widehat{\mathcal D}|=10^{-48}$--$10^{-56}$. The ladder has surface-gravity quarter spacing, follows the parameter-free Casals--Ottewill branch-cut-strength phase, and pairs with damping projections of QNMs $n=62,\ldots,129$. At its first member, a finite-frequency calculation finds a branch-strength zero at $α_q=15.07832396512359$, between the asymptotic prediction and the C1 root, supporting, but not proving, sequence-wide cut-phase locking. Thus accurate finite-pencil eigenvalues can fail the invariant Jost/Evans pole criterion. Whether representation-dependent nodes with Keldysh weights converge collectively to the Schwarzschild cut response and Price tail remains open.

</details>


### [92] [Exact dynamics and the spin wall for large-spin particles in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29115)
*Chao-Jun Feng,Rui-Hui Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文对 Schwarzschild 时空中自旋测试粒子的平面运动进行了精确的非微扰处理，基于 Tulczyjew–Dixon 自旋补充条件将 Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon（MPD）方程组化为封闭代数形式，揭示了线性化理论中无法出现的自旋墙（spin wall）现象及其筛选规则，并给出了弱场极限下的内禀最内稳定圆轨道和近日点进动的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲时空中自旋测试粒子的运动由 MPD 方程（Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon equations）描述，在自旋的一阶即偏离测地线运动；但现有研究几乎全部将动力学截断在自旋的线性阶，无法捕捉可能的非微扰效应。作者旨在建立一种不做任何自旋展开的精确处理方法，以检验并发现线性近似无法触及的定性特征。

Method: 在 Tulczyjew–Dixon 自旋补充条件下，对 Schwarzschild 时空中的平面运动进行非微扰处理：通过消去四速度（four-velocity）将 MPD 系统转化为封闭代数形式，将径向运动归结为有效势（effective potential）问题，全过程不对自旋做任何展开。

Result: 对于足够大的自旋，有效势在仅由粒子质量和自旋决定的特征半径处形成二重根，构成纯源于自旋的不可穿透自旋墙；超过临界自旋时，该墙位于事件视界之外并屏蔽视界。自旋墙对不同自旋、角动量和能量的粒子起筛选作用。此外，最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit）在弱场极限下获得闭式解，并在足够大的自旋下消失；同时还计算了弱场极限下近日点进动的自旋修正，所有结果在自旋为零时回归标准形式。

Conclusion: 自旋墙及其筛选规则是真正的非微扰现象，对自旋的任何有限阶展开都不可见，可能对致密天体的吸积流（accretion flows）产生可观测的观测特征。该工作证明线性自旋近似不仅在定量上有误差，在定性上也遗漏了本质性物理效应。

Abstract: The motion of a spinning test particle in a curved spacetime is governed by the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon (MPD) equations and deviates from geodesic motion already at first order in the spin. While essentially all existing studies truncate the dynamics at linear order in the spin, we present an exact, nonperturbative treatment of planar motion in Schwarzschild spacetime under the Tulczyjew--Dixon spin supplementary condition: eliminating the four-velocity recasts the MPD system into a closed algebraic form and reduces the radial motion to an effective-potential problem, with no expansion in the spin at any stage. This exact framework uncovers qualitative features that are absent from---and in fact unattainable within---the linearized description. Most notably, for sufficiently large spin the effective potential develops a double root at a characteristic radius determined solely by the particle mass and spin, marking an impenetrable \emph{spin wall} of purely spin origin; beyond a critical spin the wall lies outside the event horizon and shields it from generic infalling particles. Moreover, the wall is a filter for particle: only orbits with a specific combinations of spin, angular momentum and energy can penetrate it, all others being reflected before reaching the horizon. In addition, the innermost stable circular orbit, which in linear treatments merely shifts continuously with spin, is obtained in closed form in the weak-field limit and is shown to cease to exist at sufficiently large spin. We further compute the spin correction to the perihelion precession in the weak-field limit and verify that all results reduce to the standard ones at vanishing spin. The spin wall and its filtering rule are genuine nonperturbative phenomena, invisible to any finite-order expansion in the spin, with potential observational signatures in accretion flows around compact objects.

</details>


### [93] [Yukawa modification of Traversable Wormholes supported by Holographic Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29205)

What we have found is that the Yukawa-Bekenstein-Hawking HDE is the only positive profile, while the Yukawa-Moradpour and the Yukawa-Renyi have a limited region where the energy density is positive. The remaining Yukawa-Mixed Energy Density is always negative. This means that the Yukawa distortion introduces a kind of negative energy density.

Abstract: This work investigates the original Penrose process (PP), as well as black hole (BH) thermodynamics, within the framework of conformal Weyl gravity. Abstract: Superconducting microwave cavities loaded with radio frequency fields are a powerful tool to search for weak forces or electromagnetic perturbations due to new physics. By incorporating the interdependencies of all relevant parameters and the back-action of the electromagnetic fields on the cavity structure, we describe how figures of merit can be derived for a broad class of relevant experimental setups. Using a two-dimensional model, we demonstrate this formalism and present examples for optimized cavity geometries. We find that the choice of geometry alone can increase the signal-to-noise power ratio by an order of magnitude compared to an existing prototype of the same size. We also show that different physics goals lead to different optimal cavities, thus proving the need to consider such figures of merit at an early stage when designing a new detector.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波应变数据中的瞬态噪声毛刺会提高天体物理搜索的虚警率，并在与信号重叠时降低参数估计精度。此前没有任何方法能够识别毛刺的时间边界，导致减法操作必须使用填充窗口，既耗时又可能抹除信号本身。

Method: 提出三种边界识别方法：AMPS基于稳健幅度阈值设定边界，FLARE基于分段样条拟合的最佳拟合度定位边界，CRISP基于频谱图（spectrogram）功率的连通区域识别。配以三种减法技术：自适应样条拟合、小波收缩（wavelet shrinkage）及其组合。

Result: 在四种宽带毛刺上注入啁啾（chirp）信号，组合技术恢复95-97%的注入信噪比，而单独小波收缩仅约64%。CRISP的边界宽度在毛刺间最均匀；AMPS和CRISP识别边界仅需不到一秒，比FLARE快两到三个数量级。对于与GW170817重叠的毛刺，默认边界下残留有低频余波。

Conclusion: 三种边界识别方法与减法技术的组合有效改善了毛刺处理中的信号恢复效果，其中CRISP在边界均匀性和计算效率上表现突出。散射光毛刺尚未测试，是未来工作的主要方向。

Abstract: Transient noise artifacts, or glitches, in gravitational wave strain data elevate the false alarm rate of astrophysical searches and degrade parameter estimation when overlapping a signal. No method previously identified a glitch's time boundary: existing detection and classification tools flag and label glitches without resolving their extent, forcing subtraction to run over padded windows that cost time and erase signal beyond the glitch itself. We present three boundary identification methods, AMPS (Amplitude-based Multi-glitch Pulse Segmenter), FLARE (Fitness-based Localization And Refinement Extractor), and CRISP (connected-region identification via spectrogram power), paired with three subtraction techniques, adaptive spline fitting, wavelet shrinkage, and their combination, across five glitches from the GravitySpy database spanning Advanced LIGO's first three observing runs. Injecting a chirp signal on four broadband glitches, the combined technique recovers 95 to 97 percent of the injected signal-to-noise ratio, against roughly 64 percent for wavelet shrinkage alone. Abstract: Not much. It has been proposed that applying a rational filter to a black hole ringdown gravitational waveform reveals a direct wave component sourced near the event horizon. By solving the Teukolsky equation for a point particle plunging into a Kerr black hole, I show that the maximum amplitude of the waves sourced within the prograde light ring is $\lesssim 10\%$ that of those sourced by the full trajectory no matter if the waves are filtered or not. This agrees with the interpretation of quasinormal modes as waves orbiting the light ring and the direct wave as the power of these modes redistributed in time. Therefore, both quasinormal modes and direct waves are more a probe of the light ring than the near-horizon region. ### [99] [Cutoff-Stable Null Convergence and Directional Rigidity in Bianchi I Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29556)
*Ye Zhou,Alan Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了一个无端点的光学刚性定理（endpoint-free optical rigidity theorem），并利用它在不假设空间度量在固定基上对角的条件下，将Bianchi I时空划分为两种刚性（rigidity）区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在推广现有光学刚性结果，消除对空间度量对角化的假设，并希望通过无端点的Raychaudhuri方程处理来建立更一般的几何分类框架。

Method: 利用仿射Raychaudhuri方程（affine Raychaudhuri equation），将有限零里奇积分（null-Ricci integral）表示为展开边界项减去非负光学体项；通过独立移除过去与未来截断并检验两端liminf非负条件，结合两侧完备性推出光学张量逐点消失。在Bianchi I框架下对饱和几何进行分类，并区分弱截断稳定（cutoff-stable）与强逐点零会聚（pointwise null convergence）两种情形。

Result: 发现非静态Bianchi I度量可具有零、一或两条无向饱和线（等价于零、二或四条有向射线），第三条线则迫使度量静态。在更强条件下，一条两侧完备的零测地线即迫使空间度量常数且宇宙时间区间为整个实数轴，笛卡尔通用覆盖上的时空为Minkowski。周期模型在弱截断稳定区域达到四射线饱和界，且在非饱和方向满足$I_p^{ind}=-\infty$，但逐点零会聚在开放时间区间上失效。

Conclusion: 该定理为Bianchi I时空提供了完整的刚性分类，并建立了弱与强刚性条件之间的明确区分；物质与平均零能量条件（averaged-null-energy condition）后果在明确的场方程与截断规定匹配假设下成立。

Abstract: We derive an endpoint-free optical rigidity theorem and use it to separate two rigidity regimes in Bianchi I spacetimes, without assuming that the spatial metric is diagonal in a fixed basis. For a smooth, regular, twist-free null congruence, the affine Raychaudhuri equation expresses a finite null-Ricci integral as an expansion boundary term minus a nonnegative optical bulk. If the past and future cutoffs are removed independently and their two-end liminf is nonnegative, two-sided completeness forces the optical tensor and the null Ricci contraction to vanish pointwise. In Bianchi I this freezes the spatial metric on the fixed kernel of the conserved covector. We classify the resulting saturation geometry: a non-static metric has zero, one, or two unoriented saturated lines, equivalently zero, two, or four oriented rays, and a third line forces staticity. Under the stronger pointwise null convergence condition, the existence of a single two-sided complete null geodesic already forces the spatial metric to be constant and the cosmic-time interval to be all of $\mathbb R$; on the Cartesian universal cover the spacetime is Minkowski. Periodic models attain the four-ray saturation bound in the weaker cutoff-stable regime, with $I_p^{\mathrm{ind}}=-\infty$ in every nonsaturated direction, while pointwise null convergence fails on open time intervals. </details>

*Chaymae Karam,Dalale Mhamdi,Taoufik Ouali,Rachid Ahl Laamara,Mohamed Bennai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在幂律 $f(Q)$ 引力框架下，结合对数暗能量参数化 $ω_{de}(z)=ω_0+ω_1\ln(1+z)$ 的宇宙晚期加速膨胀。作者推导出哈勃参数（Hubble parameter）的解析表达式，并利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov Chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）方法，与 Pantheon⁺ Ia 型超新星、暗能量光谱仪第二次数据发布（DESI DR2）的重子声学振荡及宇宙学计时器的观测数据进行比较；随后使用赤池信息准则（Akaike Information Criterion）和贝叶斯信息准则（Bayesian Information Criterion）对模型与标准 ΛCDM 模型进行统计对比。结果表明，对 $f(Q)=Q+6γH_0^2(Q/Q_0)^n$ 模型采用对数参数化是标准暗能量模型的有效且更灵活的替代方案，在低红移处提供了更丰富的现象学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准暗能量模型（如 ΛCDM）中的常数态方程（equation of state）无法捕捉暗能量在红移演化中的可能变化，需要更加灵活的暗能量参数化方案来描述宇宙晚期加速膨胀。幂律 $f(Q)$ 引力和对数型暗能量参数化的结合，旨在为低红移宇宙学现象提供更丰富且可观测检验的理论框架。

Method: 在幂律 $f(Q)$ 引力模型 $f(Q)=Q+6γH_0^2(Q/Q_0)^n$ 中引入对数暗能量参数化 $ω_{de}(z)=ω_0+ω_1\ln(1+z)$，该形式在整个红移范围内给出对常数态方程的平滑偏离。通过解析推导获得哈勃参数表达式，并使用 MCMC 方法结合 Pantheon⁺ Type Ia 超新星、DESI DR2 重子声学振荡和宇宙学计时器数据对模型进行拟合，再以 AIC 和 BIC 与标准 ΛCDM 模型进行统计比较。

Result: 模型与最新的多类观测数据（Pantheon⁺超新星、DESI DR2 BAO、宇宙学计时器）取得了良好的一致性。AIC 和 BIC 的统计比较表明，采用对数参数化的 $f(Q)$ 引力模型是标准暗能量模型的可行且更灵活的替代方案，尤其在低红移区域能提供更丰富的现象学表现。

Conclusion: 对数暗能量参数化与幂律 $f(Q)$ 引力的结合构成了一种有效且灵活的暗能量模型替代方案，能够在低红移处展现出比标准模型更丰富的动力学行为。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe in power law $f(Q)$ gravity, where a logarithmic dark energy parametrization is considered: $ω_{de}(z)=ω_0+ω_1\ln(1+z)$. This description gives a smooth deviation from a constant equation of state within the complete range of redshifts. We obtain an analytical expression for the Hubble parameter and we use a Markov Chain Monte Carlo to compare the model with the most recent observational data obtained from Pantheon$^+$ Type Ia supernovae, Baryon Acoustic Oscillation measurements from the second data release (DR2) of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument, and Cosmic Chronometers. We then perform a statistical comparison between our model and the standard $Λ$CDM model using the Akaike Information Criterion and the Bayesian Information Criterion. From our results, we conclude that the use of logarithmic parametrization in the $f(Q)$ gravity model $f(Q)=Q+6γH_0^2\left(\frac{Q}{Q_0}\right)^n$ is a valid and more flexible alternative to standard dark energy models, as it provides a richer phenomenology at low redshifts.

</details>


### [101] [Peeling property for the Einstein scalar field equations with the nonzero cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29723)
*Jialue Li,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Bondi-Sachs度规框架下研究了带非零宇宙学常数的爱因斯坦标量场方程，给出了出射辐射条件下的渐近展开，并证明了在某些条件下成立的剥皮性质（peeling property）以及Bondi能量动量的损耗公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受引力波、暗物质与暗能量相互作用的启发，作者希望拓展爱因斯坦方程在非零宇宙学常数下的渐近分析，以更贴近包含暗能量的真实宇宙场景。

Method: 针对Bondi-Sachs度规，在出射辐射条件下对爱因斯坦标量场方程（含非零宇宙学常数）进行渐近展开；通过合适的坐标变换消去一个额外自由度，并推导剥皮性质与能量-动量的损耗公式。

Result: 与非零宇宙学常数为零的情形不同，渐近展开依赖于四个额外的Λ无关函数；在适当坐标变换下可使其中一个函数为零；当另一函数取零时证明了剥皮性质，并导出了非零宇宙学常数下Bondi能量-动量的损耗公式。

Conclusion: 对于来自引力波的某些真实数据，该损耗公式很可能表明Bondi能量-动量在实际观测中存在损耗性质，这将为非零宇宙学常数下的引力波分析提供理论基础。

Abstract: Inspired by interaction of gravitational waves, dark matters and dark energy, we study the Einstein scalar field equations with the nonzero cosmological constant for the Bondi-Sachs metrics. We provide the asymptotic expansions under the outgoing radiation condition. Unlike the case of the zero cosmological constant, the asymptotic expansions depend on four additional $Λ$-independent functions $B(u, θ, φ)$, $\tilde{X}(u, θ, φ)$, $\tilde{Y}(u, θ, φ)$ and $\tilde{I}(u)$. We show that, under suitable coordinate transformation, we can make $B(u, θ, φ)=0$. We prove the peeling property for the Einstein scalar field equations if $\tilde{I}=0$, and derive a loss formula of the Bondi energy-momentum for the nonzero cosmological constant. We remark that, for certain real data from gravitational waves, the loss formula is likely to indicate the loss property of the Bondi energy-momentum.

</details>


### [102] [Two Languages for the Same Resonance: From Nuclear Decay to Black-Hole Ringdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29806)
*Okuto Morikawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文梳理了量子共振（quantum resonance）与黑洞似正模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）之间数学等价关系的历史谱系，从最早的基础理论到后来的数学统一，并提供了跨学科术语对照词典，旨在使这种等价关系可操作化而不丧失其历史脉络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子共振和黑洞似正模共享紧密相关的数学结构（如辐射边界条件、Green函数解析延拓、非物理片上的极点、非自伴谱表示），但两者来自不同的物理起源——核共振理论源于放射性衰变与复合态，黑洞微扰理论源于时空稳定性与因果响应。作者旨在重建这一等价关系的谱系，而非首次引入该等价性。

Method: 文章采用历史编年法，将两条发展线索置于统一时间轴上：从Sommerfeld辐射条件与早期核衰变理论，经Regge–Wheeler微扰、Vishveshwara 1968年学位论文及1970年论文、Press 1971年提出的"似正模"术语，以及独立的Aguilar–Balslev–Combes解析延拓理论，追踪至散射共振理论中的数学统一。同时，作者构建了一个工作词典，对应不同学科中的等价概念。

Result: 文章提供了两套术语之间的系统性对应关系，包括无入射条件与视界入射/无穷远出射条件、极点能量与复频率、宽度与阻尼率、留数与激发因子、非共振背景与支割线及即时贡献之间的对照。

Conclusion: 文章的目的在于使跨学科等价关系在实际研究中可操作化，同时不抹平其各自的历史发展脉络，并明确区分数学等价性与历史谱系之间的差异。

Abstract: Quantum resonances and black-hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) are described by closely related mathematics: radiative boundary conditions, analytic continuation of Green functions or resolvents, poles on nonphysical sheets, and non-self-adjoint spectral representations. This article does not introduce that equivalence but reconstructs its genealogy. Nuclear resonance theory grew from radioactive decay, reaction cross sections, metastable compound states, and complex energies, whereas black-hole perturbation theory grew from spacetime stability and causal response.
  We place the two developments on a common chronology, from Sommerfeld's radiation condition and early nuclear-decay theory through Regge--Wheeler perturbations, Vishveshwara's 1968 dissertation and 1970 papers, Press's 1971 quasi-normal terminology, and the independent Aguilar--Balslev--Combes theory of analytic dilation, then follow their later mathematical reunification in scattering-resonance theory.
  Alongside the chronology, we give a working dictionary relating no-incoming and horizon-ingoing/infinity-outgoing conditions, pole energy and complex frequency, width and damping rate, residues and excitation factors, and nonresonant background with branch-cut and prompt contributions. The aim is to make the cross-disciplinary equivalence operational without flattening its history, and to distinguish mathematical equivalence from historical genealogy.

</details>


### [103] [Exotic Spinor Deformation of a Schwarzschild Black Hole: Perturbations and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30024)
*Luís Rodolfo dos Santos Filho,Bertha Cuadros-Melgar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇异旋量拓扑（exotic spinorial topology）如何通过德拉西算符（Dirac operator）中的拓扑梯度项，在施瓦西几何（Schwarzschild geometry）中改变费米子模式的传播，揭示出拓扑阻抗面（topological impedance surface）的双折射传播效应，并通过灰体因子（greybody factor）和准正则模（quasinormal mode）分析验证了背景度规的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索奇异旋量拓扑对费米子在弯曲时空背景中传播行为的影响，这一领域尚未被充分研究，有助于深入理解拓扑结构如何以有效几何形变的方式改变引力背景中的量子场动力学。

Method: 通过四脚架形变（tetrad deformation）将拓扑梯度 $\partial_\mu\varphi$ 引入狄拉克算符，使奇异旋量扇区自然诱导施瓦西背景的有效几何形变；随后使用旋量微扰分析灰体因子，并计算准正则响应对应的准正则谱。

Result: 中心结果是在 $r_{\text{topo}}=1/q$ 处出现拓扑阻抗面，抑制奇异旋量模式的径向群速度，产生双折射传播图案；准正则谱显示背景度规具有稳定性，且超对称伴势（superpartner potentials）不存在等谱性（isospectrality）。

Conclusion: 奇异旋量拓扑可通过有效几何形变显著影响费米子在施瓦西背景中的传播，产生的拓扑阻抗面抑制径向群速度并导致双折射行为；准正则分析确认背景度规稳定，同时超对称伴势之间不存在等谱性，暗示该构造的独特物理内容。

Abstract: In this work we investigate how exotic spinorial topology modifies the propagation of fermionic modes in the Schwarzschild geometry. By incorporating a topological gradient, $\partial_μ\varphi$, into the Dirac operator through a tetrad deformation, we show that the exotic spinorial sector naturally induces an effective geometric deformation of the Schwarzschild background. The central consequence of this construction is the emergence of a topological impedance surface at $\rtopo=1/q$, which suppresses the radial group velocity of the exotic spinorial modes, yielding a birefringent propagation pattern. Using a spinorial perturbation we discuss the greybody factor and the quasinormal response. The quasinormal spectrum shows the stability of the background metric and the absence of isospectrality for the superpartner potentials.

</details>


### [104] [Periodic orbits around a magnetically charged black hole in $f(R,T)$ gravity coupled with Euler-Heisenberg electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30049)
*Jun-Long Huang,Chen-Kai Qiao,Jun Tao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在f(R,T)引力与欧拉-海森堡非线性电动力学耦合背景下，带磁荷黑洞周围小致密天体的zoom-whirl周期轨道，重点考察磁荷Q_m和欧拉-海森堡参数a对最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）、边际束缚轨道（MBO）及进动参数q的影响，并通过数值kludge方法生成引力波形，发现磁荷可显著改变进动参数、轨道轨迹并在引力波中产生明显相位移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在f(R,T)引力框架下，磁荷黑洞周围的周期轨道动力学及其引力波特征，以理解磁荷和其他参数对强引力场中极端质量比旋近（EMRI）系统的影响。

Method: 系统计算磁荷Q_m和欧拉-海森堡参数a对ISCO和MBO特性的影响；重点分析进动参数q并系统考察各参数对周期轨道轨迹的影响；采用数值kludge（numerical kludge）方法生成周期轨道的引力波形。

Result: 磁荷能够显著改变进动参数q以及周期轨道的轨道轨迹；改变磁荷Q_m会在引力波形中引起显著的相位移动，而其他参数对引力波的影响相对较弱。

Conclusion: 磁荷在f(R,T)引力与欧拉-海森堡非线性电动力学耦合的背景下对周期轨道及引力波信号具有主导性影响，是此类系统中不可忽视的关键参数。

Abstract: We systematically investigate the zoom-whirl periodic orbits of a small compact object orbiting a magnetically charged black hole in $f(R, T)$ gravity coupled with Euler-Heisenberg nonlinear electrodynamics. We evaluate the impact of the magnetic charge $Q_m$ and the Euler-Heisenberg parameter $a$ on the characteristics of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the marginally bound orbit (MBO). Particularly, we focus on the precession parameter q and systematically examine how the magnetic charge and other parameters affect the trajectories of periodic orbits. Using the numerical kludge method, we generate gravitational waveforms for these periodic orbits. The results presented in our work demonstrate that the magnetic charge can significantly modify not only the precession parameter but also the orbital trajectories of the periodic orbits. Moreover, changing the magnetic charge $Q_m$ could cause a significant phase shift in the gravitational waveforms, while other parameters exert a relatively weaker influence on the gravitational waves.

</details>


### [105] [Spacelike reduction of the gravitational topological terms and associated helicity densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30154)
*Hongxi Huang,Jiang Long,Gan Zhao,Xin-Hao Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将拓扑的Pontryagin项和Nieh-Yan项约化到常时空间超曲面上，在线性化水平应用SVT分解后得到四种不同的螺旋度密度，并研究了它们在多种物理系统中的模式与多极展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力中的螺旋度（helicity）是一个多面性概念，文献中存在多种不等价的定义，本文旨在厘清不同定义之间的关系并给出系统的构造。

Method: 将拓扑的Pontryagin项和Nieh-Yan项约化到常时空间超曲面（spacelike hypersurface）上，在线性化水平应用标量-矢量-张量（SVT）分解，进而得到四种不同的螺旋度密度；随后对其模式和多极展开进行研究。

Result: 由Nieh-Yan项导出的螺旋度密度称为自旋1和自旋2引力磁螺旋度密度，分别源于矢量和张量模式；由Pontryagin项导出的螺旋度密度称为自旋1和自旋2引力流螺旋度密度。这些螺旋度泛函被应用于领先阶牛顿二体系统、弱场加速Kerr解、局部定义的缓变陀螺脉冲平面波模型以及线性化的引力Hopfion。

Conclusion: 四种螺旋度密度的构造及其在不同物理系统中的应用表明，这些螺旋度泛函可以区分不同类型模式的贡献，为引力螺旋度的系统研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Helicity in gravity is a multifaceted concept, and several inequivalent definitions exist in the literature. In this work, we reduce the topological Pontryagin and Nieh-Yan terms to a constant time spacelike hypersurface. Remarkably, upon applying the SVT decomposition at the linearized level, we obtain four distinct helicity densities. The helicity density arising from the Nieh-Yan term is termed the spin-1 and spin-2 gravitomagnetic helicity density, depending on whether it originates from vector or tensor modes. Meanwhile, the helicity density arising from the Pontryagin term is termed the spin-1 and spin-2 gravito-current helicity density, according to the corresponding mode contributions. We study the mode and multipole expansions of these helicity functionals and apply them to leading order Newtonian two-body systems, weak field boosted Kerr, a locally defined slowly varying gyratonic pp-wave model, and a linearized gravitational Hopfion.

</details>


### [106] [Black hole correspondences of quasi-topological gravity : Shadows, quasinormal modes, graybody factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30169)
*Sihao Fan,Chen Wu,Wenjun Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在拟拓扑引力（quasi-topological gravity）框架下，系统研究了五维规则黑洞的阴影（shadow）、准正则模（quasinormal modes）与灰体因子（graybody factors）三类可观测量之间的对应关系，检验了GBF–QNM对应与shadow–GBF对应在低模式（l=2）和高维度（D=5）下的适用性，并通过Langer修正将阴影-灰体因子对应的误差降至与GBF–QNM对应相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证阴影-灰体因子对应关系（shadow–GBF correspondence）在高维规则黑洞中的适用性，探索通过单一可观测量预测黑洞谱（black hole spectrum）与霍金辐射剖面（Hawking radiation profile）的可能性，以支持引力理论的多信使（multi-messenger）检验。

Method: 利用WKB近似（WKB approximation）和GrayHawk数值积分方法，在五维正则黑洞背景下检验GBF–QNM对应关系和shadow–GBF对应关系；并引入Langer修正（Langer correction），以有效角动量κ = √[l(l+D-3)]取代l来改进低模式的对应精度。

Result: 数值结果表明，GBF–QNM对应与数值结果之间的差异幅度保持较小；经Langer修正后，shadow–GBF对应在低模式下的误差被降至与GBF–QNM对应相当的水平。

Conclusion: 该工作将shadow–GBF对应推广至高维规则黑洞，为从单一可观测量预测黑洞谱和霍金辐射剖面提供了可行路径，从而有望实现引力的多信使检验。

Abstract: Within the framework of quasi-topological gravity, we systematically investigate the correspondences among three classes of observables---the shadow, the quasinormal modes, and the graybody factors---of regular black holes in $D = 5$ dimensions. Using the WKB approximation and GrayHawk numerical integration, we test the applicability of the GBF--QNM correspondence and the shadow--GBF correspondence in the low-mode ($l=2$) and higher-dimensions ($D=5$): the difference between the GBF--QNM correspondence and the numerical results remains small in magnitude; we apply the known Langer correction (replacing $l$ by the effective angular momentum $κ= \sqrt{l(l+D-3)} \simeq l + (D-3)/2$), which reduces the error of the shadow--GBF correspondence at low modes to a level comparable with that of the GBF--QNM correspondence. Our work extends the shadow--GBF correspondence to higher-dimensional regular black holes, providing a viable route to predict the black hole spectrum and the Hawking radiation profile from a single observable, and thereby to realize multi-messenger tests of gravity.

</details>


### [107] [External magnetic-field effects on dipolar-particle orbits and critical collisions in Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30755)
*Haowei Chen,Hengyu Xu,Shao-Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在精确的Kerr–Bertotti–Robinson时空中，通过研究电中性磁化粒子的赤道轨道和近视界碰撞，区分了强磁场通过引力反作用（gravitational backreaction）与直接磁场矩耦合（direct dipole coupling）两条通道对粒子动力学的影响。结果表明，几何形变可消除束缚轨道和有效势阱，直接偶极耦合则打破磁场反转对称性并依赖取向地移动最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）参数；在Bañados–Silk–West机制中，两种磁场效应均改变近视界势垒，但偶极耦合不影响近视界发散的主导阶行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强磁场对带电或磁化粒子的动力学影响存在两条难以区分的通道——引力反作用改变时空几何，以及磁场对固有磁矩的直接耦合（dipole coupling）。作者希望厘清这两种效应各自的贡献与后果。

Method: 在精确的Kerr–Bertotti–Robinson时空中，对电中性的磁化粒子分析其赤道面上的类时轨道与近视界碰撞。通过设定不同磁场方向与偶极取向，逐一考察几何形变途径与直接偶极耦合途径对转向点、圆轨道区、束缚势阱、近日点进动、最内稳定圆轨道（innermost stable circular orbit）及Bañados–Silk–West碰撞机制的独立影响。

Result: 磁场引起的几何形变会移动转向点与圆轨道域，且可消除有限有效势阱及其束缚轨道；即使无偶极耦合也能抵消克尔（Kerr）近日点进动并产生有限半径零进动轨道。直接偶极耦合则打破磁场反转对称性，并按取向依赖地移动ISCO的半径、能量与角动量；而超相对论轨道终点的形式端点仍由背景几何固定并趋近圆零轨道。在Bañados–Silk–West机制中，精确临界粒子在代表性非零场情形下于致极视界（extremal horizon）前被阻挡，但局部可允许的临界-普通粒子碰撞仍可产生无界质心能量；偶极耦合移动势垒但不改变近视界主导发散，非致极视界附近临界粒子被排除且碰撞能量有限。

Conclusion: 磁场通过几何形变与直接偶极耦合两条通道对轨道结构和近视界碰撞产生可分离的、定性不同的影响；几何效应对束缚轨道与零进动轨道有决定性作用，而偶极耦合仅在取向与反转对称性层面起作用，且不改变近视界碰撞能量发散的主导行为。

Abstract: Strong magnetic fields affect particle dynamics through two distinct channels: gravitational backreaction deforms the spacetime, while direct coupling to an intrinsic magnetic moment depends on the relative orientation of the field and the dipole. We disentangle these effects by studying equatorial orbits and near-horizon collisions of electrically neutral magnetized particles in the exact Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson spacetime. The field-induced geometric deformation shifts turning points and circular-orbit domains and can eliminate a finite effective-potential well together with its bound orbits. Even without direct dipole coupling, it can also offset the Kerr periapsis advance and produce a finite-radius zero-precession orbit. Direct dipole coupling breaks the symmetry under magnetic-field reversal and shifts the radius, energy, and angular momentum of the innermost stable circular orbit in an orientation-dependent manner. The formal ultrarelativistic endpoints of these orbit branches, however, remain fixed by the background geometry and approach circular null orbits. In the Bañados--Silk--West mechanism, both magnetic effects modify finite-radius potential barriers and hence the ability of a critical particle to reach the near-horizon collision region. In the representative nonzero-field cases examined here, an exactly critical particle released from infinity is blocked before reaching an extremal horizon, although a locally admissible collision between critical and usual particles can still produce unbounded center-of-mass energy. Finite dipole coupling shifts the barriers but does not change the leading near-horizon divergence. Near a nonextremal horizon, an exactly critical particle is excluded and the collision energy remains finite.

</details>


### [108] [Geometric origin of intrinsic rigidity for extremal horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30759)
*Wojciech Kamiński,Adam Szereszewski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文阐明了近视界几何（near horizon geometries）增强对称性的起源，将其与由非扩张视界（non-expanding horizons）构成的特殊叶状结构（foliation）的存在性联系起来，并在满足空汇聚条件（null convergence condition）的任意时空中证明了该叶状结构的存在性，证明基于几何方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近视界几何中观测到的增强对称性现象此前缺乏系统性的几何解释，本文旨在从几何层面厘清其物理根源。

Method: 采用纯几何方法，通过分析近视界几何时空中的零超曲面（null hypersurfaces），建立增强对称性与非扩张视界叶状结构之间的关联，并在空汇聚条件下给出一般性证明。

Result: 证明了在满足空汇聚条件的任意时空中，近视界几何必定存在由非扩张视界构成的特殊叶状结构，从而从几何上解释增强对称性的起源。

Conclusion: 近视界几何的增强对称性并非偶然现象，而是由非扩张视界叶状结构的存在性所保证的普适几何性质，其适用范围涵盖所有满足空汇聚条件的时空。

Abstract: Our work clarifies the origin of enhanced symmetry of near horizon geometries. We relate this property to an existence of specific foliation by non-expanding horizons. The existence of this foliation can be shown in any spacetime satisfying null convergence condition. Our proof is geometric and based on analyzing null hypersurfaces in near horizon geometry spacetimes.

</details>


### [109] [Gravitational-Wave Echoes from Layered Compact Objects: A Double-Shell Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30784)
*Qi Su,Ding-Fang Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由两个同心薄壳组成的致密天体的线性扰动与引力波（gravitational-wave）回波，发现双层壳结构在有效势中引入额外势垒，将波传播空间划分为四个耦合的有效腔室；内壳质量比增大时，回波谱峰表现出"谱峰排队"（spectral-peak queueing）模式，表明引力波回波可作为探测致密天体内部结构的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层（layering）是天体物理天体的普遍特征，且物理黑洞在外部固定位置观测者的时间坐标下保留其前身恒星的分层结构，因此研究具有分层结构的致密天体的振动与引力波特征具有物理意义。

Method: 对由两个同心薄壳组成的致密天体系统进行线性扰动分析，并与单壳层情形对比，考察双层结构对有效势和波传播空间的影响；通过改变内壳质量比系统扫描回波谱峰的变化规律。

Result: 双层壳结构在有效势中引入额外势垒，将波传播空间分为四个耦合有效腔室；内壳质量比增大时，新谱峰从高频侧进入回波谱，第二及后续谱峰向高频移动，最低频谱峰先向低频移动再返回其q=0位置，形成谱峰排队（SQ）变化模式。

Conclusion: 谱峰排队模式的存在表明引力波回波可用作探测致密天体内部结构的探针。

Abstract: Layering is a ubiquitous feature of astrophysical objects. Motivated by the fact that physical black holes retain the layered structure of their progenitor stars when viewed in the time concepts synchronizable with the clock of an outside fixed-position probe, we investigate linear perturbations and gravitational-wave (GW) echoes from a compact object composed of two concentric thin shells. Compared with the single-shell case, the double-shell structure introduces an extra barrier in the effective potential and partitions the wave propagation space into four coupled effective cavities. As the mass ratio of the inner shell increases, new spectral peaks enter from the high-frequency side of the echo spectrum; the second and later peaks shift toward higher frequencies; and the lowest-frequency peak first shifts toward lower frequencies and then returns to its $q=0$ position. We call this variation pattern spectral-peak queueing (SQ). Its existence suggests that GW echoes can be used as probes for the internal structure of compact objects under consideration.

</details>


### [110] [Saha's ionization in the Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30950)
*Lázaro L. Sales,Jonatas A. Silva,Amilcar R. Queiroz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究外部史瓦西时空（exterior Schwarzschild spacetime）中的萨哈电离平衡（Saha ionization equilibrium），通过质量壳条件推导出与类时Killing对称性相关的守恒能量，并将其引入麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布以构建渐进观测者视角下的电离平衡。研究表明引力场通过能量与热力学变量的红移修正萨哈关系，在满足Tolman–Ehrenfest和Klein平衡条件下，纯局域量表述可还原为标准平直时空的萨哈方程，并具体分析了电离能引力红移、电离率偏离及电离分数随渐近温度的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在静态引力场中建立一致的电离平衡描述，澄清引力红移效应与局域原子物理内在修正之间的区别，弥补现有萨哈方程仅适用于平直时空的局限。

Method: 从有质量粒子的质量壳条件出发，推导类时Killing对称性对应的守恒能量及其以史瓦西度规函数（lapse function）表达的非相对论极限；将该能量纳入麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布，构建渐进观测者的电离平衡；通过施加Tolman–Ehrenfest和Klein平衡条件，将纯局域量表达式约化为平直时空标准形式；并以麦克斯韦-居特纳（Maxwell–Jüttner）分布给出相对论经典对应。

Result: 引力场通过粒子能量与热力学变量的红移同时修正萨哈关系；局域量表述下方程还原为标准平直时空萨哈方程；分析给出电离能引力红移、相对平直时空电离率偏离以及电离分数随渐近温度的移动；相对论修正项在中性氢占显著多数的温度区间可忽略。

Conclusion: 研究提供了静态引力场中电离平衡的一致描述，明确区分了引力红移效应与局域原子物理的内在修正，表明局域观测者在满足平衡条件时仍适用标准平直时空的萨哈方程。

Abstract: We investigate the Saha ionization equilibrium in the exterior Schwarzschild spacetime. Starting from the mass-shell condition for a massive particle, we derive the conserved energy associated with the timelike Killing symmetry and obtain its nonrelativistic limit in terms of the Schwarzschild lapse function. This energy is then incorporated into the Maxwell--Boltzmann distribution to construct the ionization balance as seen by an asymptotic observer. We show that the gravitational field modifies the Saha relation through the redshift of both the particle energies and the thermodynamic variables. By imposing the Tolman--Ehrenfest and Klein equilibrium conditions, the formulation expressed entirely in terms of locally measured quantities reduces to the standard flat-spacetime Saha equation. We further analyze the gravitational redshift of the ionization energy, the relative departure from the flat-spacetime ionization ratio, and the corresponding shift of the ionization fraction as a function of the asymptotic temperature. We also present the relativistic classical counterpart based on the Maxwell--Jüttner distribution and show explicitly that its leading correction is negligible in the temperature range where neutral hydrogen is appreciably abundant. These results provide a consistent description of ionization equilibrium in a static gravitational field and clarify the distinction between gravitational redshift effects and intrinsic modifications of local atomic physics.

</details>


### [111] [Constraining equation of state of neutron star using neutron star-black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30995)
*Vasudev Dubey,Rahul Kashyap,Yugesh Bhoge*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究分析了中子星-黑洞（neutron star-black hole, NSBH）并合引力波瞬变信号中的潮汐效应，利用贝叶斯推断框架Bilby对数百个NSBH并合事件进行参数估计，评估当前和未来引力波探测器网络测量中子星裸潮汐形变率（bare tidal deformability）的可行性。结果表明，在包括爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）和宇宙探测器（Cosmic Explorer）在内的未来探测器网络中，至少需要20个事件才能清晰区分中子星的物态方程（equation of state）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星的潮汐形变携带物态方程及中子星物质组成的信息。与双中子星并合中只能测量两个中子星综合效应的lambda tilde参数不同，中子星-黑洞并合能够清晰测量中子星单一组分的潮汐形变率，因此有必要研究现有及未来引力波探测器对此参数的测量能力。

Method: 采用参数估计计算框架Bilby，对当前及未来引力波探测器网络中的数百个NSBH并合事件执行贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference），获取双星参数的后验概率分布函数（posterior probability distribution function）。

Result: 研究发现在包括爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探测器在内的未来探测器网络中，至少需要20个NSBH并合事件才能清晰区分中子星的物态方程。

Conclusion: 通过足够的NSBH并合事件样本，未来引力波探测器有望实现单次测量中子星裸潮汐形变率，从而为约束中子星物态方程提供独特途径。

Abstract: In this work, we study tidal effects that can be observed in gravitational wave transient from neutron star-black hole (NSBH) mergers. It has been well known that neutron star's tidal deformation has imprint of equation of state and hence composition of neutron star matter. Compared to binary neutron star mergers, where we observe a combined effect of two neutron stars by measuring the parameter known as lambda tilde. In neutron star black hole mergers, we measure the tidal deformability of the neutron star component clearly. In this work, we study the feasibility of measuring the bare tidal deformability of a neutron star with planned gravitational detectors in the near and far future. We use the parameter estimation computation framework, Bilby is used to perform Bayesian inference for hundreds of NSBH mergers in current and future GW detector networks and obtain the posterior probability distribution functions of binary parameters. We find that we need at least 20 events to clearly distinguish the equation state of neutron stars in future detectors, including the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer.

</details>


### [112] [Bekenstein--Hod Bound: A $3.3σ$ Confirmation from GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31039)
*Hai-Tian Wang,Shao-Peng Tang,Yi-Zhong Fan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用GW250114双子黑洞并合引力波信号对Bekenstein-Hod松弛率上限进行了首次高精度观测检验。通过在峰值前至少10个引力质量单位(M)处截断的数据推断剩余黑洞温度，并用并合后数据测量最长寿命衰变时间，避免直接重用同一应变样本。该约束在3.3-3.6σ显著性水平上得到验证，显著优于GW150914设定的91%置信水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞通过受扰系统松弛率的上限将引力、热力学和信息联系起来。Bekenstein-Hod约束在固定温度下规定了最小松弛时间，但其观测验证同时需要黑洞热力学表征和衰变时间测量，此前缺乏足够的观测数据支持。

Method: 利用GW250114（迄今最响亮的双子黑洞并合引力波信号）进行分离数据检验：从峰值前至少10M处截断的并合前数据推断余留黑洞温度，从并合后数据测量最长寿命衰变时间；环频率和阻尼时间允许独立变化而非固定为Kerr谱；对并合前截断位置和包含短寿命第一泛音进行了稳健性检验。

Result: 在t小于等于-10M的并合前截断分析中，Bekenstein-Hod约束在3.3-3.6σ水平上得到验证（横跨焦点环起始时间），相较于GW150914的91%置信水平有实质性提升。结论在多种并合前截断设置及显式包含第一泛音时依然稳健。

Conclusion: 这种分离数据的测量方法将信息论意义上的松弛约束转化为对单个天体物理黑洞的精确检验，为在真实黑洞系统上验证Bekenstein-Hod界提供了迄今最强的观测证据。

Abstract: Black holes link gravity, thermodynamics and information via limits on the relaxation rate of a perturbed system. The Bekenstein-Hod bound imposes a minimum relaxation time at fixed temperature, but its observational test demands both black hole thermodynamic characterization and decay time measurement. Here we test this bound with GW250114, the loudest gravitational-wave signal yet observed from a binary black-hole merger. We infer the remnant temperature from pre-merger data truncated at least $10\,M$ before the peak and its longest-lived decay time from post-merger data, thereby avoiding direct reuse of the same strain samples. The ringdown frequencies and damping times are allowed to vary independently rather than being fixed to the Kerr spectrum. For the primary $t_{<}=-10\,M$ analysis, the bound is verified at $3.3-3.6σ$ across the focal ringdown start times, representing a substantial improvement over the $91\%$ confidence level set by GW150914. The conclusion remains robust under varied pre-merger cutoffs and explicit inclusion of the short-lived first overtone in waveform modelling. This separated-data measurement converts an information-theoretic relaxation bound into a precision test of a single astrophysical black hole.

</details>


### [113] [Constants of motion and fundamental frequencies for elliptic orbits at fourth post-Newtonian order: aligned-spin contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31072)
*David Trestini,Jan Steinhoff*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在第四后牛顿（4PN）阶计算了具有对齐自旋的偏心系统的守恒量与基本频率之间的规范不变映射，扩展到自旋的线性、二次、三次和四次贡献，并推导了红移、陀螺不变量、圆轨道链接等衍生量，以及无界轨道的散射角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前在arXiv:2511.10735中推导的无自旋偏心系统映射推广到包含自旋效应的情形，弥补自旋对轨道动力学（能量-角动量与频率之间关系）的缺失。

Method: 在第四后牛顿阶（fourth post-Newtonian order）计算具有对齐自旋（aligned spins）偏心系统的规范不变（gauge-invariant）守恒映射，涵盖自旋的线性、二次、三次及四次项，并关联自旋形变参数（spin-deformability parameters）。

Result: 得到了包含所有自旋贡献的映射，以及红移不变量（redshift invariant）、陀螺不变量（gyroscopic invariant）和圆轨道链接（circular links）等衍生量；发现Blanchet-Iyer-Favata后牛顿稳定性判据与圆轨道近心点进动（periastron advance）之间具有内在联系；补齐了4PN阶无界轨道散射角（scattering angle）的全部自旋贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了4PN阶偏心自旋系统的守恒量-频率映射，并揭示了ISCO稳定性判据与近心点进动之间的深层关联，为完整描述自旋系统动力学提供了统一框架。

Abstract: For eccentric systems with aligned spins, we compute all spin contributions to the gauge-invariant conservative map between the constants of motion (energy and angular momentum) and the fundamental (radial and azimuthal) frequencies at the fourth post-Newtonian order. This extends the nonspinning map derived in [arXiv:2511.10735] to include terms that are linear, quadratic, cubic, and quartic in spin, as well as the associated spin-deformability parameters. We also obtain derived quantities such as the redshift and gyroscopic invariants, as well as circular links. We notice that the Blanchet-Iyer-Favata post-Newtonian stability criterion that determines the dimensionless frequency of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) is in fact intricately related to the periastron advance for circular orbits. Finally, in the case of unbound orbits, we complete the 4PN scattering angle with all spin contributions.

</details>


### [114] [Asymptotic Gravitational Radiation of Photon Rockets with $Λ$: Point, String, and Sheet Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31129)
*Francisco Fernández-Álvarez,José M. M. Senovilla*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带有一个类空Killing向量和任意符号宇宙学常数Λ的纯辐射Robinson-Trautman度量在无穷远处的引力辐射存在性。对于点源，将Λ=0时Kinnersley火箭唯一无辐射的结论推广到Λ≠0情形；对于弦源和片源，则发现了新的无辐射可能性。文章为Λ>0和Λ<0分别给出了基于渐近超Poynting向量和Cotton-York张量与全息应力张量比例性的无辐射判据，并给出了两者可交换但不成比例的显式反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对Robinson-Trautman度量引力辐射的研究主要局限于Λ=0的情形，且对字符串源和片源产生的辐射特征的刻画尚不完整。本文的目的是将引力辐射存在性的判据系统推广到任意符号的宇宙学常数Λ，并统一理解不同判据之间的关系。

Method: 利用基于渐近超Poynting向量（asymptotic super-Poynting vector）的判据判断引力辐射的存在性；对Λ>0情形，以scri的单位法向量为参照计算规范化的渐近超Poynting向量，要求其消失；对Λ<0情形，要求所有与scri相切的单位类时向量对应的渐近超Poynting向量的法向分量消失，等价于Cotton-York张量与全息应力张量在scri处成比例。同时通过分析scri处重标Weyl张量的主零方向（principal null directions）来联系几何与张量判据。

Result: 对于点源情形，Kinnersley火箭是唯一无引力辐射的火箭，该结论从Λ=0推广到Λ≠0；弦源和片源则存在新的无辐射可能性。Λ>0时，规范化渐近超Poynting向量的消失精确刻画了无辐射；Λ<0时，正确的判据是Cotton-York张量与全息应力张量的比例性。文中还构造了Cotton-York与全息应力张量可交换但不成比例从而仍产生辐射的显式例子，并确定了两种Λ符号下重标Weyl张量的主零方向。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了不同符号宇宙学常数下引力辐射判据的本质差异，Λ<0情形需要比Λ>0更精细的张量比例性判据而非简单的交换性。主零方向相对于scri法向的几何构造与张量判据之间建立了明确对应关系。

Abstract: We study the existence of gravitational radiation at infinity for pure-radiation Robinson-Trautman metrics with one spacelike Killing vector and a cosmological constant $Λ$ of arbitrary sign. These metrics describe "photon rockets" with point, string, and sheet sources moving through spacetime while emitting null radiation. For $Λ\neq 0$, we use criteria for the existence of gravitational radiation based on the asymptotic super-Poynting vector. For point sources, we extend to $Λ\neq 0$ the known $Λ=0$ result that the Kinnersley rocket is the only rocket without gravitational radiation. However, for string and sheet sources, new possibilities without gravitational radiation arise. For $Λ>0$, we show that the vanishing of the canonical asymptotic super-Poynting vector---computed with respect to the unit normal to scri---exactly characterizes the absence of radiation. On the other hand, for $Λ<0$, we show that the appropriate criterion is the vanishing of the components normal to scri of the asymptotic super-Poynting vectors associated with any unit timelike vector tangent to scri or, equivalently, the proportionality between the Cotton-York tensor and the holographic stress tensor at scri. In particular, we provide explicit examples of metrics for which the Cotton-York and holographic stress tensors commute, yet gravitational radiation is present because the two tensors are not proportional. We also determine the principal null directions (PNDs) of the rescaled Weyl tensor at scri for both signs of $Λ$ and relate their geometry relative to the normal to scri to the tensorial criteria for gravitational radiation.

</details>


### [115] [Evolution of Cosmic String Loops under Gravitational Backreaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31163)
*Lasse Gerblich,Richard A. Battye,E. P. S. Shellard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Nambu-Goto宇宙弦环（cosmic string loops）在引力反作用（gravitational backreaction）下尖点（cusp）的演化与引力波波形修正。作者通过新的规范选取改写了弦环在自身动态源引力场中的运动方程，实现了首个连续数值演化，发现尖点在反作用下依然存在，但引力波波形被显著修正：暴发强度减弱，并在高频端引入截止频率 f_c ∝ (Gμ)^{-3/2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nambu-Goto宇宙弦环通常会产生尖点，即以光速瞬间运动的点，由此产生特征谱形为 \tilde{h}(ω) ∝ (Gμ)ω^{-4/3} 的强引力波暴发，是引力波搜索的重要目标。然而此前的标准图像未考虑引力反作用的影响，因此需要建立包含自引力动态反馈的运动方程与数值演化框架，以检验并修正尖点暴发预测。

Method: 作者通过选取一种便捷规范（gauge），将Nambu-Goto弦环在其自身动态产生的引力场中的运动方程重新表述，使得在引力反作用下对弦环进行连续数值演化成为可能。该形式体系直接利用弦自身引力场构造方程，从而将反作用效应纳入动力学计算。

Result: 关键发现在于，尖点在引力反作用作用下依然存续。然而与该形式体系计算出的引力波波形相比，尖点暴发显著减弱，并在高频端出现截止频率 f_c ∝ (Gμ)^{-3/2}。该截止频率以上的信号被抑制，使得当前与近未来引力波探测器相比未受扰动的波形预测，预期的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）降低。

Conclusion: 引力反作用不消灭弦环的尖点，但会显著削弱其引力波暴发强度并在高频引入截止。这意味着对宇宙弦引力波的搜寻策略和灵敏度预估需要基于包含反作用效应的修正波形，而不是未扰动图像。

Abstract: Nambu-Goto cosmic string loops generically develop cusps, points where the string momentarily reaches the speed of light. These cusps produce strong gravitational wave bursts with a characteristic strain spectrum $\tilde{h}(ω)\propto (Gμ)\,ω^{-4/3}$, making them prime targets for gravitational wave searches, where $μ$ is the string mass per unit length. However, this picture is modified when one accounts for gravitational backreaction. Using a convenient gauge, we reformulate the Nambu-Goto equations of motion for a loop moving in its own dynamically sourced gravitational field, enabling the first continuous numerical evolution of loops under this backreaction. A key finding is that locally cusps survive backreaction. Nevertheless, the gravitational waveform calculated in this formalism is significantly modified, weakening the cusp burst and introducing a high-frequency cutoff at $f_c\propto(Gμ)^{-3/2}$. This suppression above $f_c$ reduces the expected signal-to-noise ratio in current and near-future detectors relative to unperturbed waveform predictions.

</details>


### [116] [Polytropic wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16969)
*Remo Garattini,Emmanuel N. Saridakis,Athanasios G. Tzikas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在一个统一框架下研究了由多方状态方程（polytropic equation of state）支撑的穿越虫洞（traversable wormhole），分别考察了均匀与非均匀构型，并分析了多方参数对几何和能量条件的影响。研究发现非均匀情形下几何完全由径向多方系数决定，且当指数满足特定关系时自然涌现出一种广义"荒谬良性"穿越虫洞构型，建立了一个将多方物质与虫洞几何系统化联系起来的灵活框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中穿越虫洞需要非标准物质源，这使得寻找具有物理动机的状态方程尤为重要。该研究旨在补充这一领域中对物理上可实现的虫洞物质源的探索。

Method: 采用多方状态方程作为物质模型，在统一框架下分别处理均匀（homogeneous）和非均匀（inhomogeneous）构型。推导对应解，并利用功率律（power-law）剖面色谱构造显式解，分析多方参数对几何、能量条件及径向支撑的影响。

Result: 均匀情形下得到由常数多方参数控制的虫洞内部解；非均匀情形下获得一个一般解析表达式，证明几何完全由径向多方系数ω(r)决定。对于正的ω(r)，物理意义要求多方指数限于奇数整数取值。当指数满足α=2γ-3时自然涌现广义"荒谬良性"穿越虫洞构型；虽然耀外条件（flare-out condition）在喉部违反零能量条件（null energy condition），但非均匀框架允许其径向分布可控。

Conclusion: 研究建立了多方物质与虫洞几何之间的系统性联系，为构建具有局域化奇异物质（exotic matter）的紧凑虫洞提供了灵活框架。

Abstract: Traversable wormholes in general relativity require non-standard matter sources, making the identification of physically motivated equations of state particularly important. We investigate wormholes supported by a polytropic equation of state, considering homogeneous and inhomogeneous configurations within a unified framework. We derive the corresponding solutions and analyze the effects of the polytropic parameters on the geometry and energy conditions. In the homogeneous case, the polytropic construction yields a consistent wormhole interior whose geometry and matter content are governed by the constant polytropic parameters. For the inhomogeneous case, we obtain a general analytical expression showing that the geometry is completely determined by the radial polytropic coefficient $ω(r)$. For positive $ω(r)$, the requirement for physically meaningful solutions naturally restricts the polytropic exponent to odd integer values. Using a power-law profile, we construct explicit classes of solutions exhibiting distinct parameter regimes and finite radial support. Interestingly enough, for an exponent $α=2γ-3$, a generalized absurdly benign traversable wormhole-like configuration emerges naturally. Although the flare-out condition implies null-energy-condition violation at the throat, the inhomogeneous framework allows its radial distribution to be controlled. Our results establish a systematic connection between polytropic matter and wormhole geometry, providing a flexible framework for constructing compact wormholes with localized exotic matter.

</details>
