### [117] [A route to the thermodynamics of colloid-polymer mixtures from structural information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29124)
*Vikki Anand Varma,Andrew J. Archer,Alberto Scacchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新框架，仅利用单一热力学状态点（state point）的结构关联信息（径向分布函数或静态结构因子）即可构建自由能泛函（free-energy functional），从而直接预测复杂流体的热力学性质与相行为，无需预先知道微观相互作用势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液态理论为微观结构与宏观热力学行为之间提供了基础联系，但复杂流体的有效相互作用往往未知或依赖于近似，这构成了建模的核心困难。作者希望绕过对相互作用势的显式需求，直接从实验可测的结构信息出发推断相行为。

Method: 方法核心是从单一热力学状态点获取的结构相关性（径向分布函数radial distribution function或静态结构因子static structure factor）出发，构建自由能泛函，进而预测热力学性质和相行为。该框架不需要显式知道底层相互作用势的信息。

Result: 该框架在多种模型流体上进行验证，包括胶体（colloidal）体系和胶体—聚合物（colloid–polymer）体系，预测结果与分子模拟数据吻合良好。结果表明单一状态点的结构信息足以预测系统的更广泛热力学响应。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了从实验测得的结构数据直接推断相行为的可行路径，即使微观相互作用事先未知，也能实现热力学性质的预测。

Abstract: Liquid-state theory provides a fundamental connection between microscopic structure and macroscopic thermodynamic behaviour. Here, we develop a framework for predicting thermodynamics and phase behaviour directly from structural correlations, using either radial distribution functions or static structure factors as input. The approach constructs a free-energy functional from structural information obtained at a single thermodynamic state point, without requiring explicit knowledge of the underlying interaction potentials. This circumvents a central difficulty in modelling complex fluids, for which effective interactions are often unknown or rely on approximations. We demonstrate the framework for a range of model fluids, including colloidal and colloid--polymer systems, with predictions in good agreement with molecular simulation data. The results show that structural information at a single state point can provide sufficient information to predict the broader thermodynamic response of a system. This establishes a route toward inferring phase behaviour directly from experimentally measured structure, even when the microscopic interactions are not known a priori.

</details>


### [118] ["Ultra-weak" First-Order Phase Transition in Biaxial Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29343)
*Soumyajit Pramanick,Mrinal Kanti Debnath,Nababrata Ghoshal,Soumen Kumar Roy,Sudeshna Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟和多重直方图重加权（multiple histogram reweighting）技术，系统表征了双轴向列相液晶（biaxial nematogens）中不同相变强度的层级结构，发现各向同性到单轴（I → N_U）相变为对称性强制的弱一级相变，而单轴到双轴（N_U → N_B）相变在三相点附近强度极弱，比大型系统中前一类转变低一个数量级以上，属于超弱一级相变（ultra-weak first-order transition）的新类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双轴向列相液晶中不同类型相变（各向同性—单轴—双轴）的相对强度层级，尤其是单轴到双轴相变在临界点与三相点之间的基本性质，为探索超弱一级相变提供新的研究方向。

Method: 在大型格点系统上采用高分辨率蒙特卡罗模拟，并运用多重直方图重加权技术精确获取自由能景观与相变强度信息，从而对不同相变进行准确分级。

Result: 各向同性到单轴的 I → N_U 相变被证实为对称性强制的（symmetry-enforced）一级相变；而位于三临界点（tricritical point）与三相点（triple point）之间（λ = 0.26）的单轴到双轴 N_U → N_B 相变属于邻近驱动型（proximity-driven）转变，在大系统中其强度比前一类相变小一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该类别超弱一级相变的存在提供了一条新的研究路径，对未来探索弱相变现象及其机理具有潜在意义。

Abstract: We report high-resolution Monte Carlo evidence characterizing the hierarchy of transition strengths in biaxial nematogens. By employing multiple histogram reweighting on large lattices, we demonstrate that while the isotropic-to-uniaxial ($I \to N_U$) transition is symmetry-enforced first-order, the uniaxial-to-biaxial ($N_U \to N_B$) transition in the region between the tricritical point and the triple point ($λ= 0.26$) is proximity-driven and more than an order of magnitude smaller for large system sizes. Such a class of ultra-weak first-order transitions could be a new pathway for future investigations.

</details>


### [119] [From Freezing to Terminal Packing: A Puzzle and a Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29676)
*Biman Bagchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了拉马克里希南-尤索夫（Ramakrishnan–Yussouff）非线性分岔框架，发现基于密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）的平衡冻结理论在高密度端也存在一个接近终端堆积的端点，并指出该端点对应亚稳液体沿有限波矢密度模的热力学边际性（thermodynamic marginality），而非平衡冻结或机械堵塞（mechanical jamming）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在厘清密度泛函理论中平衡冻结、终端边际性与高密度终止点之间的概念关系，并揭示液态相关函数如何编码不同维度系统向无序高密度堆积逼近的普遍规律。

Method: 作者重新考察非线性Ramakrishnan–Yussouff分岔框架，区分第一个晶体折叠（crystal fold）、热力学共存与终端边际性；在终端点处分析大势（grand potential）沿主有限波矢密度模的曲率消失条件及非线性自洽方程刚度的丧失，并分别对一维硬棒、二维硬圆盘与三维硬球进行数值构造。

Result: 一维硬棒给出精确的近排堆积（close packing）；二维硬圆盘终端堆积分数约为0.8407；三维硬球终端数值约为0.629，接近随机近排堆积（random close packing）。该端点被识别为亚稳液体延续的有限波矢热力学边际性。

Conclusion: 研究表明该高密度端点并非平衡冻结或机械堵塞，而是亚稳液体延续的有限波矢热力学边际性，并由此暴露一个持久谜题：为何液态相关函数能在不同物理维度和不同系统中编码终端无序堆积的趋近行为。

Abstract: A density-functional theory developed for equilibrium freezing also produces a high-density endpoint close to terminal packing. We revisit the nonlinear Ramakrishnan--Yussouff bifurcation framework and distinguish the first crystalline fold, thermodynamic coexistence, and terminal marginality. At the terminal point, the grand-potential curvature vanishes along the principal finite-wavevector density mode, exactly when the nonlinear self-consistency equation loses stiffness. The construction gives exact close packing for one-dimensional hard rods, a terminal hard-disk packing fraction near 0.8407, and a hard-sphere value near 0.629, close to random close packing. These results identify a finite-wavevector thermodynamic marginality of a metastable liquid continuation, rather than equilibrium freezing or mechanical jamming, and expose a persistent puzzle: why should liquid-state correlations encode the approach to terminal disordered packing across different physical dimensions and systems?

</details>


### [120] [Active embracement enables autonomous tweezing in active star polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29728)
*Marco Musacchio,Davide Breoni,Iman Abdoli,Luca Tubiana,Hartmut Löwen,Lorenzo Caprini*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文报道了活性拥抱（active embracement）这一非平衡现象：活性星形聚合物（active star polymers）在内部自推进驱动下，可从未折叠状态自发转变为紧密塌缩的"拥抱"态，实现单体自塌缩、多体互缠互拥以及捕获周围被动粒子等平衡系统中不可达的行为，从而可作为自主镊子（autonomous tweezers）用于靶向货物捕获和自导装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主结构重构能力是生命系统的核心特征之一，但在人工活性物质（active matter）中仍难以实现。本文旨在探索如何使人工活性聚合物获得类似生命的自主重构与捕获能力，弥补宏观机器人集群与微观聚合物物理之间的鸿沟。

Method: 研究结合了聚合物实验与模拟：实验采用连接振动机器人（vibrobots）构建活性星形聚合物系统，模拟用于验证和解释实验观察结果。活性星形聚合物由中心核心和自推进单体臂组成，通过对比被动聚合物行为，考察内部自推进对空间排斥效应的抑制作用及由此产生的多种非平衡行为。

Result: 研究发现内部自推进从根本上压倒了维持被动聚合物分散的位阻排斥（steric repulsion），使活性星形聚合物实现球状自塌缩（globular-like self-collapse）、多体互相缠结拥抱，以及自发拥抱并捕获周围被动颗粒等平衡系统不可达的行为。该活性拥抱被确认为一种独特的动力学相态（kinetic phase），使星形聚合物可作为自主镊子运作。

Conclusion: 活性拥抱是一种独特的动力学相态，赋予星形聚合物自主镊子功能。该工作为设计能够在复杂环境中实现靶向货物捕获与自导装配的智能材料提供了通用蓝图，架起了宏观机器人集群与微观聚合物物理之间的桥梁。

Abstract: The capacity for autonomous structural reconfiguration is a defining trait of living systems, yet it remains elusive in artificial active matter. Here, we report the discovery of active embracement, a non-equilibrium phenomenon where active star polymers, comprising a central core and self-propelled monomeric arms, transition from open configurations to tightly collapsed, ``hugging'' states. By combining polymer experiments using connected vibrobots with simulations, we demonstrate that internal self-propulsion fundamentally overrides the steric repulsion that keeps passive polymers dispersed. This active drive enables a suite of behaviors unattainable in equilibrium systems: individual star polymers undergo a globular-like self-collapse, multiple star polymers mutually intertwine and mutually embrace, and can spontaneously embrace and capture surrounding passive particles. Our findings reveal that active embracement is a distinct kinetic phase that allows star polymers to function as autonomous tweezers. By bridging the gap between macroscopic robotic collectives and microscopic polymer physics, this work provides a versatile blueprint for the design of smart materials capable of targeted cargo capture and self-directed assembly in complex environments.

</details>


### [121] [Chiral cellulose fibril organization in a plant cell wall as liquid crystal confined in a cylindrical boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29859)
*Jieh-Wen Tsung,Bo-Hsien Wu,Li-Yan Hung,Yu-Chieh Huang,Yueh-Ching Huang,Shao-Chun Hsieh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 利用偏振光学显微镜（polarized optical microscopy）识别了木质部细胞壁中纤维素微纤丝（cellulose microfibrils）的排列方式，建立了五种组织结构（线状、螺旋、环状、交叉螺旋、扭曲螺旋）的计算模型以模拟双折射彩色剖面，并与巨桉（Eucalyptus grandis）横截面的实际观测结果对应，揭示了细胞壁的液晶相（liquid crystal phase）与图案形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解细胞壁中纤维素微纤丝的精确组织结构，对于揭示树木如何形成三维螺旋通道网络并实现细胞分化与增厚机制具有重要意义。通过将细胞壁薄层描述为一类自组织体系（self-organizing system），可以从物理学角度统一解释其多种微观织构（texture）。

Method: 利用偏振光学显微镜（polarized optical microscopy）识别木质部细胞壁中的微纤丝排列；将圆柱壳（cylindrical shell）中的平衡构型视为弹性形变（elastic deformation）与表面锚定（surface anchoring）相互平衡的结果；建立由线、螺旋、环、交叉螺旋和扭曲螺旋五种组织对应的计算模型，用于模拟其双折射彩色剖面（birefringent-colored profiles）。

Result: 在巨桉（Eucalyptus grandis）横截面上，五种织构呈现出清晰可辨的独特光学纹理：支持作用的韧型纤维（libriform fiber）细胞壁呈扭曲螺旋结构，高速吸水的导管（vessel）细胞壁由螺旋层与涡旋阵列层构成，径向运输的射线细胞（ray cell）呈交叉螺旋结构。纤维细胞壁中，微纤丝角（microfibril angle, MFA）与曲率、弯曲和表面锚定显著相关；导管细胞壁的涡旋阵列包含手性序焦锥畴（focal conic domains）和非手性序顶端缺陷（topological defects），表明纤维素纤维的相变（phase transition）导致图案形成。

Conclusion: 细胞壁中的液晶相和图案揭示了细胞壁增厚与细胞分化的机制。长而刚硬的扭曲微纤丝在细长管腔中的堆积所引发的失谐（frustration），促使树木生成螺旋状通道网络，从而将脆性薄层转化为具有弹性、抗撕裂且可自愈的组织。

Abstract: The order of cellulose microfibrils in xylem cell walls is identified using polarized optical microscopy. The line, helix, ring, crossed helix, and twisted helix organizations in a cylindrical shell are the equilibrium states of balanced elastic deformation and surface anchoring. A computational model of the five organizations is established to simulate their birefringent-colored profiles. These five textures present unique optical textures, which are clearly recognized in the cross sections of Eucalyptus grandis. The libriform fiber cell for support has a twisted helical cell wall. The vessel cell for high-speed water uptake consists of a helical layer covered by a layer of vortex array. The ray cell for radial transportation is crossed helical. The microfibril angles versus the radius of the cell wall were measured utilizing the distribution of birefringence colors. In fiber cell walls, the MFA is significantly correlated with the curvature, bending, and surface anchoring, respectively. In vessel cell walls, the vortex array includes focal conic domains of chiral order and topological defects of nematic order, suggesting that the phase transition of cellulose fibrils leads to pattern formation. Liquid crystal phases and patterns in the cell walls reveal how the cell wall thickens and how cells differentiate. Out of the frustration of long, stiff, twisting fibrils packed in slender tubes, trees generate the helical channel networks, transforming the brittle lamina into an elastic, tear-resistant, self-healing tissue.

</details>


### [122] [Birefringent-colored optical profiling of wood presenting the 3D cellulose microfibril architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29866)
*Jieh-Wen Tsung*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于偏光光学显微镜（polarized optical microscopy）双折射颜色（birefringent color）成像的木材细胞壁微纤维结构分析方法，通过建立光学模型模拟五种典型细胞壁结构（线状、螺旋状、环状、交叉螺旋和扭转螺旋）的双折射彩色显微图像，并利用延迟波片区分不同取向的微纤维，实现了对巨桉（Eucalyptus grandis）树干与枝条细胞壁复杂螺旋结构的准确、高通量识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 木材微纤维中双折射纤维素在偏光显微镜下呈现多种干涉颜色，但目前缺乏系统的光学模型来解释和利用这一现象。电子显微镜和原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）无法分辨被木质素和半纤维素基质包裹的纤维素微纤维，亟需一种能够直接、准确识别细胞壁复杂螺旋结构的方法。

Method: 建立光学模型模拟木材的双折射彩色显微图像，考虑线状、螺旋状、环状、交叉螺旋和扭转螺旋五种典型细胞壁结构；使用延迟波片（retardation wave plate）区分不同取向的纤维；将可见纤维趋势、双折射彩色光学纹理和模拟查漏库三种识别方法进行对比验证。

Result: 每种细胞壁结构在延迟波片下呈现独特的双折射彩色纹理，清晰展示其潜在三维结构；巨桉树干和枝条的横截面呈现双折射彩色剖面；三种识别方法结果一致，证明双折射彩色标签准确高效。

Conclusion: 偏光光学显微镜通过选择性检测双折射纤维素，克服了电子和原子力显微镜无法分辨基质包裹微纤维的局限，实现了对复杂螺旋结构的准确识别。该方法可同时对数十个细胞进行剖面分析，为植物科学、生物力学和仿生纤维素材料提供潜在的全自动化高通量分析框架。

Abstract: Wood slices under a polarized optical microscope show a spectrum of interference colors, such as cyan, blue, magenta, yellow, and bluish gray because of the birefringent cellulose in the microfibrils. An optical model is established to simulate the birefringent-colored micrograph of wood. Five typical cell wall architectures, line, helix, ring, crossed helix, and twisted helix, are considered. With a retardation wave plate to distinguish fibrils of different orientations, each structure displays its unique birefringent-colored texture, presenting its underlying 3D structure with the vivid colors. Cross sections of the trunk and twig of Eucalyptus grandis presented birefringent-colored profiles. Three identification methods were compared: visible fibril trends, birefringent-colored optical textures, and the simulated look-up library. The three methods gave consistent results, proving that the birefringent-color tags are efficient and accurate. Electron and atomic force microscopy are unable to resolve cellulose microfibrils embedded in the lignin and hemicellulose matrix. Polarized optical microscopy overcomes this by selectively detecting birefringent cellulose, enabling accurate identification of complex helical structures. This method enables statistical and spatial analysis of complex biomaterial compositions. Capable of profiling dozens of cells simultaneously, this high-throughput optical method provides a potentially fully automated analysis framework for plant science, biomechanics, and bioinspired cellulose materials.

</details>


### [123] [A unified geometric design framework for kirigami structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30032)
*Qinghai Jiang,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的剪纸折纸超材料（kirigami metamaterials）设计框架，涵盖2D-to-2D、2D-to-3D及3D-to-3D多种形状变形效应，并通过将设计任务重构为基于长度的约束优化问题同时求解多个目标状态，提升设计灵活性与理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多种二维和三维剪纸设计方法被开发，但大多数仅适用于有限的剪纸结构类别，缺乏统一的设计框架。

Method: 将设计任务重构为基于长度的约束优化问题（length-based constrained optimization），并同时对多个目标状态进行求解；同时提供了对惯性转置、长宽比定律和角度缺陷等关键方面的严格理论分析。

Result: 通过涵盖广泛形状变形效应的实验结果验证了框架的有效性，并揭示了剪纸设计中的重要设计规则与局限性。

Conclusion: 该工作为形状变形机械超材料（shape-morphing mechanical metamaterials）的设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In recent years, kirigami metamaterials have been widely studied and applied in science and engineering. While various two- and three-dimensional kirigami design methods have been developed, most of them are only applicable to a limited class of kirigami structures. In this work, we develop a unified framework for kirigami design that encompasses a wide range of 2D-to-2D, 2D-to-3D, and 3D-to-3D shape-morphing effects, as well as additional geometric and physical properties such as compact reconfigurability and rigid deployability. In particular, by reformulating the design task as a length-based constrained optimization problem and solving it simultaneously for multiple target states of the kirigami structure, our unified design framework enables greater design flexibility and stronger theoretical support. Experimental results with a wide range of shape-morphing effects are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. We further present a rigorous theoretical analysis of several key aspects of kirigami design, covering inertia transposition, aspect-ratio law, and angle defects, thereby elucidating important design rules and limitations. Altogether, our work paves a new way for the design of shape-morphing mechanical metamaterials.

</details>


### [124] [Tunable Pathway Selection in Coupled Multistable Snap-Through Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30095)
*Weicheng Huang,Ke Huang,Jiaying Zhang,Mingchao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出双质量von Mises桁架作为通用模型，研究由耦合鞍-结点分岔（saddle-node bifurcations）控制的多稳态系统跃迁路径选择机制，揭示了弹性耦合刚度与加载速率如何协同调控三种准静态跃迁路径及动态分岔延迟导致的跃迁顺序反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多稳态机械系统可通过突跳不稳定性（snap-through instabilities）储存和释放弹性能量，但当存在多条可行路径时，精确控制稳态间的跃迁路径仍具挑战性。

Method: 引入由两个带几何缺陷的耦合突跳单元组成的双质量von Mises桁架模型，该系统产生四种稳定构型。采用伪弧长延拓法（pseudo-arclength continuation）追踪鞍-结点分岔并识别各准静态路径的参数区间；继而通过局部约化获得带耦合依赖临界点与系数的正规形（normal forms），推导惯性和过阻尼（overdamped）两个区域中不同的率依赖延迟定律。

Result: 耦合刚度能够重构准静态分岔结构，在三种跃迁路径（经由一个混合态的序列突跳、直接协作突跳、经由另一混合态的序列突跳）中进行选择；动态分岔延迟（dynamic bifurcation delay）提供额外的率依赖路径选择机制，即使在准静态结构偏好唯一序列路径时，有限加载速率也能使突跳超越静态鞍-结点并反转两个单元的突跳顺序；理论预测了临界反转速率。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个通用力学框架，通过弹性耦合和加载速率控制来调节多稳态系统中的跃迁路径，为相关工程系统的路径设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Multistable mechanical systems can store and release elastic energy through snap-through instabilities, but controlling transition pathways between stable states remains challenging when multiple routes are accessible. Here, we introduce a two-mass von Mises truss as a general model for studying pathway selection governed by coupled saddle-node bifurcations. The system consists of two coupled snap-through units with geometric imperfections, giving rise to four stable configurations: a fully inverted state, a fully natural state, and two intermediate mixed states. We show that the coupling stiffness reorganizes the quasi-static bifurcation structure and selects among three transition pathways under release: sequential snapping through one mixed state, direct cooperative snapping, or sequential snapping through the other mixed state. Using pseudo-arclength continuation, we track the relevant saddle-node bifurcations and identify the parameter regimes associated with each quasi-static pathway. We then demonstrate that dynamic bifurcation delay provides an additional rate-dependent mechanism for pathway selection. Even when the quasi-static bifurcation structure favours a unique sequential pathway, finite-rate loading delays snap-through beyond the corresponding static saddle-node points and can reorder the snapping sequence of the two units. A local reduction of the coupled dynamics near each saddle-node yields normal forms with coupling-dependent critical points and coefficients. The resulting theory identifies distinct rate-dependent delay laws in the inertia-dominated and overdamped regimes and predicts the critical rate at which the snapping order reverses. These results establish a general mechanics framework for tuning transition pathways in multistable systems through elastic coupling and loading-rate control.

</details>


### [125] [Experimental Observation of Mesoscopic Dynamic Fluctuations in Water-Alcohol Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30098)
*Yukio Kajihara,Nanako Shibata,Masanori Inui1*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文采用作者团队近年来发展的动态波动（dynamic fluctuation）测量方法——结合超声波与非弹性X射线散射技术，系统测量了液态水-甲醇混合物在 широком 组成与温度范围内的介观尺度（mesoscopic-level）异质性。结果显示动态波动强度随醇类组成减少和温度降低而增强，这一趋势同样存在于水-乙醇和水-甘油体系中，由此推断波动的来源在于纯水的低温（过冷）区域，混合醇类会消除动态波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来已知低醇组成的水-醇混合物存在多种热力学异常（thermodynamic anomalies），尽管过去已有诸多讨论，作者希望通过直接探测液体中介观尺度的异质性来理解这些异常的起源，为相关争论提供新的视角。

Method: 采用近期自行开发的动态波动测量方法，将超声波（ultrasonic）与非弹性X射线散射（inelastic X-ray scattering）技术相结合，利用声波检测液体中介观尺度的波动——即异质性程度，并在宽范围的组成与温度条件下对液态水-甲醇混合物进行测量，同时对照了水-乙醇和水-甘油体系。

Result: 测量结果表明，动态波动强度随醇类组成比例下降而增强，随温度降低而增强；该趋势在水-乙醇和水-甘油混合物中同样得到验证。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：动态波动的起源在于纯水单独特的低温（过冷）区域，混合醇类物质会消除这些动态波动。这一结论初看反直觉，但与解释水热力学异常的液-液相变假说（liquid-liquid phase transition hypothesis）框架相一致，并与其团队近期在宽温度与压力范围内对纯水中动态波动强度的测量结果相吻合。

Abstract: We applied a dynamic fluctuation measurement method to liquid water-methanol mixtures over a wide range of compositions and temperatures. This method, which we developed in recent years, combines ultrasonic and inelastic X-ray scattering techniques to detect mesoscopic-level fluctuations-that is, the degree of heterogeneity-in liquids using sound waves. The results showed that the strength of dynamic fluctuations increased as the alcohol composition decreased and as the temperature decreased. This trend was also observed in water-ethanol and water-glycerol mixtures. From these results, we concluded that the origin of these fluctuations lies in the low-temperature (supercooled) region of water alone, and that mixing with alcohol eliminates the dynamic fluctuations. This conclusion-that mixing substances reduces fluctuations or heterogeneity-may seem strange at first glance, but it is actually consistent with the framework of the liquid-liquid phase transition hypothesis, which explains the thermodynamic anomalies of water. It also aligns with the results of our recent measurements of the strength of dynamic fluctuations in water across a wide range of temperature and pressure conditions. It has long been known that various thermodynamic anomalies exist in water-alcohol mixtures at low compositions, and while there have been various discussions on this topic in the past, our current findings offer a new perspective on these discussions.

</details>


### [126] [Linear stability and viscoelastic response in weakly jammed frictional granular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30151)
*Masanari Shimada,Yusuke Hara,Shihori Koyama,Ryohei Seto,Maria Yokota*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文采用离散元方法（discrete element method, DEM）研究由摩擦球体组成的静态堆积体的线性稳定性，探讨了静摩擦建模的关键困难与 rattler 识别问题，并分析了支配线性黏弹性响应的能量矩阵谱，发现中间和高频区域的谱结构对摩擦系数与堆积分数均具有定性不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料中的摩擦作用会引发多种非平凡性质，理解其静态堆积体的线性稳定性是揭示这些性质的基础。

Method: 采用离散元方法对摩擦球体间的相互作用进行建模，分析能量矩阵（energy matrix）的谱以考察系统线性黏弹性响应，并讨论静摩擦建模的难点与 rattler（未参与力链的浮动颗粒）识别问题。

Result: 能量矩阵谱的中间频率（intermediate-frequency）和高频（high-frequency）区域的定性特征不随摩擦系数（friction coefficient）和堆积分数（packing fraction）变化而变化。

Conclusion: 摩擦系数与堆积分数对谱的中间及高频区域无定性影响，表明相关频段的振动响应具有某种普适性。

Abstract: Granular particles typically feature contact friction, which gives rise to various nontrivial properties of granular materials. In this work, we investigate the linear stability of static packings composed of frictional spheres, whose interactions are modeled using the discrete element method. We discuss a key difficulty in properly modeling static friction and address the problem of identifying rattlers. We also explore the spectrum of the energy matrix, which governs the linear viscoelastic response of the system. The intermediate- and high-frequency regimes of the spectrum remain qualitatively independent of both the friction coefficient and the packing fraction.

</details>


### [127] [Optimal Transport of an Anisotropic Tracer in Dense Active Suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30377)
*Chandranshu Tiwari,Sunil P. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过布朗动力学（Brownian dynamics）模拟研究椭圆示踪粒子在活性哑铃（active dumbbells）悬浮体中的输运行为，发现平移与转动输运随活性哑铃面积分数增加而增强超过一个数量级，但在中等填充率下出现最优值，呈非单调变化。这种非单调输运源于活性哑铃在示踪粒子表面的各向异性聚集，建立了集体组织与涌现输运之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性示踪粒子在活性流体中的输运展现出丰富的动力学行为，源于粒子形状、活性和空间位阻相互作用之间的复杂耦合。理解这一输运机制对于揭示活性物质系统中非平衡输运的基本规律具有重要意义。

Method: 采用布朗动力学模拟方法，研究一个椭圆示踪粒子浸没在活性哑铃悬浮体中的运动行为。通过分析平均平方速度（mean-square speed）和扩散系数（diffusivity）来表征平移和转动输运特性，并考察不同面积分数φ下的输运变化。

Result: 研究发现平移和转动输运均随活性哑铃面积分数增加而增强超过一个数量级；沿长轴的输运优于沿短轴（v∥>v⊥，D∥>D⊥）；输运系数在中等填充率下达到最优值，高密度时反而下降，呈现非单调行为。这种非单调性源于活性哑铃在示踪粒子周围的各向异性积累与聚集所导致的非平衡力与力矩涨落。

Conclusion: 活性哑铃在示踪粒子表面的集体组织方式与其涌现的平移和转动输运之间存在直接联系，各向异性聚集控制了非平衡力与力矩涨落，从而决定输运的非单调特性。

Abstract: The transport of anisotropic tracers in active fluids exhibits rich dynamical behavior arising from the interplay between particle shape, activity, and steric interactions. We employ Brownian dynamics simulations to investigate the motion of an elliptical tracer immersed in a suspension of active dumbbells. We find that both translational and rotational transport, characterized by the mean-square speed and diffusivity, are enhanced by more than an order of magnitude with increasing area fraction, $φ$, of active dumbbells. Notably, tracer motion is enhanced along the major axis relative to the minor axis, with $\mathrm{v}_{\parallel}>\mathrm{v}_{\perp}$ and $D_{\parallel}>D_{\perp}$. Remarkably, both translational and rotational transport exhibit an optimum at an intermediate packing fraction of active dumbbells, with the corresponding transport coefficients decreasing at higher densities. We show that this non-monotonic transport arises from the anisotropic accumulation and aggregation of active dumbbells around the tracer, which control the non-equilibrium force and torque fluctuations. Thus, establish a direct connection between the collective organization of active dumbbells at the tracer surface and its emergent translational and rotational transport.

</details>


### [128] [Viscoelastic interfacial structures in undersaturated calcium solutions under nanoconfinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30464)
*Shurui Miao,Timothy S. Groves,Kieran J. Agg,Susan Perkin*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过表面力测量实验，发现在低饱和度（undersaturated）条件下钙离子溶液在纳米受限（nanoscopic confinement）界面处会形成稳定的水合离子网络（hydrated ion networks），表现为一种长程非DLVO排斥相互作用，为矿物成核（nucleation）前的中间离子组装阶段提供了实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙离子矿化在生物矿化（biomineralisation）到规模化碳封存（carbon storage）等过程中至关重要，但其最早期的成核阶段机制尚不明确。经典成核理论与非经典路径之间的桥梁缺乏直接的实验证据。

Method: 在保持溶液对块体碳酸钙沉淀不饱和的条件下，使用灵敏的表面力测量（surface force measurements）技术研究纳米受限空间中的含钙溶液。通过测量力曲线识别特定于钙溶液的长程非DLVO排斥相互作用，并进一步测试其粘弹性、pH依赖性和对竞争离子的响应。

Result: 识别出钙溶液特有的长程非DLVO排斥力，归因于矿物界面处的水合离子网络。该网络表现出不同于本体溶液（bulk solution）的粘弹性（viscoelastic）行为，在宽pH范围内稳定存在，并会被强表面亲和力的竞争离子所破坏。

Conclusion: 实验证实了在低饱和度条件下矿物界面可自发形成稳定的水合离子网络，支持界面与受限（confinement）环境在成核前稳定中间离子组装体的可能性。这一发现弥合了经典与非经典成核描述之间的鸿沟，凸显了界面与受限在调控结晶路径中的核心作用。

Abstract: Calcium mineralisation underpins processes ranging from biomineralisation to scalable carbon storage, yet the earliest stages of nucleation remain unresolved. Here we investigate calcium-containing solutions under nanoscopic confinement while remaining undersaturated with respect to bulk calcium carbonate precipitation. Using sensitive surface force measurements, we identify a long-range non-DLVO repulsive interaction specific to calcium solutions, which we attribute to hydrated ion networks at mineral interfaces. These interfacial networks exhibit viscoelastic behaviour distinct from the bulk solution, persist across a broad pH range, and can be disrupted by competing ions with strong surface affinity. Our findings provide experimental evidence that stable hydrated ion networks can emerge at mineral interfaces under undersaturated conditions, supporting the possibility that interfaces and confinement stabilise intermediate ion assemblies prior to nucleation. These results bridge classical and nonclassical descriptions of mineral nucleation and highlight the central role of interfaces and confinement in directing crystallisation pathways.

</details>


### [129] [A multi-scale study to unravel the dehydration mechanism of hydrated salts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30469)
*A. C. Claude,H. Derluyn,J. van de Groep,N. Shahidzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过拉曼共聚焦显微镜、动态失重测量、扫描电子显微镜（SEM）和微型计算机断层扫描（micro CT）等多尺度手段，揭示了十水硫酸钠（芒硝）脱水动力学由“成核控制”和“相界控制”两个阶段组成，产物形成具有双孔结构的纳米晶体，并提出晶体学对称性可预测其他水合盐的脱水路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水合盐的脱水机制在固态化学中具有基础重要性，其行为影响热存储、文物保护、药物结晶等多个领域，但宏观测量方法无法揭示脱水过程中的微观动力学细节。

Method: 综合运用拉曼共聚焦显微镜（Raman confocal microscopy）、动态失重测量（dynamic weight loss measurements）、扫描电子显微镜（SEM）和微型计算机断层扫描（micro CT）等多种表征手段，对十水硫酸钠（芒硝，mirabilite）的脱水过程进行多尺度观测，并将分析拓展至其他硫酸盐水合物。

Result: 脱水动力学呈现两个阶段：初始成核控制阶段中原子重排驱动二维侧向生长并遵循指数规律；后续相界控制阶段中生长向晶体深度推进，受水空位形成速率限制。产物无水芒硝（thenardite）收缩约35%，形成具有双孔结构的纳米晶层状结构，相对湿度直接调控晶体尺寸。其他硫酸盐水合物的分析表明水合物与无水相之间的晶体学对称性变化决定表面形貌。

Conclusion: 该多尺度框架揭示了宏观测量所隐藏的动力学过程，提出结构指标可预测其他水合盐家族的脱水路径，为无需大量实验筛选即可设计热能存储材料开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding the dehydration mechanism of hydrated salts remains fundamentally important in solid state chemistry, as their behavior affect fields ranging from heat storage to heritage conservation and pharmaceutical crystallization. Combining Raman confocal microscopy, dynamic weight loss measurements, SEM, and micro CT, we show that dehydration kinetics of sodium sulfate decahydrate (mirabilite) unfold through two regimes: an initial nucleation controlled phase, where atomic rearrangement drives two dimensional lateral growth following an exponential law, and a subsequent phase boundary controlled regime, where growth advances into the crystal depth, limited by water vacancy formation at the hydrated dehydrated interface. The resulting thenardite product shows around 35 percent shrinkage and forms a porous, layered dual porosity nanocrystalline structure, with relative humidity directly governing crystal size. Extending this analysis to other sulfate hydrates reveals that crystallographic symmetry changes between hydrate and anhydrous phases dictate surface morphology, linking microstructure to mechanism. This multiscale framework uncovers dynamics hidden from bulk measurements and suggests structural indicators could predict dehydration pathways in other hydrated salt families, and opening route toward designing thermal energy storage materials without exhaustive experimental screening.

</details>


### [130] [Polymer Membrane Tensegrity: Inverse Design of Polymer Films Morphing into Freeform 3D Surfaces with Digital Photopatterning Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30501)
*Shuto Ito,Yuta Shimoda,Haruka Fukunishi,Mikihiro Hayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为聚合物膜张拉结构（Polymer Membrane Tensegrity，PMT）的合成制造框架，通过单侧数字光刻（photopatterning）在弹性膜中嵌入刚性杆件，利用收缩-刚度差异生成约50%的面内应变差和约2000倍的模量对比，配合基于折纸（origami）的逆向设计算法，可将平面聚合物膜变形为预定的三维曲面，平均偏差仅为目标尺寸的1.0–2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 歌德提出植物各器官源于共同叶片结构的形态学原理，现代力学将其归因于非均匀面内应变与空间图案化刚度两个材料因素。作者旨在将该原理转化为可扩展的合成制造框架，突破传统双层（bilayer）方法需要前后对齐（front-to-back alignment）的限制。

Method: 将溶胀有第二单体的扁平弹性膜通过液晶显示器（LCD）光掩模进行单侧数字光刻，选择性紫外固化形成嵌入软质可收缩膜中的刚性杆。提取未反应单体并干燥后，膜收缩远大于杆，产生约50%应变差与约2000倍模量对比；采用基于折纸的逆向设计算法计算杆的布局以赋予目标三维形貌，并通过周长曲线优化进一步降低误差。

Result: PMT在圆顶（dome）、双曲曲面（hyperbolic surface）和螺旋面单元（gyroid unit cell）三种正负曲率目标上均实现了1.0–2.1%目标尺寸的平均偏差；周长曲线优化使螺旋面的平均偏差减半。

Conclusion: 由于仅需单侧图案化，PMT消除了双层方法所需的前后对齐工艺，为从平面聚合物膜大规模制造自由形态三维曲面提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: In Metamorphosis of Plants (1790), Goethe traced diverse plant organs to transformations of a common leaf-like structure -- a principle modern mechanics attributes to two material ingredients: non-uniform in-plane strain from differential growth or shrinkage, and spatially patterned stiffness. Here we translate this principle into a synthetic fabrication framework called Polymer Membrane Tensegrity (PMT). A flat elastomeric film swollen with a second monomer is selectively UV-cured through a liquid-crystal display (LCD) photomask in a single-side digital photopatterning step, producing rigid rods embedded in a soft, shrinkable membrane. After the unreacted monomer is extracted with a solvent and the film is dried, the membrane shrinks far more than the rods, generating a ~50% in-plane strain differential and a ~2,000-fold modulus contrast -- conditions under which the contracting membrane is held in tension by mutually unconnected rods, a tensegrity-inspired arrangement within a single film. An origami-based inverse design algorithm computes the rod layout that morphs the film into a prescribed 3D surface. We demonstrate PMT on a dome, a hyperbolic surface, and a gyroid unit cell -- positively and negatively curved targets -- reproducing all three with mean deviations of 1.0-2.1% of the target size; perimeter curve optimization halves the mean deviation of the gyroid. Because patterning occurs on one side only, PMT eliminates the front-to-back alignment demanded by bilayer methods, offering a scalable route from flat polymer films to freeform 3D surfaces.

</details>


### [131] [A closed-form theory of charge-regulated electrostatic interactions between anisotropically charged spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30566)
*Anže Božič*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的闭式理论，在线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼（linearized Poisson–Boltzmann）近似下同时考虑蛋白质等胶体粒子的位点不均匀性（patchiness）和 pH 响应电荷调节（charge regulation），推导了平均场相互作用能与 Kirkwood–Shumaker 涨落相互作用，并揭示了电中性各向异性粒子间由邻近诱导电荷调节产生的新效应。理论在溶菌酶和α-胰凝乳蛋白酶原A两种蛋白上合理复现了第二维里系数（second virial coefficient），并预测电荷调节可扩大蛋白相互作用表现为吸引的 pH 和盐浓度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质等胶体粒子表面的可电离基团同时具有空间不均匀性（patchy）和 pH 响应性（charge-regulating），但现有理论通常将这两方面分开处理。作者旨在建立一个统一理论框架，将电荷调节与斑块性纳入同一描述，以解决单独处理二者时无法捕捉的耦合效应。

Method: 在线性化泊松–玻尔兹曼近似下，针对具有各向异性可滴定位点分布的球形粒子，分别推导了前导阶平均场相互作用能（mean-field interaction energy）和 Kirkwood–Shumaker 涨落相互作用。理论首次在同一闭式解中将电荷调节和斑块性统一处理，并对能量修正项进行了符号分析。

Result: 理论揭示了一个定性新效应：两个电中性各向异性粒子在靠近时，电荷调节会在其线性固定电荷模型中退化的取向分支之间产生分裂，并生成净电单极子（net monopole）。该能量修正无固定符号，可软化同号电荷斥力，也能在等电点（isoelectric point）附近加深或创造吸引力。理论在溶菌酶（lysozyme）和α-胰凝乳蛋白酶原A（α-chymotrypsinogen A）两个蛋白体系上合理复现了第二维里系数，并预测电荷调节可扩大蛋白–蛋白相互作用为吸引性的 pH 与屏蔽强度（screening strength）范围。

Conclusion: 统一的闭式理论为同时具有斑块性和电荷调节的胶体粒子提供了一致的静电相互作用描述，揭示了现有固定电荷模型无法预见的耦合效应。该理论不仅与已有实验测量合理吻合，还给出可检验的预测，表明现有模拟尚未覆盖的组合场景正是捕获核心预测的关键方向。

Abstract: Proteins and many other colloids carry ionizable surface groups that are both spatially inhomogeneous (patchy) and pH-responsive (charge-regulating). In the description of the electrostatic interactions between such particles, these two aspects are often treated separately, especially from a theoretical perspective. We present a unified, closed-form theory that unites charge regulation and patchiness within the linearized Poisson--Boltzmann approximation. For spherical particles with anisotropic distributions of titratable sites, we derive both the leading-order mean-field interaction energy as well as the Kirkwood--Shumaker fluctuation interaction. The theory exposes a qualitatively new effect between two electroneutral anisotropic particles: proximity-induced charge regulation generates a net monopole, and splits orientational branches that are degenerate in any linear fixed-charge model. The accompanying correction to the interaction energy has no fixed sign---it softens like-charge repulsion but can deepen or create attraction away from the isoelectric point. We benchmark three ingredients of the theory against published results; no existing simulation probes their combination, which would capture the central prediction of the theory. Lastly, we apply the theory to two proteins---lysozyme and $α$-chymotrypsinogen A---and show that it not only reproduces the measured second virial coefficients reasonably well but also predicts how charge regulation expands the range of pH and screening strength where the protein--protein interaction is attractive.

</details>


### [132] [Active Brownian Dynamics from a Hamiltonian Model: Transitioning from Equilibrium to Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30670)
*Antik Bhattacharya,Smarajit Karmakar,Jürgen Horbach*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一个哈密顿活性布朗粒子（Hamiltonian Active Brownian Particle, HABP）模型，将平衡态动力学与标准活性布朗粒子（ABPs）的二维过阻尼非平衡行为联系起来。通过将平动和转动自由度耦合到不同温度的热浴驱动系统脱离平衡，该模型在自由空间和谐波势阱中均能定量复现ABP行为，并通过熵产生分析揭示了ABP极限下的能量耗散通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准活性布朗粒子模型是研究活性物质（active matter）非平衡行为的重要工具，但其过阻尼动力学缺乏对应的平衡态描述，限制了将平衡统计力学（statistical mechanics）工具应用于活性系统的可能性。本文旨在建立一个能够连接平衡动力学与非平衡ABP行为的理论框架，为活性物质的平衡启发表述开辟新路径。

Method: 作者引入HABP模型，其中平衡态下系统遵循严格满足涨落耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem）的过阻尼朗之万方程（overdamped Langevin equations）。通过将平动与转动自由度分别耦合至温度不同的热浴（$T_\theta > T_{\mathrm{tr}}$）驱动系统脱离平衡。在自由空间中匹配扩散系数，并在谐波势阱中分析有限温度比下的对应关系；利用熵产生（entropy production）分析考察能量注入与耗散的分布。

Result: 在自由空间中，当$T_\theta/T_{\mathrm{tr}}\to\infty$时，匹配扩散系数后HABP模型与ABP模型定量一致；在谐波势阱中，即使在有限温度比下也能恢复这种一致性。熵产生分析表明，在ABP极限下，游动力（swim force）注入的全部能量均耗散至平动热浴，而转动热浴作为零成本熵源（zero-cost entropy source）存在。

Conclusion: 该工作为活性物质的平衡启发表述提供了初步步骤，有望推动发展新的理论框架，用以研究远离平衡态的生命与非生命软物质系统中的活性诱导涨落、输运和相行为。

Abstract: We introduce a Hamiltonian Active Brownian Particle (HABP) model that connects equilibrium dynamics with the non-equilibrium, two-dimensional overdamped behavior of standard Active Brownian Particles (ABPs). In equilibrium, the system follows overdamped Langevin equations that strictly satisfy the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. Coupling translational and rotational degrees of freedom to separate heat baths ($T_θ > T_{\textrm{tr}}$), drives the system out of equilibrium. In free space, matching the diffusion coefficients yields quantitative agreement with the ABP model in the limit $T_θ/T_{\textrm{tr}}\to \infty$, whereas in a harmonic potential, this matching is recovered even at finite temperature ratios. Entropy production analysis demonstrates that in the ABP limit, all energy injected by the swim force dissipates into the translational bath, leaving the rotational bath as a zero-cost entropy source. These insights provide the first steps toward equilibrium-inspired descriptions of active matter, facilitating the development of new theoretical frameworks to study activity-induced fluctuations, transport, and phase behavior in living and non-living soft matter systems far from equilibrium.

</details>


### [133] [Coarse-grained simulations of dsDNA polycatenanes and network formation in annular nanochannels with topoisomerase II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30742)
*Carolina Palombo,Davide Breoni,Luca Tubiana*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了拓扑异构酶II（TopoII）存在下，初始未连接的切口双链DNA环（nicked dsDNA rings）在环形方形纳米通道（annular square nanochannel）中的行为。结果表明，通道限域可以通过使不同环的高度弯曲区域相互接近来提高连锁（catenation）概率，同时减少纽结（knots）的产生，从而实现对DNA拓扑产物的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究DNA环在受限环境中的拓扑行为对于理解体内DNA组织、制备DNA互锁材料以及验证拓扑异构酶II的计算模型具有重要意义。作者旨在通过设计环形纳米通道构型，系统研究通道限域对DNA连锁和纽结形成的影响。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，将初始未相连的切口双链DNA环置于环形方形纳米通道中，在TopoII存在的条件下进行模拟。通过环形通道拓扑消除周期性边界条件，简化系统表征，并在不同模型参数和不同限域水平下刻画稳态系统的平衡与动力学性质。

Result: 研究揭示了通道限域能够增强连锁形成的可能性，其机制为将不同DNA环的高度弯曲区域拉近距离；同时限域降低了纽结在系统中的出现频率。环形通道拓扑有助于形成环形连锁体（circular catenanes），并表征了连锁程度和系统可探索的拓扑结构随参数变化的规律。

Conclusion: 该模拟设置可在受控条件下实现实验与模拟的直接对比，为选择和调优TopoII的计算模型提供途径，同时也为以受控方式制备双链DNA互锁材料提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We numerically investigate the behavior of a system of initially unlinked nicked dsDNA rings confined into an annular square nanochannel in the presence of TopoII. Channel confinement can enhance the catenation likelihood by bringing highly bent regions from different rings in close proximity, while at the same time reducing the emergence of knots. The annular channel topology simplifies the characterization of the system by removing periodic boundary conditions and can lead to the formation of circular catenanes.We characterize the equilibrium and dynamical properties of the steady-state system, including the amount of catenation and the topologies explored by the system, under different parameters of the model and for different levels of confinement. We argue that a similar setup could allow for a direct comparison between experiments and simulations under well characterized and controlled conditions, thus providing a way to select and tune computational models of TopoII, as well as provide a way to obtain dsDNA interlocked materials in a controlled fashion.

</details>


### [134] [Self-Diffusion of Water through Thermally Activated Membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30863)
*Carlos Handrey A. Ferraz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究利用分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，在高温范围内考察了热激活膜（thermally activated membranes, TAMs）对SPC/E水自扩散系数、四面体有序参数（tetrahedral order parameter）和氢键（hydrogen-bond, HB）寿命的影响。结果显示扩散系数随膜数量增加而减小，且与Arrhenius近似在高温下吻合良好，而四面体有序参数和氢键寿命仅对膜作用呈局部敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膜作为选择性界面，在多个长度和时间尺度上调控质量、电荷和信息传输，理解膜扩散过程对科学与技术均具有基础重要性。

Method: 采用MD模拟方法，在存在热激活膜的宽高温范围内计算SPC/E水的自扩散系数、四面体有序参数和氢键寿命。膜的热控随机行为由依赖于系统约化温度（reduced temperature）的sigmoid型轮廓控制，粒子穿越膜时会被概率性地诱导弹性散射事件。

Result: 扩散系数总体上随膜数量的增加而降低，且在高温下与Arrhenius近似合理一致；针对若干膜构型估算了有效扩散活化能（activation energy）；四面体有序参数和氢键寿命仅对膜作用呈局部敏感。

Conclusion: 热激活膜对水的输运性质产生全局性影响（降低扩散系数），但对水的局域结构（四面体有序性和氢键）影响有限，表明膜主要调控输运动力学而非整体结构特征。

Abstract: Diffusion processes involving membranes are of fundamental importance in both science and technology, since membranes serve as selective interfaces that regulate the transport of mass, charge, and information across multiple length and time scales. In this study, we employ molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to calculate the self-diffusion coefficient, tetrahedral order parameter and hydrogen-bond (HB) lifetime of SPC/E water over a wide high-temperature range in the presence of thermally activated membranes (TAMs). These membranes exhibit thermally controlled stochastic behavior that locally influences particle dynamics by probabilistically inducing elastic scattering events as particles traverse the membrane. The stochastic behavior of the membranes is governed by a sigmoidal profile, which depend on the reduced temperature of the system. The effective activation energy for diffusion is estimated for several membrane configurations. It is found that the diffusion coefficients generally decrease with an increase in the number of membranes and are in reasonable agreement with the Arrhenius approximation at high temperatures. Additionally, both the tetrahedral order parameter and the HB lifetime are only locally sensitive to the action of the membranes.

</details>


### [135] [Anomalous temperature dependence in phase transitions via ballistic coalescence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30918)
*Nalina Vadakkayil,Sutapa Roy,Jiarul Midya,Subir K. Das*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文研究单组分Lennard-Jones模型中的成核相变动力学，发现低密度下不连通的团簇可在无黏性蒸气背景中弹道运动，经黏性碰撞形成更大聚集体，并观察到平均团簇质量的代数增长指数随温度呈非单调变化，通过弹道聚集理论成功捕捉了二维和三维空间中的这一异常行为，甚至揭示弹道聚集可指数级快速发生的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统被动物质相变研究通常假定团簇运动不相关，且遵循代数增长规律。本文旨在揭示弹道聚集理论的适用范围远大于通常考虑的极限情形，并探索与常规代数图像截然不同的爆轰式（指数级）聚集增长场景。

Method: 采用单组分Lennard-Jones模型，通过改变粒子密度和温度进行动力学模拟，考察低密度下不连通团簇的形成及其弹道运动行为；将平均团簇质量的代数增长指数随温度的变化规律，在二维和三维空间维度下与弹道聚集理论（ballistic aggregation theory）进行对照验证。

Result: 发现团簇在极冷温度下可形成分形结构；平均团簇质量的代数增长指数随温度变化表现出显著的非单调特征；弹道聚集理论在二维和三维中均能捕获该异常行为；理论分析进一步表明弹道聚集可呈指数级快速发生，与被动物质相变的传统代数图像形成鲜明对比，并讨论了实现这种爆炸式增长的条件。

Conclusion: 弹道聚集理论的适用范围远超出通常假设团簇运动不相关的情形，且可产生指数级爆炸式增长。该研究成果对气溶胶、宇宙尘埃等聚集过程的微观结构与生长机制理解具有广泛参考价值。

Abstract: We study kinetics of phase transitions within a single component Lennard-Jones model. For low enough particle densities disconnected clusters form that can move ballistically in an inviscid vapor background. The clusters undergo sticky collisions, thereby forming larger aggregates, which can be of fractal nature at ultra cold temperatures. As the temperature is varied, the exponent of the algebraic growth of average cluster mass changes, exhibiting pronounced nonmonotonic character. We capture this anomalous behavior, in two and three space dimensions, within a ballistic aggregation theory. We show that the scope of the theory is much broader than the typically considered case where cluster motions are uncorrelated. An analysis of the theory shows that ballistic aggregation can even occur exponentially fast. This is in sharp contrast with the conventional algebraic picture, regarding passive matter phase transitions. We discuss scenario where such explosive growth can be realized. In addition, our results are widely relevant in understanding structure and growth in aerosols, cosmic dust and other aggregation processes.

</details>


### [136] [Particle-Mediated Tuning of Defect Stability in Lamellar Block Copolymer Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30981)
*Le Qiao,Daniel A. Vega,Friederike Schmid*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过混合粒子/Ginzburg–Landau模型研究胶体夹杂物如何改变层状嵌段共聚物系统中位错对的形成能，并采用有限尺寸标度方法获得热力学极限下的缺陷形成能。研究发现粒子尺寸和表面图案化显著影响缺陷稳定性，均质粒子和Janus粒子的作用方向相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解胶体夹杂物对层状嵌段共聚物中拓扑缺陷稳定性的调控机制，为控制这类软物质系统中的缺陷行为提供定量指导。

Method: 采用混合粒子/Ginzburg–Landau（particle/Ginzburg–Landau）模型，将无缺陷和有缺陷的状态与有无嵌入胶体的状态进行比较来计算缺陷形成能；通过有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）获得热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）下的形成能。

Result: 均质粒子随尺寸增大越能稳定位错对；大于一个层状畴的粒子优先占据位错核，替代应变聚合物而非使无缺陷层状结构变形。平衡Janus粒子因竞争性表面偏好无法在位错核附近同时满足而增加形成能；改变补丁比例可在两端极限之间插值。

Conclusion: 表面图案化对小于一个层状畴域的粒子影响甚微，但对较大粒子可改变形成能数十个k_BT，为控制层状嵌段共聚物系统中拓扑缺陷稳定性提供了定量准则。

Abstract: We study how colloidal inclusions modify the formation energy of dislocation pairs in lamellar block copolymer systems. Using a hybrid particle/Ginzburg--Landau model, we calculate defect formation energies by comparing defect-free and defect-containing states with and without embedded colloids. Finite-size scaling is used to obtain formation energies in the thermodynamic limit. The effect of colloid insertion depends strongly on particle sizes and surface patterning. Homogeneous particles increasingly stabilize dislocation pairs with increasing particle sizes. Particles larger than one lamellar domain preferentially occupy the dislocation cores, where they replace strained polymer rather than deforming defect-free lamellae. The magnitude of this stabilization depends on surface affinity. Balanced Janus particles instead increase the formation energy, because their competing surface preferences cannot be satisfied simultaneously near the curved core. Varying the patch ratio interpolates between these limits. Surface patterning has little effect for particles smaller than one lamellar domain but changes the formation energy by several tens of $k_BT$ for larger particles. These results provide quantitative guidelines for controlling topological defect stability in lamellar block copolymer systems.

</details>


### [137] [Intercoupling of Segregation and Rheology in Spatially Developing Granular Chute Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30994)
*Soniya Kumawat,Sayeedul Islam Sheikh,Satyabrata Patro,Vishal Singh,Anurag Tripathi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究基于耦合粒子力分选模型与惯性数局部流变学的连续介质框架，对长溜槽中密度不同的二元颗粒混合物（binary granular mixtures）的流动与分选（segregation）行为进行数值研究，并与DEM模拟验证后考察密度比、混合物组成及溜槽倾角对分选的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元颗粒混合物在倾斜流道中因密度差异产生分选，现有研究对分选与流动之间双向耦合关系的定量理解不足，尤其是分选发展距离与速度场发展距离的关联尚待阐明。

Method: 采用连续介质框架，将基于粒子力的分选模型与基于惯性数（inertial number）的局部流变（local rheology）模型耦合，同时求解稳态动量方程与对流-扩散-分选方程（convection-diffusion-segregation equations），显式考虑分选与流动的双向耦合，并与DEM模拟结果进行验证。

Result: 模型预测的浓度场和速度场与DEM模拟高度一致。在低密度比和/或低倾角条件下，分选发展距离远大于速度场发展距离；而在高倾角和较大密度差条件下，两者长度尺度相当，表明忽略流动发展会显著低估分选演化。

Conclusion: 研究定量给出了达到完全分选所需溜槽长度，并与单分散颗粒流的发展长度进行比较，揭示了分选与流动发展长度之间的竞争关系，强调在建模中不可忽略流动发展对分选的影响。

Abstract: We investigate the flow and segregation of binary granular mixtures with density differences down a long chute using a continuum framework that couples a particle-force-based segregation model with an inertial-number-based local rheology. The steady-state momentum and convection-diffusion-segregation equations are solved simultaneously, explicitly accounting for the two-way coupling between segregation and flow. Predicted concentration and velocity fields at different streamwise locations are validated against representative DEM simulations. The validated model is then used to examine the influence of density ratio, mixture composition, and chute inclination on segregation over a wide range of conditions. The chute length required to achieve fully developed segregation is quantified and compared with the development length for monodisperse granular flow. At low density ratios and/or low inclinations, segregation develops over much longer distances than the velocity field. In contrast, at higher inclinations and larger density contrasts, the two length scales become comparable, demonstrating that neglecting flow development can significantly underestimate segregation evolution.

</details>

### [138] [Primordial Black Hole Seeds for Little Red Dots in $f(R)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29727)
*Saeed Fakhry*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一混合组装框架，通过修改引力理论（modified gravity）——Hu-Sawicki f(R) 引力中致密星团内层级式原始黑洞（primordial black hole, PBH）并合形成中等质量种子黑洞，再经星系气体吸积演化，以解释JWST观测到的高红移小致密红点（little red dots, LRDs）和超大质量黑洞的快速形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST发现的高红移密集小红点星系和超大质量黑洞数量众多，对传统从恒星残骸开始的爱丁顿受限增长（Eddington-limited growth）标准模型构成了严峻的时间挑战，需要在宇宙黎明前极短时间内组装出10的6次方到10的9次方太阳质量的致密天体。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一混合组装框架：在Hu-Sawicki f(R)引力中，致密星团内的层级式PBH并合产生中等质量种子黑洞，随后经历星系气体吸积。f(R)引力的屏蔽第五力通过引力波辐射俘获核的二次增强效应非线性加速早期种子形成，而环境变色龙屏蔽（chameleon screening）机制动态自我调节增长过程。

Result: 将活动质量函数与JWST数据对比表明，在合理的活动星系核占空比（δ≈10⁻²）下，较小的聚集PBH占比（f_PBH≲10⁻³）即可自然匹配z≈5.5-6.5处的LRD空间密度，同时完全满足LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA观测限制；修改引力自然驱动高质端分布。参数简并可通过三种多信使诊断联合破缺：残余自转态特征、随机引力波背景截止以及PBH空间关联函数中的尺度依赖反转。

Conclusion: 该框架表明修改引力与种子黑洞形成机制相结合可为高红移超大质量黑洞的快速组装提供可行路径，并提出了可通过多信使观测加以检验的独特可观测特征，从而为区分修改引力效应与星团密度效应提供了鉴别手段。

Abstract: The discovery by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) of an abundant population of compact little red dots (LRDs) and supermassive black holes at high redshifts poses a severe timing challenge for standard Eddington-limited growth from stellar remnants. In this work, we present a unified hybrid assembly framework in which intermediate-mass seed black holes are formed via hierarchical primordial black hole (PBH) mergers within dense clusters in Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ gravity, followed by galactic gas accretion. We demonstrate that the screened $f(R)$ fifth force nonlinearly accelerates early seed formation through a quadratic enhancement of the gravitational-wave radiation-capture kernel, while environmental chameleon screening dynamically self-regulates the growth. Because subsequent gas accretion scales multiplicatively with seed mass, the $f(R)$ seed enhancement is preserved throughout the accretion history, drastically reducing the time-averaged Eddington ratios required to assemble $10^{6}\text{-}10^{9}\,M_{\odot}$ LRDs by cosmic dawn. Confronting our active mass functions with JWST data reveals that a small clustered PBH fraction ($f_{\rm PBH} \lesssim 10^{-3}$) under a realistic active galactic nucleus duty cycle ($δ\approx 10^{-2}$) naturally matches observed LRD space densities at $z \sim 5.5\text{-}6.5$ while remaining fully compliant with LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA limits, with modified gravity naturally driving the high-mass tail. Finally, we show that parameter degeneracies between modified gravity and cluster density can be cleanly broken by combining three multi-messenger diagnostics: remnant spin state signatures, a stochastic GW background cutoff, and a scale-dependent inversion in the PBH spatial correlation function.

</details>

### [139] [Dimensionality-induced critical phase transition in stochastic Lotka-Volterra equation: From statistical averaging to systemic tipping point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30655)
*Xiu-deng Zheng,Cong Li,Hui Zhang,Hui-jie Qiao,Shao-peng Wang,Yi Tao*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析随机Lotka-Volterra（LV）方程，将生态学中多样性与稳定性之争的底层逻辑归纳为一种由维度引发的临界相变（critical phase transition），即统计平均主导区与系统临界翻转点之间的转变，进而为这一长期争论提供了统一框架，并为随机生态群落设定了内在的多样性上限（diversity ceiling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态学中关于多样性与稳定性关系的长期争论存在对立预测，缺乏统一的底层理论解释。作者试图从统计物理相变视角重新审视这一问题，以弥合分歧。

Method: 作者对随机Lotka-Volterra方程进行解析分析，将多样性-稳定性问题建模为维度（dimensionality）驱动的临界相变，区分统计平均主导的稳定区与系统性翻转（tipping）的不稳定区。

Result: 研究发现多样性与稳定性之间的关系本质上是维度诱导的临界相变，该框架能够统一生态学中多样性与稳定性之争中相互对立的预测，并确定了随机生态群落的固有多样性上限。

Conclusion: 多样性与稳定性的对立观点可以被相变理论统一解释，且随机生态群落的多样性存在一个由系统维度决定的内在上限，超出该上限将触发系统性临界翻转。

Abstract: By analyzing a stochastic Lotka-Volterra (LV) equation, we show that the underlying logic behind the diversity-stability debate in ecology can be framed as a dimensionality-induced critical phase transition, that is, a transition separating the regime dominated by statistical averaging and a systemic tipping point. This phase transition framework unifies the opposing ecological predictions in the diversity-stability debate and sets an intrinsic diversity ceiling for stochastic ecological communities.

</details>

### [140] [Determinant Quantum-Quantum Monte Carlo: Coherent Auxiliary-Field Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28742)
*Xuepeng Wang,Sagnik Banerjee,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出行列式量子-量子蒙特卡洛（DQ²MC）算法，将行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）的辅助场采样与平均过程移植到量子计算机上，通过行列式预言机在单粒子层面直接合成DQMC振幅，无需枚举或存储指数多个Hubbard-Stratonovich权重，并提出了全量子与量子-经典混合两种协议，分别消除马尔可夫链自相关时间或实现无拒绝的簇更新。此外，该算法将费米符号问题（fermion sign problem）背后的重加权估计量精确映射为量子弱值（quantum weak value）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DQMC在大规模系统上受限于辅助场采样所需的指数级Hubbard-Stratonovich权重枚举与存储，以及马尔可夫链的自相关时间和费米符号问题带来的指数开销。作者旨在利用量子计算从原理上规避这些瓶颈，为强关联费米子系统提供更高效的蒙特卡洛计算方法。

Method: 算法核心是利用量子奇异值变换（quantum singular value transformation）从单粒子作用量矩阵的块编码（block encoding）出发，由行列式预言机直接合成DQMC振幅。全量子协议将可观测量转化为干涉振幅，完全去除马尔可夫链；混合协议保留恒定大小的活动量子比特块，以精确热浴抽样替换Metropolis-Hastings接受步骤，实现无拒绝更新并支持并行回火与跨量子处理器分布式执行。

Result: 系统量子比特数为O(log N_st)（N_st为时空体积），无需Jordan-Wigner或Bravyi-Kitaev编码；电路深度随空间体积的缩放优于经典DQMC。代价是后选择（post-selection）开销，其大小由最大目标概率决定——对平滑分布为多项式阶，对尖锐分布为指数阶。费米符号问题的重加权估计量被精确映射为量子弱值，其提取的后选择开销与符号问题导致的指数代价建立精确对应。

Conclusion: DQ²MC提供了一种将DQMC核心计算过程量子化的新范式，以单粒子层面的量子操作替代多体采样，从根源上规避了权重枚举、马尔可夫链自相关和符号问题等经典DQMC的核心困难，同时以可控的后选择开销换取更优的电路深度缩放。

Abstract: We introduce determinant quantum-quantum Monte Carlo (DQ$^2$MC), a quantum algorithm that lifts the auxiliary-field sampling and averaging at the operational core of determinant quantum Monte Carlo onto a quantum computer. A determinant oracle synthesizes the DQMC amplitudes directly from a block encoding of the single-particle action matrix via quantum singular value transformations, so that the exponentially many Hubbard-Stratonovich weights are never enumerated, precomputed, or stored. Since the fermions are free for fixed auxiliary fields, the construction operates entirely at the single-particle level, requiring $O(\log N_{\mathrm{st}})$ system qubits and no Jordan-Wigner or Bravyi-Kitaev encoding, where $N_{\mathrm{st}}$ is the space-time volume. A full-quantum protocol makes observables interference amplitudes, eliminating the Markov chain and its autocorrelation time altogether; a hybrid quantum-classical protocol retains a constant-size active block of qubits and replaces the Metropolis-Hastings acceptance step with an exact heat-bath draw, so that cluster updates of any size are rejection-free, and passes only classical information between updates, admitting parallel tempering and distributed execution across quantum processors. The circuit-depth scales more favorably with spatial volume than classical DQMC, at the price of a post-selection overhead determined exactly by the largest target probability --- polynomial for smooth distributions, exponential for sharply peaked ones. Finally, the reweighting estimator underlying the fermion sign problem maps exactly onto a quantum weak value, placing the exponential cost of sign-problematic DQMC in precise correspondence with the post-selection overhead of weak-value extraction.

</details>


### [141] [Floquet engineering of competing antiferromagnetism and $d$-wave superconductivity on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28750)
*Zhaoyu Han,Subir Sachdev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受驱动的Lieb–Hubbard量子模拟器，通过周期性调制电荷转移偏移量，在平方晶格上实现一种自旋1/2费米子模型，其中可独立调节的排斥性在位（on-site）作用与吸引性键（bond）作用并存。通过两次受控消去（elimination），尽管p轨道的微观相互作用是排斥的，仍能在简化的仅含d轨道的模型中产生紧凑的键吸引。在中间耦合区间，平均场计算发现反铁磁序与d波超导序相邻，且存在窄的共存区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子模拟器中实现对具有竞争性序参量的强关联模型的可控实现，特别是同时包含可独立调节的排斥和吸引相互作用的费米子模型，是量子模拟领域的重要目标。作者希望克服传统方案中难以独立控制不同相互作用通道的局限。

Method: 采用周期性驱动Lieb晶格中d轨道与p轨道之间的电荷转移偏移量，使p轨道双占据态（doublon）能量上与d流形中的一对粒子相近，同时保持p轨道单占据态（singlon）远离共振，从而在Lieb晶格键位点上产生合成“负U”中心。随后通过两步受控消除，在平方晶格上仅含d轨道的简化模型中生成紧凑的键吸引，并对该模型在半填充下进行平均场计算。

Result: 生成的相互作用J可独立于d轨道上的Hubbard排斥U_d进行调节；光子辅助通道之间的干涉允许进入中间耦合区间，在该区间内J与U_d均与有效跳跃振幅相当。半填充下的平均场计算发现相邻的反铁磁相和d波超导相，以及窄的共存区域，暗示这些序之间存在紧密竞争。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了将该形式化构造转化为光学晶格方案所需的分支制备（branch preparation）、预热（prethermal）和高能带条件。更广泛地，该工作揭示了Floquet系统与电子-声子问题之间的结构相似性，可能为设计新型量子模拟协议提供指导。

Abstract: We propose a driven Lieb--Hubbard quantum simulator whose prethermal dynamics realize a square-lattice spin-1/2 fermion model with independently tunable repulsive on-site and attractive bond interactions. Periodically modulating the charge-transfer offset between the site ($d$) and bond ($p$) orbitals of the Lieb lattice brings a $p$-orbital doublon energetically close to a pair in the $d$ manifold while keeping all $p$-orbital singlons off resonance, thereby creating a synthetic ``negative-$U$'' center on a Lieb-lattice bond site. Two controlled eliminations then generate a compact bond attraction in the reduced $d$-only model on the square lattice, despite the microscopic repulsion in the $p$ orbital. The resulting interaction $J$ is tunable independently of the Hubbard repulsion $U_d$ on the $d$ orbitals, while interference between photon-assisted paths provides access to an intermediate-coupling regime in which $J$ and $U_d$ are both comparable to the effective hopping. At half filling, a mean-field calculation in this regime finds adjacent antiferromagnetic and $d$-wave superconducting phases, as well as narrow coexistence regions, suggesting close competition between these orders. We discuss the branch-preparation, prethermal, and higher-band conditions required to translate the formal construction into an optical-lattice protocol. More broadly, our work identifies a structural similarity between Floquet systems and electron-phonon problems that may guide the design of novel quantum-simulation protocols.

</details>


### [142] [Phase stability, charge ordering, and charge-liquid formation in the Falicov-Kimball model on triangular and kagome lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28898)
*Ammar Nejati,Marvin Lenk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自旋无关Falicov-Kimball模型在三角格子和kagome格子这两种二维非二部格子上的相图稳定性，在不同填充条件及微扰次近邻跃迁下，发现三角格子呈现Anderson绝缘体、Mott绝缘体和电荷密度波等相，而kagome格子上CDW完全缺失，仅存在Mott型绝缘基态，凸显了晶格几何与粒子-空穴对称性破缺的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对相关电子系统相图的研究多集中于二部格子，而格子几何对相图结构的影响尚未被系统阐明；同时，粒子-空穴对称性破缺如何改变相图稳定性也是一个开放问题。作者旨在通过非二部格子（三角格子和kagome格子）填补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov-chain Monte Carlo）模拟方法，对自旋Falicov-Kimball模型在三角格子和kagome格子上的相图进行系统性数值研究。考察了三种填充或化学势条件（固定化学势μ_f=μ_c=U/2、广义半填充\bar{n}_f=1/3,\bar{n}_c=2/3、常规半填充\bar{n}_f=\bar{n}_c=1/2），并在每种条件下研究了微扰性小的次近邻跃迁效应。

Result: 在三角格子上，所有条件下均发现Anderson绝缘体、Mott绝缘体和电荷密度波（charge-density wave, CDW）相，其中CDW由库仑相互作用介导的嵌套（nesting）驱动；在弱关联区与CDW之间发现电荷液态区域，其特征为竞争性电荷有序波矢并存且缺乏普适标度行为，该区域范围从巨正则条件到常规半填充条件逐渐缩小。在kagome格子上，CDW相完全缺失，仅发现Mott型绝缘基态，并出现零温量子相变的迹象。

Conclusion: 研究表明，晶格几何（包括格子类型与晶格对称性）和粒子-空穴对称性破缺在塑造相关电子系统相图中起决定性作用，非二部格子上的相图结构与二部格子存在本质差异，为理解几何效应在强关联系统中的作用提供了重要数值依据。

Abstract: We present a systematic investigation of the stability of the phase diagram of the spinless Falicov-Kimball model on two-dimensional non-bipartite lattices, the triangular and kagome lattices, using Markov-chain Monte Carlo simulations. Three filling or chemical potential conditions are examined: fixed chemical potentials ($μ_f = μ_c = U/2$), "generalized" half-filling ($\bar{n}_f = 1/3$, $\bar{n}_c = 2/3$), and conventional half-filling ($\bar{n}_f = \bar{n}_c = 1/2$). For each condition, the effects of a perturbatively small next-nearest-neighbor hopping are studied. On the triangular lattice, Anderson insulator, Mott insulator, and charge-density wave (CDW) phases are found in all cases, with the CDW driven by Coulomb-interaction-mediated nesting. Evidence for a charge-liquid regime is found in the region between the weakly correlated regime and the CDW, characterized by competing charge-ordering wavevectors and the absence of universal scaling behavior, whose extent shrinks progressively from the grand-canonical to the conventional half-filling condition. On the kagome lattice, the CDW phase is completely absent; instead, an insulating ground state, plausibly of the Mott type, is found, with signatures of a possible quantum phase transition at zero temperature. Our results establish the crucial role of lattice geometry and particle-hole symmetry breaking in shaping the phase diagram of correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [143] [Enhanced Diffusion from Twist-Angle Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29062)
*Nicole S Ticea,Yi-Ming Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭转双层体系（twisted bilayer systems）中扭转角无序（twist-angle disorder）对单粒子性质的影响，提出将扭转角建模为双层间相对位移的旋度（curl），发现几何散射约束显著抑制了动量弛豫，并将问题映射到非线性sigma模型（non-linear sigma model, NLSM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层材料中，局部的扭转几何变化会导致哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）产生空间相关的改变，进而影响衬底的输运和有序倾向等粗粒化性质。作者希望理解这种微小、不可控的空间变化如何变得异常具有后果性，并量化其动力学影响。

Method: 类比电动力学（electrodynamics）中磁场为矢势的旋度，作者将扭转角建模为两层间相对位移的旋度。通过考虑由扭转结构施加的几何散射约束，对比其与均值匹配的白噪声（white noise）在动量弛豫上的差异，并将该问题映射到非线性sigma模型。

Result: 发现扭转结构施加的几何散射约束相对于均值匹配的白噪声，能显著抑制动量弛豫（momentum relaxation）。作者对这些效应进行了量化，并展示了问题可被映射到非线性sigma模型。

Conclusion: 扭转角无序对扭转双层体系的单粒子性质产生重要影响，其特有的几何约束使得动量弛豫被显著抑制，且该问题可通过非线性sigma模型框架进行统一的描述。

Abstract: We consider the effect of twist-angle disorder on the single-particle properties of twisted bilayer systems. These materials are a natural playground for studying how tiny, uncontrolled spatial variations may become unusually consequential; a local change in the twist geometry can give rise to spatially-correlated alterations of the parent Hamiltonian, which in turn affect the coarse-grained properties of the substrate such as transport and ordering tendencies. In analogy to electrodynamics, where the magnetic field is given by the curl of the vector potential, we model the twist angle as the curl of the relative displacement between the two bilayers. We find that geometric scattering constraints imposed here by the twist structure substantially suppress momentum relaxation relative to mean-rate-matched white noise. We quantify these effects and show that the problem can be mapped onto a non-linear sigma model (NLSM).

</details>


### [144] [Duality Between Twist-Angle Disorder and Non-Hermitian Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29064)
*Yi-Ming Wu,Nicole S. Ticea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一种莫尔异质双层系统模型，其中扭转角仅在有限范围内保持均匀且良定义，在更大距离上发生随机变形。通过复制技巧（replica trick）框架，作者发现无序平均产生副本间交替符号的非平凡关联，并进一步证实在低能莫尔带中，该关联可通过费米子耦合非厄米无序的二元模型（dual model）精确生成，从而规避安德森局域化（Anderson localization）。这一二元性为研究莫尔系统中的扭转角无序提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔系统中的扭转角在实验制备中往往并非全局均匀，其无序（twist-angle disorder）对低能物理的影响尚未被充分理解。作者希望建立理论框架来刻画这种扭转角空间涨落产生的效应，并揭示它与传统无序在物理本质上的差异。

Method: 作者构建了一种局部扭转角受概率分布支配的莫尔异质双层模型，该分布对偏离均值的较大涨落施加惩罚。利用复制技巧进行无序平均，分析副本间的关联结构；随后引入费米子耦合非厄米（non-Hermitian）无序的二元模型，证明其在低能莫尔带中产生完全相同的关联。

Result: 无序平均产生一种非平凡的、副本间交替符号的关联，仅在每个局域域（local domain）边界附近及特定方向上达到最大值。二元模型中的非厄米无序能够动态规避安德森局域化，且与原始模型的低能关联精确等价。

Conclusion: 该二元性为研究扭转角无序提供了新的理论视角，并揭示了扭转角无序与传统无序之间存在的本质差异。

Abstract: We propose and study a model of moire heterobilayer systems where the twist angle remains uniform and well defined only within a finite range, but deforms randomly at larger distances. The local twist angle is then subject to a certain probability distribution that penalizes large fluctuations around its mean value. Within the framework of the replica trick, we show that disorder averaging produces a nontrivial correlation with alternating sign between replicas, maximized only close the boundary of each local domain and along certain directions. We show that for the low-energy moire bands, exactly the same correlation can be generated in a dual model where fermions are coupled to non-Hermitian disorder, which can evade Anderson localization dynamically. This duality provides a new perspective to investigate twist-angle disorder in moire systems, and unveils some of the essential differences between twist-angle disorder and conventional disorder.

</details>


### [145] [Real-space manifestation of ferroic multipoles in altermagnetic MnF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29089)
*Iurii Kibalin,Dalila Bounoua,José A. Rodriguez Velamazán,Oscar Fabelo,Navid Qureshi,Quentin Faure,Philippe Bourges,Victor Balédent,Jian-Rui Soh,Jeffrey Rau,Paul McClarty,Arsen Gukasov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过偏振中子衍射（polarized-neutron diffraction）重构了典型交变磁体（altermagnet）MnF₂的三维磁化密度，首次在实空间中直接观测到交变磁体中的铁性多极序（ferroic multipolar order），证实了对称性允许的秩-5磁多极O₅₂的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体是源自零自旋轨道耦合极限（zero spin-orbit coupled limit）的非常规自旋分裂磁体，其承载磁多极序参量，但此前直接观测这些多极的实空间证据一直难以获得。

Method: 利用偏振中子衍射技术重构MnF₂的三维磁化密度；通过利用对称选择性磁反射（symmetry-selective magnetic reflections），将占主导的球形Mn²⁺贡献与较弱的面异性Mn磁化及氟配体的共价自旋极化分离。

Result: 重建的自旋密度揭示了有限的氟离子磁矩，同时Mn的面异性磁化与对称性允许的交变磁性秩-5磁多极O₅₂（磁三十偶极，magnetic triacontadipole）一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果为交变磁体中的铁性多极序提供了直接的实空间证据，并确立了偏振中子衍射作为探测量子材料中隐藏磁多极的有力工具。

Abstract: Altermagnets are unconventional spin split magnets arising from the zero spin-orbit coupled limit. They host a magnetic multipolar order parameter yet direct real-space observation of these multipoles has remained elusive. Here we use polarized-neutron diffraction to reconstruct the three-dimensional magnetization density of the prototypical altermagnet MnF$_2$. By exploiting symmetry-selective magnetic reflections, we separate the dominant spherical Mn$^{2+}$ contribution from the much weaker anisotropic Mn magnetization and the covalent spin polarization of the fluorine ligands. The reconstructed spin density reveals a finite fluorine ion moment together with an anisotropic Mn magnetization consistent with the symmetry-allowed altermagnetic rank-5 magnetic multipole $O_{52}$(magnetic triacontadipole). These results provide direct real-space evidence of ferroic multipolar order in an altermagnet and establish polarized-neutron diffraction as a powerful probe of hidden magnetic multipoles in quantum materials.

</details>


### [146] [Electronic phase separation and emergence of a nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ ultrathin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29116)
*S. Inoue,D. Shiga,R. Hayasaka,K. Ozawa,A. F. Santander-Syro,H. Kumigashira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用原位光电子能谱（photoemission spectroscopy）和X射线吸收谱（X-ray absorption spectroscopy）研究了VO₂/TiO₂(110)超薄膜（ultrathin films）中电子结构和V-V二聚化（V-V dimerization）随厚度的变化。研究发现，在1.5 nm厚度下仍保持温度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变（metal-insulator transition）特征，但在1 nm时出现无V-V二聚化的绝缘相，且该相的分数随厚度减小呈指数增长，有效临界厚度约为2.2 nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究显示VO₂(001)₍R₎薄膜中因维度降低会增强Mott不稳定性（Mott instability）并导致无二聚化绝缘相的出现。然而在VO₂(110)₍R₎薄膜中，V-V链沿c₍R₎轴位于膜平面内，表面法线方向的维度降低不对V-V二聚化施加几何约束，因此作者希望探究在这种几何构型下，维度效应是否仍能驱动非二聚化绝缘相的出现。

Method: 采用原位光电子能谱和X射线吸收谱两种表面灵敏的谱学手段，对不同厚度的VO₂/TiO₂(110)超薄膜进行电子结构和V-V二聚化状态的系统表征，并将结果与VO₂(001)₍R₎薄膜进行对比分析。

Result: 厚膜中与温度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变相关的特征谱学变化可持续至1.5 nm厚度，但在1 nm时观察到无V-V二聚化的绝缘电子相。该非二聚化绝缘相与温度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变相共存，其分数随厚度减小呈指数增长，在1 nm时占主导，有效临界厚度估计为2.2 nm。

Conclusion: 维度降低导致的Mott不稳定性增强是VO₂超薄膜中非二聚化绝缘相出现的普遍且关键的驱动力，并非依赖于V-V链的几何取向。然而V-V链的几何取向决定了电子相分离（electronic phase separation）的空间演化方式，这是通过应变介导的相竞争（strain-mediated phase competition）实现的。

Abstract: Using in situ photoemission spectroscopy and x-ray absorption spectroscopy, we investigated the thickness dependence of the electronic structure and V-V dimerization in VO$_2$/TiO$_2$ (110) ultrathin films, in which the one-dimensional V-V chains along the $c_{\mathit{R}}$ axis lie in the film plane. In VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ films, the reduction in dimensionality along the surface-normal direction is not expected to impose a geometric constraint on V-V dimerization, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$ films. Nevertheless, the characteristic spectral changes associated with the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition observed in thick films persist down to 1.5 nm, whereas at 1 nm an insulating electronic phase is observed without V-V dimerization. This behavior is highly similar to that reported for VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, suggesting that the enhancement of Mott instability resulting from reduced dimensionality is a common and essential driving force for the emergence of the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ ultrathin films. Meanwhile, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ coexists with the phase exhibiting the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition. Its fraction increases exponentially with decreasing thickness and becomes dominant at 1 nm. The corresponding effective critical thickness is estimated to be 2.2 nm. These results imply that the geometric orientation of the V-V chains dictates the spatial evolution of electronic phase separation via strain-mediated phase competition.

</details>


### [147] [Evolving charge order in the CDW state of AV$_3$Sb$_5$ metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29142)
*Anshu Kataria,Francis Pratt,Peter J. Baker,Stephen Cottrell,Miki Bonacci,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Zurab Guguchia,Samuele Sanna,Pietro Bonfà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究将此前针对RbV₃Sb₅的avoided level crossing (ALC) μ子自旋谱（muon-spin spectroscopy）研究扩展到A = Cs和K的AV₃Sb₅ kagome金属体系中，识别出一个不能仅归因于内部磁场来源的二次转变，表明电荷密度波（CDW）相内存在额外的电荷调制机制，该调制发生在T* < T_CDW（Cs和Rb）或在T_CDW附近（K）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV₃Sb₅ kagome金属以电子序与结构序的相互交织为特征，是近年来的研究热点，但超导相之前电子态的具体细节仍知之甚少。作者希望通过系统研究不同A位（Rb、Cs、K）体系，进一步理解超导前电子态的性质。

Method: 采用avoided level crossing (ALC) μ子自旋谱（muon-spin spectroscopy）技术，将此前对RbV₃Sb₅的研究方法扩展到CsV₃Sb₅和KV₃Sb₅体系中，通过观察ALC共振特征来探测材料内部的磁场分布和电荷分布变化。

Result: 在Cs和K体系的AV₃Sb₅ kagome金属中同样识别出第二次转变，该转变无法单由内部磁场解释。ALC结果表明，在电荷密度波（CDW）相内存在额外的电荷调制：对于A = Cs和Rb，该调制发生在T* < T_CDW；对于A = K，则发生在T_CDW附近。

Conclusion: 额外电荷调制机制在A = Cs、Rb和K的AV₃Sb₅体系中普遍存在，暗示这是该材料家族共有的电子机制，其发生温度随A位元素不同而有所差异，可能与A位离子尺寸或耦合强度相关。

Abstract: AV$_3$Sb$_5$ kagome metals are characterized by intertwined electronic and structural orders, which motivated extensive studies in recent years. Yet the details of the electronic state preceding the superconducting phase remain poorly understood. Here we extend our previous investigation [Phys. Rev. Research 7, L032046 (2025)] of RbV$_3$Sb$_5$ using avoided level crossing (ALC) muon-spin spectroscopy to the $A$ = Cs and K systems. Consistent with our previous study, we identify a second transition whose origin cannot be attributed solely to an internal magnetic field, indicating the involvement of an additional electronic mechanism that subtly modifies the charge distribution within the V plane. In particular, the ALC results point towards an additional charge modulation taking place within the charge density wave (CDW) phase and occurring at $T^{*}<T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = Cs and Rb, or in the vicinity of $T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = K.

</details>


### [148] [Crystal electric field excitations and an effective-spin-$1/2$ ground doublet in the hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29276)
*Tingjun Zhang,Zehao Wang,Douglas L. Abernathy,Rong-Zhu Lin,Steven J. Gomez Alvarado,Chien-Lung Huang,Pengcheng Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用非弹性中子散射技术确定了稀土超kagome磁体Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂中Yb³⁺的晶体电场（crystal electric field, CEF）激发谱，观测到约58、68和74 meV三个近无色散的磁激发，并通过Stevens算符分析重构了能级方案，确立了各向异性Ising型基态g张量和有效J_eff=1/2自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该材料作为超kagome几何中的稀土磁体，其低温集体磁性行为依赖于精确的单离子晶体电场能级信息，而此前仅从体测量间接推断的能级方案需要直接验证和修正，以构建有效的交换哈密顿量并解释低能集体激发。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering, INS）直接测量CEF激发，结合局部222（D₂）对称性约束的Stevens算符分析（Stevens-operator analysis），并通过两种独立拟合方案交叉验证，进一步用单离子模型复现磁化率、磁化强度及比热Schottky异常的场演化。

Result: 观测到58、68和74 meV三个近无色散磁激发，分别对应基态Kramers双重态到J=7/2多重态中三个激发双重态的跃迁；拟合得到Ising型基态g张量，首个激发双重态距基态约58 meV，确立了低温下良好隔离的有效J_eff=1/2自由度；直接测量的CEF谱显著修正了此前体测量推断的能级方案，且两种独立拟合方案给出一致的激发能量、基态波函数和g张量。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂微观单离子基态描述，为构建有效交换哈密顿量并解读未来低能集体激发测量提供了必需的微观基础，同时验证了低温有效J_eff=1/2赝自旋描述的稳健性。

Abstract: We use inelastic neutron scattering (INS) to determine the crystal electric field (CEF) excitations of Yb$^{3+}$ in the rare-earth hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$. Three nearly dispersionless magnetic excitations are observed near 58, 68, and 74~meV, corresponding to transitions from the ground-state Kramers doublet to the three excited doublets of the $J=7/2$ multiplet. A Stevens-operator analysis constrained by the local $222$ ($D_2$) symmetry reproduces the excitation energies and spectral weights and yields an Ising-type ground-state $g$ tensor. The first excited doublet lies approximately 58~meV above the ground state, establishing a well-isolated effective $J_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ degree of freedom over the low-temperature regime relevant to collective magnetism. Notably, the directly measured CEF spectrum substantially revises the level scheme previously inferred from bulk measurements, while preserving the essential low-energy pseudospin description. Two independent fitting protocols give consistent excitation energies, ground-doublet wave functions, and $g$ tensors, despite the nonuniqueness of the individual CEF parameters. The resulting single-ion model also reproduces the characteristic susceptibility, magnetization, and field evolution of the Schottky anomaly in specific heat. These results establish the microscopic single-ion basis needed to construct an effective exchange Hamiltonian and to interpret future measurements of low-energy collective excitations in Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$.

</details>


### [149] [Symmetry-Enforced Topological Structures in Quantum Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29915)
*Linhao Li,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过识别有能隙相图中环路的不可收缩性（$S^1$-族）来研究多体系统的量子相图拓扑结构，重点考察对称性在其中所起的角色，并展示了由混合反常、半直积对称关系及非可逆算子对称性所导致的、先前基于“独立”对称性的分类无法触及的新颖 $S^1$-族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对量子相图拓扑结构的分类通常依赖于“独立”对称性假设，但实际物理系统中对称性之间存在复杂交互关系（如混合反常与非可逆算子），这些结构可能带来此前分类框架无法覆盖的新奇拓扑分类。

Method: 作者通过识别有能隙相图中环路（即 $S^1$-族）的不可收缩性来研究相图的拓扑结构，并系统考察了三类对称性交互机制：(i) 非平凡混合反常（mixed anomalies）；(ii) 自发破缺对称性与未破缺对称性之间的半直积关系；(iii) 具有非可逆算子（noninvertible operators）的对称性。此外，作者为这些 $S^1$-族构造了格点实现（lattice realizations）。

Result: 研究发现上述三类对称性交互结构均能导致先前基于独立对称性的分类所无法触及的新颖 $S^1$-族分类，并在格点模型中明确验证了这些 $S^1$-族的新奇代数结构。

Conclusion: 对称性之间非平凡的交互关系（而非仅独立对称性）能够产生更丰富的量子相图拓扑分类，这凸显了在分类有能隙多体系统时须纳入更一般的对称性代数结构。

Abstract: We study the topological structure of the quantum phase diagram of gapped systems by identifying the noncontractibility of loops, so-called $S^1$-families, within the gapped phase diagram in which many-body Hamiltonians can have nontrivial ground-state degeneracy. We manifest the role of symmetries in such $S^1$-family classifications by the exotic symmetry interplay: (i) nontrivial mixed anomalies, (ii) semi-direct product relation between the spontaneously broken and the unbroken symmetries, and (iii) symmetry with noninvertible operators. We find that such structures lead to the novel $S^1$-family classifications inaccessible by earlier classifications based on ``independent'' symmetries. Furthermore, we construct lattice realizations of these $S^1$-families and explicitly demonstrate their novel algebraic structures.

</details>


### [150] [Experimental observation of decoupled spin subsystems in decorated square kagomé lattice magnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29323)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过电子顺磁共振（EPR）技术研究了nabokoite家族化合物中的复杂交换网络，发现其可分裂为两个几乎去耦合的自旋子系统，其中一个仅有部分铜自旋在Néel点处有序，另一个对EPR沉默，两者共存于同一化合物中，挑战了传统统一交换网络模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方kagomé晶格（square kagomé lattice, SKL）为研究二维系统中的几何阻挫（geometric frustration）提供了理想平台，而nabokoite家族化合物将其拓展至三维网络。本研究的动机在于探索这种由二维SKL层与层间自旋装饰构成的复杂体系中的磁性行为。

Method: 利用电子顺磁共振（EPR）技术，通过EPR吸收的绝对标定来定量测定EPR活跃自旋的比例，并将EPR吸收与静态磁化率进行比较，以分离并表征两个自旋子系统的不同磁性贡献。

Result: EPR吸收的绝对标定表明，nabokoites中仅有部分铜自旋是EPR活跃的，且这一部分自旋在Néel点处发生磁有序。EPR吸收与静态磁化率的对比显示，EPR沉默自旋子系统对总磁化率的贡献随温度降低而减小，表明系统中磁有序与可能的自旋液体动力学共存。

Conclusion: 该工作直接观察到了同一化合物中磁有序与可能的自旋液体动力学的共存现象，这一发现对具有装饰阻挫晶格（decorated frustrated lattices）中统一交换网络的传统模型提出了挑战。

Abstract: The square kagomé lattice (SKL) offers a model platform for investigating geometric frustration in 2D systems. Nabokoite-family compounds \nabok{A}{X} (A=Na, K, Cs, Rb and X=Cl, Br) extend this physics to a 3D network, where 2D SKL layers are decorated by interlayer spins. Using electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR), we demonstrate a dramatic splitting of this complex exchange network into two virtually decoupled spin subsystems: absolute calibration of the electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) absorption reveals that only a fraction of all copper spins in nabokoites is EPR-active and this fraction of the spins orders at the Néel point. Comparison of the EPR absorption and static susceptibility indicates that contribution of the EPR-silent spin subsystem to total magnetic susceptibility decreases on cooling. This direct observation of coexisting magnetic order and possible spin-liquid dynamics within a single compound challenges conventional models of unified exchange networks in decorated frustrated lattices.

</details>


### [151] [Collinear and noncollinear antiferromagnetic ordering in a highly frustrated decorated square kagomé lattice antiferromagnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29330)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,K. V. Zakharov,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过多频电子自旋共振（electron spin resonance）光谱和热力学测量系统研究了Nabokoite家族化合物ACu₇(TeO₄)(SO₄)₅X的磁有序行为，发现轻碱金属（K、Na）与重碱金属（Rb、Cs）化合物在低温基态选择上存在本质差异：重碱金属化合物表现为常规共线反铁磁有序，而轻碱金属化合物则通过铁电相变和两步非共线磁相变实现更为复杂的磁有序路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nabokoite家族化合物承载了受挫的二维方形kagomé（square kagomé）晶格层以及额外层间磁性离子，是研究几何受挫磁性与磁有序之间竞争的理想平台。理解不同碱金属离子取代如何定性改变低温基态的选择，对于揭示该材料体系中受挫解除机制和磁结构演化规律具有重要意义。

Method: 本研究采用多频电子自旋共振光谱技术以及比热、磁化率和介电常数等热力学测量手段，系统表征Nabokoite家族化合物在低温下的磁序与自旋动力学性质。此外，对Nabokoite结构的金字塔形结构单元进行了有限尺寸团簇建模分析。

Result: 重碱金属（Rb、Cs）Nabokoite化合物呈现常规共线反铁磁（collinear antiferromagnetic）有序，具有易轴各向异性，且整个重碱金属子家族的有序态参数非常接近。轻碱金属（Na、K）化合物则先经历25-90K的铁电相变解除受挫，随后通过两步相变（T_c1≈5-6K和T_c2≈3-4K）发展出非共线磁有序，并表现出三个零场磁振子能隙和两个自旋重取向场。有限尺寸团簇建模表明，Nabokoite交换耦合参数恰好处在不同量子基态分界线附近，这可能是轻、重碱金属化合物磁有序态定性差异的关键。

Conclusion: Nabokoite家族中碱金属离子的大小决定了低温磁基态的本质差异：重碱金属化合物倾向于常规共线反铁磁序，而轻碱金属化合物通过铁电相变解除受挫后形成非共线磁序。Nabokoite结构的金字塔形结构单元中交换耦合参数的临界相近性可能是决定这一差异的微观机制。

Abstract: Nabokoite family compounds ACu$_7$(TeO$_4$)(SO4)$_5$X (A=Na, K, Rb, Cs; X=Cl, Br) host frustrated 2D square kagom'{e} lattice layers decorated by additional inter-layer magnetic ions. We study magnetic order in nabokoites with multi-frequency electron spin resonance spectroscopy and thermodynamic measurement (specific heat, magnetization and dielectric permittivity). Our study reveals that the choice of the low-temperature ground state is qualitatively different in light-alkali-ion (K, Na) and heavy-alkali-ion (Rb, Cs) compounds. Heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites order in conventional collinear antiferromagnetic pattern with easy-axis anisotropy. The parameters of the ordered antiferromagnetic state are very close for all heavy-alkali-ion subfamily. Light-alkali-ion members of nabokoite family demonstrate much more complicated route to the ordered state: firstly, a ferroelectric transition at 25-90K lifts the frustration and thus pre-cooks the low-temperature ordering; secondly, an unusual noncollinear magnetic order develops via two-step phase transition with first transition temperature $T_{c1}\simeq 5-6$K and the second transition at $T_{c2}\simeq 3-4$K. Noncollinear order is evidenced by observation of characteristic non-Larmor antiferromagnetic resonance mode. Spin dynamics of light-alkali-ion nabokoites is characterized by three zero-field magnon gaps and two spin-reorientation fields, the values of magnon gaps and critical fields are quite different for different compounds. The finite-size cluster modeling of pyramidal structural block of nabokoite structure combined suggests that the critical closeness of the nabokoite exchange coupling parameters to the border-line between the different quantum ground state of pyramidal building block of nabokoite structure could be the clue to the choice of qualitatively different ordered state in light- and heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites.

</details>


### [152] [Chiral Color Ice: Exact Local Handedness Constraints and Möbius Zero Modes in Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30802)
*Péter Kránitz,Yasir Iqbal,Karlo Penc*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文表明手性（chirality）本身可以被施加为精确的局域量子约束，且不要求在自旋空间中选取某个轴。作者构造了SU(2)不变的半正定母哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian），在四面体上给出指定的手性符号的完整零能空间。该框架将局域手性、非线性约束几何与量子简并联系起来，不同格点连接方式产生截然不同的集体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫物质（frustrated matter）的低能物理受局域约束支配，但传统冰规则型约束（ice-type constraint）仅约束类通量量（flux-like quantities），对手性不敏感。作者希望将手性本身作为一种精确的局域量子约束来构造，以建立手性与约束网络连通性之间的直接联系。
