Method: 作者构造正半定且SU(2)不变的母哈密顿量，其局域项对自旋1/2是到手性四面体单重态的秩一投影（rank-one projector）；对任意自旋则通过单重态湮灭算符（singlet-annihilation operator）因子化为B†B形式，其核由全局旋转的手性色冰态（chiral color-ice states）张成。对相干态，零能条件化为自旋方向间的Möbius变换非线性约束，其复合定义了拓展格点上的约束和乐（holonomy）。

Result: 角共享（corner-sharing）格点保留指数大的量子基态核，下界严格超过传统冰基准；边共享（edge-sharing）格点支持次维度的平面或线零模；三角形构型抑制非均匀相干形变，并在零能处包含完整的天顶塔（Anderson tower）四面体磁序。两个不等价三角形覆盖显示谐波零模计数不能决定量子核的大小。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个可处理的框架，能够在其中将局域手性、非线性约束几何与量子简并解耦，并直接将其与约束网络的连通性相关联，为受挫磁性中的手性约束物理提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: Local constraints govern the low-energy physics of frustrated matter, but familiar ice-type rules constrain flux-like quantities and are insensitive to handedness. Here we show that handedness itself can be imposed as an exact local quantum constraint without selecting an axis in spin space. We construct positive-semidefinite, SU(2)-invariant parent Hamiltonians whose complete zero-energy space on a tetrahedron has a prescribed chirality sign, rather than selecting a particular chiral wave function. For spin-1/2 the local term is a rank-one projector onto a chiral tetrahedral singlet, while for arbitrary spin it factorizes as $B^\dagger B$ through a singlet-annihilation operator, with a completely characterized kernel given by the span of the globally rotated chiral color-ice states. For coherent states, the same zero-energy condition becomes an $S$-independent nonlinear constraint in which three spin directions determine the fourth through a Möbius transformation; compositions of these maps define constraint holonomies on extended lattices. Connecting the same local constraint in different ways produces qualitatively different collective regimes: corner-sharing lattices retain exponentially large quantum ground-state kernels, with rigorous lower bounds exceeding conventional ice benchmarks; edge-sharing lattices support subdimensional plane or line zero modes; while triangular constructions suppress nonuniform coherent deformations and contain the complete Anderson tower of tetrahedral magnetic order at exactly zero energy. Two inequivalent triangular coverings further show that harmonic zero-mode counting does not determine the size of the quantum kernel. These results establish a tractable setting in which local handedness, nonlinear constraint geometry, and quantum degeneracy can be disentangled and related directly to the connectivity of the constraint network.

</details>


### [153] [Magnetic Field-Tunable Repulsive Exciton-Exciton Interaction in the van der Waals Antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29701)
*Kaiyang Huang,Jaena Park,Zhuo Yang,Je-Geun Park,Atsuhiko Miyata,Yoshimitsu Kohama,Yasuhiro H. Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过脉冲磁场（最高178特斯拉）下对二维范德瓦尔斯晶体NiPS₃进行系统的磁光测量，观察到两个尖锐激子峰之间显著的相互排斥和振子强度重新分布，构建最小两能级相互作用模型与实验数据半定量对比，揭示了磁场可调的激子-激子耦合是NiPS₃的高场主导响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiPS₃中两个超窄吸收峰（约1.5 eV）被认为源自自旋-轨道纠缠三重态到单重态的跃迁，具有显著的自旋依赖特征；一个有趣的问题是超高磁场是否会改变这两个峰之间由相互作用驱动的杂化。

Method: 在最高178特斯拉的脉冲磁场下对NiPS₃进行系统的磁光测量，并构建最小两能级相互作用模型，与实验数据进行半定量比较。

Result: 观察到两个尖锐激子峰之间显著的相互排斥以及振子强度的重新分布，而带边在实验分辨率内未显示可检测的场致位移。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了磁场可调的激子-激子耦合（exciton-exciton coupling）是NiPS₃的高场主导响应，并将该材料确立为探索磁性范德瓦尔斯绝缘体中强关联激子物理的新实验平台。

Abstract: Two ultra-narrow absorption peaks around 1.5 eV, which are widely believed to originate from a transition from a spin-orbital entangled triplet to a singlet state, in the two-dimensional van der Waals crystal NiPS$_3$, have attracted considerable attention because of their pronounced spin-dependent character. An interesting question is whether ultrahigh magnetic fields modify an interaction-driven hybridization between those two peaks. In this work we perform systematic magneto-optical measurements of NiPS$_3$ in pulsed magnetic fields of up to 178 T and observe a pronounced mutual repulsion between the two sharp exciton peaks accompanied by a redistribution of oscillator strength, while the band edge shows no detectable field-induced shift within our experimental resolution. We construct a minimal two-level interaction model and compare it semi-quantitatively with the experimental data. Our results reveal a magnetic-field-tunable exciton-exciton coupling as the dominant high-field response of NiPS$_3$, and clarify this material as a new experimental platform for exploring strongly correlated exciton physics in magnetic van der Waals insulators.

</details>


### [154] [Emergent Pair Density Wave and Incoherent Metallic State in a Strongly Correlated Doped System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29742)
*Soham Maiti,Nandan Pakhira,A. Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在奴隶转子（slave-rotor）平均场框架下，研究了反铁磁（antiferromagnetism, AFM）与单态d波超导（singlet d-wave superconductivity, SC）相互作用导致的动力学态涌现，发现配对密度波（pair-density-wave, PDW）序在AFM与SC共存区域中动态涌现，并系统分析了相关效应如何修正相图、改变四临界点位置及载流子凝聚振幅等物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平均场方法难以充分描述强关联效应，作者希望通过将电子分解为电荷与自旋自由度，捕捉超越常规平均场框架的强关联效应，并理解AFM与SC共存区域中可能涌现的新量子态（如PDW序）。

Method: 采用奴隶转子平均场框架（slave-rotor mean-field framework），将电子分解为电荷自由度与自旋自由度，在AFM与SC共存区域内研究PDW序的动态涌现，并考察掺杂量与温度对AFM、SC和PDW序参量以及转子凝聚振幅φ的演化规律。

Result: PDW序在AFM与SC共存区域中动态涌现；AFM-SC共存区域随相关效应显著改变，导致四临界点（tetra-critical point）发生系统性移动；序参量随掺杂和温度的演化显示存在从相干金属到非相干金属的交叉（crossover），伴随相干准粒子谱权被抑制。在AFM+φ≠0区域，相干准粒子带与非相干Hubbard型激发共存；而在AFM+φ=0区域仅存非相干谱特征。SC相表现出与d波配对一致的点状（nodal）准粒子。

Conclusion: 相关效应在AFM-SC共存区域中扮演关键角色，不仅促成PDW序的动态涌现，也通过电荷相干性（由φ刻画）的丧失驱动体系从相干金属向非相干金属的交叉过渡，同时保证SC相保持d波配对特征。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical emergence of a state driven by the interplay between antiferromagnetism (AFM) and singlet $d$-wave superconductivity (SC) within a slave-rotor mean-field framework. By decomposing the electron into charge and spin degrees of freedom, the formalism captures strong-correlation effects beyond conventional mean-field approaches. A PDW order is found to emerge dynamically in the coexistence region of AFM and SC. The AFM-SC coexistence region is significantly modified with correlation, leading to a systematic shift of the tetra-critical point. The doping and temperature evolution of the AFM, SC, and PDW order parameters, together with the rotor condensate amplitude $φ$, which characterizes charge coherence, shows a crossover from a coherent to an incoherent metal with the suppression of coherent quasi-particle spectral weight. Moreover, in the AFM + $(φ\neq 0)$ region, coherent quasi-particle bands coexist with incoherent Hubbard-like excitations, whereas only incoherent spectral features survive in the AFM + $(φ= 0)$ regime. The SC phase exhibits nodal quasi-particles consistent with $d$-wave pairing.

</details>


### [155] [Thermal Hall Signatures of Distinct Schwinger-Boson Flux Sectors on the Honeycomb Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29792)
*Daiki Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过有限温度Schwinger玻色子平均场理论（Schwinger-boson mean-field theory）和Kubo公式，系统比较了阻挫J1-J2海森堡模型在三种不同通量（flux）扇区下的热霍尔电导率，发现0-通量和π-通量扇区的热霍尔响应符号相反，而π/2-通量扇区随温度出现符号翻转，从而提出热输运可作为区分不同部分子平均场结构的通量敏感探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热输运是探测阻挫磁体中电中性激发的体探针，但尚不清楚其能否区分同一自旋系统的不同部分子平均场结构。该问题在蜂窝格子上尤为重要，因为已提出0-通量、π-通量和手性π/2-通量三种不同的通量扇区。

Method: 采用有限温度Schwinger玻色子平均场理论求解自洽鞍点解，并结合玻色Bogoliubov-de Gennes系统的Kubo公式，计算本征自旋子热霍尔电导率（spinon thermal Hall conductivity）。模型为含次近邻Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用和垂直磁场的阻挫J1-J2海森堡模型。

Result: 在三个通量扇区都保持有能隙的共同参数点处，采用文中约定（D>0, h>0）时，0-通量扇区热霍尔响应为正，π-通量扇区为负，而π/2-通量扇区的响应随温度升高发生符号翻转。此外，结合六重旋转与时间反演的投影对称性迫使零场下固定手性π/2-通量畴的响应消失。

Conclusion: 在考察的平均场参数范围内，κxy/T的符号和温度依赖能够提供区分通量扇区的输运签名，因此热输运可作为一种区分相同自旋系统不同部分子结构的有效手段。

Abstract: Thermal transport offers a bulk probe of charge-neutral excitations in frustrated magnets. It remains challenging, however, to determine whether such transport can distinguish different parton mean-field structures of the same spin system. This question is particularly relevant on the honeycomb lattice, where distinct $0$-flux, $π$-flux, and chiral $π/2$-flux sectors have been proposed. In this paper, we compare self-consistent saddle-point solutions in these three sectors for a frustrated $J_1-J_2$ Heisenberg model with a next-nearest-neighbor Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and a perpendicular magnetic field. Using finite-temperature Schwinger-boson mean-field theory and the Kubo formula for bosonic Bogoliubov-de Gennes systems, we evaluate the intrinsic spinon thermal Hall conductivity. At a common parameter point where all three branches remain gapped, the $0$-flux response is positive and the $π$-flux response is negative for $D>0$ and $h>0$ under the sign conventions used in this paper, whereas the $π/2$-flux response changes sign with increasing temperature. We further show that a projective symmetry combining a sixfold rotation with time reversal forces the zero-field response of a fixed chiral $π/2$-flux domain to vanish. Within the mean-field regime examined in this paper, the sign and temperature dependence of $κ_{xy}/T$ therefore provide a flux-sensitive transport signature.

</details>


### [156] [Holographic Representations of Topological Quantum Criticality: Emergent Symmetry Approach around the Bott Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30335)
*Fan Yang,Fei Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文运用涌现对称性（emergent symmetry）的思想，为Bott时钟（Bott clock）中费米子类别的拓扑量子临界点（topological quantum critical points, tQCPs）构造全息表示，通过构建(d+1)维晶格模型并加倍自由度，证明了其d维边界表现出与相应tQCP相同的红外动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将涌现对称性的思想推广到拓扑量子临界点的研究，为Bott时钟中费米子类别的一类广泛tQCPs建立全息（holographic）表示，从而为理解这些临界点的性质提供新的理论框架。

Method: 作者首先证明在d维空间中，位于受对称性G_p保护的对称性保护有隙相（symmetry protected gapped phases）之间的tQCP处存在涌现对称性U_EM。利用该涌现对称性，构造具有升级对称性（如U_EM×G_p或U_EM⋊G_p）的(d+1)维晶格模型。通过加倍相邻拓扑类的自由度，证明这些(d+1)维模型拥有精确属于Bott时钟中逆时针相邻拓扑类的所需扩大对称群。

Result: 成功证明按此方法构造的(d+1)维晶格模型具有精确的目标扩大对称群，且这些有隙拓扑类模型的d维边界展现出与Bott时钟中d维相邻拓扑相中对应tQCP相同的红外动力学，但对称性较低。

Conclusion: 该结果表明涌现对称性可以为费米子类别的拓扑量子临界点提供有效的全息描述，其边界物理与临界点红外物理一致，为拓扑量子临界现象提供了新的维度和对称性视角。

Abstract: In this article, we apply the idea of emergent symmetries to construct holographic representations of a broad class of topological quantum critical points (tQCPs) that appear in fermionic classes in the Bott clock. We show explicitly that around the Bott clock, an emergent symmetry $U_{EM}$ exists at a tQCP between different symmetry protected gapped phases with protecting symmetry $G_p$ in $d$ spatial dimensions. This emergent symmetry can be used to construct a $(d+1)$ spatial dimensional lattice model with a properly upgraded symmetry such as $U_{EM}\times G_p$ or $U_{EM} \rtimes G_p$, etc. By doubling the degrees of freedom of the adjacent topological class in $d$-dimensions, we successfully show that the $(d+1)$-dimensional lattice models constructed in this way have the desired enlarged symmetry groups that precisely belong to the adjacent topological classes, counterclockwise around the Bott clock. Furthermore, $d$-dimensional boundaries of these $(d+1)$ dimensional lattice models of gapped topological classes are shown to exhibit identical infrared dynamics as the corresponding tQCPs in the $d$-dimensional adjacent topological phases in the Bott clock, with lower symmetries.

</details>


### [157] [Pseudospin Dynamics of Charge Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30381)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从扩展Hubbard模型出发，推导了一个有效赝自旋模型，将电荷有序态映射为交错的赝自旋序，其动力学由类似Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert方程描述。研究表明外加电场可直接控制电荷有序极性，并可在超过阈值场强时驱动其相干翻转，从而为电荷有序的动态控制提供了类似自旋电子学中自旋有序动力学的微观框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷有序（charge order）通常被视为电子密度的静态调制，但相对于自旋有序系统，其非平衡态演化及外部驱动下动力学控制的微观理论框架仍然缺乏，这构成了研究的主要动机。

Method: 从扩展Hubbard模型出发，推导出有效的赝自旋（pseudospin）模型，将电荷有序态映射为交错的赝自旋序；电荷有序动力学由类Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert方程描述，与二分子反铁磁体的动力学方程高度类似。

Result: 研究表明，外加电场可以直接控制交错的赝自旋序；当电场超过阈值时，通过使得集体赝自旋激发失稳，能够驱动电荷有序极性发生相干反转。

Conclusion: 该工作确立电荷赝自旋作为电荷有序相干开关与控制的微观动力学自由度，为电荷扇区提供了与自旋电子学中自旋有序动力学框架相对应（analogue）的理论基础。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally viewed as a static modulation of the electronic density, despite growing experimental capabilities to probe and manipulate its nonequilibrium evolution. In contrast to spin-ordered systems, a microscopic framework for charge-order dynamics and its control under external driving remains largely underdeveloped. Here, starting from an extended Hubbard model, we derive an effective pseudospin model in which the charge-ordered state maps onto staggered pseudospin order. The resulting charge-order dynamics is governed by Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert-like equations for the pseudospins, closely analogous to those of a bipartite antiferromagnet. We show that an external electric field directly controls the staggered pseudospin order and, above a threshold field, drives coherent reversal of the charge-order polarity by destabilizing collective pseudospin excitations. Our results establish the charge pseudospin as a microscopic dynamical degree of freedom for coherent switching and control of charge order, providing a charge-sector analogue of the well-established framework for spin-order dynamics in spintronics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Direct Evidence of Unconventional Superconductivity in Doped Kagome system RV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28848)
*Avior Almoalem,Yuqing Xing,Iksu Jang,Daniel J. Schultz,Grgur Palle,Andrea N. Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Rafael M. Fernandes,Jörg Schmalian,Vidya Madhavan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过杂质束缚态谱学（impurity-bound state spectroscopy）研究最优掺杂的RbV₃Sb₅，发现其超导态为全能隙且具有两个不同能量尺度，束缚态特征与传统s波和s±配对不相容，而与手性序参量（chiral order parameter）完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome金属的超导配对对称性仍是一个核心未解问题，主要原因是能够区分不同序参量的相位敏感（phase-sensitive）实验难以实施。需要一种新的手段来探测超导序参量的微观特征。

Method: 利用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope）的谱学成像技术测量杂质束缚态的空间与能量特征，并结合理论计算进行对比分析。研究对象为电荷密度波（charge-density wave）不稳定性被完全抑制的最优掺杂RbV₃Sb₅。

Result: 发现超导态为全能隙（fully gapped）并呈现两个不同的能量尺度，符合多带序参量（multiband order parameter）特征；非磁性缺陷周围的原子级谱学显示出显著的粒子-空穴不对称束缚态。理论对比表明这些束缚态与常规s波（s-wave）及符号反转的s±配对不相容，但与手性序参量完全一致。

Conclusion: 杂质束缚态谱学可作为Kagome材料中超导性的强有力相位敏感探针，为最优掺杂RbV₃Sb₅实现全能隙手性超导态提供了有力证据。

Abstract: The superconducting pairing symmetry of kagome metals remains a central unresolved question largely because phase-sensitive experiments capable of distinguishing between different possible order parameters have been difficult to implement. The spatial and energetic characteristics of impurity bound states measured by spectroscopic-imaging scanning tunneling microscopy encode information on the superconducting order parameter. Here, we use impurity-bound state spectroscopy to investigate optimally doped RbV$_3$Sb$_{5}$ where the charge-density wave instability is fully suppressed. We find that the superconducting state is fully gapped and exhibits two distinct energy scales, consistent with a multiband order parameter. Atomic-scale spectroscopy around nonmagnetic defects reveals pronounced particle-hole asymmetric bound states. Comparison with theoretical calculations demonstrates that these bound states are incompatible with conventional $s$-wave and sign-changing $s^{\pm}$ pairing. Our calculations also show that the data are fully consistent with a chiral order parameter. Our results establish impurity-bound-state spectroscopy as a powerful phase-sensitive probe of superconductivity in kagome materials and provide strong evidence that optimally doped RbV$_3$Sb$_{5}$ realizes a fully gapped chiral superconducting state.

</details>


### [159] [Superconductivity and Magnetism in Bi-Ni System: From Bulk to Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28855)
*Gabriel Sant'Ana,Yutao Xing,Milton A. Tumelero*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文对Bi-Ni体系中两类超导系统（NiBi₃金属间化合物和Bi/Ni双层异质结构）的电子性质进行了系统综述，重点讨论了非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）中与磁性相关的时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal breaking）、三重态配对（triplet pairing）及邻近效应（proximity effect）等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导兼具磁性要素，如时间反演对称性破缺、三重态配对和邻近效应，是物理学和材料科学中长期追求的热点课题，有望催生创新现象并推动技术进步。Bi-Ni体系因其易于制备样品的特点，相较于铀基化合物等传统候选材料，已成为研究此类现象的理想平台。

Method: 本文采用文献综述（literature review）的研究方法，对Bi-Ni体系中两个子系统的电子性质研究成果进行了系统梳理与归纳，并围绕尚未解决的关键问题展开讨论。

Result: Bi-Ni体系包含两种截然不同的子系统：NiBi₃金属间化合物是4 K超导体，其正常相中的复杂磁性主导电学性质，但不涉及超导性质；而Bi/Ni双层异质结构表现出强烈的超导相位特征，具有时间反演对称性破缺和三重态配对的明确证据，构成复杂的非常规超导态。

Conclusion: Bi-Ni体系是研究非常规超导中磁性相关现象的理想候选系统，其样品制备的便利性显著加速了该领域的研究进展，但仍存在若干开放性问题，需进一步研究以实现对该体系电子性质的完整理解。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity with ingredients of magnetism such as time reversal breaking, triplet pairing and proximity effects has been a long time pursued topic in physics and material science as a new source of innovative phenomena and effects to drive further scientific advances and technologies. The Bi-Ni system has emerged as a convenient candidate for the investigation of such phenomenology. Strictly speaking, the term Bi-Ni system refers to two different schemes, first to the NiBi$_{3}$ intermetallic compound, a 4 K superconductor in which a complex magnetism seems to govern electrical properties in the normal phase, but without implication in the superconducting properties. The second Bi-Ni system is not quite a material but a heterostructure formed by a bilayer of Bi and Ni. Here the superconducting phase has to steal the spotlight by showing strong evidence of time-reversal breaking and triplet pairing featuring a complex unconventional state. The ease of preparing the samples, compared to other candidates for such unconventionality, usually Uranium-based compounds, has accelerated the research in this system. Here, we went through a detailed review of the main results about the electronic properties of these two systems as well as a discussion of the main open questions to be solved in order to reach the complete understanding in this topic.

</details>


### [160] [Electronic state of vortices at twin boundaries in a nematic superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28994)
*Keita Goto,Hiroto Adachi,Masanori Ichioka*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 理论研究了 s±d 波向列超导体中涡旋的局域电子态，发现钉扎在孪晶边界（twin boundary）上的单个涡旋由一对分数涡旋（fractional vortices）组成，并伴随弱诱导的 s±id 波分量，计算结果与 FeSe 的 STM 实验涡旋图像进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解向列超导体中涡旋芯（vortex core）的电子结构及其与孪晶边界的相互作用，对阐明铁基超导体中向列序（nematicity）与超导涡旋态之间的关系具有重要意义。

Method: 采用基于紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）的 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 理论，其中向列性通过转移积分和各向异性近邻吸引相互作用的各向异性来表征；计算了 s 波与 d 波分量的配对势（pair potentials）以及局域态密度（local density of states），分析了有无孪晶边界两种情形下向列性对涡旋芯结构的影响。

Result: 发现在孪晶边界处捕获的单个涡旋由一对分数涡旋束缚配对组成，并伴随弱诱导出的 s±id 波分量；而在零磁场下并不出现此类 s±id 波分量。计算得到的局域电子态空间结构与铁基超导体 FeSe 的 STM 涡旋图像具有可比性。

Conclusion: 向列性与孪晶边界的协同效应可在涡旋系统中诱导出零磁场下不存在的 s±id 波超导分量，为理解 FeSe 等铁基超导体中涡旋芯的电子结构与实验观测提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Local electronic states of vortices in an s $\pm$ d wave nematic superconductor are studied both in the absence and presence of twin boundaries. The Bogoliubov-de Gennes theory for a tight-binding model is used with its nematicity represented by the anisotropy in the transfer integrals and attractive interactions between the nearest-neighbor sites. We evaluate s and d wave components of the pair potentials and the local density of states, and analyze the effects of nematicity on the vortex core structures with/without twin boundaries. We find that a single vortex trapped at the twin boundary is composed of a bound pair of fractional vortices accompanied by weakly-induced s $\pm$ id wave components, despite that such s $\pm$ id wave components do not appear in a zero magnetic field. The calculated spatial structures of the local electronic states are compared with the vortex image measured by STM experiments in an iron-based superconductor FeSe.

</details>


### [161] [Doping-driven evolution of pairing symmetry in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29091)
*Hai-Yang Zhang,Yu-Jie Bai,Fan-Jie Kong*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究加压La₃Ni₂O₇中超导配对对称性及其随掺杂的演化：未掺杂时最有利的配对态为s±波，源于双层镍酸盐磁性奇模介导的排斥相互作用；空穴掺杂可使配对称性从s±波转变为d_xy波，而电子掺杂下s±波始终存在，甚至在发生Lifshitz相变的重电子掺杂区依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明加压La₃Ni₂O₇中超导配对对称性的具体形式及其随掺杂（空穴/电子掺杂）的演化规律，从而为理解双层镍酸盐超导配对机制提供检验依据。

Method: 通过分析未掺杂与不同掺杂条件下的配对相互作用细节，结合费米面口袋（γ、α、β口袋）的拓扑与磁激发模式（奇模与偶模）对配对排斥的贡献，判断优势配对态的对称性及其转变条件。

Result: 未掺杂时s±波为最有利配对态；空穴掺杂使γ口袋扩张并放大其袋内排斥（由磁偶模驱动），在重空穴掺杂区驱动配对对称性由s±波转变为d_xy波；电子掺杂下s±波始终存在，且在γ口袋因Lifshitz相变消失后因α-β口袋间良好嵌套而更加稳健。

Conclusion: γ费米口袋并非双层镍酸盐超导出现的必要条件，s±波配对在无γ口袋时反而更强；该发现将为加压La₃Ni₂O₇的超导配对机制提供新的检验途径。

Abstract: We investigate the superconducting pairing symmetry and its doping evolution in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. For the undoped compound, the most favorable pairing state is found to be $s_{\pm}$-wave, characterized by sign reversal of the gap functions between the Fermi pockets. A detailed analysis of the pairing interactions reveals that this unconventional state originates from repulsive interactions mediated by the magnetic odd modes of the bilayer nickelate. Upon hole doping, the $γ$ Fermi pocket expands, which amplifies the intrapocket repulsions on this pocket. These repulsions, driven by the magnetic even modes, gradually dominates the pairing interactions and ultimately drive a transition in pairing symmetry from $s_{\pm}$-wave to $d_{xy}$-wave in the heavily hole-doped regime. In stark contrast, the $s_{\pm}$-wave pairing persists under electron doping, even deep into the heavily electron-doped regime where a Lifshitz transition occurs. This finding suggests that the $γ$ Fermi pocket is not essential for the emergence of superconductivity in bilayer nickelates. In fact, the $s_{\pm}$-wave pairing becomes more robust in the absence of the $γ$ pocket, as spin fluctuations are strongly enhanced by the favorable nesting between the $α$ and $β$ pockets --- a condition guaranteed by Luttinger's theorem and the Fermi surface topology. We believe that exploring the doping evolution of superconducting pairing will open a new realm for testing the pairing mechanism in pressurized La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$.

</details>


### [162] [Topological pair density waves in kagome superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29156)
*Jia-Xin Yin,Xianxin Wu,Mark H. Fischer,Xiao-Yu Yan,Yigui Zhong,Kozo Okazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文综述了拓扑配对密度波（topological pair density wave, TPDW）的研究进展，分析了其在kagome超导体中的潜在实现路径，并讨论了相关的实验特征与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对密度波（PDW）是一种非常规超导态，具有有限动量Q配对导致的周期配对调制。在二维体系中，多重Q分量的相对相位可破坏时间反演对称性，产生拓扑电子结构。作者旨在系统梳理这类拓扑PDW的研究现状，并探讨其与kagome超导体的关联。

Method: 文章采用综述方法，首先从Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov（FFLO）态出发引入TPDW概念并分析其实现挑战；随后探讨kagome晶格中TPDW的可能性，将其与手性超导和环电流相联系；最后整理AV3Sb5（A = Cs， Rb， K）超导体中TPDW的实验证据。

Result: 综述总结了TPDW在AV3Sb5超导体中的实验特征，包括可切换的手性配对调制、Bogoliubov费米态、超导二极管效应和反常热霍尔效应等量子效应。

Conclusion: 文章展望了该关联拓扑量子相的未来研究机会，指出TPDW对有限动量配对、拓扑物质和手性超导等领域具有广泛影响。

Abstract: The pair density wave (PDW) is an unconventional superconducting state exhibiting periodic pairing modulations due to pairing with finite momentum Q. In two dimensions, multiple Q components of the PDW can have a non-trivial relative phase, breaking time-reversal symmetry and resulting in a topological electronic structure. Here we review progress on exploring such topological PDWs (TPDWs) and discuss their potential realization in kagome superconductors. We first introduce the concept of a TPDW starting from the Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) state and examine the challenges toward its realization. We then discuss the possibility of a TPDW in the kagome lattice, which intertwines with chiral superconductivity and loop currents, thus connecting to models describing the quantum anomalous Hall effect. Furthermore, we review the experimental signatures of a TPDW in AV3Sb5 (A = Cs, Rb, K) superconductors and highlight related quantum effects, including switchable chiral pairing modulations, Bogoliubov Fermi states, the superconducting diode effect, and the anomalous thermal Hall effect. Finally, we project the future research opportunities of this correlated topological quantum phase and discuss its broad implications for finite-momentum pairing, topological matter, and chiral superconductivity.

</details>


### [163] [Controlling Intertwined Electronic Orders in FeSe with Exfoliation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29407)
*Wenyao Liu,Gabriel Natale,Kyung-Mo Kim,Birender Singh,Piyush Sakrikar,Stephen D. Funni,Leo Kondo,Augustin Davignon,Antoine de Lagrave,Kota Ishihara,Michael Geiwitz,Maia G. Vergniory,Takasada Shibauchi,Jun Sung Kim,Michał Papaj,Judy J. Cha,Kenneth S. Burch†*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过机械剥离（exfoliation）对非常规超导体FeSe进行维度降低（dimensional reduction），发现剥离抑制了体材料的电子向列序（electronic nematic order），并将超导对称性从体材料的s波切换为以d波为主导。结合输运、电子显微、拉曼光谱和角度依赖的Andreev反射谱实验及理论建模，实验与理论结果共同证实了这一对称性转变，为调控量子序提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维超导体中交织电子序的控制对回答基础问题和构建新型量子器件至关重要，但现有调控手段（化学掺杂、应变、界面工程）通常复杂耗时。作者希望探索一种更简洁的调控方式，即通过维度降低（dimensional reduction）这一物理本征途径来调控竞争序之间的平衡。

Method: 通过对非常规超导体FeSe进行机械剥离实现维度降低；利用输运测量（transport）、电子显微术（electron microscopy）和拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）表征薄片中向列序的削弱；在原始晶体边缘进行角度依赖的Andreev反射谱（Andreev reflection spectroscopy）测量，并结合注入角、磁场和温度依赖关系及理论建模来确证超导对称性的变化。

Result: 薄片FeSe中向列序显著减弱，体材料的电子向列响应被抑制；超导对称性从体材料的s波切换为以d波为主导。角度依赖的Andreev反射谱从零能束缚态（zero-energy bound states）演化为相干峰（coherence peaks），验证了对称性转变。

Conclusion: 研究表明维度降低是调控超导体交织电子序的一种有效且无需复杂化学或工程手段的本征途径。该工作为工程化量子序提供了一个通用超导平台，并对非常规超导的配对机制提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Controlling intertwined electronic orders in two-dimensional superconductors offers an effective route to answering fundamental questions and engineering new quantum devices. However, tuning the balance between competing orders typically requires complex chemistry, strain, or interface engineering. Here, we show that a pristine alternative is the dimensional reduction of the unconventional superconductor FeSe. Exfoliation suppresses the bulk electronic nematic response and switches the superconducting symmetry from bulk s-wave to d-wave-dominant. Transport, electron microscopy, and Raman spectroscopy establish the substantial weakening of nematic order in thin flakes. To probe superconductivity, we perform angle-dependent Andreev reflection spectroscopy on pristine crystal edges. As the junction's orientation is rotated, the spectra evolve from zero-energy bound states to coherence peaks. The injection angle, field, and temperature dependence, along with theoretical modeling, confirm that exfoliation switches the superconducting symmetry. Our results suggest a versatile superconducting platform for engineering quantum orders and provide fresh insights into the underlying pairing mechanisms.

</details>


### [164] [Competition between on-site and off-site pairing phases and surface spectra in superconducting wallpaper fermion systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29691)
*Kaito Yoda,Ai Yamakage*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本论文从理论上研究了拓扑晶体绝缘体（topological crystalline insulator）表面态中壁纸费米子（wallpaper fermions）在超导配对（superconducting pairing）下的位点内（on-site）与位点间（off-site）配对竞争，发现位点间配对能稳定具有 B₂u 和 Eu 表示的新占据相，并导致光谱杂交和扭曲的表面能谱（twisted surface energy spectra）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壁纸费米子是由非对称（nonsymmorphic）对称性保护的表面态，其超导配对行为尚未被充分阐明。明确位点内与位点间配对的竞争机制，对于理解这类体系的拓扑超导性质具有重要意义。

Method: 作者在理论上考察了面上最近邻配对跳跃（in-plane nearest-neighbor pair hopping）以及其他位点间配对通道对壁纸费米子表面态的稳定作用，通过对称表示分析（representation analysis）区分了 A₁u、B₂u 和 Eu 等配对相，并分析了不同配对相下壁纸费米子谱之间的杂交行为（spectral hybridization）。

Result: 面上最近邻配对跳跃稳定 A₁u 相，而位点间配对通道还产生 B₂u 和 Eu 表示的新主导相。B₂u 配对态支持壁纸费米子谱之间的杂交，而时间反演不变（time-reversal-invariant）的向列型（nematic）Eu 态则导致壁纸费米子与双重马约拉纳克莱默斯对（double Majorana Kramers pairs）之间的谱杂交，产生扭曲的表面能谱。

Conclusion: 杂交现象所产生的扭曲色散（twisted dispersions）在位点间配对相互作用存在时依然能够保持，表明这类表面态的光谱特征对配对机制的细节具有较强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the competition between on-site and off-site pairings in superconducting topological crystalline insulators hosting wallpaper fermions, a class of surface states protected by nonsymmorphic symmetries. In-plane nearest-neighbor pair hopping stabilizes the $\mathrm{A_{1u}}$ phase, while off-site pairing channels yield additional dominant phases with $\mathrm{B_{2u}}$ and $\mathrm{E_u}$ representations. We further show that the $\mathrm{B_{2u}}$ pairing state supports the hybridization between wallpaper-fermion spectra themselves, whereas a representative time-reversal-invariant nematic $\mathrm{E_u}$ state exhibits spectral hybridization between wallpaper fermion and double Majorana Kramers pairs, yielding twisted surface energy spectra. These results demonstrate that the twisted dispersions arising from hybridization phenomena persist in the presence of off-site pairing interactions.

</details>


### [165] [Magnetic contacts on freestanding superconducting LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ micromembranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29771)
*Thies Jansen,Pinelopi Konstantinopoulou,Niklas Martin,Fabio Miletto Granozio,Alessia Sambri,Rasmus Bjørk,Felix Trier,Thomas S. Jespersen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在LaAlO₃/SrTiO₃（LAO-STO）界面的超导二维电子气（2DEG）上，通过侧接触几何实现了铁磁镍触点集成，低温输运测量表明超导态得以保留，且超导态受镍触点磁历史（magnetic history）的显著调控，产生可调有效磁场，并通过有效场模型从最大超导响应中推断出触点磁化强度，为氧化物超导体的自旋注入研究及基于复杂氧化物界面的超导自旋电子器件提供了平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAO-STO界面的超导二维电子气是超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）的有前景平台，但将铁磁触点与超导2DEG集成仍具挑战性，作者旨在实现这一集成并探索其物理行为。

Method: 采用侧接触几何（side-contact geometry）将铁磁镍触点与超导LAO-STO微膜器件相连，并通过低温输运测量研究超导态在铁磁接触存在下的特性；利用有效场模型分析超导响应以推断触点磁化强度。

Result: 低温输运测量表明，在铁磁触点存在下超导性得以保留；超导态显著受镍触点磁历史影响，产生在2DEG中可调的有效磁场；通过有效场模型，可从最大超导响应推断出触点磁化强度。

Conclusion: 铁磁接触的LAO-STO被确立为未来研究氧化物超导体自旋注入（spin injection）的平台，并为基于复杂氧化物界面的超导自旋电子器件提供了实现路径。

Abstract: The superconducting two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) at the LaAlO$_3$/SrTiO$_3$ (LAO-STO) interface is a promising platform for superconducting spintronics, however, integrating ferromagnetic contacts with the superconducting 2DEG remains challenging. Here, we realize superconducting LAO-STO micromembrane devices contacted by ferromagnetic nickel contacts through a side-contact geometry. Low-temperature transport measurements demonstrate that superconductivity is preserved in the presence of the ferromagnetic contacts. We show that the superconducting state is strongly influenced by the magnetic history of the nickel contacts, which generates a tunable effective magnetic field in the 2DEG. Through an effective field model, the magnetization of the contacts can be inferred from the maximum superconducting response. Our results establish ferromagnetically contacted LAO-STO as a platform for future investigations of spin injection into oxide superconductors and provide a route towards superconducting spintronic devices based on complex oxide interfaces.

</details>


### [166] [Interplay of Excitonic Charge Density Wave and Superconductivity in Transition Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29822)
*Ayussh Anubhav Patel,Amit Ghosal*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文基于1T-TiSe2相图的独特特征，在二维三角格子上构建了一个每格点含两个轨道的最小模型，并使用平均场框架研究激子电荷密度波（excitonic CDW）与超导（SC）之间的复杂相互作用。研究发现CDW随调控参数逐渐减弱并最终被SC取代，且CDW性质从公度转变为非公度，定性相图对能带结构细节不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-TiSe2相图具有独特特征：在不同调控参数（如插层、压力、替代和门控）作用下，材料表现出CDW逐渐衰减并伴随SC出现的普遍趋势，且CDW的本征性质会从公度（commensurate）转变为非公度（incommensurate）。理解CDW与SC之间这种引人注目的相互作用是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 在二维三角晶格上设定每格点两个轨道的最小模型，模型基于实验中观测到的激子CDW以及费米面上的电子和空穴口袋（electron and hole pockets）而构建，并在简单的平均场（mean-field）框架内对其进行分析。

Result: 该模型能够解释实验中若干显著的典型特征，包括CDW随调控参数的逐渐衰减以及SC的出现。此外，研究发现定性相图对能带结构的细节不敏感，说明该机制具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了过渡金属二硫化物（transition-metal dichalcogenides）中CDW与SC相互作用的本质，为理解此类材料中电荷密度波与超导共存的物理机制提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Motivated by the unique characteristics of the phase diagram of 1T-TiSe2, we investigated the complex interplay of excitonic charge density wave (CDW) and superconductivity (SC) on a two-dimensional triangular lattice, each site accommodating two orbitals. In response to various tuning parameters, such as intercalation, pressure, substitution, and gating, these materials exhibit a generic and gradual waning of CDW, followed by the emergence of SC. Intriguingly, the nature of CDW changes from commensurate to incommensurate with the appearance of SC. Setting up a minimal model based on observed excitonic CDW and electron and hole pockets in the Fermi surface, and analyzing it within a simple mean-field framework, we can comprehend some salient experimental features. We also found that the qualitative phase diagram remains insensitive to details of the band structure. These results offer crucial insights into the nature of the interplay between CDW and SC in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [167] [Ballistic-to-Localized Dynamics as Signature of Quantum Phase Transition in Josephson Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29830)
*F. G. Capone,A. de Candia,G. Di Bello,V. Cataudella,R. Fazio,N. Nagaosa,C. A. Perroni,G. De Filippis*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用最先进的数值技术，系统研究了小电容约瑟夫森结在欧姆（Ohmic）、亚欧姆（sub-Ohmic）和超欧姆（super-Ohmic）耗散区间的量子相变与动力学行为，揭示了耗散强度对相变类型及相粒子动力学本质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森结在耗散环境下的量子涨落与准粒子隧穿行为对于理解宏观量子现象和超导器件物理至关重要，尤其是耗散形式偏离欧姆型后相变行为的变化尚不清楚。

Method: 采用最先进的数值技术（state-of-the-art numerical techniques），结合实频线性响应（real-frequency linear-response）计算，系统分析不同耗散区间下的量子相变与动力学响应。

Result: 研究发现：欧姆耗散强度增强可驱动Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless量子相变；超欧姆区间无相变，亚欧姆区间呈现连续二级相变，与自旋-玻色子模型（spin-boson model）的普适类一致；欧姆区间内相粒子并不经历通常假设的扩散-局域化转变，而表现为弹道到局域化（ballistic-to-localized）的变化；有限频率下环境耦合产生长寿命激发并演化为共振。

Conclusion: 耗散形式对约瑟夫森结的量子相变本质具有决定性影响，且其动力学行为与现有常见假设显著不同，为理解耗散超导结的临界现象和输运特性提供了新的理论图像。

Abstract: Using state-of-the-art numerical techniques, we investigate how quantum phase fluctuations and quasiparticle tunneling shape the behavior of a small-capacitance Josephson junction across Ohmic, sub-Ohmic, and super-Ohmic dissipation regimes. We show that increasing the Ohmic dissipation strength drives a Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless quantum phase transition at thermodynamic equilibrium. Deviations from Ohmic behavior profoundly alter this scenario: the super-Ohmic regime exhibits no phase transition, whereas the sub-Ohmic regime displays a continuous second-order transition, consistent with the universality classes of the spin-boson model. Within the Ohmic regime, real-frequency linear-response calculations reveal that the phase particle does not undergo the commonly assumed diffusive-to-localized crossover. Instead, finite resistance progressively suppresses the singular zero-frequency response, producing a ballistic-to-localized change in the dynamics. At finite frequencies, coupling to the environment generates a long-lived excitation in the charge response, which evolves into a resonance as the subgap and shunt resistances are reduced.

</details>


### [168] [Superheating Field of Extreme Type-II Superconductors Calculated from the Eliashberg Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29842)
*Ramesh Paudel,Alex Gurevich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文基于各向同性Eliashberg理论计算了具有大Ginzburg-Landau参数的II型超导体过热度场H_s，揭示了H_s(T)的温度依赖可通过热力学方法在不同非磁性杂质散射参数p下获得，并发现Eliashberg理论给出的H_s/H_c比值超过弱耦合BCS模型，且Nb₃Sn在p≈0.2处H_s(0,p)存在极大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体过热度场H_s是限制射频超导腔性能的关键参数，此前对H_s的理论计算主要基于弱耦合BCS模型，缺乏基于强耦合Eliashberg理论且考虑非磁性杂质散射影响的系统研究。

Method: 从各向同性Eliashberg理论出发，采用热力学途径计算不同非磁性杂质散射参数p=ħ/2πτ_N k_B T_c下H_s(T)的温度依赖关系，覆盖从清洁极限(p≪1)到脏极限(p≫1)的宽范围；对Nb和Nb₃Sn，使用从隧道测量提取的电子-声子谱函数进行具体数值计算。

Result: Eliashberg理论计算的H_s(T,p)/H_c(T)比值超过弱耦合BCS模型的对应结果；尽管非磁性杂质在载流状态下是破对源，但对Nb₃Sn在T=0时，H_s(0,p)在p≈0.2处达到极大值，此时H_s/H_c比值比BCS清洁极限约高6%。

Conclusion: 强耦合效应显著影响超导体过热度场，Eliashberg理论可给出超过BCS预测的H_s/H_c比值；非磁性杂质在特定浓度下可增强T=0时的过热度场，这对优化超导射频腔材料与工艺具有指导意义。

Abstract: We calculate the superheating field $H_s$ of a type-II superconductor with a large Ginzburg-Landau parameter from the isotropic Eliashberg theory. It is shown that the temperature dependencies of $H_s(T)$ can be obtained from a thermodynamic approach for different scattering parameters $p=\hbar/2πτ_Nk_BT_c$ in a wide range of the nonmagnetic impurity scattering times $τ_N$ ranging from the clean $(p\ll 1)$ to the dirty $(p\gg 1)$ limits. Specific calculations were done for Nb and Nb$_3$Sn using their electron-phonon spectral functions extracted from tunneling measurements. We show that the ratio $H_s(T,p)/H_c(T)$ calculated from the Eliashberg theory exceeds that of the weak coupling BCS model, where $H_c$ is the thermodynamic magnetic field. We also show that, even though nonmagnetic impurities are pairbreakers in the current-carrying state, $H_s(0,p)$ for Nb$_3$Sn at $T=0$ has a maximum at $p\approx 0.2$ at which the ratio $H_s/H_c$ is about $6\%$ higher than that in the BCS clean limit.

</details>


### [169] [Chemical Perspectives on Cuprate Superconductivity: Hole-Protected Spin Dimers as d Wave Cooper Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30001)
*Myung-Hwan Whangbo,Reinhard K. Kremer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文基于铜酸盐（cuprate）超导体中极小的相干长度这一观测事实，提出了一种实空间结构模型：每个d波库珀对（Cooper pair）是一个被反铁磁空穴二聚体（AHD）保护的反铁磁耦合的自旋二聚体，且AHD组成的反铁磁链为这些自旋二聚体提供无扰动的集体移动通道。该模型能够自然解释铜酸盐超导体中若干令人困惑的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜酸盐超导体中极小的相干长度提示超导配对可能存在实空间结构，这促使研究者探究空穴掺杂在CuO4单元中的后果，以寻找该结构的微观起源。

Method: 研究者分析了从空穴掺杂的CuO2层中相分离（phase-separate）出来的空穴掺杂CuO4单元聚集体，并定义了分离压指数（segregation-pressure index）来量化这些聚集体的稳定性与分离趋势。

Result: 分析结果表明，每个d波库珀对实际上是反铁磁耦合的自旋二聚体，并被封装在反铁磁空穴二聚体（AHD）中；由AHD构成的反铁磁链为这些自旋二聚体提供了可以集体移动而不受扰动的通道。

Conclusion: 基于上述建议及其推论，作者检验了铜酸盐超导体中多个看似费解的实验观察，发现这些观察均可被该模型自然解释。

Abstract: The observation that the coherence lengths in cuprate superconductors are extremely small motivated us to search for its real-space structure by examining the consequence of hole-doping in CuO4 units in cuprates. We analyzed aggregates of hole-doped CuO4 units that phase-separate from hole-doped CuO2 layers in terms of their segregation-pressure indices defined in this work. Our search suggests that each d-wave Cooper pair is an antiferromagnetically coupled spin dimer protected in an antiferromagnetic hole dimer (AHD), and that the antiferromagnetic chains of AHDs provide channels through which such spin dimers can move collectively without perturbation. Based on these suggestions and their implications, we examined the causes for several seemingly puzzling observations on cuprate superconductors to find that they are naturally explained by the suggestions.

</details>


### [170] [Twist-Tunable Paramagnetic Superconductivity in $d$-wave Altermagnet/Superconductor Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30010)
*Narges Kia,Saeed H. Abedinpour,Zahra Faraei*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文理论上研究了由二维d波交错磁体（altermagnet）邻近诱导的d波超导体异质结构，揭示了超导能隙节点与交错磁自旋劈裂节点之间的动量空间失配可产生稳健的、可随扭转角调控的奇频自旋三重态配对，并由此实现对宏观电磁响应从常规抗磁迈斯纳态到反常顺磁迈斯纳效应的扭转调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转角工程（twist-angle engineering）与非传统磁性之间的相互作用为控制量子现象提供了新途径，作者旨在探索扭转交错磁体异质结构是否能够产生可控的奇频配对及宏观超导相变。

Method: 作者构建了由二维d波交错磁体邻近感应d波超导体的异质结构模型，通过分析超导能隙节点与交错磁自旋劈裂节点之间的动量空间匹配关系，从理论上推导扭转角对自旋三重态配对及系统宏观电磁响应的影响。

Result: 该系统可产生稳健且随扭转角可调的奇频自旋三重态配对；宏观电磁响应可从常规抗磁迈斯纳态转变为反常顺磁迈斯纳效应。对于d_{x^2-y^2}交错磁体，顺磁响应在完全对齐（φ=0）时最大，在最大扭转角（φ=π/4）时完全抑制；而d_{xy}交错磁体呈现完全互补的行为。

Conclusion: 扭转交错磁体异质结构被确立为一种可工程化奇频配对（odd-frequency pairing）及宏观超导相的多功能平台，为扭转角调控磁性与超导协同效应提供了新方向。

Abstract: The interplay between twist-angle engineering and unconventional magnetism provides a powerful new route to control quantum phenomena. We theoretically investigate a heterostructure comprising a $d$-wave superconductor proximitized by a two-dimensional $d$-wave altermagnet. We reveal that the momentum-space mismatch between the superconducting gap nodes and the altermagnetic spin-splitting nodes generates a robust, twist-tunable odd-frequency spin-triplet pairing. Consequently, the macroscopic electromagnetic response of the system can be tuned from a conventional diamagnetic Meissner state to an anomalous paramagnetic Meissner effect driven entirely by the interfacial twist angle. For a $d_{x^2-y^2}$ altermagnet, the paramagnetic response is maximized at perfect alignment ($φ=0$) and completely suppressed at a maximal twist of $φ=π/4$, while a $d_{xy}$ altermagnet exhibits the exact complementary behavior. Our results establish twisted altermagnetic heterostructures as a versatile platform for engineering odd-frequency pairing and macroscopic superconducting phases.

</details>


### [171] [Superconductivity of Tellurium Polyhydride with Tc above 90K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30139)
*Jinfu Zhu,Guiqi Liu,Yuanhao Su,Hongyu Liu,Sijia Zhang,Panpan Kong,Qingqing Liu,Jianfa Zhao,Shaomin Feng,Jun Zhang,Haoyu Zheng,Jing Song,Luhong Wang,Fuyang Liu,Haozhe Liu,M. Bykov,Xiancheng Wang,Changqing Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了在碲（Te）多氢化物中实验发现超导电性（superconductivity），该化合物在高达263 GPa的压力下表现出约91 K的超导转变温度，通过同步辐射X射线衍射确定为TeH4相，其结构包含面共享的TeH12笼和准分子H2单元，是继SH3之后又一硫族多氢化物高温超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在氢化物高温超导领域，继SH3这一里程碑式发现之后，探索其他硫族元素的多氢化物是否也能展现高温超导电性，以拓展该体系的研究范围。

Method: 采用金刚石压砧（diamond anvil cell）结合激光加热系统在高温高压条件下合成碲多氢化物；随后在高压下进行随温度和磁场变化的原位输运（transport）测量；并利用同步辐射X射线衍射实验确定超导相的晶体结构。

Result: 该化合物在263 GPa下表现出约91 K的超导临界温度Tc；超导相被确定为TeH4，具有面共享TeH12笼并形成准分子H2单元的结构特征；磁场下的超导行为分析给出金兹堡-朗道（Ginzburg-Landau, GL）相干长度约47埃。

Conclusion: 碲多氢化物成为继首个多氢化物高温超导体SH3之后又一硫族多氢化物超导体，丰富了多氢化物高温超导家族。

Abstract: We report experimental diacovery of superconductivity (SC) in tellurium (Te) polyhydride. The compound was synthesized at high pressure and high temperature conditions using a diamond anvil cell combined with a laser heating system. Subsequent in situ transport measurements at high pressures, performed as a function of temperature and applied magnetic field, revealed a superconducting transition with a critical temperature Tc about 91 K at 263 GPa. The superconducting phase is assigned to TeH4 with characterized face shared TeH12 cage forming quasi molecular H2 units based on synchrotron x-ray diffraction experiments. Analysis of the SC behavior at magnetic fields yielded a Ginzburg Landau (GL) coherence length of approximately 47 angstroms. Tellurium polyhydride thus becomes another chalcogen polyhydride superconductor in addition to the landmark discovery of the first polyhydride high Tc SC SH3.

</details>


### [172] [Machine learning reveals common features of unconventional superconductors with high transition temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30588)
*Haosheng Xu,Dongheng Qian,Yijun Yu,Jing Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用可解释机器学习（interpretable machine learning）揭示了非常规高温超导体的共同材料空间特征，并构建了数据驱动的材料发现策略。通过整合组分统计、结构信息和属性预测模型的潜在表示，无监督分析发现铜氧化物和铁基超导体共享一个特征区域，主要由大电负性偏差和中等平均价电子数刻画；监督的临界温度模型独立识别出相同描述符，进一步验证其相关性。基于该材料空间先验与临界温度模型，研究者成功优先排序候选材料、复现镍基超导体发现，并提出化学多样性新候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规高温超导体难以用声子介导机制（phonon-mediated regime）解释，但其发现缺乏像常规超导体那样的广泛适用的预测性理论指导，因此亟需构建数据驱动的发现策略。

Method: 将组分统计、结构信息以及训练好的属性预测模型中的潜在表征（latent representations）整合为统一特征表示；对实验确认的超导数据集进行结构感知过滤（structure-aware filtering）；结合无监督分析与有监督的临界温度模型，识别关键描述符并建立材料空间先验。

Result: 无监督分析显示铜氧化物（cuprate）和铁基（iron-based）超导体占据共同的材料空间区域，主要由大电负性偏差（electronegativity deviation）和中等平均价电子数（mean valence-electron number）表征；独立的监督临界温度模型确认相同描述符为主导特征。该策略成功优先排序候选材料并复现了镍酸盐（nickelate）超导体的发现。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动策略为非常规超导体的发现提供了新的预测框架，无需依赖跃变温度信息即可识别共性材料特征，并已识别出化学上不同的未来候选材料，为后续实验探索提供了具体方向。

Abstract: Superconductors with high critical temperatures that emerges beyond the phonon-mediated regime are usually considered unconventional in nature, yet unlike conventional superconductors, no broadly applicable predictive theory currently guides their discovery. Here, we use interpretable machine learning to uncover a common materials-space signature of high-$T_{\mathrm{c}}$ unconventional superconductors and develop a data-driven strategy for materials discovery. We construct a unified feature representation for each material by integrating compositional statistics, structural information, and latent representations from trained property-prediction models, followed by structure-aware filtering of an experimentally established superconducting dataset. Without using transition-temperature information, unsupervised analysis shows that cuprate and iron-based superconductors occupy a common region of materials space, characterized primarily by large electronegativity deviation and intermediate mean valence-electron number. A supervised $T_{\text{c}}$ model independently identifies the same descriptors as dominant features, providing complementary evidence for their relevance. Using this empirical materials-space prior together with the $T_{\text{c}}$ model, we prioritize candidate materials, recover recently discovered nickelate superconductors, and identify chemically distinct candidates for future investigation.

</details>


### [173] [Quadratic pair-breaking absorption edge from Anderson localization in a superconducting wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30598)
*Bahruz Suleymanli,B. Tanatar*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文证明了安德森局域化（Anderson localization）从根本上重塑了耗散超导电动力学，将线性Mattis–Bardeen对破缺起始替换为参数化更弱的二次Mott边界，同时保持准粒子谱不变，并为局域化超导纳米线的微波与自旋弛豫实验提供了全阶基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无序对超导电动力学的影响是凝聚态物理的核心问题，特别是局域化效应如何改变超导体的吸收行为尚未被系统阐明。

Method: 对弱无序单通道金属线采用Nambu空间的Berezinskii图技术扩展，全阶重求弹性杂质散射；利用Bogoliubov支守恒将Nambu图层次塌缩为精确可解的局域化问题。

Result: 局域化长度保持与正常态相同，而局域化时间在能隙边缘发散；新的吸收边界由超导对产生相干因子与Mott抑制的电流矩阵元乘积产生，呈现特征双对数行为；Hebel–Slichter相干峰也被局域化减弱。

Conclusion: 该理论为局域化超导纳米线的微波和自旋弛豫实验提供了全阶（all-orders）基准，揭示了局域化在超导吸收谱中诱导的Mott型二次边界这一普适特征。

Abstract: We show that Anderson localization fundamentally reshapes dissipative superconducting electrodynamics, replacing the linear Mattis--Bardeen pair-breaking onset with a parametrically weaker quadratic Mott edge while leaving the quasiparticle spectrum unchanged. We derive this behavior for a weakly disordered single-channel wire with a spatially uniform $s$-wave pair potential using a Nambu-space extension of the Berezinskii diagram technique that resums elastic impurity scattering to all orders. Conservation of the Bogoliubov branch collapses the Nambu diagram hierarchy onto an exactly solvable localization problem. The localization length remains equal to its normal-state value, whereas the localization time diverges at the gap edge. The new absorption edge results from the product of the superconducting pair-creation coherence factor and the Mott-suppressed current matrix elements between localized orbitals, which generates the characteristic double logarithm. Localization also reduces the Hebel--Slichter coherence peak. The resulting theory provides an all-orders benchmark for microwave and spin-relaxation experiments on localized superconducting nanowires.

</details>


### [174] [Microwave-Induced Optomagnetism in High-Temperature Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31127)
*Anoop Dhillon,Amir Borji,Hamed Majedi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究首次在高温超导体中实验观测到稳态微波驱动的逆法拉第效应（inverse Faraday effect）：圆偏振微波辐射在外延YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋δ薄膜中产生依赖螺旋度的光磁响应，该响应仅在超导转变温度T_c以下出现，有效光磁转换效率高达1.75 T/(W·cm⁻²)，并伴随涡旋相滑移区域的信号塌缩与涡旋洗板谐波处的再出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导体系中稳态微波驱动的逆法拉第效应，旨在开辟无需接触、非感应的磁控手段，并为超导量子系统中的非平衡涡旋光谱学提供新途径。

Method: 采用外延YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋δ薄膜作为样品，施加圆偏振微波辐射，通过同调霍尔输运（homodyne Hall transport）探测由微波螺旋度（helicity）决定的光磁响应信号；同时进行线偏振对照试验及不同微波功率下的信号测量。

Result: 圆偏振微波仅在T_c以下产生螺旋度依赖的光磁响应，正常态下消失；有效的光磁转换达到1.75 T/(W·cm⁻²)，超过光学基准数个数量级。高微波功率下，当自生磁场超过下临界场B_c1时信号塌缩，标志涡旋相滑移（vortex phase-slip）区域开始，随后在锁模涡旋洗板谐波（mode-locked vortex-washboard harmonics）处信号重新出现。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了稳态微波光磁效应（steady-state microwave optomagnetism）作为超导量子系统中接触式、非感应的磁场调控及非平衡涡旋光谱学的可行途径。

Abstract: We report the first experimental observation of a steady-state, microwave-driven inverse Faraday effect in a high-temperature superconductor. Circularly polarized microwave radiation generates a helicity-dependent response in an epitaxial $\mathrm{YBa_2Cu_3O_{7-δ}}$ film, detected using homodyne Hall transport. The optomagnetic response emerges exclusively below $T_c$, vanishes in the normal state, and exhibits no power-dependent counterpart under linearly polarized excitation. The effective optomagnetic conversion reaches $1.75\,\mathrm{T}/(\mathrm{W\,cm^{-2}})$, surpassing optical benchmarks by several orders of magnitude. At higher microwave powers, the signal collapses when the self-generated field exceeds $B_{c1}$, marking the onset of a vortex phase-slip regime, and subsequently re-emerges at mode-locked vortex-washboard harmonics. These results establish steady-state microwave optomagnetism as a route to contactless, non-inductive magnetic control and nonequilibrium vortex spectroscopy in superconducting quantum systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Reimagining Gravity: Generalized Symmetries, Double Copy, and Spinning Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28736)
*Joon-Hwi Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以博士学位论文的形式，从三个视角重新审视广义相对论：将引力视为洛伦兹群的规范理论以建立一形式对称性，探讨引力作为微分同胚规范理论与色-运动学对偶及双拷贝（double copy）的联系，以及将四维引力视为自对偶与反自对偶部分的非线性相互作用，并据此推导自旋黑洞解及其点粒子有效理论中的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽已百年历史，但广义对称性、散射振幅和有效场论等现代工具提出了若干未解问题，促使对引力、时空和自旋的传统观点进行重新审视。具体的开放问题包括：动力学引力在低能有效场论（EFT）中是否存在广义对称性？广义相对论与杨-米尔斯理论之间的树级双拷贝关系是否有场论解释？四维自旋黑洞在外场中的精确运动方程或拉格朗日量在点粒子有效理论中是什么？

Method: 论文从三个不同视角展开。第一部分将引力视为洛伦兹群的规范理论，建立动力学引力的一形式对称性。第二部分研究引力作为微分同胚规范理论的边界，联系色-运动学对偶与双拷贝。第三部分将四维引力视为自对偶与反自对偶部分的非线性相互作用，用于推导四维自旋黑洞解及其点粒子有效理论中的动力学，包括通过经典双拷贝推导新的自对偶黑洞度规，以及与Newman-Janis算法等效的动力学探针并给出自旋黑洞Compton振幅的拉格朗日推导。

Result: 论文表明Kerr度规可以表示为自对偶与反自对偶Taub-NUT解的配对，从而将Newman-Janis算法提升为严格的推导。此外，提出了Newman-Janis算法的动力学探针对应物，给出了自旋黑洞Compton振幅的拉格朗日推导，该振幅对所有螺旋度构型均具有正确的分解因式且无虚假极点，同时发展了手征形式主义以展现并最大化利用自对偶扇区的简洁性。

Conclusion: 论文通过三种互补视角（规范对称性、双拷贝关系和自对偶分解）回应了现代引力理论中的三个核心问题，为广义相对论的传统理解提供了新的场论视角，并构建了连接黑洞解与散射振幅的完整理论框架。

Abstract: General relativity is a century-old subject. Yet modern explorations through generalized symmetries, scattering amplitudes, and effective field theory have raised open problems, motivating a reassessment of conventional views on gravitation, spacetime, and spin. First, do generalized symmetries exist in dynamical gravity as a low-energy EFT? Second, is there a field-theoretic explanation for the tree-level double copy relationship between general relativity and Yang-Mills theory? Third, what are the exact equations of motion or Lagrangians of four-dimensional spinning black holes in external fields, in point-particle effective theory? In this dissertation, three different perspectives on gravity are developed to shed light on each of these puzzles.
  In Part I, we view gravity as a gauge theory of Lorentz group to establish a one-form symmetry of dynamical gravity. In Part II, we investigate how far gravity can be viewed as a gauge theory of diffeomorphisms, in connection with color-kinematics duality and double copy. In Part III, we view four-dimensional gravity as a nonlinear interaction between self-dual and anti-self-dual parts. We apply this view to the derivation of four-dimensional spinning black hole solutions as well as their dynamics in point-particle effective theory. First, we derive a new self-dual black hole metric from classical double copy and show that the Kerr metric represents a pair of self-dual and anti-self-dual Taub-NUT solutions, elevating the Newman-Janis algorithm to a rigorous derivation. Second, we propose a dynamical probe counterpart of Newman-Janis algorithm. We give a Lagrangian derivation of spinning black hole Compton amplitudes that exhibit correct factorizations and are free of spurious poles for all helicity configurations while developing a chiral formalism that manifests and maximally utilizes the simplicity of the self-dual sector.

</details>


### [176] [Carroll-Cotton Tensors and Gravitational Radiation at Null Infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28741)
*Adrien Fiorucci,Simon Pekar,P. Marios Petropoulos,Matthieu Vilatte*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过规范共形Carroll代数（conformal Carroll algebra），首次以第一性原理定义了一类用于共形Carroll几何（conformal Carroll geometry）的Cotton张量（Cotton tensor）。该张量既可作为Carroll几何中偏离共形平坦性的协变度量，也可用于描述四维渐近平直时空共形边界处的引力辐射自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carroll几何中的引力辐射描述缺乏自然的协变工具，而现有的Bondi news（Bondi新闻）张量需要借助补充数据（即Geroch张量）才能实现全协变定义，因此需要寻找更自然的几何对象来量化引力波发射导致的偏离平稳性（stationarity）的程度。

Method: 作者通过规范（gauging）共形Carroll代数来构造Carroll-Cotton张量，从而从第一性原理出发在共形Carroll几何上定义Cotton张量，使其天然具备共形协变性（conformal covariance）。

Result: 得到的Carroll-Cotton张量在数学上是量化Carroll几何中共形平坦性（conformal flatness）偏离的共形协变对象；在物理上，它可作为描述四维渐近平直时空共形边界处引力辐射自由度的几何工具。

Conclusion: 相较于Bondi news需要额外补充Geroch张量（Geroch tensor）构造完全协变定义，Carroll-Cotton张量是量化引力波发射引起偏离平稳性的更自然的几何对象。

Abstract: We provide a first-principles definition of Cotton tensors on conformal Carroll geometries by gauging the conformal Carroll algebra. On the mathematical side, this analysis yields a conformally covariant object designed to quantify deviations from conformal flatness in Carrollian geometries. On the physical side, it offers a powerful geometric tool to describe gravitational radiative degrees of freedom at the conformal boundary of four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Unlike the Bondi news, whose fully-covariant definition requires amendment by supplementary data that we identify with the Geroch tensor, the Carroll-Cotton tensors offer the natural geometric objects to quantify deviations from stationarity due to the emission of gravitational waves.

</details>


### [177] [OPE and correlation functions in a generally covariant form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28743)
*Arpit Das,Anatoly Konechny,Naveen Balaji Umasankar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究维度D≥3的弯曲时空上的欧几里得共形场论（conformal field theories, CFT），扩展了标量初级场在共形平坦空间上的算符乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）结果，并讨论了非共形平坦空间上的三点函数短距离行为，发现需要额外修正以保证局部协变OPE的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲时空上的共形场论是理解共形对称性在非平凡几何中如何表现的关键课题。已有工作（如Konechny的工作）处理了共形平坦空间上的标量通道OPE，但对于非共形平坦空间中的短距离行为和三点函数结构，此前的研究尚不完整。

Method: 本文首先在共形平坦空间上给出标量初级场OPE中后裔（descendant）贡献的一般性构造，推广了Konechny的结果。随后在环境空间形式（ambient space formalism）框架下讨论非共形平坦空间上的CFT，利用Parisini、Skenderis和Withers提出的通用拟设（general ansatz）研究这类空间上三点函数的短距离行为。

Result: 研究发现，为了确保局部协变OPE（local covariant OPE）的存在，需要对Parisini-Skenderis-Withers拟设添加额外的修正项。

Conclusion: 本文提供了弯曲空间上共形场论OPE构造的推广，并指出了非共形平坦空间中既有拟设的不足与修正方向，为局部协变OPE在一般弯曲几何中的成立奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper we consider some general aspects of Euclidean conformal field theories on curved spaces of dimension $D\ge 3$. We first look at the OPE of scalar primary fields on conformally flat spaces. Extending the results of \cite{Konechny:2026bqg}, we give a general construction of the descendants' contributions to the OPE in the scalar channel. We then discuss CFTs on non-conformally flat spaces in the ambient space formalism. We investigate the short-distance behaviour of three-point functions on such spaces using the general ansatz proposed by Parisini, Skenderis, and Withers \cite{Parisini:2022wkb, Parisini:2023nbd}. We find that some additional corrections need to be added to the ansatz to ensure the existence of a local covariant OPE.

</details>


### [178] [Holographic warped CFTs from General Relativity on AdS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28751)
*Kristiansen Lara,Ricardo Troncoso*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为三维负宇宙学常数爱因斯坦引力引入了一组新的边界条件，使规范生成元直接以标准形式实现 Virasoro 代数与 $u(1)$ Kac-Moody 代数的半直和。通过要求欧几里得 BTZ 黑洞与热 AdS$_3$ 微分同胚，诱导出化学势的翘曲模 $S$ 变换，对应的翘曲 Cardy 公式精确再现了 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵，并以欧几里得 AdS$_3$ 为基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三维引力中，边界条件的选择决定了渐近对称代数与全息对应关系。已有的 Brown-Henneaux 与 Compere-Song-Strominger 边界条件在实现 Virasoro 与 $u(1)$ Kac-Moody 代数的半直和时往往需要对全局电荷进行额外重定义。本文旨在构造一组新的边界条件，使该代数以标准形式自然实现，并精确匹配正则系综中的熵计算。

Method: 作者为三维负宇宙学常数爱因斯坦引力构造了一组新边界条件，从而规范生成元无需额外重定义即可直接实现 Virasoro 与 $u(1)$ Kac-Moody 代数的标准半直和。文中进一步要求欧几里得 BTZ 与热 AdS$_3$ 互为微分同胚，由此推导出化学势的翘曲模 $S$ 变换，并利用翘曲 Cardy 公式进行熵的精确计算。此外，与 Brown-Henneaux 和 Compere-Song-Strominger 两种边界条件进行了明确对比。

Result: 在新的边界条件下，规范生成元以标准形式自然实现了 Virasoro 与 $u(1)$ Kac-Moody 代数的半直和。翘曲 Cardy 公式精确复现了 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵，且基态被识别为欧几里得 AdS$_3$。与已有边界条件的比较揭示了本方案在电荷重定义问题上的优越性。

Conclusion: 该新边界条件为三维负宇宙学常数引力提供了一种规范而自洽的全息描述框架，使得对称代数、模变换与熵公式三者之间形成了精确的对应关系，为理解 AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ 对偶提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We introduce a new set of boundary conditions for three-dimensional Einstein gravity with negative cosmological constant. The canonical generators plainly realize the semidirect sum of Virasoro and $u(1)$ Kac-Moody algebras in its standard form, without the need of additional redefinitions of the global charges. We show that requiring Euclidean BTZ and thermal AdS$_3$ to be diffeomorphic precisely induces the warped modular $S$-transformation of the chemical potentials. The corresponding warped Cardy formula then exactly reproduces the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, where the ground state is identified as Euclidean AdS$_3$. An explicit comparison with the boundary conditions of Brown-Henneaux and Compere-Song-Strominger is also addressed.

</details>


### [179] [Action-angle variables and phase space formulation of Hermitian matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28942)
*Arghya Chattopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文从正交多项式（orthogonal polynomials）满足的递推关系出发，为大型厄米单矩阵模型发展了相空间（phase space）描述。在单切口相中，递推格点自然产生半经典作用-角变量对，可正则映射到本征值和动量变量；该相空间结构可通过Christoffel-Darboux投影子的Wigner变换及平面谱曲线再现。构造进一步推广到对称四次双切口相及多切口相的有限能隙（finite-gap）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型N厄米单矩阵模型是弦理论、拓扑弦等物理领域中的核心工具，但其几何理解依赖于谱曲线（spectral curve）等代数几何对象。作者希望直接从正交多项式的递推关系出发，建立一种底层的相空间描述，从而更直接地揭示矩阵模型的经典极限结构，并将单切口相推广到多切口相。

Method: 该文利用正交多项式递推关系所定义的递归格点，在单切口相中构造半经典作用-角变量对，通过正则变换将其映射至本征值和动量变量。动量轮廓由正交多项式零点密度决定；并使用Wigner变换（Wigner transform）处理Christoffel-Darboux投影子以及平面谱曲线来交叉验证相空间结构。对于双切口相，通过周期二的Jacobi递推产生两个Bloch带和两个不连通的相空间分量，其作用量由部分't Hooft耦合给出；最后概述了多切口相的有限能隙推广。

Result: 研究表明，正交多项式递推格点在其半经典极限下自然给出作用-角变量结构，该结构正则等价于本征值-动量相空间；相空间的动量轮廓与正交多项式零点密度一致。Wigner变换和谱曲线两种独立途径复现了相同的相空间结构，高斯模型提供了简单的一致性验证。在对称四次双切口相中，相空间分裂为两个分量，作用量分别由部分't Hooft耦合确定。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种从正交多项式递推出发的统一相空间描述框架，能够系统地处理单切口相、双切口相乃至一般多切口相（通过有限能隙推广）的矩阵模型。该框架在两种独立方法（Wigner变换与谱曲线）下自洽，并接受高斯模型验证，为矩阵模型经典极限的几何理解提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We develop a phase space description of large $N$ Hermitian one matrix models directly from the recursions satisfied by the orthogonal polynomials. In the one-cut phase, the recursion lattice naturally gives rise to a semiclassical action-angle pair, which can be mapped canonically to eigenvalue and momentum variables. We show that the resulting momentum profile is determined by the density of zeros of the orthogonal polynomials, and that the same phase space structure is reproduced from the Wigner transform of the Christoffel-Darboux projector and from the planar spectral curve. The Gaussian model provides a simple consistency check of the proposal. We then extend the construction to the symmetric quartic two-cut phase, where a period-two Jacobi recursion produces two Bloch bands and two disconnected phase space components with actions given by the partial 't Hooft couplings. Finally, we outline the finite-gap generalization appropriate to multicut phases.

</details>


### [180] [Flat connections on moduli spaces I: Local (1,0)-extension of the DHS connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29169)
*Eric D'Hoker,Benjamin Enriquez,Oliver Schlotterer,Federico Zerbini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是系列研究中的第一篇，旨在将构造于紧黎曼曲面构型空间上的平坦丢番图连接∇DHS扩展为Teichmüller空间上的整体平坦连接。论文聚焦于其中与Teichmüller空间全纯方向对应的部分∇L，推导其满足的方程组并给出了局部显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有构造给出了紧黎曼曲面上n点构型空间上的平滑平坦连接∇DHS，取值于无穷维李代数，并满足模群不变性。该文的目标是把这一连接扩展到更大的Teichmüller空间上，实现从局部到整体的推广。

Method: 选取适应于Teichmüller空间到其底层黎曼模空间映射的局部坐标，将扩展连接分解为三个部分：原有的∇DHS、对应于全纯方向的部分∇L，以及对应于反全纯方向的部分。作者隔离出∇L所满足的方程系统，并在局部显式地求解。

Result: 成功推导出∇L满足的方程组，并在局部坐标下获得了显式解；同时明确了后续系列论文中将处理的全局扩展以及另一类亚纯连接扩展任务。

Conclusion: 本工作为将∇DHS扩展为Teichmüller空间上的全局平坦连接提供了关键的第一步，即全纯组成部分的局部解；整体构造推迟至系列后续论文中完成。

Abstract: The flat DHS connection $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$ constructed in arXiv:2602.01461 is smooth on the configuration space of $n$ points on a fixed compact Riemann surface $Σ$ of arbitrary genus $h$, takes values in an infinite-dimensional Lie algebra $\hat{\mathfrak t}_{h,n}$ and is invariant under the modular group $\mathrm{Sp}(2h,\mathbb Z)$. This paper is the first in a series for a program whose goal is to extend the connection $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$ to a global flat connection on the Teichmüller space $\mathcal T_{h,n}$ valued in the Lie algebra of derivations of $\hat{\mathfrak t}_{h,n}$. Upon the choice of local coordinates adapted to the map $\mathcal T_{h,n}\to \mathcal T_h$, such a connection splits into three pieces: $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$, a piece $\mathcal L$ corresponding to holomorphic directions of $\mathcal T_h$ and a third piece corresponding to anti-holomorphic directions in $\mathcal T_h$. In this paper, we isolate the system of equations satisfied by $\mathcal L$ and obtain its solution locally and explicitly. The construction of a global extension of $\mathcal J_{\mathrm{DHS}}$ to $\mathcal T_{h,n}$ and the extension of the meromorphic connection of arXiv:1112.0864 to $\mathcal T_{h,n}$, are relegated to future publications in this series.

</details>


### [181] [One-loop renormalization group flow of the translation-invariant noncommutative Yukawa theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29213)
*Karim Bouchachia,Smain Kouadik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维欧氏Moyal空间上平移不变的非交换赝标量Yukawa理论的一圈重整化群流。由于Moyal星Yukawa顶点有两种不等价排序，产生了两个独立耦合常数，其beta函数构成耦合非线性系统。作者解析求解了该系统，发现其在变量u和v上解耦，并给出精确不变量。动力学上，流在紫外方向恢复两种Moyal排序的对称性，在红外方向放大初始不对称性（以对数分数幂形式）。由此，非平面1/(θ²p²)结构的Yukawa诱发系数在p→0时被动力学压制，但奇异性本身未被消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非交换场论中两种不等价Moyal星排序对重整化群流的影响，以及该动力学对非平面奇点（non-planar singularity）结构的物理后果。

Method: 在四维欧氏Moyal空间上构建平移不变的非交换赝标量Yukawa理论，识别两个独立耦合g₁和g₂；计算其一圈beta函数；通过变量变换u=g₁²+g₂²和v=g₁²−g₂²将系统解耦；利用精确不变量将一般非对称流约化为单一求积，并在对称表面上通过Riccati约化获得四次耦合。

Result: 一圈所有发散均可由已在作用量中的抵消项吸收，确立了该理论的一圈可重整性。解析解显示比率r=v/u具有紫外吸引对称面r=0和红外吸引非对称方向r=±1。Yukawa诱发的非平面1/(θ²p²)系数在p→0时以对数分数幂被动力学压制，但奇点本身未被移除。对称临界轨迹上有量纲扇形(M²,m,a²)以闭式求解。

Conclusion: 该非交换Yukawa理论的一圈重整化群流在紫外方向恢复Moyal排序对称性，在红外方向放大不对称性；非平面奇异结构被部分缓和但需IR改进项才能彻底解决。结果全程与交换（commutative）理论对比。

Abstract: We study the one-loop renormalization group flow of the translation-invariant noncommutative pseudoscalar Yukawa theory on four-dimensional Euclidean Moyal space. The two inequivalent orderings of the Moyal-star Yukawa vertex give rise to two independent couplings $g_{1}$ and $g_{2}$, whose beta functions form a coupled nonlinear system; every one-loop divergence is absorbed by a counterterm already present in the action, establishing one-loop renormalizability of the theory. We solve the one-loop system analytically: it decouples in the variables $u=g_{1}^{2}+g_{2}^{2}$ and $v=g_{1}^{2}-g_{2}^{2}$, the quartic coupling is obtained by a Riccati reduction on the symmetric surface $g_{1}=g_{2}$, and the generic asymmetric flow is reduced to a single quadrature by the exact invariant $\mathcal{I}=(g_{1}g_{2})^{3}/(g_{1}^{2}-g_{2}^{2})^{4}$. Two dynamical consequences follow. The ratio $r=v/u$ has a UV-attractive symmetric surface $r=0$ and IR-attractive asymmetric directions $r=\pm1$, so that the flow restores the symmetry between the two Moyal orderings towards the ultraviolet and amplifies any initial ordering asymmetry towards the infrared, as a fractional power of the logarithm. In consequence the Yukawa-induced coefficient of the non-planar $1/(θ^{2}p^{2})$ structure is dynamically suppressed towards $p\rightarrow 0$; the singularity itself is not removed, and remains the task of the IR-improving term. We also solve the dimensionful sector ($M^{2}$, $m$, $a^{2}$) in closed form on the symmetric critical trajectory, and compare throughout with the commutative theory.

</details>


### [182] [Inequalities and Positivity in Modular Flowed Entanglement Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29329)
*Liangyu Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究量子场论与AdS/CFT对偶中模块哈密顿量（modular Hamiltonian）诱导的动力学过程，引入“模块纠缠位移”（modular entanglement shift, MES）概念，证明确立了子系统A和B的MES之和等于互信息（mutual information）的精确熵平衡关系，并在二维共形场论（CFT）真空态中证明了两个不相交区间的MES相等且沿模块流非负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块哈密顿量在量子信息论、量子场论和AdS/CFT对应中扮演重要角色，但其诱导的动力学过程中纠缠熵如何变化此前缺乏系统性研究。作者旨在探讨一个特定动力学设置下纠缠熵的变化规律，并寻求其遵守的一般约束条。

Method: 从参考态（reference state）的约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrix）的张量积出发，在此态自身生成的模块流（modular flow）下演化该乘积态，考察给定区域纠缠熵的变化（即模块纠缠位移）。利用共形变换正则化（conformally transported regulator）以及Araki相对熵（Araki relative entropy）两种方式进行分析。

Result: 对于子系统A和B的二分剖分，证明了精确的熵平衡关系：A和B的MES之和等于沿轨道生成的互信息，因此其总和非负，尽管单个位移可能为负。对于二维CFT真空态中两个不相交区间，全局共形对合（global conformal involution）强制两个MES相等，从而每个MES沿模块流均非负。

Conclusion: 将模块流轨道与Connes余循环轨道（Connes cocycle orbit）建立联系，并讨论了该结果在AdS/CFT对应中的潜在应用，为理解模块动力学中纠缠结构的变化提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The modular Hamiltonian plays an important role in quantum information theory, quantum field theory, and the AdS/CFT correspondence. In this paper, we study a specific dynamical setup: starting from the tensor product of the reduced density matrices of a reference state, we evolve this product state under the modular flow generated by the reference state itself. We then investigate the resulting change in the entanglement entropy of a given region, which we call the modular entanglement shift (MES). Unlike evolution under a fixed quantum channel, this modular evolution obeys no general principle requiring the MES to have a definite sign. For a bipartition into subsystems A and B, we establish an exact entropy balance: the sum of the MESs for A and B equals the mutual information generated along the orbit. Consequently, their sum is nonnegative, even though either individual shift may be negative. For two disjoint intervals in the vacuum of a two-dimensional conformal field theory, however, we prove that a global conformal involution exchanging the intervals forces the two MESs to be equal. It follows that each MES is nonnegative along the modular flow. We establish this result both using a conformally transported regulator and intrinsically in terms of Araki relative entropy. Finally, we identify the modular-flow orbit with a Connes cocycle orbit and discuss possible applications to the AdS/CFT correspondence.

</details>


### [183] [Bound States in Perturbative Quantum Gravity with Hydrogen-like Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29402)
*Callum R. T. Jones,Shruti Paranjape,Marcos Skowronek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在自旋0、1/2、1、3/2、2的无质量中介粒子交换的长程相互作用通用有效场论（EFT）框架下，系统研究了隐藏的Laplace-Runge-Lenz（LRL）矢量守恒的条件，涵盖经典与量子两方面。研究发现经典无进动是广泛模型类的普适性质，但量子力学下保持简并性需要引力子与6个Majorana引力微子之间的意外抵消，暗示$\mathcal{N}=6$超引力具有特殊地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期引力散射振幅研究表明，某些高对称相对论系统可能精确守恒二体束缚态中的LRL矢量版本。作者希望系统性地厘清在何种条件下（经典无轨道进动与量子氢原子型简并）这一隐藏对称性得以保持。

Method: 作者在由自旋0、1/2、1、3/2、2的无质量中介粒子交换所产生长程相互作用的通用EFT框架中，系统研究LRL矢量守恒条件。技术上需要两个新进展：将相对论性post-Minkowskian势匹配方法推广至量子修正层面，以及纳入成对无质量费米子交换所导致的长程力。

Result: 经典层面上，无进动性质是包括Kaluza-Klein理论在内的一大批具有矢量与标量交换的模型类的普适属性；然而在量子力学层面，保持束缚态简并性需要对量子修正进行细致分析，其结果要求引力子与6个Majorana引力微子之间存在精确抵消。

Conclusion: 量子力学层面保持LRL矢量守恒所需的抵消条件指向$\mathcal{N}=6$超引力（$\mathcal{N}=6$ supergravity）扮演特殊角色，表明该理论的隐藏对称性与束缚态结构可能存在深刻的内在联系。

Abstract: Recent results in the study of gravitational scattering amplitudes indicate that some highly-symmetric relativistic systems may exactly conserve a version of the Laplace-Runge-Lenz (LRL) vector in two-body bound states. We make a systematic study, in the context of a generic EFT of long-range interactions due to the exchange of massless mediator particles of spins 0, $\frac{1}{2}$, 1, $\frac{3}{2}$, 2, of the conditions for the conservation of a hidden LRL vector; both classically (no orbital precession) and quantum mechanically (hydrogen-like degeneracy of bound states). The calculations require several new technical developments including the extension of relativistic post-Minkowskian potential matching to quantum corrections and the incorporation of long-range forces due to the exchange of pairs of massless fermions. We find that while classically the absence of precession is a generic property of a large class of models, including Kaluza-Klein theories, with vector and scalar exchanges, maintaining the degeneracy quantum mechanically requires a surprising cancellation between gravitons and 6 Majorana gravitinos, hinting at a special role for $\mathcal{N}=6$ supergravity.

</details>


### [184] [Teleparallel formulation of $E_{8(8)}$ Exceptional Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29541)
*Davide Rovere*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在远平行表述（teleparallel formulation）下推导了适用于研究三维最大超对称紧化的 $E_{8(8)} \times \mathbb{R}^+$ 例外场论（exceptional field theory）势能，通过要求其对偶群最大紧子群的无穷小转动不变性，从固有挠率（intrinsic torsion）的一般二次标量缩并中选出势能，从而在几何层面凸显固有挠率在高维超引力紧化中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望阐明固有挠率在高维超引力（higher-dimensional supergravities）紧化中的几何意义，并系统推导例外场论的势能形式。

Method: 采用远平行表述，从固有挠率二次标量缩并的最一般组合ansatz出发，该ansatz在广义微分同胚（generalised diffeomorphisms）下显式不变；随后通过施加对偶群最大紧子群的无穷小转动不变性条件，筛选出势能的具体形式。

Result: 成功在远平行表述下推导出 $E_{8(8)} \times \mathbb{R}^+$ 例外场论的势能，并揭示了固有挠率在几何层面上的主导角色：固有挠率被识别为所得规范超引力（gauged supergravity）的嵌入张量（embedding tensor）。

Conclusion: 远平行表述使固有挠率在紧化中的核心作用在几何层面变得显式，其与嵌入张量的对应关系为理解三维最大超对称紧化提供了清晰框架。

Abstract: The potential of $E_{8(8)} \times \mathbb{R}^+$ exceptional field theory, suited for studying three-dimensional maximally supersymmetric compactifications of higher-dimensional supergravities, is derived in teleparallel formulation. This means that, starting from an ansatz of the most general combination of the quadratic scalar contractions of the intrinsic torsion, which is manifestly invariant under generalised diffeomorphisms, one selects the potential by imposing the invariance under infinitesimal rotation of the maximal compact subgroup of the duality group. The teleparallel formulation makes manifest at geometric level the preeminent role of the intrinsic torsion in studying compactifications of higher-dimensional supergravities, where the intrinsic torsion is identified with the embedding tensor of the resulting gauged supergravity.

</details>


### [185] [Higher-Spin dS/CFT beyond minimal type-A, and the doubled nature of de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29594)
*Julian Lang,Yasha Neiman*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维德西特（de Sitter）空间中自由矢量模型与高自旋引力（Higher-Spin Gravity）之间的全息对偶。作者引入了新的现实性条件（reality conditions），允许在体边中包含奇数自旋，并将对偶从类型A（边界标量）扩展至类型B（边界旋量）。通过两种视角——平行宇宙与扭量空间中的勒让德对偶——统一了类型A与类型B理论，并以Euclidean AdS的单边界性质协调了de Sitter空间的双边界性质。此外，统一类型A与类型B及体边费米子的超对称理论在相应现实性条件下不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于拓展de Sitter空间中的全息对偶框架，使其能够包含奇数自旋场，并将对偶范围从标量类型的对偶扩展到费米子（旋量）类型，从而更全面地理解高自旋引力与边界理论的对应关系。

Method: 作者在体边与边界两侧分别引入新的现实性条件，该条件与de Sitter空间中单粒子（超）多重态的高自旋对称希尔伯特空间范数紧密相关。论文构建了两种等价视角：一是将类型A与类型B视为de Sitter空间偶/奇反对径识别（antipodal identification）下的平行宇宙；二是将两者视为Euclidean AdS中同一扭量空间理论（twistor-space theory）的勒让德变换（Legendre transform）描述，利用高自旋配分函数与其自身的勒让德对偶性实现统一。

Result: 新现实性条件成功允许体边包含奇数自旋场，并将全息对偶从类型A扩展至类型B。类型A与类型B理论可通过两种等价途径统一：平行宇宙观点或Euclidean AdS中扭量空间理论的勒让德对偶描述。高自旋全息由此调和了de Sitter空间的双边界性质与Euclidean AdS的单边界性质。但统一类型A、类型B与体边费米子的超对称理论在相应现实性条件下无法自洽。

Conclusion: 论文建立了de Sitter全息对偶中类型A与类型B的统一框架，揭示了高自旋配分函数的自勒让德对偶性这一关键结构。同时指出超对称推广在该框架下面临一致性障碍，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We study the holographic duality between free vector models and Higher-Spin Gravity in 4-dimensional de Sitter space. On both the bulk and boundary sides, we introduce new reality conditions, which allow us to include odd spins in the bulk, and to extend the duality from type-A to type-B (i.e. from boundary scalars to spinors). The new reality conditions are closely related to a higher-spin-symmetric Hilbert-space norm for the single-particle (super)multiplet in de Sitter space. We construct two alternative viewpoints on the type-A and type-B theories. In one view, the two co-exist in "parallel universes", specifically in even vs. odd antipodal identifications of de Sitter space. In the other view, type-A and type-B are Legendre-transformed descriptions of the same twistor-space theory in Euclidean AdS (this involves the observation that the higher-spin partition function is Legendre-dual to itself). In this way, higher-spin holography reconciles the two-boundary nature of de Sitter space with the one-boundary nature of Euclidean AdS. The supersymmetric theory that unifies type-A and type-B with bulk fermions fails to be consistent under the appropriate reality conditions.

</details>


### [186] [Genuine Multi-Entropy in Abelian Chern--Simons Theory: Exact Key-Ring Collapse and Its Breakdown for Generic Link States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29627)
*Yen-Cheng Chang,Kaberi Goswami,Norihiro Iizuka,Akihiro Miyata*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿贝尔$U(1)_k$陈-西蒙斯（Chern-Simons）理论中的Genuine多熵（genuine multi-entropy）。对于关键环链接态（key-ring link states），推导了$\mathtt{q}$分Rényi多熵的精确闭式表达式；对于一般的四分量链接态，数值扫描显示全$n$坍缩关系仅在$n=2$时对所有能级精确成立，且破坏模式与Rényi指标及能级的算术结构存在复杂耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠（multipartite entanglement）的量化是量子信息与拓扑量子场论交叉领域的重要问题，而Genuine多熵作为一种超越三分信息（tripartite information）的度量，其在拓扑场论背景下的结构与行为尚不清晰。

Method: 作者在阿贝尔$U(1)_k$陈-西蒙斯理论中，针对关键环链接态推导了$\mathtt{q}$分Rényi多熵的精确闭式解；随后利用数值扫描（Chern-Simons能级$2\leq k\leq 24$）研究一般四分量链接态，系统检验不同Rényi指标$n$下多熵分解关系的成立与否。

Result: 对$\mathtt{q}=4$关键环链接态，Genuine多熵$\mathrm{GM}^{(4)}_n$对所有$n$精确坍缩为三分信息$I_{3,n}$；$\mathtt{q}=5$时则由三分与二分多熵的线性组合完全确定。对一般链接态，全$n$坍缩失效，但$n=2$时对所有能级仍精确成立；$n=3$时仅当$3\mid k$时出现违反，且依赖$k$的3-adic赋值；$n=4$和$n=5$时所有能级均出现违反。

Conclusion: 多熵分解坍缩的破坏不被复合$\mathbb{Z}_k$的零因子结构单独控制，而是由Rényi指标与陈-西蒙斯能级的算术结构共同决定，揭示了拓扑纠缠度量的非平凡数论依赖性。

Abstract: We study genuine multi-entropy in Abelian $U(1)_k$ Chern-Simons theory. For key-ring link states, where only the linking numbers between one distinguished component $K$ and the remaining $\mathtt{q}-1$ components are nonzero, we derive an exact closed-form expression for the $\mathtt{q}$-partite Rényi multi-entropy for general $\mathtt{q}$, level $k$, and Rényi index $n$. For $\mathtt{q}=4$, this shows that the genuine multi-entropy $\mathrm{GM}^{(4)}_n$ collapses exactly onto the tripartite information $I_{3,n}$ for all $n$, while for $\mathtt{q}=5$ it is likewise completely determined, for all $n$, by a linear combination of tripartite and bipartite Rényi multi-entropies. We then go beyond the key-ring class and study general four-component link states with arbitrary pairwise linking numbers. A numerical scan over Chern--Simons levels $2\leq k\leq24$ shows that the all-$n$ collapse found analytically for key-ring states does not survive for generic link states. Remarkably, the collapse remains exact at $n=2$ for every level examined. At $n=3$, violations occur, within the scanned range, only when $3\mid k$, with a further dependence on the $3$-adic valuation of $k$. At $n=4$ and $n=5$, violations occur for every level examined, with rates that vary strongly with $k$. These results show that the breakdown is not controlled simply by the zero-divisor structure of composite $\mathbb Z_k$, but instead exhibits a nontrivial joint dependence on the Rényi index and the arithmetic structure of the Chern--Simons level.

</details>


### [187] [Latent Geometry and the Emergence of Lorentzian Time in Matrix Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29686)
*Badis Ydri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在BFSS/BMN矩阵模型中引入"潜在几何"（latent geometry）概念，论证了大$N$高斯化将涌现几何信息编码在由"小模糊球"（baby fuzzy sphere）挑选出的例外$N=2$扇区中，并通过维数约化、有限$N$高斯化与Molien-Weyl投影将编码传递到质量形变BFSS$_2$模型。在$N=2$处，同一稳定的BFSS$_2$振子可接受不等价的$\mathbf{H}^2_\theta=\mathbf{EAdS}^2_\theta$与$\mathbf{dS}^2_\theta$两种解码，实现时空转（spacetime transmutation）及洛伦兹时间的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在矩阵模型中，几何通常在几何相变（geometric transition）处蒸发（evaporate）而丢失信息；本文旨在揭示在几何蒸发之前，通过高斯化机制将几何信息编码保存于一个例外的小扇区中，从而为随后在更低维度模型中实现时空解码提供基础。

Method: 作者利用大$N$高斯化（Gaussianization）将信息编码于由小模糊球挑出的单活性自旋-1/2泡利块（Pauli block）的$N=2$例外扇区；随后采用维数约化（dimensional reduction）、有限$N$高斯化以及精确的Molien-Weyl投影（Molien-Weyl projection）将此编码传输至质量形变（mass-deformed）的BFSS$_2$模型。

Result: 在$N=2$能级上，同一稳定的BFSS$_2$振子能够承载两个不等价的幺正（unitary）解码——$\mathbf{H}^2_\theta = \mathbf{EAdS}^2_\theta$（欧氏反德西特空间）与$\mathbf{dS}^2_\theta$（德西特空间），分别对应不同的时空几何解释。

Conclusion: 该结果实现了时空转（spacetime transmutation），表明同一微观振子结构可在不同解码下呈现反德西特或德西特几何；同时洛伦兹时间（Lorentzian time）作为涌现（emergent）性质由此产生，为理解矩阵模型中的时空涌现提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{latent geometry} in BFSS/BMN models. Before an emergent geometry evaporates at the geometric transition, large-$d$ Gaussianization encodes its information in the exceptional $N=2$ sector selected by the baby fuzzy sphere, a single active spin-$1/2$ Pauli block. Dimensional reduction, finite-$N$ Gaussianization and exact Molien--Weyl projection transport this encoding to mass-deformed BFSS$_2$. At $N=2$, the same stable BFSS$_2$ oscillator admits inequivalent unitary $\mathbf{H}^2_θ=\mathbf{EAdS}^2_θ$ and $\mathbf{dS}^2_θ$ decodings, realizing spacetime transmutation and the emergence of Lorentzian time.

</details>


### [188] [One-Loop Fluctuation Response Along a Constrained Noncommutative Modulus in the Lorentzian IIB Matrix Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29688)
*Tetsuyuki Muramatsu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在N=5的洛伦兹IIB（IKKT）矩阵模型中研究局部约束问题，对一维物理模进行数值延拓。研究发现固定Qρ约束解族满足恒等式导致经典作用量为常数，23个接受的背景实现了该关系；沿采样分支，洛伦兹宽度算符谱在数值精度内保持一致，而微观非交换不变量则变化。有限维单圈涨落的费米子积分给出Pfaffian，其大小在颜色规范和洛伦兹变换下不变但沿模变化。完整量子涨落数据区分了粗粒度二次谱无法区分的背景。框架化高L SO(5)结构探针显示有序3+6谱分离随L增大而增大，Pfaffian与高L响应沿分支密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望探索IKKT矩阵模型中微观非交换结构与量子涨落是否包含与维度动力学相关的信息，而不试图建立维度选择机制。

Method: 在N=5的洛伦兹IKKT矩阵模型中，对固定约束的一维物理模进行数值延拓；计算有限维单圈涨落，其中费米子格拉斯曼积分精确给出Pfaffian；并采用框架化的高阶L SO(5)结构探针诊断谱分离。

Result: 23个接受的背景均满足静止恒等式（经典作用量常数）；洛伦兹宽度算符谱保持公共数值精度；Pfaffian幅度在颜色规范和固有时空变换下不变但沿模变化；完整涨落测度定义密度一形式而非标量势；高L探针在L=2,3以及大L极限下均显示更大的3+6谱分离；Pfaffian与高L响应高度相关。

Conclusion: 微观非交换结构和量子涨落可能包含与维度动力学相关的信息，但本文不建立维度选择机制；量子涨落数据能区分粗粒度二次谱无法区分的背景。

Abstract: We study a local constrained problem at matrix size $N=5$ in the Lorentzian IIB (IKKT) matrix model and numerically continue a one-dimensional physical modulus. For an exact fixed-$Q_ρ$ constrained solution family, the stationary identity implies constant classical action; the 23 accepted backgrounds realize this relation to the quoted precision. Across the sampled branch, the spectrum of the Lorentzian extent operator remains common to numerical precision while microscopic noncommutative invariants vary.
  We evaluate finite-dimensional one-loop fluctuations along this branch. At fixed bosonic background, the fermionic Grassmann integral is exact and gives $\operatorname{Pf}\mathcal M_F$; its magnitude is invariant under color-gauge and connected proper-Lorentz transformations and varies along the modulus. The complete boson--ghost--fermion quotient measure defines a Lorentz-representative-independent density one-form rather than a scalar potential. Thus quantum fluctuation data distinguish backgrounds unresolved by the coarse quadratic spectrum.
  We also apply a framed higher-$L$ SO(5) structural probe. At the reference background, the ordered $3+6$ spectral separation is larger at $L=2,3$ than at $L=1$, and larger still in the canonical large-$L$ limit. This is a frame-defined diagnostic, not an intrinsic observable or a higher-$N$ solution. The Pfaffian and higher-$L$ responses are closely correlated along the sampled branch, which we treat only as descriptive. These results suggest that microscopic noncommutative structure and quantum fluctuations may contain information relevant to dimensional dynamics, without establishing dimensional selection.

</details>


### [189] [BMN Spread Complexity Across Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29816)
*Dibakar Roychowdhury*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带质量形变的BMN矩阵模型在有限温度β⁻¹和化学势ν下的量子态动力学行为与展布复杂度（spread complexity），研究发现低温间隙相呈非混沌振荡行为，高温连续相呈现饱和Krylov Lyapunov指数λ_K = π/β的最大混沌加扰，中间温度区间则表现为亚指数交叉过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带质量形变且具有涌现U(1)全局电荷的BMN矩阵模型在强耦合区间的量子动力学与混沌特征，并刻画展布复杂度在不同热力学相中的演化规律。
