Method: 利用带荷热场双态（charged Thermofield Double, cTFD）描述强耦合动力学，通过展布复杂度跟踪量子态演化，并在三个热力学区域（低温间隙相、高温连续相、中间温度区间）中分别分析Lanczos系数与Krylov指数。附录提供了KMS边界条件、巨正则谱矩、留数分析及Krylov空间中时间反演对称性破缺的推导。

Result: 低温间隙相（βμ≫1）中离散质量间隙束缚态主导，波包弥散被束缚，早期增长呈非混沌振荡；高温连续相（βμ≪1）中热激发克服质量间隙，系统经历到连续平流场的转变，呈现最大混沌加扰，Krylov Lyapunov指数λ_K = π/β饱和普适界；中间温度区间（βμ∼1, βν∼1）中质量间隙束缚态与连续热背景的相互作用导致Lanczos系数b_n ∼ (π/β)n + γ√n的次领头修正，支配全混沌热化前的连续亚指数交叉。

Conclusion: 质量形变的BMN矩阵模型在强耦合下展现出依赖于温度与化学势的丰富量子动力学相图：从非混沌振荡到饱和混沌边界再到亚指数交叉过渡，展布复杂度能够有效刻画这些不同热力学相的量子演化特征，并为全息对偶（holographic duality）中的混沌研究提供了新的微观探针。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical behavior and spread complexity of quantum states within the mass-deformed BMN matrix model equipped with an emergent $U(1)$ global charge at finite temperature $β^{-1}$ and chemical potential $ν$. Focusing on the strong-coupling regime ($μ\gg 1$), we model the dynamics via a charged Thermofield Double (cTFD) state and trace the evolution of spread complexity across three distinct thermodynamic regimes. In the low-temperature gapped phase ($βμ\gg 1$), discrete mass-gap bound states dominate, trapping wavepacket dispersion and producing non-chaotic, oscillatory early-time growth. Conversely, in the high-temperature continuum phase ($βμ\ll 1$), thermal excitations overwhelm the mass gap, driving a transition to a continuous advection field that exhibits maximal chaotic scrambling with a Krylov Lyapunov exponent $λ_K = π/ β$ that saturates the universal bound. In the intermediate temperature regime ($βμ\sim 1, βν\sim 1$), the interplay between mass-gap bound states and the continuous thermal background induces a sub-leading correction to the Lanczos coefficients $b_n \sim \fracπβ n + γ\sqrt{n}$, governing a continuous sub-exponential crossover before full chaotic thermalization. Technical derivations regarding KMS boundary conditions, grand canonical spectral moments, residue analysis, and time-reversal symmetry breaking in Krylov space are detailed in four dedicated appendices.

</details>


### [190] [The warp factor of supersymmetric $D=11$ near-horizon geometries: single-point rigidity, the $Spin(7)$ perfect square, and global constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29879)
*Usman Kayani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究超对称M-膜视界（supersymmetric M-horizon）上紧致连通截面中Killing旋量双线性量（Killing-spinor bilinears）所满足的点态恒等式，并从该未约化的恒等式推导出三个推论：单点刚性定理、Spin(7)分解下通量对h的代数确定，以及静态偏差与通量Spin(7)不匹配之间的点态预算关系。文中还给出若干关于常数性假设、权重积分、Komar角动量以及两个不可行性（no-go）陈述的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称M-膜视界（supersymmetric M-horizon）的几何与通量结构依赖于Killing旋量的双线性量之间精确的关系。在以往的工作中，通常需要对Killing旋量范数作额外假设，而本文旨在移除这一假设，直接从未约化的点态恒等式出发，提取其几何与物理后果。

Method: 作者在紧致连通截面S上建立由Killing旋量双线性量给出的点态恒等式（无关于Killing旋量范数的假设），然后从三个角度对该恒等式进行分析：（1）通过单点消失条件推导刚性定理；（2）利用Spin(7)代数分解通量，将回转一形式（rotation one-form）h代数地表示出来并展示翘曲因子（warp factor）的精确形式；（3）将恒等式整合为点态预算（pointwise budget），将超对称常数分配给静态性偏离与通量Spin(7)失配两个物理量。

Result: 论文得出三项主要结果：（1）单点刚性定理——若Δ与h在截面某点同时消失，则通量恒为零、S为Ricci平直（Ricci-flat），且视界取R^{1,1}×S形式；（2）Spin(7)分解下Δ=(1/108)||w_{\bf 7}±σ(Y_{\bf 7})||²，仅两个7维求和分量影响翘曲因子，且该平方在w_{\bf 7}=∓σ(Y_{\bf 7})的线性子空间上退化而非在原点处退化，故Δ的正性不产生情形分类列表；（3）点态预算关系表明超对称常数被分配给静态性偏差与通量Spin(7)不匹配，各自受该常数上界约束。

Conclusion: 本文建立的未约化恒等式统一地刻画了超对称M-视界的多种几何性质，包括单点刚性、通量分解、翘曲因子的退化平方结构以及点态平衡关系。这些恒等式同样适用于常数性假设（等价于V=-fh），但该假设在已知翘曲AdS_2解的静态分支上失效；文中还固定了翘曲因子的加权积分，将视界的Komar角动量化为正的积分形式，并给出两条关于隐藏对称性的不可行性陈述及其成立条件。

Abstract: On a compact connected section ${\cal S}$ of a supersymmetric $M$-horizon the Killing-spinor bilinears give a pointwise identity relating the warp factor $Δ$, the rotation one-form $h$ and the two spinor norms, with nothing assumed about the norm of the Killing spinor. We derive three consequences of that unreduced identity.
  First, a single-point rigidity theorem: if $Δ$ and $h$ vanish at one point of the section, then the flux vanishes identically, ${\cal S}$ is Ricci-flat and the horizon is $\mathbb{R}^{1,1}\times{\cal S}$. A single point replaces the usual global spinorial hypothesis.
  Second, the ${\rm Spin}(7)$ decomposition of the flux fixes $h$ algebraically and exhibits the invariant flux content of the scalar square $Δ=4Φ^2$ known in an adapted gauge,
  $Δ=\frac{1}{108}\|w_{\bf 7}\pmσ({\cal Y}_{\bf 7})\|^2$.
  Only the two ${\bf 7}$-summands $w_{\bf 7}$, ${\cal Y}_{\bf 7}$ reach the warp factor, and the square is degenerate rather than definite: it vanishes on the linear subspace $w_{\bf 7}=\mpσ({\cal Y}_{\bf 7})$ rather than at the origin, so positivity of $Δ$ yields no case list.
  Third, the two combine into a pointwise budget: the single constant of supersymmetry is shared between the deviation from staticity and the ${\rm Spin}(7)$ mismatch of the flux, each bounded by that constant.
  The same identities characterise the constancy hypothesis, equivalent to $V=-fh$ for the bilinear one-form $V$, which fails on the static branch of known warped $AdS_2$ solutions; they fix a weighted integral of the warp factor; and they reduce the Komar angular momentum of the horizon to a positive bilinear integral. Two no-go statements, for hidden symmetries built from the Killing spinor and for a second isometry from its bilinears, are stated with their hypotheses.

</details>


### [191] [Carrollian Wave Equations for Arbitrary Spin: Anyons and the Exotic Particle on the Noncommutative Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29931)
*Mauricio Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了Cortés–Plyushchay平面任意子（planar anyons）波方程组的Carroll极限，通过按c的幂次对自旋塔分量进行分级并在固定静止能量下取极限，得到描述任意实自旋Carroll粒子（Carrollian anyons）的线性系统，该系统是标准Carroll波动方程的一阶平方根。还探索了Jackiw–Nair极限的Carroll类比，得到具有第二中心荷、非对易boost和非对易坐标平面的奇异Carroll代数，并揭示了Galilei与Carroll理论共享一个相似变换根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非相对论极限（Galilei极限）之外，Carroll极限作为另一种光速趋于零的极限，其物理粒子谱与线性波方程结构尚未被系统构造。作者旨在通过Cortés–Plyushchay平面任意子方程构建Carroll极限下的任意子体系，并探讨其代数结构与两种非相对论极限间的分支关系。

Method: 将Cortés–Plyushchay波方程组中的自旋塔分量按光速c的幂次分级，在固定静止能量条件下取c→0极限，构建出线性Carroll任意子系统。该系统作为标准Carroll波动方程(∂t²+E²)Φ=0的一阶平方根，其谱包含±静止能量两条平坦分支。同时，仿照Jackiw–Nair极限将自旋与c一起缩放，推导奇异Carroll代数及其几何性质，并通过相似变换对比Galilei与Carroll极限的差异。

Result: 得到描述任意实自旋的线性Carroll系统，在(半)整数自旋时分别对应Carroll玻色子、费米子及高自旋场。该系统的谱包含两条平坦能量分支。在自旋随c缩放的Carroll类比Jackiw–Nair极限下，导出奇异Carroll代数，具有第二中心荷、非对易boost以及可观测坐标张成的非对易平面。同时证明Galilei与Carroll理论源自同一相似变换：Galilei极限剔除负能量模式，而Carroll极限保留两个相对论分支。

Conclusion: 该工作系统构建了Carroll任意子及其波方程，确立了Carroll极限下粒子物理的基本框架，并揭示了Galilei与Carroll两类非相对论极限从同一相似变换出发的分叉结构，为后续研究提供了代数与动力学基础。

Abstract: We construct the Carrollian limit of the Cortés--Plyushchay wave equations for planar anyons, grading the spin-tower components by powers of $c$ and taking the limit at fixed rest energy. The result is a linear system describing Carrollian particles of any real spin --- \emph{Carrollian anyons} --- and, at (half-)integer spin, Carrollian bosons, fermions and higher-spin fields.
  The system is a first-order square root of the standard Carroll wave equation $(\partial_t^2+E^2)Φ=0$, whose spectrum consists of two flat branches at plus and minus the rest energy. Scaling the spin along with $c$, in the Carrollian analogue of the Jackiw--Nair limit, yields instead the exotic Carroll algebra, with a second central charge, non-commuting boosts, and observable coordinates spanning a noncommutative plane. We show how the Galilean and Carrollian theories branch from one common similarity transformation: the Galilean limit expels the negative-energy modes, whereas the Carroll limit retains both relativistic branches.

</details>


### [192] [Admissible higher-spin algebras in flat space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30039)
*Dmitry Ponomarev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维闵可夫斯基空间中的可容许高自旋代数（admissible higher-spin algebras），即能够将无质量高自旋场塔（tower of massless higher-spin fields）作为其表示的代数体系。在若干简化假设下，作者证明这些代数可分为共线型（collinear）和半共线型（half-collinear）两类，并通过求解雅可比恒等式（Jacobi identity）找到了两个共线李代数族，同时发现了一个可结合的共线代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自旋场理论中的代数结构是理解无质量高自旋场之间相互作用的数学基础，系统地分类这类可容许代数是必要的。

Method: 在四维闵可夫斯基空间中，在若干简化假设下对可容许高自旋代数进行分类，对共线型ansatz直接求解雅可比恒等式以确定李代数的结构。

Result: 可容许高自旋代数被划分为共线型与半共线型两类；其中手性高自旋代数为半共线型的一个实例。对于共线型ansatz，雅可比恒等式的直接求解给出两个李代数族，此外还发现一个可结合共线代数，该代数在未添加内部自由度时是交换的。

Conclusion: 四维闵可夫斯基空间中的可容许高自旋代数在简化假设下仅有共线与半共线两种可能类型，且共线型结构已通过雅可比恒等式得到显式刻画。

Abstract: We study admissible higher-spin algebras in four-dimensional Minkowski space, namely algebras that admit the tower of massless higher-spin fields as a representation. Under several simplifying assumptions, we show that these can be of two types, which we refer to as collinear and half-collinear. The chiral higher-spin algebra provides an example of the latter. For the collinear ansatz considered here, direct solution of the Jacobi identity yields two families of Lie algebras. We also find an associative collinear algebra, which is commutative unless internal degrees of freedom are added.

</details>


### [193] [From Self-Dual to Physical $\mathbb{C}P^{N-1}$: Anomalies, Boundary Stokes Phenomenon, and Global Structure of $θ$-vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30160)
*Yui Hayashi,Mithat Ünsal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了 $C P^{N-1}$ 模型的二耦合广义形式，使其能在自对偶（$ε=0$）与物理（$ε=g$）理论之间连续插值，并证明了一阶与二阶表述之间严格的量子等价性需要由玻色子手征反常（bosonic chiral anomaly）提供的拓扑抵消项。在大 $N$ 极限下求解该形变理论，解析确定了自对偶理论的非微扰真空结构（含动力学生成的场强凝聚），并通过推广 Lefschetz 胞腔（thimble）分析纳入边界胞腔，解决了具有 $O(N)$ 行动量的鞍点错误地产生低于物理基态能量密度的悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为 $C P^{N-1}$ 模型提供一种新的非微扰工具：通过引入两个耦合参数来统一自对偶理论与物理理论之间的插值关系。同时，现有的大 $N$ 展开面临一个基本悖论——某些具有 $(θ+2πn) \sim O(N)$ 的鞍点会虚假地给出低于物理基态的能量密度，需要通过更严格的分析加以解决。

Method: 论文采用二耦合广义化的 $C P^{N-1}$ 模型，其中 $ε\neq g$ 时模型具有手征不平衡（可视为虚 $θ$ 的实拓扑形变）。作者通过玻色子手征反常引入拓扑抵消项（topological counterterm），以确保一阶与二阶表述的精确量子等价性；随后在大 $N$ 极限下解析求解该形变理论，并将 Lefschetz 胞腔分析推广到包含边界胞腔（boundary thimbles）。

Result: 解析得到了自对偶理论的非微扰真空结构，该真空具有动力学生成的场强凝聚（field-strength condensate）。此外，通过证明有效作用量在 $F=0$ 处存在本性奇点（essential singularity），边界 Stokes 现象（boundary Stokes phenomenon）使得问题鞍点在拓扑上不活跃，从而彻底恢复了强耦合理论中大 $N$ 展开的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，二阶公式化的 $C P^{N-1}$ 模型的量子等价性必须依赖玻色子手征反常提供的拓扑抵消项；而通过引入边界胞腔推广的 Lefschetz 分析，能够消除虚假低能鞍点的悖论，严格巩固了大 $N$ 展开在强耦合体系中的适用性。这一框架为理解非微扰真空结构和拓扑形变理论提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce a two-coupling generalization of $\mathbb{C}P^{N-1}$ model that continuously interpolates between the self-dual ($ε=0$) and the physical ($ε=g$) theories as a useful nonperturbative tool. At $ε\neq g$, this model possesses a chiral imbalance, which may be viewed as a real topological deformation (imaginary-$θ$). We demonstrate that exact quantum equivalence between first- and second-order formulations strictly requires a topological counterterm sourced by a bosonic chiral anomaly. Solving this deformed theory at large $N$ yields two primary results. First, we analytically determine the nonperturbative vacuum structure of the self-dual theory, a self-dual vacuum with a dynamically generated field-strength condensate. Second, we resolve a fundamental paradox where saddles with $(θ+ 2πn) \sim O(N)$ ($n$ is branch number) spuriously yield lower energy densities than the physical ground state. Because the effective action possesses an essential singularity at $F=0$, we show that the Lefschetz thimble analysis must be generalized to include boundary thimbles. A boundary Stokes phenomenon renders the problematic saddles topologically inactive, fully restoring the validity of the large-$N$ expansion for strongly coupled theories.

</details>


### [194] [Generalised Symmetries, Anomalies, and Maximal Branches of 3d Chern-Simons Matter Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30349)
*Fabio Marino,Francesca Pretto,Marcus Sperling*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了Type IIB弦论构型中实现的线性幺正三维N≥3 Chern-Simons物质quiver理论的广义对称性（generalised symmetries）、't Hooft反常及其与最大分支（maximal branches）的相互作用。通过Wilson线屏蔽和弦格点（string lattice）两种独立方法确定1-形式对称性（1-form symmetry）及其自反常和最大无反常子群，并揭示了1-形式规范（1-form gauging）对最大分支的影响与冻结D3-膜扇区的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有超对称的Chern-Simons物质理论中广义对称性及其反常结构，是揭示其红外行为的核心问题；同时，弄清1-形式对称性如何影响理论的真空模空间分支，对刻画这类理论的整体结构具有重要意义。

Method: 采用Type IIB弦论构型框架，通过两种独立途径（Wilson线屏蔽与弦格点）系统地提取1-形式对称性及其异常数据；利用分数量子磁单极（fractional monopole）的Gukov-Witten缺陷端点抽取反常，通过整数磁单极的中心荷（centre charges）确定忠实0-形式对称性群及其连通覆盖和可能的2-群（2-group）结构，并通过引入非简单lace边的磁quiver延拓来复现对称性与混合反常。

Result: 在线性幺正理论中，与非阿贝尔0-形式因子的混合反常相互抵消；1-形式规范效应通过冻结的D3-膜扇区影响最大分支，这些扇区流向具有任意子线（anyonic lines）的Chern-Simons TQFT，从而阻碍所需的缺陷端点；据此得到受影响的 分支判据，最多两个分支可同时受影响；在此情形下提出了磁quiver延拓方案，可以复现1-形式对称性及其与分支等距的混合反常。

Conclusion: 研究表明，三维N≥3线性幺正Chern-Simons物质quiver理论中的广义对称性结构可通过弦构型完全确定，且1-形式规范对最大分支的影响存在明确的物理机制和判据，同时磁quiver延拓为超过临界数量的分支受影响情形提供了统一描述。

Abstract: The generalised symmetries, 't Hooft anomalies, and their interplay with maximal branches are analysed for linear unitary three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}\geq 3$ Chern-Simons Matter quiver theories realised in Type IIB brane configurations. The 1-form symmetry, its self-anomaly and its maximal anomaly-free subgroup are determined independently from Wilson-line screening and from the string lattice. Fractional monopole endpoints of the generating Gukov-Witten defect are used to extract the anomaly data, while centre charges of integer monopoles determine the faithful 0-form symmetry group, its connected cover and possible 2-group structures. Mixed anomalies with non-abelian 0-form factors are found to cancel in the linear unitary theories considered. The effect of 1-form gauging on maximal branches is related to frozen D3-brane sectors, which flow to Chern-Simons TQFTs whose anyonic lines obstruct the required defect endpoint. This yields a criterion for affected branches and implies that at most two can be affected simultaneously. In such a case, a magnetic-quiver extension by non-simply laced edges is proposed, wherein the 1-form symmetry and its mixed anomaly with the branch isometry are reproduced.

</details>


### [195] [Examples of Gribov copies in the presence of planar boundaries, and an emergent boundary gauge invariance in Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30447)
*David Dudal,Luigi Rosa,Sebbe Stouten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在平行板边界条件下显式构造了Yang-Mills Faddeev-Popov算子的零模，证明了非平凡界面下Gribov副本（Gribov copies）的存在性（具有有限作用量甚至有限L₂范数），并讨论了平行板配置下涌现边界规范不变性（emergent boundary gauge invariance）现象及其对Gribov-Zwanziger程序应用的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gribov副本的存在对规范固定（gauge fixing）程序有根本性影响，但此前在含非平凡界面（如平行板）的Yang-Mills理论中尚无显式构造。此外，非Abel Casimir效应（non-Abelian Casimir effect）的非微扰研究需要平行的Gribov-Zwanziger框架，理解边界条件下的规范结构是必要前提。

Method: 作者采用Henyey构造方法（Henyey's construction）并将其推广到平行板边界条件的情形；针对理想电导（PEC）和理想磁导（PMC）两种边界条件，分别对规范场的ansatz施加额外约束，显式构建Faddeev-Popov算子的零模（zero-modes）。

Result: 证实了在带非平凡界面的Yang-Mills理论中存在具有有限作用量乃至有限L₂范数的Gribov副本。此外，发现PEC和PMC平行板配置下会出现涌现边界规范不变性现象。

Conclusion: 该结果将为未来将Gribov-Zwanziger程序应用于平行板体系扫清障碍，为从非微扰角度研究非Abel Casimir效应奠定理论基础。

Abstract: First, we explicitly construct zero-modes of the Yang-Mills Faddeev-Popov operator in the presence of two parallel plates, with either perfect electric (PEC) or perfect magnetic (PMC) boundary conditions imposed. This establishes the existence of Gribov copies with finite action, and even finite L_2-norm, in Yang-Mills theory with non-trivial interfaces. We adapt Henyey's construction, although the boundary configuration imposes extra restrictions on the ansatz for the gauge field. Second, we discuss the phenomenon of an emergent boundary gauge invariance for PEC and PMC plates in Yang-Mills theory. This shall be important when applying the Gribov-Zwanziger procedure to parallel plate setups, of relevance for non-perturbatively studying the non-Abelian Casimir effect in the future.

</details>


### [196] [Brane effective actions and their island rule from $T\overline T$ flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30533)
*Nele Callebaut,Matteo Selle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文通过导数展开求解径向哈密顿流的方法，为带任意张力EOW膜的半经典AdS₃引力导出了二维有效作用量，并将其识别为T̄T型变形共形场论（CFT），提出全息对偶可通过将T̄T型变形CFT设为自由获得。论文在多种鞍点构型下验证了这一提议，并将有效作用量用于岛法则（island rule）计算，与Ryu-Takayanagi结果精确一致，同时揭示了T̄T纠缠的Wald熵解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典AdS₃引力与边界EOW膜的二维有效理论之间的全息对应关系尚不清晰，特别是当膜具有任意张力时的具体有效作用量形式及其与可积变形场论的联系。作者希望通过系统的径向哈密顿流求解来建立这一联系，并利用所得有效作用量为纠缠熵的岛法则提供非渐近区域的全息验证。

Method: 作者在导数展开（derivative expansion）的框架下求解径向哈密顿流，为半经典AdS₃引力（含任意张力EOW膜）推导二维有效作用量。将径向流解释为重整化群流（RG flow），从而将有效理论识别为T̄T型变形CFT。随后通过具体鞍点验证、三种有效膜理论分解方式（物质扇区分别为T̄T变形、T̄T型变形和未变形CFT，并分别耦合对应的膜世界引力扇区），以及岛法则计算和壳上作用量比较来完成论证。

Result: 推导出半经典AdS₃含EOW膜系统的二维有效作用量，并将其识别为T̄T型变形CFT；在空AdS₃中(A)dS₂膜的鞍点构型下验证了"将变形CFT设为自由"的全息对偶提议。三种分解方案中，T̄T变形CFT耦合T̄T型变形Liouville引力的方案将整体Weyl反常的Liouville描述自然延伸到体区域。岛法则计算在非渐近EOW膜情形下与Ryu-Takayanagi结果精确一致，且T̄T纠缠熵获得Wald熵解释。有效理论及其高阶导数修正通过壳上作用量计算与含脉动径向截断的AdS₃引力建立了联系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了半经典AdS₃含任意张力EOW膜的二维有效理论刻画，表明其等价于T̄T型变形CFT的自由理论，并推广了岛法则的全息验证到非渐近区域，同时为T̄T纠缠提供了Wald熵的物理诠释。

Abstract: We derive a $2d$ effective action for semi-classical AdS$_3$ gravity with an EOW brane of arbitrary tension by solving the radial Hamiltonian flow in a derivative expansion. Interpreting the radial flow as an RG flow, the effective theory is identified with a $T\bar T$-like deformed CFT. This leads us to propose that the holographic dual for semi-classical AdS$_3$ gravity with Neumann or conformal boundary conditions is obtained by setting the $T\bar T$-like deformed CFT free. We verify our proposal explicitly for saddles corresponding to $(A)dS_2$ branes in empty AdS$_3$. We discuss three useful ways to decompose the effective brane theory into a matter sector ($T\bar T$-deformed, $T\bar T$-like deformed, and undeformed CFT, respectively) and a corresponding braneworld gravity sector. In particular, the split into $T\bar T$-deformed CFT coupled to $T\bar T$-like deformed Liouville gravity directly extends the Liouville description of the integrated Weyl anomaly into the bulk. The effective action is then used to perform an island-rule calculation for a non-asymptotic brane in an AdS/bCFT set-up, finding exact agreement with the Ryu-Takayanagi result. This extends previous holographic checks on the island rule to non-asymptotic regimes of application, as well as provides a Wald entropy interpretation for $T\bar T$ entanglement. Finally, we relate our effective theory and its higher derivative corrections to AdS$_3$ gravity with a fluctuating radial cutoff by explicit calculation of the on-shell action.

</details>


### [197] [A Truncated Majorana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30723)
*Ali Vahedi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究通过时间依赖通道快门截断马约拉纳波包的量子场动力学，证明该协议存在两个互补物理极限：全局上产生费米型Bogoliubov变换并导致红外软模记忆及正交性灾难型对数发散；局域上在数守恒限制下快门仅起因果过滤器作用，保留扇区等价于单粒子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为拓扑平台中的时间依赖控制提供严格的场论基础，澄清有效操作基准与微观边界动力学之间的界限。

Method: 将截断建模为手性传播的受控修改而非字面空间切割，分析1+1维无质量马约拉纳场在手性分量间时间依赖旋转下的动力学，并分别从全局渐近变换与局域可观测代数两个层面进行解析推导。

Result: 全局上，早晚期通道识别失配诱导费米型Bogoliubov变换，其渐近变化普适生成红外软模记忆β(ω,ν)∝(ω+ν)^{-1}，导致Hilbert-Schmidt范数对数发散，且在无质量极限下即使无限光滑切换仍存在。局域上，在数守恒区域（对产生消失）内，限制在偶局域可观测代数上，保留扇区精确等价于单粒子态，丢弃扇区归约为真空。

Conclusion: 全局红外记忆与局域因果截断是同一动力学操作的两个互补诊断，二者共同构成时间依赖控制的理论基础，为区分有效操作基准与微观边界动力学提供了严格框架。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum field dynamics of truncating a Majorana wave packet via a time-dependent channel shutter. Modeling truncation as a controlled modification of chiral propagation rather than a literal spatial cut, we analyze a $1+1$D massless Majorana field subjected to a time-dependent rotation between its chiral components. We demonstrate that this protocol manifests two complementary physical limits. Globally, a mismatch between early- and late-time channel identifications induces a fermionic Bogoliubov transformation. We prove this asymptotic change universally generates an infrared soft-mode memory, $β(ω, ν) \propto (ω+ν)^{-1}$, leading to a logarithmic divergence in the Hilbert--Schmidt norm. This orthogonality-catastrophe-like obstruction persists even under infinitely smooth switching in the massless limit. Conversely, in the number-conserving regime where pair production vanishes, the shutter acts as a purely causal filter. We establish exact local field identities showing that, restricted to the even local observable algebra, the retained sector is exactly equivalent to a single-particle state while the discarded sector reduces to the vacuum. Ultimately, we show that global infrared memory and local causal truncation are complementary diagnostics of the same dynamical operation. These results provide a rigorous field-theoretic foundation for time-dependent control in topological platforms, cleanly separating effective operational benchmarks from microscopic boundary dynamics.

</details>


### [198] [Anomalies and discrete torsion in Type II and Type I worldsheet theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30774)
*Masashi Kawahira,Shotaro Kawanago,Shota Saito,Hiroki Wada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了十维时空中Type II和Type I弦理论世界面理论（worldsheet theory）的异常（anomaly）与离散挠率（discrete torsion），证明异常消除的充要条件是目标时空可定向且具有自旋结构（spin structure），并阐明了离散挠率的物理意义，包括Type IIA与Type IIB、普通Type I与Sugimoto弦理论之间的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代理解中，世界面理论的异常由三维可逆场论（invertible field theory）刻画，而可逆场论由适当的配边理论（bordism theory）的Anderson对偶分类。作者希望利用Yamashita和Yonekura构造的Anderson对偶的显式模型，系统研究弦理论世界面理论的异常消除条件。

Method: 采用现代异常分析框架，将世界面理论的异常等价于三维可逆场论，并利用Yamashita和Yonekura构造的Anderson对偶的显式模型来研究异常消除条件；同时分析离散挠率及其物理含义。

Result: 对于Type II和Type I弦理论，世界面理论的异常消除当且仅当目标时空可定向且容许自旋结构。此外，确定了离散挠率并阐明其物理意义，它区分了Type IIA与Type IIB弦理论，以及普通Type I与Sugimoto弦理论。

Conclusion: 本文给出了十维弦理论世界面异常消除的完整分类判据（可定向性加自旋结构），并通过离散挠率解释了不同弦理论之间的区分机制，为理解弦理论的一致性条件提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We provide a comprehensive study of anomalies and discrete torsion in the worldsheet theories of Type II and Type I string theories on a general ten-dimensional target space. In the modern understanding, the anomaly of a worldsheet theory is characterized by an invertible field theory in three dimensions, and invertible field theories are classified by the Anderson dual of a suitable bordism theory. To study the anomaly cancellation condition, we employ the explicit model of the Anderson dual constructed by Yamashita and Yonekura. We find that, for both Type II and Type I string theories, the anomaly of the worldsheet theory is cancelled if and only if the target space is orientable and admits a spin structure. We also determine the discrete torsion and clarify its physical meaning. It includes the distinctions between Type IIA and Type IIB string theories and between ordinary Type I and Sugimoto string theories.

</details>


### [199] [Holographic subregion complexity in insulator/superconductor transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30852)
*Yu Shi,Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Weike Deng,Sheng Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS-soliton背景中研究了全后反应绝缘体/超导体相变中的全息子区域复杂度（HSC），并将其与全息纠缠熵（HEE）和基于复杂度=体积（CV）提议的全息复杂度进行比较。研究发现HSC和HEE都能指示二级相变，但HSC在超导相中临界宽度处出现有限跳变，且其响应敏感依赖于子区域尺度和纠缠楔拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探究全息复杂度（holographic complexity）特别是全息子区域复杂度在凝聚态全息模型（如绝缘体/超导体相变）中的行为，检验其作为相变探测器的能力，并理解其与纠缠熵和CV复杂度之间的关系。

Method: 研究采用AdS-soliton背景下的全后反应绝缘体/超导体相变模型，对条形（strip）子区域计算全息子区域复杂度（HSC）、全息纠缠熵（HEE）和CV复杂度，比较它们在相变过程中的行为，并考虑连接的与断开的Ryu-Takayanagi曲面对应的禁闭/解禁闭转变。

Result: HSC和HEE均能检测到二级绝缘体/超导体相变。在超导相中固定化学势时，HSC在临界宽度处出现有限跳变而HEE连续；超过该宽度后HSC随宽度线性增长而HEE保持恒定。固定宽度时，对于ℓ<ℓ_c，HSC随化学势先降后升，与HEE相反；对于ℓ>ℓ_c，则单调递增。经过一致归一化和扣除绝缘参考态后，半空间HSC与CV复杂度密度解析等价。

Conclusion: 全息子区域复杂度（HSC）可以作为绝缘体/超导体相变的诊断工具，但其定性行为对子区域尺度和纠缠楔（entanglement wedge）拓扑高度敏感，理解这些依赖关系对于正确解释HSC结果至关重要。

Abstract: We study holographic subregion complexity (HSC) across a fully backreacted insulator/superconductor transition in an AdS-soliton background and compare it with holographic entanglement entropy (HEE) and holographic complexity based on the complexity=volume (CV) proposal. Both HSC and HEE signal the second-order transition. For a strip subsystem, competing connected and disconnected Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces give rise to a confinement/deconfinement transition. At fixed chemical potential in the superconducting phase, HSC exhibits a finite jump at the critical width, whereas HEE remains continuous. Beyond this width, HSC grows linearly with the strip width, while HEE is constant. At fixed strip width, HSC first decreases and then increases with chemical potential for $\ell<\ell_c$, opposite to HEE, but increases monotonically for $\ell>\ell_c$. After consistent normalization and subtraction of the respective insulating references, the half-space HSC and CV complexity densities are analytically identical. These results show that HSC can diagnose the insulator/superconductor transition, but its qualitative response remains sensitive to the subsystem scale and entanglement-wedge topology.

</details>


### [200] [The large $N$ vector model with angular velocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31151)
*Justin R. David,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了大N极限下临界O(N)矢量模型在S¹×S²几何上、无单态约束、带角速度μ̂的自由能，解析确定了高温主导行为在μ̂r=0和μ̂²r²=1两个展开区域的形式，并用数值分析加以补充验证；高温自由能在μ̂²r²=1处出现极点，其留数与无质量自由理论一致，但次主导项展现出非解析的角速度依赖，区别于自由理论；通过pp波几何也能获得相同的极点留数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临界O(N)矢量模型在非平凡几何（如带角速度的球面）下的高温自由能行为，特别是检验热有效场论（thermal effective field theory）的预测，并对比临界固定点结果与自由理论结果的异同。

Method: 在S¹×S²几何上、无单态约束（singlet constraint）条件下，于大N极限下解析求解临界O(N)矢量模型的高温自由能，分别在μ̂r=0和μ̂²r²=1附近做展开分析，并辅以数值计算以验证解析结果并在两个区域间光滑插值；同时利用pp波（pp-wave）几何进行交叉验证。

Result: 高温自由能的领头阶贡献在μ̂²r²=1处出现极点，其留数与无质量自由理论一致，符合热有效场论的预期；次主导项则表现出对角速度的非解析依赖，将临界固定点结果与自由理论结果区分开来。pp波几何上的计算同样给出了与直接计算一致的极点留数。

Conclusion: 临界O(N)矢量模型在无单态约束下的高温自由能主导行为在极点处与自由理论一致，但次主导项的非解析角速度依赖揭示了临界固定点与自由理论在更精细层次上的差异，为理解旋转系统中的热场论行为提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We study the free energy of the critical $O(N)$ vector model at large $N$ on $S^{1}\times S^{2}$ with an angular velocity $\hatμ$ without the singlet constraint. The leading high-temperature behaviour is determined analytically both as an expansion about $\hatμr=0$ and $\hatμ^{2}r^{2}=1$ where $r$ is the radius of the sphere. We supplement the analytic results with a numerical analysis that agrees with both the analytical expansions in their respective regimes and smoothly interpolates between them. The leading high-temperature contribution to the free energy develops a pole at $\hatμ^{2}r^{2}=1$, in agreement with expectations from the thermal effective field theory. Its residue coincides with that of the massless free theory. Sub-leading terms, however, exhibit non-analytic dependence on the angular velocity and distinguish the critical fixed-point result from the free theory answer. The residue at the pole can also be obtained by placing the $O(N)$ model on the pp-wave geometry. We show that the residue agrees with that obtained from the direct computation.

</details>


### [201] [Coarse-grained models for loop quantum gravity and their renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31152)
*Mehdi Assanioussi,Martin Zeiß*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入一族有效理论（effective theories），据此定义了正则圈量子引力（canonical loop quantum gravity）中一类特定的粗粒化模型。每个有效理论由两个参数刻画，包含一个粗粒态希尔伯特空间和一个有效哈密顿量算子，并基于旋量网络态（spin network states）的系统性粗粒化程序构建。文中给出了实现哈密顿量非微扰重整化（non-perturbative renormalization）框架的方案，并显式写出重整化流方程，旨在为正则圈量子引力中连续极限的涌现和唯象模型的推导建立研究平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正则圈量子引力框架下，尚缺乏系统的粗粒化与重整化途径来研究连续极限的涌现机制以及推导唯象模型。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过构建一族有效理论框架，为正则圈量子引力的连续极限研究和唯象推导提供一个规范化的研究平台。

Method: 方法包括三个环节：首先，基于旋量网络态的系统性粗粒化程序构建粗粒化希尔伯特空间；其次，通过分析该粗粒化程序与圈量子引力中各类哈密顿量算子作用之间的相互作用，导出有效哈密顿量；最后，提出在粗粒化模型中实施哈密顿量非微扰重整化框架的处方，并显式写下重整化流方程。

Result: 成功构造了一族由两个参数表征的有效理论，每个理论包含明确的粗粒态希尔伯特空间与有效哈密顿量算子，并给出了相应的重整化流方程形式。

Conclusion: 作者将这一框架定位为其研究目标的基础平台，即用于在正则圈量子引力背景下探索连续极限的涌现，并从中推导出可用于唯象预测的模型。

Abstract: We introduce a family of effective theories and use them to define a specific type of coarse-grained models for canonical loop quantum gravity. Each effective theory is characterized by two parameters and it consists mainly of a Hilbert space of coarse states and an effective Hamiltonian operator. The construction of the coarse Hilbert spaces relies on a systematic coarse-graining procedure for spin network states. The effective Hamiltonians are then obtained from an analysis of the interplay between this coarse-graining procedure and the action of various Hamiltonian operators defined in loop quantum gravity. Finally, we provide a prescription for implementing the Hamiltonian non-perturbative renormalization framework for the coarse-grained models, and we explicitly write down the renormalization flow equations. The aim of this work is to build an arena for studying the emergence of the continuum limit and the derivation of phenomenological models in the context of canonical loop quantum gravity.

</details>


### [202] [Superfluid memory effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31172)
*Kristan Jensen,Alfredo Pérez,Stefan Prohazka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本论文在超流体（superfluid）中识别出一种由声子散射（phonon scattering）产生的记忆效应（memory effect）：局域散射事件会生成一个时间积分（time integral）为零但一阶时间矩（first temporal moment）非零的远场压力脉冲，对应远场速度的prepotential发生永久性位移。该效应受声子发射的软因子（soft factor）控制，并包含非线性色散（nonlinear dispersion）的修正。在超流体⁴He中，散射宏观声子波包产生的信号幅度可能处于实验可探测范围，从而为电磁和引力记忆效应提供实验室类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力波物理中，记忆效应（memory effect）是重要且可观测的现象；本文旨在寻找凝聚态系统中的类似效应，进而提供一个可在实验室实现和验证的类比，以深化对记忆效应普适性的理解。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究了超流体中声子散射产生的远场压力脉冲结构，计算其时间积分和一阶时间矩，并利用声子发射的软因子（含非线性色散修正）来刻画该记忆效应的强度。

Result: 发现远场压力脉冲的时间积分为零，但一阶时间矩非零，表明存在永久性的prepotential位移。在超流体⁴He中，宏观声子波包散射所产生信号的估计幅度可能进入实验可探测范围。

Conclusion: 超流体中的声子散射记忆效应可为电磁记忆和引力记忆提供实验室层面的类比平台，其可测性使得相关物理现象得以在地面实验中检验。

Abstract: We identify a memory effect produced by phonon scattering in a superfluid: a localized scattering event generates a far-field pressure pulse with vanishing time integral but a nonzero first temporal moment, corresponding to a permanent shift in a prepotential for the far-field velocity. The effect is controlled by the soft factor for phonon emission, including corrections from nonlinear dispersion. In superfluid $^4$He, scattering macroscopic phonon wave packets produces a signal whose estimated magnitude may lie within experimental reach, thereby providing a laboratory analogue of electromagnetic and gravitational memory.

</details>

### [203] [First-principles determination of anomaly-induced pion decay beyond the chiral limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31024)
*Tian Lin,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Chuan Liu,Qi-Yuan Luo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）中的反常PCAC关系，首次从头计算（ab initio）了中性π介子双光子衰变中由非零夸克质量引起的修正，得到衰变宽度Γ(π⁰→γγ)=8.09(22) eV，并首次确证了π⁰-η-η′混合增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性π介子的双光子衰变在手征极限（chiral limit）下由Adler-Bell-Jackiw反常精确固定，但非零夸克质量会引入百分之几的修正，这些修正必须精确确定才能对手征极限之外的QCD进行精密检验。

Method: 利用反常PCAC关系，在格点QCD中计算质量修正作为q²=0处精确反常条件的偏离，从而避免了四点和关联函数以及传统方法中手征对数（chiral logarithm）消去所带来的限制。由于修正正比于轻夸克质量，统计和系统不确定度相应被压低。计算使用两个近似物理质量的域壁（domain-wall）系综。

Result: 每个系综的衰变宽度统计精度达到约1%，经连续极限外推后得到Γ(π⁰→γγ)=8.09(22) eV。衰变振幅的质量修正为2.3(1.4)%，符号为正且主要由同位旋破缺（isospin breaking）主导，首次提供了π⁰-η-η′混合增强效应的从头确证。

Conclusion: 该方法使质量修正的精密计算成为可行，所得结果对π⁰→γγ衰变宽度提供了高精度的第一性原理预言，并为π⁰-η-η′混合对衰变振幅的增强提供了直接的理论验证。

Abstract: The two-photon decay of the neutral pion is fixed in the chiral limit by the Adler-Bell-Jackiw anomaly, while nonzero quark masses induce few-percent corrections that must be determined for precision tests of QCD beyond the chiral limit. Using the anomalous PCAC relation, we compute them in lattice QCD as deviations from the exact anomaly condition at $q^2=0$, thereby avoiding both four-point functions and the cancellation of chiral logarithms that limits conventional approaches. Because the correction is proportional to the light-quark masses, both statistical and systematic uncertainties are correspondingly suppressed, making a precision calculation feasible. On two nearly-physical domain-wall ensembles we achieve $\sim1\%$ statistical precision each for the decay width, obtaining $Γ(π^0\toγγ)=8.09(22) eV$ after continuum extrapolation. The $2.3(1.4)\%$ mass correction to the decay amplitude, positive and isospin-breaking dominated, provides the first ab initio confirmation of the $π^0$-$η$-$η'$ mixing enhancement.

</details>


### [204] [Symmetric Mass Generation for Domain-Wall Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29963)
*Sho Araki,Hidenori Fukaya,Tetsuya Onogi,Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次通过二维欧几里得格点上的数值模拟研究了畴壁费米子（domain-wall fermion）的对称质量生成（symmetric mass generation, SMG）现象，证明局域四费米相互作用可选择性地对镜像壁上的边缘费米子打开能隙，同时保持物理壁不受影响，并提供了一种实用的非微扰框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁费米子构造中存在镜像费米子（mirror fermion）解耦的挑战，而对称质量生成可能提供一种非微扰的途径来实现这一目标。此前尚无对畴壁费米子SMG的数值检验，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在二维欧几里得格点上模拟Fidkowski-Kitaev马约拉纳链（Majorana chain）的路径积分，该链具有对应于物理壁和镜像壁的两个边界。通过计算格点Dirac算符的本征值谱来分析SMG效应，并采用带Pfaffian复相位重加权（reweighting）的常规混合蒙特卡罗（hybrid Monte Carlo）算法执行数值模拟。

Result: 数值结果表明：局域四费米相互作用选择性地对镜像壁上的边缘费米子打开能隙，而物理壁保持完整；SMG的特征性信号表现为Dirac算符能隙沿复谱的虚轴（imaginary axis）方向打开，而非标准质量项诱导的实轴（real direction）方向位移；模拟中Pfaffian的复相位极小，使得常规混合蒙特卡罗算法仍然切实可行。

Conclusion: 这些结果为格点畴壁费米子的相互作用诱导镜像费米子解耦提供了一种实用的非微扰框架，并为后续相关数值研究奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We report the first numerical examination of symmetric mass generation (SMG) for domain-wall fermions. Our simulation on a two-dimensional Euclidean lattice computes the path-integral of the Fidkowski-Kitaev Majorana chain with two boundaries, corresponding to the two (physical and mirror) walls. We demonstrate by evaluating the lattice Dirac operator eigenvalue spectrum that the local four-Fermi interaction selectively gaps the edge fermions on the mirror wall while leaving the physical wall intact. We further identify a characteristic signature of SMG in the path-integral: the gap of the Dirac operator opens along the imaginary axis of the complex spectrum, in contrast to the standard mass shift in the real direction. The conventional hybrid Monte Carlo algorithm remains practical with reweighting the complex phase of the Pfaffian, which is found to be remarkably small throughout the simulation. These results provide a practical nonperturbative framework for interaction-induced mirror-fermion decoupling of lattice domain-wall fermions.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Algorithmic threshold for high-dimensional projection pursuit I: general theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29416)
*Brice Huang,Mark Sellke,Nike Sun*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究高维投影追踪（projection pursuit）的零模型：给定高维标准高斯分布中采样的M个点，精确刻画沿数据相关方向投影的经验分布集合在算法约束下的可达性，并将其归结为一维随机控制问题。由此获得球面或伊辛感知机（perceptron）模型哈密顿量的精确算法阈值，主要创新在于将随机控制理论引入分支重叠间隙性质（branching overlap gap property, OGP）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维投影追踪问题的核心挑战在于精确刻画数据沿任意方向投影的经验分布可达集合，而这一非算法问题本身仍悬而未决。作者旨在从算法约束（algorithmic setting）角度出发，对具有维度无关Lipschitz依赖性的算法分类下的可达经验分布进行精确刻画。

Method: 方法包含两部分：在困难性证明中，作者将随机控制理论（stochastic control theory）嵌入到此前由前两位作者提出的分支重叠间隙性质框架中，用于确立精确算法阈值；在算法匹配方面，构造了一种全新的增量式近似消息传递（incremental AMP）算法，通过对高斯无序的布朗桥揭示（Brownian-bridge revelation）过程进行操作，模拟同一族受控随机微分方程（controlled SDEs）。

Result: 主要结果精确刻画了该算法分类下经验分布的可行集合，等价于一维随机控制问题；据此获得球面或伊辛感知机模型在一般有界连续激活函数下的精确算法阈值。该结果显著扩展了分支OGP框架能够定位精确算法阈值的适用场景。

Conclusion: 即使非算法的可行投影刻画问题仍未被解决，本文方法仍能在算法约束下获得精确阈值，表明算法约束与无约束问题可在复杂性上存在本质差异。相关工作（Montanari和Zhou, 2024）针对球面问题刻画了两阶段AMP算法的可达分布，本文结果与之互补并列。

Abstract: We study a null model of high-dimensional projection pursuit: we are given $M$ points sampled i.i.d. from a standard gaussian in $N$ dimensions, where $M,N\to\infty$ with $M/N\toα\in(0,\infty)$. Our goal is to characterize the possible empirical distributions of these points' projections along a data-dependent direction $x$, which ranges over either the sphere $S_N=\sqrt{N}\mathbb{S}^{N-1}$ or cube $Σ_N=\{-1,+1\}^N$. We consider this problem in an algorithmic setting, where $x$ must be the output of an algorithm with dimension-free Lipschitz dependence on the input; this class of algorithms includes general gradient-based methods such as Langevin dynamics and approximate message passing (AMP). Our main result exactly characterizes the set of empirical distributions attainable by this class in terms of a one-dimensional stochastic control problem. As a consequence of our main result, we obtain exact algorithmic thresholds for optimizing the Hamiltonian of a spherical or Ising perceptron model with general bounded continuous activation. For the spherical problem, independent work of Montanari and Zhou (2024) characterized the empirical distributions attainable by a related two-stage AMP algorithm, also in terms of stochastic control.
  Our proof of hardness builds on the branching overlap gap property introduced in earlier work by the first two authors. Our main innovation is to develop stochastic control theory within the branching OGP framework, significantly expanding the settings in which it locates an exact algorithmic threshold. Notably, our methods apply even though the non-algorithmic problem of characterizing all feasible projections remains a major outstanding challenge. For the matching algorithmic result, we construct a new incremental AMP algorithm that acts on a Brownian-bridge revelation of the gaussian disorder and simulates the same family of controlled SDEs.

</details>


### [206] [Colorful Exponential Random Graph Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31130)
*Bhaswar B. Bhattacharya,Pierfrancesco Dionigi,Ankan Ganguly,Giulio Zucal*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地研究彩色指数随机图模型（colored ERGMs），利用概率图论（probability graphons）框架推导其极限自由能的变分表示，识别具有复制对称性（replica symmetry）的模型族，提出高温唯一性与零温两级选择机制，并通过诱导楔图和彩虹三角模型结合数值实验加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络数据中常存在多种类型的边关系（如多关系网络），但传统指数随机图模型（ERGM）主要针对单一类型边关系建模。本文旨在建立彩色ERGMs的理论框架，揭示其典型样本的渐近结构，并连接极值组合学（extremal combinatorics）中的经典结果。

Method: 借助概率图函数（probability graphon）框架导出极限自由能的变分表示；通过识别具备复制对称性的模型族，研究变分问题具有常数最大化子的条件；利用欧拉-拉格朗日（Euler-Lagrange）不动点方程导出高温唯一性判据；在零温极限下建立两级选择原理，将能量项和熵的作用分层解析。

Result: 识别出若干具有复制对称性的彩色ERGM族，其模型渐近集中于具有独立边的乘积着色（product colorings）；建立了通用高温唯一性判据；在零温极限下提出两级选择机制，并应用于诱导楔图和彩虹三角ERGMs，发现其零温结构连接极值组合学的已知结果；此外证明这两个模型在有限温度下会发生对称性破缺（symmetry breaking），数值实验与理论结果一致。

Conclusion: 本文为彩色ERGMs建立了从变分表示到高温/零温渐近行为的完整理论框架，揭示了多类型网络模型中能量与熵在不同温度区间的竞争机制，并为后续研究提供了理论工具与数值验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we initiate the study of colored exponential random graph models (ERGMs), a class of exponential-family models for networks with multiple types of edge relations. Using the framework of probability graphons, we first derive a variational representation for the limiting free energy, whose maximizers determine the asymptotic structure of typical samples from the model. Then we identify several general families of colored ERGMs exhibiting replica symmetry, where the variational problem has constant maximizers and the model asymptotically concentrates on product colorings with independent edges. For general colored ERGMs, we derive Euler-Lagrange fixed-point equations for the variational maximizers, which in turn yield a general high-temperature uniqueness criterion. In the complementary zero-temperature regime, we establish a two-level selection principle: the leading energy term determines the ground states, while the lower-order energy terms, combined with entropy, act as a tie-breaker to determine the asymptotic zero-temperature structure of the model. We illustrate this principle through the induced wedge and rainbow triangle ERGMs. Both models have natural interpretations in multitype networks, and their zero-temperature limits exhibit interesting structures that connect to well-known results in extremal combinatorics. We further establish finite-temperature symmetry breaking for both these models and complement the rigorous results with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [207] [Equilibrium fluctuations of the weakly asymmetric inclusion process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29957)
*Simon Gabriel*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维弱非对称简单包含过程（weakly asymmetric simple inclusion process）的平稳密度涨落场收敛于随机Burgers方程（stochastic Burgers equation）的能量解，关键在于针对微观电流定制的二阶玻尔兹曼-吉布斯原理（Boltzmann-Gibbs principle）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维非对称包含过程作为一类重要的相互作用粒子系统，其宏观极限行为与随机Burgers方程密切相关，而此前缺乏严格的收敛性证明。作者旨在通过建立新的技术工具来填补这一空白。

Method: 主要技术手段是推导一个针对微观电流（microscopic current）定制的二阶玻尔兹曼-吉布斯原理，以此作为证明平稳密度涨落场收敛到随机Burgers方程能量解的关键步骤。

Result: 证明了一维弱非对称简单包含过程的平稳密度涨落场收敛到随机Burgers方程的能量解；作为推论，还获得了弱凝聚（weakly condensing）区制下对称包含过程（symmetric inclusion process）的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克）型涨落结果。

Conclusion: 该结果从严格数学上确证了一维弱非对称包含过程与随机Burgers方程之间的尺度极限关系，同时为包含过程族中的涨落行为提供了新的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the stationary density fluctuation field of the one-dimensional weakly asymmetric simple inclusion process converges to the energy solution of the stochastic Burgers equation. The key technical ingredient is a second-order Boltzmann-Gibbs principle tailored to the microscopic current. As a consequence, we also obtain Ornstein-Uhlenbeck fluctuations for the symmetric inclusion process in a weakly condensing regime, a result of independent interest.

</details>


<div id='math.CT'></div>

# math.CT [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Models for dagger $(\infty,1)$-categories I: Dagger simplicial sets and unitary cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29730)
*Keima Akasaka*

Main category: math.CT

TL;DR: 本文为 daggers 的 $(\infty,1)$-范畴开发了单纯集模型，建立了 daggers 单纯范畴与 daggers 单纯集上的模型结构，并证明了 dagger 刚化—神经伴随是一个 Quillen 等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 $(\infty,1)$-范畴的模型结构并未考虑涉及伴随结构（dagger structures）的情形，而量子计算与数学物理中的许多范畴天然带有 dagger 结构，因此需要建立对应的 $\infty$-范畴模型。

Method: 作者首先在 dagger 单纯范畴上建立模型结构，其弱等价为局部弱等价且本质上满射（essential surjectivity）需满足一致幺正等价（coherently unitary equivalence）；随后构造 dagger 单纯集上的 dagger Joyal 模型结构，并证明 dagger 刚化—神经（rigidification–nerve）伴随构成 Quillen 等价；进一步引入幺正核（unitary core）来内在地刻画 dagger Joyal 等价；最后与 DCH 反对合 Joyal 模型结构进行比较。

Result: 成功建立了 dagger Joyal 模型结构，证明了 dagger 刚化—神经伴随的 Quillen 等价性，并通过幺正核给出了 dagger Joyal 等价的刻画，同时建立了与 DCH 反对合 Joyal 模型结构的比较关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为带 dagger 结构的 $\infty$-范畴提供了系统的单纯集模型，为后续研究 dagger $\infty$-范畴的形变理论与高阶结构奠定了模型范畴论基础。

Abstract: We develop simplicial-set models for dagger $(\infty,1)$-categories. We first establish a model structure on dagger simplicial categories whose weak equivalences are local weak equivalences that are essentially surjective up to coherently unitary equivalence. We then construct the dagger Joyal model structure on dagger simplicial sets and prove that the dagger rigidification--nerve adjunction is a Quillen equivalence. We next introduce the unitary core and use it to characterize dagger Joyal equivalences intrinsically. Finally, we compare the dagger Joyal model structure with the DCH anti-involutive Joyal model structure.

</details>

### [209] [Higgs boson pair production in SMEFT: shape and truncation studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29132)
*Manosch Bär,Bakar Chargeishvili,Ramona Gröber,Gudrun Heinrich,Konstantin Schmid*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了SMEFT框架下（最高到维度6，包含环压抑制算子）Wilson系数对希格斯玻色子对产生截面的影响，并纳入NLO QCD修正。研究发现某些基准情景下$m_{hh}$和$p_{T,h}$分布出现显著形状畸变，且在线性截断下部分情景会产生非物理结果，暴露了线性近似的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）中，Wilson系数对希格斯玻色子对产生过程的影响需要系统性比较，特别是高阶项和环阶算子可能改变截面行为，而现有参数化与纯线性截断可能无法覆盖所有物理情形。

Method: 采用SMEFT框架构建最高至维度6的有效拉氏量，同时纳入环压抑制算子；计算时包含NLO QCD修正，并将截面参数化为Wilson系数的多项式形式；通过比较微分分布（希格斯对不变质量$m_{hh}$和前导希格斯玻色子横动量$p_{T,h}$）来识别形状畸变的基准情景。

Result: 识别出多个在$m_{hh}$和$p_{T,h}$分布上呈现显著形状畸变的基准情景；部分情景在线性截断下仍保持物理合理，而另一些在线性截断下产生非物理的微分截面，特别是当二次维度6贡献被忽略时，表明线性近似在大反常耦合下失效。

Conclusion: 线性SMEFT截断对于大Wilson系数情形可能不可靠，需保留二次贡献才能获得物理合理的微分截面；研究为使用SMEFT分析希格斯对产生过程时的理论截断选择提供了重要警示。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of the influence of Wilson coefficients on Higgs boson pair production cross sections in SMEFT up to dimension-6, also including loop-suppressed operators. Our study accounts for NLO QCD corrections, and is based on updated parametrizations of the cross section as a polynomial in the Wilson coefficients. We focus on differential distributions for the Higgs boson pair invariant mass, as well as the transverse momenta of the leading Higgs bosons, identifying benchmark scenarios exhibiting pronounced shape distortions in the $m_{hh}$ and $p_{T,h}$ distributions. While some of these scenarios remain physically meaningful under a purely linear truncation of the SMEFT expansion, others yield unphysical differential cross sections when quadratic dimension-6 contributions are omitted, exposing the breakdown of the linear approximation for large anomalous couplings.

</details>


### [210] [On-shell renormalization in NREFT: a simplified description of resonances, bound states, and coupled channel effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29683)
*Guo-Ying Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Weinberg复合性判据的现代推广，证明了非相对论有效场论（NREFT）中的在壳重整化（on-shell renormalization）能够复现Weinberg的原始结果，并为共振态、束缚态及耦合道效应提供了简洁描述；同时给出了包含带电道的近阈值p波态完整传播子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weinberg的复合性判据（compositeness criterion）在现代推广中存在多个有待澄清的问题，作者旨在通过讨论和论证解决这些疑问。

Method: 采用非相对论有效场论（non-relativistic effective field theory）框架，利用在壳重整化方法论证其与Weinberg原始结果的等价性，并推导含带电道（charged channel）的完整传播子（propagator）。

Result: 证明在壳重整化在NREFT中可复现Weinberg的判据结果，能简洁描述共振态（resonances）、束缚态（bound states）及耦合道（coupled channel）效应；给出了近阈值p波态（near-threshold p-wave states）含带电道的完整传播子。

Conclusion: NREFT中的在壳重整化是推广Weinberg复合性判据的有效工具，并可为近阈值p波态提供包括带电道在内的完整描述。

Abstract: We discuss several issues regarding the modern generalization of Weinberg's compositeness criterion. In particular, we demonstrate that the on-shell renormalization in NREFT reproduces Weinberg's result and provides a simple description of resonances, bound states, and coupled channel effects. Additionally, we present the complete propagator, including the charged channel for the near-threshold p-wave states.

</details>


### [211] [The inclusive $\bar B \to X_s γ$ decay rate with higher precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29863)
*M. Misiak,H. M. Asatrian,K. Brune,M. Czaja,M. Czakon,M. Fael,C. Greub,T. Huber,F. Lange,L. T. Moos,M. Niggetiedt,A. Rehman,K. Schönwald,M. Steinhauser*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对B介子（B meson）在标准模型内外的无包容弱辐射衰变进行了全新升级的分析，完整纳入O(α_s)微扰修正，并在α_s²阶精确计算了粲夸克质量的依赖，最终给出标准模型下CP及同位旋平均的分支比为(3.54±0.14)×10⁻⁴，与实验平均值(3.49±0.19)×10⁻⁴一致，并对双希格斯二重态模型II中带电希格斯玻色子质量给出M_{H±}>670 GeV（95%置信度）的下限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对B介子固有弱辐射衰变的分析在α_s²阶对粲夸克质量的依赖处理不够精确，导致可观测量存在显著不确定性；同时需要利用最新实验数据和更高阶微扰修正来重新评估标准模型预言，并为超出标准模型的新物理提供更严格的约束。

Method: 在形式上完整地纳入O(α_s)阶微扰修正；在α_s²阶对主导修正项中粲夸克质量的依赖进行精确计算，消除此前分析中的大不确定性；全局归一化因子及多数非微扰贡献以b夸克的动力学方案（kinetic scheme）质量和重夸克展开（heavy-quark expansion）矩阵元表达，参数取自包含O(α_s³)修正和最新实验结果的半轻子拟合。

Result: 在光子能量下界E_γ>1.6 GeV条件下，得到标准模型中CP及同位旋平均分支比B_{sγ}^{SM}=(3.54±0.14)×10⁻⁴，与实验平均值B_{sγ}^{exp}=(3.49±0.19)×10⁻⁴一致；基于此对二希格斯二重态模型II给出带电希格斯玻色子质量M_{H±}>670 GeV（95%置信度）的约束。

Conclusion: 标准模型理论预言与实验测量在误差范围内吻合，验证了理论框架的自洽性；同时该结果对超出标准模型的新物理提供了更有力约束，特别是在双希格斯二重态模型II中限定了带电希格斯玻色子的质量下限。

Abstract: We present a new and upgraded analysis of the inclusive weak radiative decay of the $B$ meson in the standard model and beyond. The ${\mathcal O}(α_s)$ perturbative corrections are included in a formally complete manner. At the order $α_s^2$, exact dependence on the charm quark mass is calculated for the dominant corrections, which removes a sizeable uncertainty that was present in all the previous analyses. The global normalization factor and many of the non-perturbative contributions are expressed in terms of the kinetic-scheme mass of the $b$-quark and the heavy-quark-expansion matrix elements. These parameters are adopted from the most recent semileptonic fits that include ${\mathcal O}(α_s^3)$ corrections and up-to-date experimental results. We find ${\mathcal B}_{sγ}^{\rm SM} = (3.54 \pm 0.14)\times 10^{-4}$ for the CP- and isospin-averaged branching ratio of the considered decay in the standard model, with a lower cut on the photon energy $E_γ> 1.6\,{\rm GeV}$. It agrees with the current experimental average ${\mathcal B}_{sγ}^{\rm exp} = (3.49 \pm 0.19)\times 10^{-4}$, which provides constraints on beyond standard model physics. In particular, we find $M_{H^\pm} > 670\,{\rm GeV}$ at $95\%\,{\rm C.L.}$ for the charged Higgs boson mass in the two-Higgs-doublet model~II.

</details>


### [212] [Polarized jet anisotropy at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30492)
*Zhong-Bo Kang,Hongxi Xing,Fanyi Zhao,Yiyu Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用横向动量依赖（TMD）QCD因子化方法，首次证明喷注（jet）诱导的各向异性对极化不对称性施加了宇称选择规则：将横向极化结构函数按谐波 $\cos(nφ_{qJ})$ 展开时，每个谐波的对称性由 $n$ 的奇偶性决定。EIC运动学下高阶谐波预计可独立测量，为核子结构精密提取提供了新的观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注是探测核子三维自旋结构的有力探针，但观测到的喷注轴会打破软胶子辐射的方位角各向同性，从而改变用于提取该结构的非对称性。需要阐明这种喷注诱导的各向异性对极化不对称性的具体影响。

Method: 采用横向动量依赖（transverse-momentum-dependent, TMD）QCD因子化框架，将横向极化结构函数按谐波 $\cos(nφ_{qJ})$ 展开（$φ_{qJ}$ 为喷注与轻子-喷注动量不平衡之间的夹角），系统分析各谐波的对称性。

Result: 推导出宇称选择规则：每个谐波的对称性由 $n$ 的奇偶性唯一确定，与谐波系数大小无关。Sivers 函数在偶数 $n$ 保持左-右不对称，但在奇数 $n$ 反转为反直觉的左-右对称；worm-gear 函数在每个谐波保持上-下不对称，同时在偶数 $n$ 左-右对称、奇数 $n$ 左-右不对称。EIC运动学下高阶谐波预计可独立测量。

Conclusion: 喷注诱导的各向异性为极化不对称性引入了新的宇称选择规则，该规则提供了软辐射方位角动力学的新观测量，是核子结构精密提取的关键组成部分。

Abstract: Jets provide a powerful probe of the three-dimensional spin structure of the nucleon, a central goal of the Electron-Ion Collider. Yet the observed jet defines an axis that breaks the azimuthal isotropy of soft-gluon radiation, thereby reshaping the very asymmetries used to extract that structure. Using transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) QCD factorization, we show for the first time that this jet-induced anisotropy imposes a parity selection rule on polarized asymmetries. Expanding the transversely polarized structure functions in harmonics $\cos(nφ_{qJ})$, where $φ_{qJ}$ is the angle between the jet and the lepton-jet momentum imbalance, makes this rule explicit: the symmetry of each harmonic is fixed by the parity of $n$, independently of the magnitudes of the harmonic coefficients. For the Sivers function, the canonical left-right asymmetry about the proton spin survives for even $n$ but is replaced by a counterintuitive left-right symmetry for odd $n$. The worm-gear function retains its up-down asymmetry at every harmonic while being left-right symmetric for even $n$ and asymmetric for odd $n$. At EIC kinematics, the higher harmonics studied here are predicted to be individually measurable, providing new observables for the azimuthal dynamics of soft radiation and an essential ingredient in precision extractions of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [213] [Taming lepton portal dark matter by a non-invertible selection rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29051)
*Junichiro Kawamura*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出非可逆选择定则（non-invertible selection rule）可以抑制轻子门户暗物质（lepton portal dark matter）中固有的味破坏（flavor violation），并提出基于Z5对称性Z2规范化的简单构造，实现门户耦合的单味主导（one-flavor dominance）与PMNS混合矩阵的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻子门户暗物质作为一种弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）候选者，可以规避直接探测实验的严格限制，但其味破坏耦合难以用普通可逆对称性（invertible symmetries）抑制，同时还需兼容PMNS矩阵中的大混合角。现有方法无法同时满足这两点要求，因此需要寻找新的对称性框架。

Method: 作者首先论证普通可逆对称性无法在保持PMNS大混合角的同时抑制味破坏的门户耦合，随后提出基于Z5对称性的Z2规范化（Z2 gauging of a Z5 symmetry）构造，利用非可逆选择定则实现门户耦合的单味主导。作者还讨论了一型跷跷板（type-I seesaw）机制下可能的味泄漏（flavor leakage），并研究模型通过轻子观测量（尤其Z玻色子衰变中的轻子味非普适性）的实验探测途径。

Result: 该构造能够同时实现门户耦合的单味主导与PMNS混合矩阵的一致性，克服了普通可逆对称性的局限。此外，一型跷跷板补全中可能出现味泄漏效应，Z玻色子衰变中的轻子味非普适性（lepton flavor non-universality）可作为该模型的重要实验探针。

Conclusion: 非可逆选择定则为轻子门户暗物质的味结构提供了新的理论工具，能够在满足PMNS矩阵约束的同时有效抑制味破坏门户耦合，并可通过Z玻色子衰变的轻子观测量进行实验检验。

Abstract: We point out that a non-invertible selection rule can control the flavor violation inherent in lepton portal dark matter, a weakly interacting massive particle candidate that can evade severe constraints from direct detection experiments. We show that ordinary invertible symmetries cannot suppress flavor-violating portal couplings while accommodating the large mixing angles in the PMNS matrix. We then present a simple construction based on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauging of a $\mathbb{Z}_5$ symmetry, which realizes one-flavor dominance of the portal couplings consistently with the PMNS mixing matrix. We also discuss possible flavor leakage in a type-I seesaw completion and study experimental probes of the model through lepton observables, especially lepton flavor non-universality in $Z$ boson decays.

</details>


### [214] [Embedding dependence of fermion mass hierarchies in $S_3$-symmetric Yukawa sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29259)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在不可约表示出现多重性时，族对称性（family symmetry）S₃在复对称三族张量空间中对Yukawa扇区的谱学后果。通过将不变Yukawa子空间参数化为CP¹×CP¹连续族，作者解决了逆奇异值问题，发现固定单态嵌入会限制质量层级，而改变嵌入可容纳任意正质量谱，并展示了该框架对Clifford代数构造的应用及其排除观测电子轻子层级的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当不可约表示以多重性出现时，族对称性虽固定了Yukawa扇区的表示内容，但未必固定其在生成张量空间中的嵌入方式。作者希望澄清这种额外嵌入数据对质量谱的谱学后果，从而区分"表示内容"与"嵌入方式"各自对质量层级限制的贡献。

Method: 论文以S₃赋值V=1⊕2的复对称三族张量体系为研究对象。由于Sym²V=2·1⊕2·2，三维不变Yukawa子空间（类型1⊕2）构成在有限仿射图中参数化的连续族CP¹×CP¹，记为L_{t,κ}。作者在κ=0分支上求解逆奇异值问题，获取任意指定有序正质量谱的充要条件；对一般嵌入，通过秩一矩阵存在性与2tκ²=1的等价性导出σ₃/σ₂无界条件，并将结果应用于Clifford代数构造。

Result: 在κ=0分支，固定单态嵌入仅允许有限层级界限，而改变嵌入则能容纳所有正三族质量谱。一般嵌入情形下，L_{t,κ}包含非零秩一矩阵当且仅当2tκ²=1，恰对应σ₃/σ₂无界的有限仿射图嵌入；在传统三Higgs扇区中该条件化为y₂y₅²=√2 y₁y₃²，允许O(1)阶Yukawa耦合比支持任意大的质量层级。对Clifford代数构造的分析表明其族分辨Yukawa张量张成L_{2,0}，奇异值满足m₁+m₂≤m₃≤m₁+3m₂，从而排除了观测的带电轻子层级。

Conclusion: 观测电荷轻子质量层级在Clifford代数构造中的缺失源于固定的多重性嵌入，而非S₃表示内容本身。该框架将质量谱限制从表示论层面延伸到嵌入几何层面，为理解族对称性对Yukawa质量结构的约束提供了更精细的判据。

Abstract: When irreducible representations occur with multiplicity, a family symmetry fixes the representation content of a Yukawa sector but need not fix its embedding in the space of generation tensors. We study the spectral consequences of this additional embedding data for complex-symmetric three-family tensors with the $S_3$ assignment $V=\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$. Since $\operatorname{Sym}^2V=2\,\mathbf{1}\oplus2\,\mathbf{2}$, three-dimensional invariant Yukawa subspaces of type $\mathbf{1}\oplus\mathbf{2}$ form a continuous $\mathbb{CP}^1\times\mathbb{CP}^1$ family, denoted $\mathcal L_{t,κ}$ on a finite affine chart. On the $κ=0$ branch, we solve the inverse singular-value problem and obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for any prescribed ordered positive mass spectrum. A fixed singlet embedding imposes a finite hierarchy bound, whereas varying the embedding accommodates every positive three-family spectrum. For general embeddings, $\mathcal L_{t,κ}$ contains a nonzero rank-one matrix if and only if $2tκ^2=1$; equivalently, these are precisely the finite-chart embeddings for which $σ_3/σ_2$ is unbounded. In a conventional complex-symmetric $S_3$ three-Higgs sector, the same condition becomes $y_2y_5^2=\sqrt{2}\,y_1y_3^2$, so order-one Yukawa coupling ratios can support arbitrarily large mass hierarchies. We then apply the result to a recent Clifford-algebraic construction and show that its family-resolved Yukawa tensors span $\mathcal L_{2,0}$, whose singular values obey $m_1+m_2\leq m_3\leq m_1+3m_2$. This excludes the observed charged-lepton hierarchy for arbitrary complex vacuum alignment within the existing Higgs directions. The resulting obstruction is therefore tied to the fixed multiplicity embedding rather than to the $S_3$ representation content alone.

</details>


### [215] [Seesaw and Axion in No-Scale Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29801)
*Anamaria Hell,Lincoln da S. Pereira,Tsutomu T. Yanagida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在Jordan框架与Einstein框架之间的变换中排除奇异点（如φ=0），可以实现两种框架的经典等价性（classical equivalence），并论证Jordan框架能揭示模型对称性，从而自然导出离散对称性、高质量轴子（axion）以及跷跷板机制（seesaw mechanism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: No-Scale引力理论能够统一描述粒子物理、引力和宇宙学，但作为更广义的标量-张量理论（scalar-tensor theories），其Jordan框架与Einstein框架之间的等价性存在广泛争议，这是该框架面临的核心理论挑战。

Method: 本文提出通过在两种框架之间的变换中剔除奇异点（singular point）来建立经典等价性；在此基础上，利用Jordan框架凸显模型对称性的优势，展示粒子物理模型构建的路径。

Result: 该方案展示了离散对称性的自然涌现，进而导出了具有高质量性质的轴子，并能自然地嵌入跷跷板机制，体现了Jordan框架在粒子物理模型构建方面的价值。

Conclusion: 排除变换奇异点可使Jordan框架与Einstein框架实现经典等价，同时Jordan框架在对称性揭示和粒子物理模型构建方面具有独特优势，为No-Scale引力框架下的统一描述提供了新的理论支撑。

Abstract: The No-Scale gravity is a compelling framework to describe particle physics, gravity and cosmology, in which all mass scales are an illusion given by the value of a scalar field $φ$. This theory, however, falls under the umbrella of more general scalar-tensor theories, which propagate different modes depending on the scalar field, and faces the extensive debate regarding the equivalence between its Jordan and Einstein frame descriptions. In this work we advocate that by excluding the singular point in the transformation between the two frames (e.g., $φ=0$) we can have the classical equivalence between them. To show the merit of removing this singular point, we argue the Jordan frame makes the symmetries of the model manifest, which creates a suitable playground for the particle-physics model building which we illustrate by showing the emergence of discrete symmetries which leads to a high-quality axion and also naturally incorporate the seesaw mechanism.

</details>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [All genus open mirror symmetry for footballs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29067)
*Zhuoming Lan,Jinghao Yu,Zhengyu Zong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了足球（football）的全亏格完整后代开镜像对称（all genus full descendant open mirror symmetry），其B模型由镜像曲线上（mirror curve）的Chekhov-Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归（topological recursion）给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开镜像对称（open mirror symmetry）是弦理论与代数几何中连接A模型与B模型的核心课题，此前对全部亏格和完整后代展开的结果尚未覆盖足球这一几何对象，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 论文采用双管齐下的方法：一方面处理足球的A模型开Gromov-Witten理论（open Gromov-Witten theory），另一方面利用镜像曲线上的Chekhov-Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归构造B模型的对应量，并证明两者在所有亏格上与所有后代方向（descendant directions）上完全一致。

Result: 成功地证明了足球的全亏格完整后代开镜像对称，即A模型的开后代不变量与B模型中由拓扑递归生成的不变量在全部方向上精确匹配。

Conclusion: 这一结果将开镜像对称的适用范围扩展至足球，并进一步验证了Chekhov-Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归作为B模型通用计算工具的效力。

Abstract: We prove an all genus full descendant open mirror symmetry for footballs. The B-model is given by the Chekhov-Eynard-Orantin topological recursion on the mirror curve.

</details>


### [217] [Quasimap critical cohomology, Coulomb branches, and quantum groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29400)
*Tommaso Maria Botta,Spencer Tamagni*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了从$\mathbb{P}^1$到Nakajima箭图簇（Nakajima quiver variety）$X$的基点拟映射（based quasimap）模空间$\QM^\xi(\mathbb{P}^1, X)$的临界上同调（critical cohomology），通过两种导出等价（derived equivalent）的全局临界轨迹（global critical locus）表示来刻画该模空间，并由此得到该模空间上的典范DT层（canonical DT sheaf）的两种等价显式实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟映射模空间的临界上同调及其上作用的量子群结构，旨在建立移位Yangian（shifted Yangian）与量子化Coulomb分支（quantized Coulomb branch）之间的相容性。

Method: 作者构造了模空间$\QM^\xi(\mathbb{P}^1, X)$的两种导出等价的全局临界轨迹表示，每种表示对应典范DT层的一种显式实现。基于每种临界表示，分别描述了移位Yangian与量子化Coulomb分支在拟映射临界上同调上的典范作用，并通过几何手段构造从适当的Hall代数（Hall algebra）到Coulomb分支的典范态射。

Result: 作者证明了在附加假设下，所描述的两种作用通过上述态射相容；并猜想该相容性一般成立，将其归结为一个可通过Joyce猜想（Joyce conjecture）途径处理的层论陈述。

Conclusion: 本文建立了拟映射模空间临界上同调上两种量子对称性之间的关联，提出了一般的相容性猜想，并将该猜想的证明约化为层论层面的具体陈述。

Abstract: This article presents a systematic study of the critical cohomology of $\QM^ξ(\bbP^1, X)$, the moduli space of based quasimaps from $\bbP^1$ to a Nakajima quiver variety $X$. We develop two derived equivalent presentations of this moduli space as a global critical locus. Accordingly, we obtain two equivalent explicit realizations of the canonical DT sheaf of the moduli space. Using each critical presentation, we describe canonical actions of shifted Yangians and quantized Coulomb branches on the quasimap critical cohomologies. We produce, by geometric means, a canonical morphism from an appropriate Hall algebra to the Coulomb branch, and show that the two actions we describe are compatible via this morphism under an additional hypothesis. We conjecture the compatibility holds generally, and reduce the claim to a sheaf-theoretic statement that may be approached via Joyce conjecture.

</details>


### [218] [Singular Points on Positroid Varieties and Planar N=4 Supersymmetric Yang-Mills Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28735)
*Joseph Fluegemann*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本论文研究了正种簇（positroid varieties）$Π_f$ 的几何奇点问题，证明了只需检查 $T$-不动点（$T$-fixed points）处的奇异性即可，并给出了两种计算这些点处重数（multiplicity）的方法：基于仿射管道梦境（affine pipe dreams）的图解方法和计算程序方法，同时提供了 $n \leq 6$ 所有点的重数表；论文第二部分将正种簇与平面 N=4 超对称杨-米尔斯（N=4 SYM）理论中的振幅物理学联系起来，探讨了 BCFW 递推、on-shell 图的管道梦境构造以及奇点与振幅的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正种簇提供了格拉斯曼流形（Grassmannian）$Gr(k,n)$ 的一种分解，其枚举与有界仿射置换（bounded affine permutations）等组合对象相关，且其非负部分参数化了平面 N=4 超对称杨-米尔斯理论中计算振幅时所积分的空间。作者希望回答的核心问题是：正种簇 $Π_f$ 是否具有几何奇点，以及如何判定和计算这些奇点。

Method: 作者证明只需检查 $T$-不动点（$T$-fixed points）$\lambda$ 处的奇异性即可，并通过计算 $Π_f$ 限制于该点的等变上同调（equivariant cohomology）来得到重数（multiplicity）。论文给出两种计算方法：(1) 利用仿射管道梦境（affine pipe dreams）的图解方法；(2) 计算程序方法。此外，通过删除/收缩（deletion/contraction）定义了配对 $(Π_f,\lambda)$ 上的序，该序与光滑性有良好交互，并揭示了仿射管道梦境与有限管道梦境（finite pipe dreams）之间的关系。第二部分中，作者引入量子场论、主导奇点（leading singularities）等物理背景，解释 BCFW（Britto-Cachazo-Feng-Witten）递推与 BCFW 桥分解，并展示如何在管道梦境上用 BCFW 桥构建 on-shell 图。

Result: 作者提供了两种计算正种簇在 $T$-不动点处重数的方法，并编写了执行该计算并输出重数的代码（托管于 josephflueg.github.io），同时列出了 $n \leq 6$ 的所有正种簇上所有点的重数表。在物理部分，作者推导了与 on-shell 图的逆软因子（inverse soft factors）相关的组合学结果，并探讨了正种簇中的奇点是否与振幅具有相关性。

Conclusion: 论文表明，正种簇的奇异性判定可约化为对 $T$-不动点的检查，并建立了从组合图解（affine pipe dreams）到几何信息（重数）的计算框架。第二部分通过 BCFW 桥与 on-shell 图的构造，将正种簇的组合结构进一步与 N=4 SYM 振幅计算联系起来，并初步探索了奇点对振幅的潜在意义。

Abstract: Positroid varieties $Π_f$ provide a decomposition of the Grassmannian $Gr(k,n)$; they can be enumerated using bounded affine permutations ($f$) which have bijections with a number of interesting combinatorial objects. Furthermore, (the nonnegative part of) positroid varieties parameterize the space that is integrated over when calculating amplitudes in planar N=4 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory.
  The main question we answer in this thesis is whether a positroid variety $Π_f$ has any geometric singularities. We show that it is sufficient to check singularity at the $T$-fixed points ($λ$) and we can obtain the multiplicity at these points $λ$ by calculating the equivariant cohomology of $Π_f$ restricted to the point. We give 2 ways of doing this: (1) A diagrammatic way using affine pipe dreams and (2) A computational method. For (2), we have written code that does the computation and outputs the multiplicity (files at josephflueg.github.io). We have included tables listing the multiplicities of all the points on positroid varieties up to $n=6$. We also describe an ordering on pairs $(Π_f,λ)$ given by deletion/contraction that interacts nicely with smoothness, and describe the relationship between affine pipe dreams and finite pipe dreams.
  In Part II of this thesis connects with physics of planar N=4 SYM. We briefly introduce quantum field theory, leading singularities, and positroids in N=4 SYM. We then explain Britto-Cachazo-Feng-Witten (BCFW) recursion and the BCFW bridge decomposition of an on-shell diagram. We describe how to build an on-shell diagram on a pipe dream using BCFW bridges. Finally, we work out some combinatorics related to inverse soft factors for on-shell diagrams and explore whether singularities in positroid varieties have relevance to amplitudes. This is a revised version of my thesis originally written in August 2024.

</details>


### [219] [Wall-crossing formula and genus-one Virasoro conjecture for Fano complete intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29870)
*Shuai Guo,Qingsheng Zhang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了光滑法诺完全交簇（smooth Fano complete intersections）在射影空间中环境状态空间上的亏格一Virasoro猜想（genus-one Virasoro conjecture），并推广了带加权标记点的拟映射不变量（quasimap invariants）在等变扭曲（equivariant twisted）框架下的墙交叉公式（wall-crossing formula）。该结果覆盖了大多数大量子同调（big quantum cohomology）在任意点都不半单（semisimple）的情形，通过桥梁连接Gromov–Witten理论与半单等变扭曲理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Virasoro猜想对所有光滑射影簇的全亏格Gromov–Witten不变量给出普适关系，但该猜想在半单（semisimple）理论中已被充分理解，而在非半单（non-semisimple）情形下仍然在很大程度上悬而未决。本文的目标是推进非半单情形下Virasoro猜想的证明。

Method: 作者将完全交簇的Gromov–Witten理论与射影空间的半单等变扭曲理论连接起来。具体工具包括：将带加权标记点（weighted markings）的拟映射不变量墙交叉公式推广到等变扭曲设定，允许在轻标记点（light markings）处引入后代插入（descendant insertions）；结合带轻标记点的拟映射亏格一量子Lefschetz（genus-one quantum Lefschetz）公式，建立从完全交的Gromov–Witten理论到半单理论的桥梁。

Result: 证明了光滑法诺完全交簇在环境状态空间（ambient state space）上的亏格一Virasoro猜想。对于此类完全交簇中的大多数，大量子同调在任意点都不半单，因此该结果覆盖了重要的非半单情形。

Conclusion: 本文通过将非半单的Gromov–Witten理论约化为半单的等变扭曲理论，有效推广了Virasoro猜想可验证的范围；所建立的墙交叉公式与量子Lefschetz公式为后续在非半单情形下探索更高亏格Virasoro猜想提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: The Virasoro conjecture predicts a set of universal relations among all genera Gromov--Witten invariants of any smooth projective variety. The conjecture is well understood for semisimple theories, but remains largely open in the non-semisimple setting.
  We prove the genus-one Virasoro conjecture on the ambient state space of smooth Fano complete intersections in projective space. For most of these complete intersections, the big quantum cohomology is nowhere semisimple. We also generalize the wall-crossing formula for quasimap invariants with weighted markings to the equivariant twisted setting, allowing descendant insertions at light markings. Together with genus-one quantum Lefschetz for quasimaps with light markings, this wall-crossing formula provides the key bridge from the Gromov--Witten theory of the complete intersection to the semisimple equivariant twisted theory of the projective space.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Towards an effective medium theory for in-vivo Magnetic Resonance: Characterizing Admissible Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30401)
*Alessandro Sbrizzi,Miha Fuderer,Ray Sheombarsing*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有效介质（effective-medium）策略以表征宏观尺度下异质材料（如生物组织）中的磁共振动力学，通过以一般非线性场替换经典Bloch方程中的弛豫项，并要求其满足旋转对称性、热平衡全局稳定性和耗散性等物理性质，从而完整刻画可容许的弛豫场并构建非线性弛豫模型，将经典Bloch动力学作为特例包含在内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Bloch方程虽构成磁共振理论的基础，但可能不足以捕捉生物组织等异质材料中观察到的复杂有效动力学，因此需要一种更一般的理论框架来描述这些现象。

Method: 提出一种有效介质策略，在保留经典Larmor力矩的同时，将弛豫项替换为一般非线性场（即有效介质项）；通过施加旋转对称性、热平衡全局稳定性及耗散性等物理约束，得到可容许弛豫场的完整刻画，并据此构建可生成非线性弛豫模型的构造性框架。

Result: 该理论完整刻画了满足所需物理性质的弛豫场，经典Bloch动力学作为该框架的一个特例被恢复，从而为磁共振现象的有效介质描述提供了原理性基础。

Conclusion: 该工作为异质材料中磁共振现象的唯象建模提供了更一般的理论基础，有望弥补经典Bloch方程在描述宏观有效动力学方面的不足。

Abstract: The classical Bloch equation forms the foundation of magnetic resonance theory but may be insufficient to capture the complex effective dynamics observed in heterogeneous materials such as biological tissue. Here an effective-medium strategy is proposed to describe magnetic resonance dynamics from observations at macro-scale (order 1 mm$^3$). In this framework, the classical Larmor torque is retained while the relaxation term is replaced by a general nonlinear field (i.e. the effective medium term). This field is required to preserve key physical properties, including rotational symmetry, global stability of thermal equilibrium, and dissipativity. These requirements lead to a complete characterization of admissible relaxation fields and provide a constructive framework for generating nonlinear relaxation models. The resulting theory recovers classical Bloch dynamics as a special case and provides a principled foundation for effective-medium descriptions of magnetic resonance phenomena.

</details>

### [221] [Anomalous horizontal track-like events at the HAWC observatory: candidate neutrino-induced charged leptons from the Pico de Orizaba volcano](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28951)
*Hermes León Vargas,Andrés Sandoval*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: HAWC合作组报道了皮科德奥里萨巴火山（Pico de Orizaba volcano）最大上覆岩层区域（>18 km.w.e.）中两个电荷沉积异常巨大的类轨迹事件。本论文仅基于已发表的公开信息（包括蒙特卡洛电荷分布和散落μ子背景模型）证明这些事件在运动学上与所有已知背景不一致，能量守恒下界将罐内沉积能量置于约190–210 GeV以上，超过散落μ子背景模型100 GeV的上限。中微子假设恰好预言了观察到的电荷窗口（10³–10⁴光电子）和最大上覆岩层分析区域中的可识别事件，两个候选事件产生的发生率超出常规大气中微子通量预期2.1–2.5σ。这些将成为墨西哥首个中微子候选体及首个通过掠地火山技术（Earth-skimming volcano technique）探测到的事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验HAWC合作组利用掠地行星火山技术搜索地球掠射中微子（Earth-skimming neutrinos）时报道的两个高电荷类轨迹事件，判断其是否真实来源于中微子而非已知背景。

Method: 仅依赖已发表的公开信息进行分析，包括已发表的蒙特卡洛电荷分布和散落μ子背景模型；通过能量守恒约束计算罐内能量沉积下界；逐一检验直入大气μ子、共线μ子簇等背景假设，并与中微子假说的预言进行定量对比。

Result: 两个候选事件的罐内能量沉积下界为约190–210 GeV（最保守情形下为122–136 GeV），均超过散落μ子背景模型100 GeV的上限；标定系统误差单方向地将沉积能量推入多TeV范围。大气μ子穿透需E_μ≥0.67 PeV，预期事件数仅4.5×10⁻⁵；共线μ子簇被五个独立论证排除。事件速率超出常规大气中微子通量预期2.1–2.5σ，所需涌现轻子能量约20 TeV以上。

Conclusion: 这两个类轨迹事件在运动学上与所有已识别背景不一致，而与中微子假说的预言相符，构成墨西哥首个中微子候选体，也是首个通过掠地火山技术获得的探测结果。已发表的标定并不对能量给出上限，事件的能量可能非常高。

Abstract: Two track-like events with exceptionally large charge deposits, pointing back to the region of maximum overburden ($>18$ km.w.e.) of the Pico de Orizaba volcano, were reported by the HAWC Collaboration in a search for Earth-skimming neutrinos. Using exclusively published information, including the published Monte Carlo charge distributions and the scattered-muon background model, we show that these events are kinematically inconsistent with all identified backgrounds. A conservative energy-conservation bound places the in-tank energy deposit of the candidates above $\approx$190--210~GeV, with a worst-case minimum of 122--136~GeV, in either case exceeding the 100~GeV maximum of the scattered-muon background model. Calibration systematics derived from the published simulations act unidirectionally, pushing the implied deposits into the multi-TeV regime. Direct atmospheric muons require $E_μ\gtrsim 0.67$~PeV to penetrate the overburden and contribute $4.5\times10^{-5}$ expected events ($\lesssim 10^{-3}$ under the largest possible prompt-charm flux); collinear muon bundles are excluded by five independent arguments. Conversely, the neutrino hypothesis predicts identifiable events precisely in the observed charge window ($10^{3}$--$10^{4}$ photoelectrons) and in the highest-overburden analysis region, both observed. The two track-like events imply a rate that exceeds the conventional atmospheric neutrino flux expectation at the 2.1--2.5$σ$ level. The charges of the observed candidates require an emerging lepton above about 20~TeV; the published calibration does not bound the energy from above. These would constitute the first neutrino candidates detected in Mexico and the first obtained with the Earth-skimming volcano technique.

</details>


### [222] [Photon--Dark Matter Elastic Scattering: An Effective-Operator Scan and First Operator-Resolved Sensitivity Estimates from the Galactic Halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29546)
*Trinity Rosebud Stenhouse,Asli Acar,Mikhail Bashkanov,Frank F. Deppisch,Chamkaur Ghag,Dan P. Watts*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究弹性光子—暗物质（photon–dark matter）散射对伽马射线谱的衰减效应，基于17年Fermi–LAT对银心方向的像素级数据，给出了首个算子分辨（operator-resolved）的光子—暗物质散射灵敏度估计，结果显示该信号弱于EFT有效性和其他宇宙学探针约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质湮灭到光子是研究暗物质光子扇区算子的常规探针，但其强度依赖暗物质密度的二次方；光子—暗物质弹性散射提供线性依赖密度的替代探针，探测相同的算子系统。此前缺乏对该探针在算子层面上的系统灵敏度量研究。

Method: 考虑标准模型规范不变的维度5至7有效算子（mass-dimension 5 to 7 effective operators），由截断尺度Λ压制，耦合标量、Majorana或Dirac暗物质到光子；采用电弱双重态偶极子门户（electroweak-doublet dipole portal）框架规避直接探测限制；利用Fermi–LAT Pass 8数据对银心方向进行像素级再分析，并基于伪实验（pseudo-experiments）校准灵敏度框架。

Result: 维度5 Dirac偶极子算子的截断尺度约束为Λ≈0.32 GeV，维度6标量Rayleigh算子为0.21 GeV，维度7 Rayleigh族为0.79–1.06 GeV。该灵敏度远弱于EFT有效性阈值（EFT-validity threshold），且在偶极子平面上被CMB和直接探测约束所超越；电弱双重态偶极子门户中非弹性劈裂同时闭合并关闭了CMB湮灭束缚，使衰减成为唯一存活的探针。

Conclusion: 弹性光子—暗物质散射框架已建立并可推广至任意提供逐能箱（per-bin）谱与误差以及视线柱密度（line-of-sight column density）数据的仪器；但目前其在银心方向上的探测灵敏度较弱的现状表明该探针尚不足以超越现有约束，需依赖更强仪器或更大数据量提升。

Abstract: Elastic photon--dark matter scattering attenuates gamma-ray spectra along a line of sight, probing the same operators as dark matter annihilation to photons but at a rate linear, rather than quadratic, in dark matter density. We consider Standard Model gauge-invariant effective operators of mass-dimension 5 to 7, suppressed by a cutoff scale $Λ$, coupling scalar, Majorana or Dirac dark matter to the photon. The leading operators with non-vanishing real-photon amplitudes enter at dimension-5 for Dirac dark matter and dimension-7 for Majorana dark matter. In the electroweak-doublet dipole portal, the inelastic splitting invoked to evade direct detection also closes the CMB annihilation bound, leaving attenuation the only one of the three photon-sector probes that survives. Applying this to a pixel-level reanalysis of 17 years of \textit{Fermi}--LAT Pass~8 data toward the Galactic centre, we derive the first operator-resolved sensitivity estimates for photon--dark matter scattering from the Galactic halo: $Λ\simeq 0.32~\mathrm{GeV}$ for the dimension-5 Dirac dipoles, $0.21~\mathrm{GeV}$ for the dimension-6 scalar Rayleigh operator and $0.79$--$1.06~\mathrm{GeV}$ for the dimension-7 Rayleigh family. The reach is weak: it lies below the EFT-validity threshold across the cold dark matter mass range, and is superseded on the dipole plane by CMB and direct-detection constraints. The framework is calibrated against pseudo-experiments, and recomputes the sensitivity for any instrument that provides a per-bin spectrum with uncertainties and a line-of-sight column density.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Positive mass and rigidity for asymptotically flat tangent bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29427)
*Sajjad Lakzian*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在渐近平坦流形 (asymptotically flat manifold) $(M,g)$ 的切丛 (tangent bundle) $TM$ 上，针对一类具缓慢渐近衰减的渐近平坦度量 $\widetilde g$，证明了正质量定理 (positive mass theorem) 和正质量刚性定理 (positive mass rigidity theorem)。尽管该度量在 $2n$ 维下的衰减低于 ADM 质量的标准阈值，作者仍以广义方式定义了其渐近质量，并证明该质量由底流形 $M$ 上的几何数据完全确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切丛上自然构造的渐近平坦度量通常伴随着缓慢的渐近衰减，导致其衰减速率低于 $2n$ 维中 ADM 质量的标准定义要求。因此，传统正质量定理无法直接适用，需要发展广义质量概念来刻画这类度量的几何性质。

Method: 作者在 $TM$ 的水平分布 (horizontal distribution) 上，在无穷远处将欧几里得度量与底流形度量 $g$ 之间进行插值 (interpolation)，从而构造出一类具有缓慢渐近衰减的渐近平坦度量 $\widetilde g$。随后通过广义方式定义并计算其渐近质量，建立正质量定理与刚性定理。

Result: 证明了此类缓慢衰减度量上的正质量定理和正质量刚性定理；所定义的广义渐近质量是有良定义的 (well-defined)，并可由底流形 $M$ 上的几何数据（即底流形度量所携带的信息）明确确定。

Conclusion: 正质量定理在切束上这类自然构造但衰减缓慢的渐近平坦度量中仍然成立，且其质量与底流形的几何结构密切相关，表明广义质量框架可有效推广经典 ADM 型质量的概念。

Abstract: Let $(M,g)$ be an asymptotically flat manifold such that its tangent bundle $TM$ is diffeomorphically asymptotically Euclidean. We prove a positive mass theorem and a positive mass rigidity theorem for a natural class of asymptotically flat metrics $\widetilde g$ on $TM$ exhibiting slow asymptotic decay. These metrics are constructed by interpolating, along the horizontal distribution of $TM$, between the Euclidean metric and the base metric $g$ near infinity. The main difficulty is that the induced metrics on $TM$ decay below the standard threshold for the ADM mass in dimension $2n$; nevertheless, we show that their asymptotic mass is well-defined in a generalized sense and is determined by geometric data on the underlying manifold $M$.

</details>


### [224] [Structure of the Riemann tensor in higher-dimensional Kerr-NUT-(A)dS spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30448)
*Igor Khavkine,David Matejov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维Kerr-NUT-(A)dS时空的黎曼曲率张量的代数与微分结构，以求解该度量族的IDEAL（Intrinsic, Deductive, Explicit and ALgorithmic）特征化问题为最终目标。研究发现存在一个障碍（no-go定理），即无法仅从未微分黎曼张量协变构造出任何非平凡2-形式（包括主张量本身），但通过对主张量定义方程的积分性和第二Bianchi恒等式的代数与微分条件，仍可特征化Kerr-NUT-(A)dS度量曲率所对应的黎曼对称张量族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于为Kerr-NUT-(A)dS度规族寻找一种IDEAL（内蕴、演绎、显式且算法化）特征化方法，以便在不依赖坐标选择的情况下唯一识别这一时空家族。

Method: 研究方法聚焦于黎曼曲率$R_{abcd}$与主张量（principal tensor）$h_{ab}$之间的代数关系；利用表征理论（representation theory）中半直积群$U(1)^n\rtimes S_n$的技术，使得计算在任意高维数上均可进行。

Result: 本研究得到一个no-go定理：不存在能从未微分黎曼张量协变构造出的非平凡2-形式（包括$h_{ab}$本身）。尽管如此，仍从$h_{ab}$定义方程的积分性及第二Bianchi恒等式出发，给出了一组代数与微分条件，用以特征化能够成为Kerr-NUT-(A)dS度规曲率的黎曼对称张量族。

Conclusion: 尽管直接IDEAL特征化受限于no-go定理，论文通过主张量方程的积分性和第二Bianchi恒等式所得的代数与微分条件，为高阶维度下Kerr-NUT-(A)dS空间曲率的可识别性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study the algebraic and differential structure of the Riemann curvature tensor of the higher-dimensional Kerr-NUT-(A)dS spaces, motivated by the eventual goal of finding an IDEAL (Intrinsic, Deductive, Explicit and ALgorithmic) characterization of this family of metrics. The special geometry of these spaces is governed by the principal tensor $h_{ab}$, a non-degenerate closed conformal Killing-Yano $2$-form, whose existence singles out the Kerr-NUT-(A)dS family; we therefore focus on the algebraic relationship between the Riemann curvature $R_{abcd}$ and $h_{ab}$. In our investigations we encountered an obstruction, which results in a no-go theorem: no non-trivial $2$-form, including $h_{ab}$ itself, can be constructed covariantly from the undifferentiated Riemann tensor alone. Despite that, we do characterize the family of Riemann-symmetric tensors that could be the curvature of a Kerr-NUT-(A)dS metric by an algebraic and a differential condition, stemming from the integrability of the defining equation of $h_{ab}$ and the second Bianchi identity. Our calculations apply in all higher dimensions, which becomes feasible by a judicious application of representation theoretic techniques related to a semi-direct product group $U(1)^n\rtimes S_n$ that stabilizes $h_{ab}$.

</details>


### [225] [The symmetric maximal surface equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30167)
*Rongli Huang,Peihe Wang,Hengyu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有退化边界条件的对称极大曲面方程（symmetric maximal surface equation）光滑解的存在性，并证明这些解最大化相应的面积泛函（area functional），该结果构成了双曲空间中极小图及其面积极小化问题的洛伦兹类比（Lorentzian analogue）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦流形中的极大曲面（maximal surface）在洛伦兹几何中具有重要地位，对应的面积最大化问题与双曲空间中的极小图面积极小化问题形成对偶关系。本文旨在建立退化边界条件下此类方程光滑解的存在性，并验证其与面积泛函极值性质的联系。

Method: 通过对称极大曲面方程的研究框架，结合退化边界条件的分析处理，建立光滑解的存在性定理，并进一步通过变分方法证明所得解最大化关联的面积泛函。

Result: 证明了退化边界条件下对称极大曲面方程光滑解的存在性，并严格证明这些解使得相应的面积泛函达到极大值。

Conclusion: 该结果在洛伦兹几何（Lorentzian geometry）中对应于双曲空间中极小图及其面积极小化问题的对偶版本，为两类几何问题之间提供了结构上的类比与补充。

Abstract: We establish the existence of smooth solutions to the symmetric maximal surface equation with degenerate boundary conditions. Moreover, we prove that these solutions maximize the associated area functionals. This result serves as the Lorentzian analogue of minimal graphs in hyperbolic spaces together with their associated area minimizing problem.

</details>


### [226] [Stability of gravitational instantons with a bounded Killing vector field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31008)
*Tristan Ozuch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个完整的 ALF Ricci 平坦四维流形（manifold）承载有界 Killing 向量场时是线性稳定的，当且仅当其局部为超凯勒（hyperkähler）。该结论统一适用于所有已知例子，并特别证明了 Li-Sun 最近发现的所有度量的不稳定性。此外，文中还证明了非平直静态 Ricci 平坦四维度量的不稳定性，并在更高维度上证明了稳定性要求万有覆盖（universal cover）分裂出一条直线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Ricci 平坦四维流形的线性稳定性（linear stability）与特殊全纯性（special holonomy）及（反）自对偶（（anti-）selfduality）之间的等价关系，并统一处理已知的各种例子，包括近期构造的度量族。

Method: 主要论证基于与 Killing 场相关的 Ricci 平坦度量在无穷小 Einstein-Maxwell 形变（deformation）框架下进行分析；另有一个更简单的恒等式用于处理静态（static）情形。

Result: 建立了 ALF Ricci 平坦四维流形线性稳定性与局部超凯勒结构之间的充要关系；证明了 Li-Sun 度量族和静态轴对称（axisymmetric）Riemannian Myers/Korotkin-Nicolai 度量的不稳定性；在高维中，对 AF 流形的推广证明了稳定性迫使万有覆盖分裂出直线，并给出了 Riemannian Myers-Perry 瞬子（instantons）与 Riemannian Schwarzschild-Tangherlini 度量在所有维度下的显式失稳张量。

Conclusion: 对所有已知的 Ricci 平坦四维流形族，线性稳定性、特殊全纯性与（反）自对偶是等价的；高维情形则揭示了稳定性与万有覆盖线分裂之间的严格约束关系。

Abstract: We prove that a complete ALF Ricci-flat 4-manifold carrying a bounded Killing vector field is linearly stable if and only if it is locally hyperkähler. This applies uniformly to all known examples and in particular proves the instability of all of the metrics recently found by Li-Sun. The argument is based on infinitesimal Einstein-Maxwell deformations of the Ricci-flat metric associated with the Killing field.
  A separate simpler identity proves the instability of nonflat static Ricci-flat 4-metrics which include the static axisymmetric smooth Riemannian Myers/Korotkin-Nicolai metrics.
  To our knowledge, this proves the equivalence between linear stability and special holonomy and (anti-)selfduality for all known families of Ricci-flat 4-manifolds.
  In higher dimensions, for generalizations of AF manifolds, we show that stability forces the universal cover to split a line. This gives explicit destabilizing tensors on the Riemannian Myers-Perry instantons and the Riemannian Schwarzschild-Tangherlini metrics in all dimensions.

</details>
