### [227] [Solute dispersion in magnetically influenced multiphase flow through a porous tube: axial transport and microrotational effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29946)
*Sohel Ahmed,Nanda Poddar,Jyotirmoy Rana,Kajal Kumar Mondal,Niall Madden*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究针对磁流体动力学多相管流中带多孔层的广义溶质弥散问题，构建了一个双流体解析模型，融合微极流体核心与牛顿血浆外围的多孔结构，并通过横向磁场与布朗动力学模拟验证，揭示了微转动动力学对溶质输运的新影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物流体和环境流体动力学中，需要一种能够统一描述磁场、微极流体和多孔血管结构对溶质输运影响的理论框架，现有文献在此方面存在空白。

Method: 建立双流体解析模型：包括代表红细胞旋转行为的微极（非牛顿）流体核心和嵌入Brinkman与Darcy多孔结构的牛顿血浆外围；引入横向磁场，并采用Sankarasubramanian & Gill广义弥散框架推导解析解。

Result: 解析预测通过布朗动力学模拟独立验证，零阶和一阶输运矩的时间演化结果一致。结果揭示了此前未被探索的微转动动力学对溶质浓度、对流系数和有效弥散的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了文献空白，建立了连接磁场、微极流体和动脉多孔结构的广义理论框架，为生物流体输运、靶向药物递送和临床工程应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical investigation of generalized solute dispersion in magnetohydrodynamic multiphase tube flow with porous layers. A two-fluid analytical model is developed for applications in biofluid and environmental fluid dynamics. The model comprises a micropolar (non-Newtonian) fluid core representing the rotational behaviour of red blood cells and a Newtonian plasma periphery embedded with Brinkman and Darcy porous structures, corresponding to the glycocalyx and endothelial layers with distinct permeability characteristics. A transverse magnetic field is incorporated to investigate how magnetic-field-induced modifications of the carrier flow influence solute localisation, with potential relevance to magnetic nanoparticle-mediated drug delivery. Using the generalised dispersion framework of Sankarasubramanian & Gill, analytical solutions are derived to investigate how the coupled axial velocity field and associated microrotational dynamics influence solute transport. The analytical predictions are independently validated through Brownian dynamics simulations, demonstrating excellent agreement for the temporal evolution of the zeroth and first transport moments. The results reveal the previously unexplored influence of microrotational dynamics on solute concentration, convection coefficients and effective dispersion, providing new insights into the coupled roles of translational and rotational fluid motion in biofluid transport. This work bridges an important gap in the literature and establishes a generalized theoretical framework linking magnetic fields, micropolar fluids and porous arterial structures for biofluid transport, targeted drug delivery and clinical engineering applications.

</details>


### [228] [Coexisting chaos and order in micro-textured elastic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30286)
*Giulio Foggi Rota,Ricardo Arturo Lopez de la Cruz,Simon J. Haward,Amy Q. Shen,Marco Edoardo Rosti*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过全解析数值模拟（fully resolved numerical simulations）与微流体实验（microfluidic experiments）相结合，揭示了低雷诺数（low-Reynolds-number）粘弹性液体流经微纹理表面（micro-textured surfaces）时，在微柱阵列上方自发产生弹性湍流（elastic turbulence），并与由流体弹性和非均匀剪切耦合引发的弹性波（elastic waves）共存的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性液体在密集微柱阵列（如纤毛覆盖的呼吸道或绒毛覆盖的肠上皮）上方的流动动力学，对运输、混合和吸收等基本生物过程至关重要，但流体弹性与复杂几何结构之间相互作用所产生的动力学尚缺乏充分研究。

Method: 研究采用全解析数值模拟与微流体实验相结合的方法，系统考察了浸没在低雷诺数粘弹性液体流动中、由密集微观柱状阵列（canopies）构成的结构上方所建立的流动动力学。

Result: 实验与模拟均观察到，在微柱顶端上方的流动中自发出现弹性湍流；该混沌态与从流体弹性和柱状几何结构引起的非均匀剪切之间耦合中产生的弹性波共存，且这种有序流体运动在广泛的流动条件和柱状阵列构型下持续存在。

Conclusion: 研究证明相干波传播（coherent wave propagation）和弹性湍流是粘弹性流体流动的互补表现形式，对生物及工程环境中的运输具有广泛意义，揭示了结构化几何形态可利用粘弹性液体的自发动力学来操控复杂流动。

Abstract: Viscoelastic fluid flows over micro-textured surfaces - densely covered by slender protrusions like cilia lining the body airways or villi covering the intestinal epithelium - underpin essential biological processes including transport, mixing, and absorption. Despite their ubiquity, the dynamics generated by the interplay between fluid elasticity and these complex geometries remain largely unexplored. Here we combine fully resolved numerical simulations with microfluidic experiments to reveal the flow dynamics established above dense arrays of microscopic pillars (canopies) immersed in the low-Reynolds-number flow of a viscoelastic liquid. We observe that the flow above the canopy tips spontaneously develops elastic turbulence. Remarkably, the chaotic state coexists with elastic waves emerging from the coupling between fluid elasticity and the heterogeneous shear induced by the canopy geometry. These ordered fluid motions persist across a broad range of flow conditions and canopy configurations. Our results demonstrate that coherent wave propagation and elastic turbulence are complementary manifestations of viscoelastic fluid flow. Beyond their fundamental significance, these mechanisms have broad implications for transport in biological and engineered environments, and reveal how structured geometries can harness the spontaneous dynamics of viscoelastic liquids to manipulate complex flows.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Analogue of Cardy's formula on the 60-degree parallelogram: a modular approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29425)
*Cody R. Strouse*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在 π/3 平行四边形上推广了 Cardy-Smirnov 函数的模块化理论：推导出以不完全 beta 积分表示的函数闭式共形映射公式，证明其可由模形式 η(τ)²η(3τ)² 的积分闭式表达，并建立了唯一性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cardy-Smirnov 函数编码了临界渗透（critical percolation）中交叉概率的猜想标度极限。Kleban 和 Zagier 已在矩形上证明该函数具有模解释，本文目标是在 π/3 平行四边形上发展对应的模块化理论。

Method: 本文采用解析与模块化方法：首先通过共形映射推导 Cardy-Smirnov 函数的不完全 beta 积分闭式公式；其次证明该函数可表示为模形式 η(τ)²η(3τ)² 的积分；最后利用单一函数方程、q-展开假设（q-expansion ansatz）及非退化条件建立唯一性定理。

Result: 在 π/3 平行四边形上获得了 Cardy-Smirnov 函数的闭式共形映射公式（不完全 beta 积分形式），其模块化闭式公式由模形式 η(τ)²η(3τ)² 的积分给出，并证明了唯一性定理。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将 Kleban-Zagier 在矩形上关于 Cardy-Smirnov 函数的模块化框架推广到 π/3 平行四边形，表明该框架在不同几何区域上均具有普适性。

Abstract: The Cardy-Smirnov function of a four-marked planar domain encodes the conjectural scaling limit of the crossing probability for critical percolation. Cardy predicted a closed formula for this limit on the rectangle in the cases of both site and bond percolation, and Smirnov proved the formula together with conformal invariance for critical site percolation on the triangular lattice. Kleban and Zagier later showed that on the rectangle the function admits a modular interpretation: it is determined by a modular functional equation together with a mild analytic ansatz. We carry out the analogue on the $π/3$ parallelogram. We derive a closed conformal-map formula for the Cardy-Smirnov function as an incomplete beta integral, prove a closed modular formula realizing it as an integral of the modular form $η(τ)^2η(3τ)^2$, and establish a uniqueness theorem showing that a single functional equation, together with a $q$-expansion ansatz and a nondegeneracy condition, determines the function uniquely.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Light-induced nonconservative static forces in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29122)
*Jinze Li,Xuemei Yang,Zhiyuan Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究光对固态系统中电子有序参数产生的静态力，发现这些力可包含源于耗散（即光吸收）的非保守分量。作者在激子绝缘体和具有不相称电荷密度波的材料两个实例中证明了该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子多体系统中，周期性驱动通常会对慢集体自由度产生有效的静态力。本文旨在探究光对固态系统中电子有序参数产生的静态力，特别是其中可能存在的非保守分量。

Method: 通过对量子多体系统中周期性驱动产生的有效静态力进行理论分析，并结合两个具体物理体系进行论证：激子绝缘体（excitonic insulator）中通过带间激发产生的光场非保守力作用于激子有序参数的相位；以及不相称电荷密度波（incommensurate charge density wave）材料中传播光场对有序参数相位产生的非保守力。

Result: 在激子绝缘体中，光场通过带间激发产生作用于激子有序参数相位的非保守力，导致相位加速，表现为激子凝聚体光子发射峰位的移动。在不相称电荷密度波材料中，传播光场驱动电荷密度波进入滑动运动，通过拓扑索尔平泵浦（topological Thouless pumping）产生直流电流。

Conclusion: 周期驱动（如光场）在固态系统中可对电子有序参数产生包含非保守分量的有效静态力，该分量源于耗散机制即光吸收，并可在激子绝缘体和电荷密度波材料中产生可观测的物理效应。

Abstract: In a quantum many-body system, a periodic drive can often generate effective static forces on the slow collective degrees of freedom. We study the static forces generated by light on electronic order parameters in solid-state systems. We show that the forces can have nonconservative components that originate from dissipation, i.e., optical absorption. This effect is demonstrated in two nontrivial examples. In excitonic insulators, via interband excitations, the light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of the excitonic order parameter. This force leads to an acceleration of the phase, which manifests as a shift of the photon emission peak from an exciton condensate. In materials with an incommensurate charge density wave, a propagating light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of its order parameter. It drives the charge density wave into sliding motion, leading to a DC electric current via topological Thouless pumping.

</details>


### [231] [Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Process Driven by Multiple Dichotomous Noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29226)
*Silvio Kalaj,Dongho Lee,Enzo Marinari,Jae-Hyung Jeon,Pascal Viot,Gleb Oshanin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究由K个独立二值噪声（dichotomous noises）叠加驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，推导出平稳位置分布及所有累积量的精确表达式，揭示了包含紧支撑、代数分支奇点、边界发散和多极值在内的丰富平稳结构，并通过映射到异质随机飞行过程（random-flight process）给出概率论解释，同时分析了大噪声源数下的高斯交叉以及淬火无序（quenched disorder）对平稳态的深远影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程由平衡高斯白噪声驱动，而现实中许多系统受有界、非平衡且具有有限关联时间的涨落支配。本文旨在研究这种更一般化的系统，探究其平稳态的统计性质是否与经典情形存在本质差异，并揭示其丰富的数学结构。

Method: 对K个具有任意固定幅度和切换率的独立二值噪声叠加驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，推导平稳位置分布和所有累积量的精确表达式；建立该过程与具有有界跳跃的异质随机飞行过程之间的映射关系；针对指数分布淬灭幅度的系综，推导无序平均（disorder-averaged）的平稳分布；并分析多种极限情形。

Result: 平稳态展现出出乎意料的丰富结构，包括紧支撑、代数分支点奇异性、边界发散及多极值；当噪声源数量增大时系统呈现向高斯统计（Gaussian statistics）的交叉行为；对指数分布淬灭幅度系综，无序会从根本上改变平稳态，产生带有非平凡指数代数前缀的指数尾。

Conclusion: 广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程在多个独立二值噪声驱动下具有远异于经典情形的丰富平稳结构，随机飞行映射为其提供了清晰的概率论解释；无序的引入进一步改变了平稳态的本质特征，使其偏离经典高斯行为。

Abstract: We study a generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by a superposition of $K$ independent dichotomous noises with arbitrary fixed amplitudes and switching rates. Unlike the classical Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by equilibrium Gaussian white noise, the present system is governed by bounded nonequilibrium fluctuations with finite correlation times. We obtain exact expressions for the stationary position distribution and all cumulants, and show that the stationary state possesses an unexpectedly rich structure, including compact support, algebraic branch-point singularities, edge divergences, and multiple extrema. We establish a mapping onto a heterogeneous random-flight process with bounded jumps, yielding a transparent probabilistic interpretation of the stationary measure. We further analyze several limiting regimes, including the crossover to Gaussian statistics for large numbers of noise sources. For ensembles with exponentially-distributed quenched amplitudes, we derive exact disorder-averaged stationary distributions and show that disorder fundamentally alters the stationary state, producing exponential tails decorated by algebraic prefactors with non-trivial exponents.

</details>


### [232] [On the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30871)
*Giuseppe Del Vecchio Del Vecchio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新表述了统计力学中的弛豫问题，将受到弛豫的对象明确为记录信号 Z(t) 的局部时间统计（local time statistics），即由“无时钟观察者”在均匀随机时间采样得到的估计直方图。论文指出，对于有限有界 N 自由度系统，全局不可逆弛豫可以发生但存在特殊初始条件，并将熵解释为观察者与系统未知过去之间的互信息（mutual information）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上统计力学中的弛豫问题所针对的“可弛豫对象”定义模糊，论文旨在通过明确操作对象来重新表述这一基本问题，从而澄清预测、不可逆性与熵的本质。

Method: 将观测信号 Z(t) 在均匀随机时间点的采样值构成直方图，即局部时间统计，作为受弛豫约束的操作对象；预测对象则是对未来全新样本读数的信念，其分布由所相信的数学模型推断。同时引入损失函数（loss function）概念以刻画不同观察者预测形式的差异。

Result: 对于任意 N≥1 个自由度的有限有界系统，全局不可逆弛豫可以发生，但存在特殊初始条件使得该过程不必然发生。预测的具体形式取决于观察者所选择的损失函数。熵的稳态值在完全一般的情况下依赖于观察者可用的信息量。

Conclusion: 统计力学中的弛豫本质上是一种关于信念更新与预测的学习过程；熵应被赋予学习论解释，即观察者与系统未知过去之间的互信息，其稳态值由观察者拥有的信息决定，而非系统的内禀属性。

Abstract: We reformulate the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics by making explicit what are the \emph{operational} objects subject to relaxation: the local time statistics of the recorded signal $Z(t)$. These local time statistics are simply the estimated histograms of observations $\{Z(t_i)\}_{i=1}^M$ performed at uniformly random times $\{t_i\}_{i=1}^M$ by a clockless observer. The subject of prediction is a belief about a future fresh out-of-sample reading of a measurement outcome whose distribution is inferred from the mathematical model believed to be true. For finite bounded systems of $N\ge 1$ degrees of freedom global irreversible relaxation of predictions can occur but special initial conditions exist. The form of the predictions depends on certain loss functions whose choice is up to the particular observer. Finally, entropy is given a learning interpretation as mutual information between the observer and the unknown past of the system under consideration and, in complete generality, its stationary value depends on the information available.

</details>


### [233] [Phase Diagram and Critical Behaviour of the Two-Dimensional Potts Model with Long-Range Quenched Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29710)
*Rodolfo Rocha,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Marco Picco*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究具有幂律空间关联无序的 $q=3$ 与 $q=8$ Potts 模型的相图与临界性质，借助 Fortuin-Kasteleyn 集团缠绕概率修正了 Chippari 等人提出的相图，并通过磁观测量澄清了磁化率双峰结构的物理起源，验证了在所有研究区间内超尺度关系的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（Chippari 等人）已提出随指数 $a$ 变化存在从有限无序不动点到无限无序不动点的转变，但该相图仍不够精确，需要更精细的方法予以完善，同时磁化率双峰结构的物理来源及超尺度关系的普适性尚待澄清。

Method: 采用 Fortuin-Kasteleyn 集团的缠绕概率（wrapping probabilities）作为修正相图的工具，并结合磁观测量（magnetic observables）研究磁化率行为及检验超尺度关系（hyperscaling relation）。研究针对 $q=3$ 与 $q=8$ 两种 Potts 模型，无序强度由幂律衰减指数 $a$ 控制。

Result: 缠绕概率方法成功细化了有限无序与无限无序不动点之间转变的相图描述；磁观测量揭示了磁化率双峰结构的物理本质，并证实超尺度关系在所有研究的无序区间内均成立。

Conclusion: 该研究为空间关联无序 Potts 模型的临界行为提供了更完整的相图，并确立了磁学观测量的物理解释力以及超尺度关系在无序体系中的普适性。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram and critical properties of the $q = 3$ and $q = 8$ Potts models with spatially correlated disorder governed by a power-law decay with exponent $a$. Building on the phase diagram proposed by Chippari et al. [3], where a transition from finite-disorder to infinite-disorder fixed points was identified as a function of a, we refine this picture by using wrapping probabilities of Fortuin-Kasteleyn clusters. Furthermore, using magnetic observables, we clarify the physical origin of the double-peak structure in the magnetic susceptibility and establish the validity of the hyper-scaling relation across all investigated regimes.

</details>


### [234] [Representation Transitions Reveal Predictive Structure in Complex Systems: A Trajectory-Level Reconstruction in a Critical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29777)
*Jiaqi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过在临界复杂系统中对同一轨迹系综（trajectory ensemble）系统性地搜索多种表征形式（representation forms），发现决定预测成败的关键因素并非相关性强度，而是表征是否保留了与预测相关的结构；只有连续低维压缩表征才能在跨八个独立尺度去除折叠中稳健地分离关键案例（r=0.677–0.717，p<0.001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的轨迹往往包含传统群体平均标量统计（population-averaged, scalar statistics）无法捕捉的结构，需要系统性地检验不同表征形式对预测可及性的影响。

Method: 通过独立数值重模拟（numerical re-simulation）而非文献综述，对同一临界复杂系统中的轨迹系综依次应用多种表征形式：从单一标量统计、两种独立的离散多特征分类尝试，到轨迹级结构表征与诊断性替代检验（surrogate test），再到连续低维压缩。

Result: 离散表征中特征最强的单个特征与目标相关性更高（r=0.588），但无法分离系综中最关键的两个案例；而连续表征在八个独立尺度去除折叠中稳健地获得r=0.677–0.717，且均p<0.001。先前预测失败并非由相关性不足造成，而是由表征未保留预测相关结构所致。

Conclusion: 表征形式的选择（choice of representation）决定了复杂系统中的预测结构是否可及，本身应被视为一个独立的科学变量，而非下游的分析选择。

Abstract: Trajectories in complex systems often contain structure that is not captured by the population-averaged, scalar statistics traditionally used to describe them. Here we reconstruct, through independent numerical re-simulation rather than literature review, a systematic search across representational forms - from single scalar statistics, through two independent discrete multi-feature classification attempts, to a trajectory-level structural representation and a diagnostic surrogate test, to a continuous low-dimensional compression - applied to the same underlying trajectory ensemble in a critical complex system. Predictive failure is not explained by insufficient correlation: one discrete representation's own strongest individual feature correlates with the target more strongly ($r=0.588$) than the eventual successful representation's own headline statistic ($r=0.540$), yet fails to separate the ensemble's two most consequential cases - while the continuous representation succeeds, robustly, across eight independent scale-removal folds ($r=0.677$-$0.717$, all $p<0.001$). The determining factor was not correlation strength but whether the representation preserved the structure relevant to prediction. These results indicate that the choice of representation determines whether predictive structure in complex systems becomes accessible - a scientific variable in its own right, not a downstream analysis choice.

</details>


### [235] [Branching stochastic mechanics. I. Clustering and connected correlations within a branching-process representation of the Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29807)
*Eric Dumonteil,Benoît Bischoff,Alain Letourneau,Loïc Thulliez,Corentin Doutre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过将薛定谔-长泽变换（Schrödinger-Nagasawa transform）推广为分支超过程（branching superprocesses），展示了扩展量子态（extended quantum state）的Born密度（Born density）与有限范围关联（finite-range correlation）可以在同一个随机核（stochastic kernel）中共存，提出了类似粒子（particle-like）组织的可能机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于探究有限范围关联能否隐藏在扩展量子态的统计特性中，即一个扩展（delocalized）的量子态是否可能在统计层面承载类似粒子的局域关联结构。

Method: 将薛定谔-长泽变换中的正、反向扩散场提升为分支超过程Φ_F和Φ_B，扩散负责采样随机路径，Bohm/Fisher控制的枝化生成替代延续的谱系（genealogies）。其分支速率在指定Born密度上求值，均值精确再现薛定谔动力学；随后让分支速率响应涨落乘积Φ_FΦ_B，在互易基（reciprocal basis）中定义符号核（signed kernel）C_FB(x,y)，并通过对关联和非关联分支的分析，将配对方程分解为集体（collective）与相对（relative）扇区。

Result: 连通扇区在约束下表现出超临界、临界和亚临界聚集，在自由空间中临界维度为d_c=2；平稳本征模保持扩展，亚临界团簇获得约化的德布罗意尺度。关联的屏蔽长度（screening length）为ξ_FB=√(D_eff/μ_FB)，表明扩展Born密度与有限关联范围能够共存于一个随机核中。

Conclusion: 扩展Born密度与有限关联范围可以在同一随机核中共存，这为量子态中类似粒子的组织方式提供了一种随机场（stochastic field）描述的可能途径。

Abstract: Can finite-range correlations hide in the statistics of an extended quantum state? The Schrödinger-Nagasawa transform represents the wave function by positive forward and backward diffusion fields whose product is the Born density. We promote them to branching superprocesses, \(Φ_F\) and \(Φ_B\): diffusion samples stochastic paths, whereas Bohm/Fisher-controlled branching generates genealogies of alternative continuations. With rates evaluated on the prescribed Born density, their means reproduce Schrödinger dynamics exactly. The connected sector exhibits supercritical, critical, and subcritical clustering in confinement and has critical dimension \(d_c=2\) in free space. Stationary eigenmodes remain extended; subcritical clusters acquire the reduced de~Broglie scale. We then let the branching rate respond to the fluctuating product \(Φ_FΦ_B\). In the reciprocal basis, the fields equal a smooth reference \(R\) plus centered fluctuations \(ψ_F,ψ_B\), defining the signed kernel \(C_{\rm FB}(x,y)=\mathbb E_ω[ψ_F(x)ψ_B(y)]\). On the anticorrelated branch, \(ρ_{\rm BSM}(x)=-C_{\rm FB}(x,x)\) is the positive paired density. Stationary recovery requires its diagonal to match the Born profile, while off-diagonal decay defines the correlation range. The pair equation splits into collective and relative sectors: spectral cancellation selects the Born collective mode, while suppression of the leading density fluctuation selects the anticorrelated source channel. With relative diffusivity \(D_{\rm eff}\) and positive relaxation rate \(μ_{\rm FB}\), correlations have screening length \(ξ_{\rm FB}=\sqrt{D_{\rm eff}/μ_{\rm FB}}\). Thus an extended Born density and a finite correlation range can coexist in one stochastic kernel, suggesting particle-like organization.

</details>


### [236] [Asymmetry-controlled resonant transport in a Brownian flashing ratchet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29991)
*Sudipta Mandal,Dipanjan Chakraborty,Debasish Chaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维布朗闪烁棘轮（Brownian flashing ratchet）在分段线性非对称周期势中的定向输运，发现稳态电流对切换频率呈非单调依赖并存在共振极大值，且建立了电流与势参数的分离开标度形式及共振频率随势不对称性的解析关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非对称周期势下布朗闪烁棘轮输运行为对微观输运机制研究具有重要意义，但切换频率对稳态电流的影响及其与势参数的具体依赖关系尚不清晰。

Method: 采用Fokker–Planck方程数值求解与布朗动力学（Brownian dynamics）模拟，研究分段线性非对称周期势中闪烁棘轮的定向输运，系统分析切换频率、势不对称性δ与势垒高度Δ对稳态电流的影响。

Result: 稳态电流对切换频率呈非单调依赖并出现共振峰；在中等不对称性|δ|<0.5时，电流满足分离标度形式，经适当缩放后数据发生塌缩；电流振幅随δ线性变化，共振频率遵循ν(δ,Δ)=ν₀(Δ)/[1−b₀δ²]的规律。

Conclusion: 将得到的(1−δ²)⁻¹标度行为归因于非对称势两条分支上的耦合弛豫（coupled relaxation），为共振频率随势不对称性依赖关系提供了物理基础解释。

Abstract: We investigate directed transport in a one-dimensional Brownian flashing ratchet with a piecewise-linear asymmetric periodic potential. Numerical solutions of the Fokker--Planck equation and Brownian dynamics simulations reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of the stationary current on the switching frequency, with a resonant maximum whose position depends on the potential asymmetry $δ$ and barrier height $Δ$. For moderate asymmetry, $|δ|<0.5$, the current obeys a scaling form that separates the dependence on the potential parameters from a common frequency dependence, resulting in a data collapse upon appropriate scaling of the current and frequency. The current amplitude varies linearly with $δ$, while the resonance frequency follows $ν(δ,Δ)=ν_0(Δ)/ [1-b_0\, δ^2]$. We interpret the resulting $(1-δ^2)^{-1}$ scaling in terms of coupled relaxation along the two branches of the asymmetric potential, which provides a physical basis for the observed dependence of the resonant frequency on the potential asymmetry.

</details>


### [237] [Chemical potentials from structure factors: II. Charged multi-component mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30060)
*Xiaoyu Wang,Roya Savoj,Musahid Ahmed,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将该方法（S0 method）从中性多组分混合物推广至带电混合物，结合组合空间框架与库仑处理的小波数静态结构因子极限，实现了从平衡分子动力学模拟中计算带电多组分混合物的化学势（chemical potentials），并分别应用于含卤化物盐水溶液和熔盐混合物的热力学性质计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电多组分混合物的化学势是电解质热力学（electrolyte thermodynamics）的核心量，但长期以来难以通过原子级模拟直接计算。S0方法此前已能从中性混合物的平衡分子动力学模拟中计算化学势，然而带电体系中的长程库仑相互作用和电荷中性约束使得现有方案无法直接适用，亟需扩展。

Method: 本文将S0方法延伸至带电混合物，采用两个关键策略：一是沿用第一部分（Part I: Neutral Multi-component Mixtures）建立的组合空间（composition-space）框架；二是对静态结构因子的小波数极限引入库仑处理（Coulombic treatment）。该方法将热力学相关的中性组成涨落（neutral composition fluctuations）与不允许的宏观电荷涨落（macroscopic charge fluctuations）分离，并显式考虑电荷中性约束条件（charge-neutrality constraints）。

Result: 利用该方法计算了多种卤化物（multiple-halide）水盐溶液的化学势，揭示了离子特异性热力学效应（ion-specific thermodynamic effects）；同时计算了熔盐混合物（molten salt mixtures）的混合自由能（mixing free energies），结果表明正确描述长波长静电关联（long-wavelength electrostatic correlations）对结果具有关键影响。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将S0框架扩展至带电体系，可准确处理电荷中性约束下的化学势计算，并强调长波长静电关联在带电混合物热力学计算中不可忽略的作用。

Abstract: The chemical potentials of charged multi-component mixtures are central to electrolyte thermo- dynamics, but remain difficult to compute from atomistic simulations. The S0 method enables computing chemical potentials of mixtures from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations. Here we extend the S0 method to charged mixtures by combining the composition-space framework developed in Part I: Neutral Multi-component Mixtures with a Coulombic treatment of the small- wavenumber limits of static structure factors. This approach separates thermodynamically relevant neutral composition fluctuations from forbidden macroscopic charge fluctuations, and further ac- counts for charge-neutrality constraints. We use the method to compute the chemical potentials of multiple-halide aqueous salt solutions, elucidating the ion-specific thermodynamic effects. We also calculate the mixing free energies of molten salt mixtures, demonstrating the importance of correctly describing long-wavelength electrostatic correlations.

</details>


### [238] [d'Q = TdS for Infinitesimal Irreversible Processes: On the Differential Form of the Clausius Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30421)
*Hiroshi Shimahara*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出，对于无限小准静态不可逆过程，克劳修斯不等式的正确一阶形式应为等号 d'Q=TdS 而非 d'Q < TdS，从而以纯热力学方式证明内部熵产生（internal entropy production）为二阶或更高阶小量，并确认熵在包含准静态不可逆过程时仍是良定义的状态量，无需借助玻尔兹曼关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，克劳修斯不等式的微分形式被用于无限小不可逆过程，但该形式在准静态不可逆情形下的正确一阶表述存在疑问。本文旨在澄清这一形式，并给出纯热力学证明，无需诉诸玻尔兹曼关系即可确认熵作为状态量的良定义性。

Method: 通过严格考察无限小准静态不可逆过程中的热力学一阶展开，证明d'Q=TdS成立，即微分形式应取等号而非不等号，并由此推导内部熵产生的高阶性质。

Result: 正确的一阶微分形式为 d'Q=TdS（等号），而非克劳修斯不等式中的 d'Q < TdS；内部熵产生为二阶或更高阶小量；熵在包含准静态不可逆过程的情况下仍是良定义的状态量。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了一个纯热力学的证明路径，在不依赖玻尔兹曼关系的条件下确立熵的状态量性质，并对克劳修斯不等式的适用范围给出了更精确的界定。

Abstract: Conventionally, the differential form of the Clausius inequality is adopted for infinitesimal irreversible processes. We show that the correct first-order form for infinitesimal quasi-static irreversible processes is the equality d'Q=TdS rather than d'Q < TdS. This provides a purely thermodynamic proof that internal entropy production is second-order or higher and confirms that entropy remains a well-defined state quantity even when quasi-static irreversible processes are included, without resorting to the Boltzmann relation.

</details>


### [239] [Time-delayed feedback turns Arrhenius escape logarithmic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30624)
*Roy Podgaetsky,Vishwajeet Kumar Arnab Pal,Ohad Shpielberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间延迟反馈（time-delayed feedback）在约束力中所诱导的非马尔可夫性（non-Markovianity）如何消除热逃逸（thermal escape）的指数标度，提出将时间延迟作为可调且实验上可行的控制参数来加速激活过程（activated processes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的阿伦尼乌斯定律（Arrhenius law）预言平均逃逸时间随势垒高度呈指数增长，这在许多物理、化学和生物过程中构成显著瓶颈。作者试图利用时间延迟反馈这一非马尔可夫机制，打破这种指数标度限制，从而为加速激活过程提供新的调控途径。

Method: 通过在约束力中引入时间延迟反馈，分析系统中逃逸动力学的改变。研究表明，当延迟参数超过由势阱底部曲率（curvature of the minimum）决定的阈值时，势阱失稳，热噪声（thermal noise）作为种子触发不稳定性，随后被确定性放大，形成所谓的"弹弓"（slingshot）逃逸轨迹。作者进而分析了逃逸时间、涨落分布及最佳延迟下的逃逸性能。

Result: 延迟的引入消除了逃逸时间对势垒高度的指数依赖，使逃逸时间变为势垒的对数函数；逃逸时间的涨落服从冈贝尔分布（Gumbel law）；在最优延迟条件下，逃逸比自由扩散（free diffusion）更快。

Conclusion: 时间延迟是一种可调且实验上可行的控制参数，可用于显著加速热激活过程，为超越阿伦尼乌斯标度限制提供了新的物理机制和实用方案。

Abstract: Thermal escape is governed by the Arrhenius law, where the mean escape time scales exponentially with the barrier height. We show that the non-Markovianity induced by time-delayed feedback in the confining force removes this exponential scaling. Beyond a threshold set by the curvature of the minimum, the delay destabilizes the well, and the thermal noise seeds an instability that is subsequently amplified deterministically to the boundary leading to \textit{slingshot} escape trajectories. The escape time becomes logarithmic in the barrier, its fluctuations follow a Gumbel law, and an optimal delay enables escape faster than free diffusion. Our results propose time delay as a tunable and experimentally feasible control parameter for accelerating activated processes.

</details>


### [240] [Effective Hardcore Exclusion Without Exclusion: Two-Species Pair Annihilation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30630)
*Su-Chan Park,Foster Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个无硬核排斥（hardcore exclusion）的粒子模型，通过非对称扩散（asymmetric diffusion）实现了与硬核排斥系统相同的密度衰减标度ρ∼t^{-1/3}，证明A+B→∅对湮灭反应中密度衰减指数并非仅由孤立粒种输运性质决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低维反应-扩散系统中，双种粒子通过A+B→∅相互湮灭时呈现反常的慢衰减幂律。此前t^{-1/3}标度仅在硬核排斥系统中观察到，作者旨在构造一个无硬核排斥的模型以复现该标度，从而检验衰减指数是否由输运性质唯一决定。

Method: 构造一个无量粒子硬核排斥的模型，其中两个物种均经历依赖于局部密度的非线性速率非对称扩散。在无另一物种存在时，此类输运属超扩散（superdiffusive），归入Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）普适类。模型中涉及粒子对的微观过程产生非对称扩散，涉及三粒子组的非对称过程则保留t^{-1/4}标度。

Result: 研究发现，当至少一个物种经历由成对粒子微观过程诱导的非对称扩散时，密度衰减呈现t^{-1/3}标度；而涉及三粒子组的非对称过程则导致与对称扩散相关的t^{-1/4}标度。该模型成功在无硬核排斥条件下复现了t^{-1/3}行为。

Conclusion: 对湮灭反应中的密度衰减指数并非仅由孤立粒种的输运性质决定，微观过程的类型（涉及粒子对的非对称过程vs涉及三粒子的过程）同样关键。这挑战了此前将t^{-1/3}归因于硬核排斥的认知。

Abstract: Reaction-diffusion systems with two species that mutually annihilate through $A+B\to\emptyset$ reactions display a slow decay of the total density $ρ$ which follows an anomalous power law in low dimensions. For hardcore particles in one dimension, this decay follows $ρ\sim t^{-1/4}$ under symmetric diffusion and $ρ\sim t^{-1/3}$ under asymmetric diffusion without relative bias between the two species. The $t^{-1/3}$ behavior, in particular, has so far been observed exclusively in systems with hardcore exclusion. Here we construct a model of particles without hardcore exclusion that reproduces this same $t^{-1/3}$ scaling. In our model, both species undergo asymmetric diffusion with a rate that depends nonlinearly on the local density, a form of transport that, in the absence of the other species, is superdiffusive and falls into the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang universality class. The scaling behavior of $t^{-1/3}$ occurs when at least one of the two species undergoes asymmetric diffusion induced by microscopic processes involving pairs of particles, while asymmetric processes involving groups of three particles lead to $t^{-1/4}$ scaling associated with symmetric diffusion. This demonstrates that the pair annihilation density decay exponent is not determined exclusively by the transport properties of the isolated particle species.

</details>


### [241] [Kinetic temperatures and inertial effects in a nonequilibrium bead-spring model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30809)
*Jetin E Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由两个任意质量耦合珠粒（coupled beads）构成的非平衡稳态模型，模型与两个不同温度的熱浴（thermal baths）接触。通过协方差矩阵方法和欠阻尼朗之万动力学（underdamped Langevin dynamics）数值模拟，作者发现熵产生和热流等不可逆性度量对珠粒质量交换不敏感，但能量存储观测量依赖于质量分布。动能温度（kinetic temperatures）被确立为统一描述能量、输运和不可逆性的组织变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示在非平衡稳态系统中，质量排列这一力学参数对热力学量（如熵产生、热输运和能量存储）的不同影响，并寻找能够统一描述该系统热力学性质的适当变量。特别地，在奇异质量极限下，不同类别观测量表现出不同的行为，需要厘清哪些变量仍然具有物理意义。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵方法（covariance-matrix approach）结合欠阻尼朗之万动力学的数值模拟，系统表征了稳态概率分布、热输运和熵产生。通过对质量交换和各种质量极限情形进行分析，比较不同类别观测量（不可逆性度量与能量存储观测量）的行为，并推导基于动能温度的热力学关系。

Result: 熵产生和热流在珠粒质量交换下保持不变，而能量存储观测量则依赖于质量排列。动能温度的差控制输运和熵产生，其和决定平均能量（通过广义均分关系）。在无限质量极限下，仅以动能温度表达的本构关系有意义。作者还推导了一个定义平衡类正则分布（canonical distribution）的有效温度，并发现时间平均观测量收敛速度显著快于系综平均。

Conclusion: 能量、输运和不可逆性通过动能温度作为组织变量得到统一描述。这揭示了非平衡系统中一类根本性区分：能量观测量在奇异质量极限下表现出路径依赖性，而输运和不可逆性始终定义良好。动能温度所定义的有效温度可构造拟平衡的正则分布，且系统在遍历性（ergodicity）方面表现出快速的时间收敛行为。

Abstract: We investigate a nonequilibrium steady-state model consisting of two coupled beads with arbitrary masses in contact with two thermal baths at different temperatures. Using a covariance-matrix approach together with numerical simulations of the underdamped Langevin dynamics, we characterize steady-state probability distributions, heat transport, and entropy production. We show that irreversibility measures such as entropy production and heat current are invariant under an exchange of the bead masses, whereas energy-storage observables depend explicitly on the mass arrangement in a symmetrical set up. This reveals a fundamental distinction: energy observables exhibit path dependence in singular mass limits, while transport and irreversibility remain well defined. We show that kinetic temperatures provide the natural variables governing the thermodynamics of the system: their difference controls transport and entropy production, while their sum determines the mean energy via a model specific generalized equipartition relation. In the infinite-mass limit, only constitutive relations expressed in terms of kinetic temperatures remain meaningful. Thus, energy, transport, and irreversibility are unified through kinetic temperatures as the organizing variables. We also derive an effective temperature that defines an equilibrium-like canonical distribution. Finally, we analyze the notion of ergodicity and show that the time-averaged observables converge significantly faster than the ensemble averages.

</details>


### [242] [Inertia-Driven Information Flow and Symmetry Breaking in a Nonequilibrium Two-Bead System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30849)
*Jetin E. Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个耦合两个热浴的非平衡双珠子系统中信息流（information flow）的产生机制，发现该系统在欠阻尼（underdamped）和过阻尼（overdamped）两种动力学机制下均能作为信息流发生器，并揭示了欠阻尼动力学中信息流的发散现象及类金兹堡-朗道（Ginzburg–Landau）几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在理解非平衡系统中信息流产生的底层机制，探索惯性效应和微观异质性如何塑造信息景观，为纳米机器中的信息引擎和类精灵（demon-like）机制提供理论基础。

Method: 采用对称性-微扰（symmetry-perturbation）分析方法研究信息流响应景观，结合Hessian矩阵本征值和本征向量分析，考察热不对称性-惯性参数空间中的信息流行为，对比过阻尼与欠阻尼两种动力学机制。

Result: 该系统在过阻尼和欠阻尼机制下均能产生信息流；欠阻尼动力学在热不对称性-惯性参数空间的特定路径上呈现尺度化信息流发散，该发散在过阻尼极限下被隐藏。对称参考态可对应信息流响应的最小值或最大值（取决于扰动方向），质量不对称会使最大值发生位移，本征值和本征向量分析揭示了主模式能级触碰（level touching）及沿恒定扩散路径的双峰性（bimodality）。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了一个最小化框架（minimal framework），用于理解惯性效应和微观异质性如何塑造非平衡系统中的信息景观，并有潜力推广到更复杂的异质网络上。

Abstract: We investigate information-flow generation in a nonequilibrium two-bead system coupled to two heat baths. We show that the system acts as an information-flow generator in both overdamped and underdamped regimes, with the underdamped dynamics revealing a divergence of the scaled information flow along specific paths in the thermal asymmetry--inertia parameter space that is hidden in the overdamped limit. This information generation suggests a possible route toward information engines and demon-like mechanisms in nanomachines. A symmetry-perturbation analysis of the response landscape of information flow reveals a geometric structure reminiscent of a Ginzburg--Landau framework: the symmetric reference state can correspond to a minimum or maximum depending on the perturbation direction, while the flat overdamped landscape develops a finite curvature under inertia. Mass asymmetry shifts the resulting maxima, and Hessian eigenvalue and eigenvector analysis reveals level touching of principal modes and bimodality along a constant-diffusion path. These results establish a minimal framework for understanding how inertia and microscopic heterogeneity shape information landscapes in nonequilibrium systems, with potential extensions to more complex heterogeneous networks.

</details>


### [243] [Compensation in continuous symmetric trilayered planar ferrimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30941)
*Olivia Mallick,Muktish Acharyya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文采用蒙特卡罗模拟方法研究了三层（A-B-A型）各向异性的单自旋XY磁性模型，该模型具有平面内铁磁相互作用和平面间反铁磁相互作用。研究发现系统在特定的相互作用参数范围内表现出高温临界行为和低温补偿行为，并通过有限尺寸分析揭示了临界温度附近的关联增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多层各向异性磁性系统的平衡性质，特别是具有平面内铁磁与平面间反铁磁竞争相互作用的体系，以理解其临界行为与补偿行为等复杂磁学现象。

Method: 采用Metropolis单自旋（随机选取）更新算法的蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟，研究三层（A-B-A类型）各向异性单自旋XY磁性模型的平衡性质；并通过有限尺寸研究分析临界温度附近的关联增长。

Result: 在给定的相互作用参数范围内，系统表现出高温临界行为（子格磁化强度和总磁化强度均消失）和低温补偿行为（总磁化强度消失而子格磁化强度不消失）。补偿温度和临界温度均随单自旋各向异性的增强而升高，并绘制了完整的相图。

Conclusion: 三层各向异性XY系统在竞争磁性相互作用下展现出丰富的相行为，各向异性强度是调控临界温度与补偿温度的关键参数；有限尺寸研究证实了临界点附近存在关联增长的物理图像。

Abstract: The trilayered (A-B-A type) anisotropic (single site) XY magnetic model is considered with the intra-plane ferromagnetic and
  the inter-plane antiferromagnetic interactions. The equilibrium properties of such trilayered anisotropic XY system has been studied by
  Monte Carlo simulation with Metropolis single spin (randomly chosen) update algorithm. In a given range of interaction parameters, the system has been found to exhibit the high temperature {\it critical} (vanishing sublattice magnetisations and total magnetisation) and low temperature {\it compensation} (vanishing total magnetisation even for non-vanishing sublattice magnetisations) behaviours. The compensation temperature and the critical temperature both are found to increase with the increase of single site anisotropy. The comprehensive phase diagram is drawn. The finite size study reveals the growth of correlations near the critical temperature.

</details>


### [244] [Overcoming critical slowing down in frustrated spin systems by learned multiscale sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31114)
*Gabriele Bandini,Giulio Biroli,Patrick Charbonneau,Andrea Gambassi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出使用小波条件重整化群（WCRG）采样方法，通过学习而非构造相关簇（cluster）来克服阻挫（frustrated）系统中传统聚类算法（如Swendsen-Wang和Wolff方法）无法缓解临界减速（critical slowing down）的根本局限。该方法在阻挫二维软自旋模型中通过从粗到细尺度递归采样条件小波分布，能以O(log2 L)的采样复杂度重现系统各相的主要统计性质，其效率远优于标准局部MCMC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类算法（cluster algorithms）是统计系统中最成功的MCMC方法之一，可有效缓解临界减速，但此类构造性算法在存在阻挫时会失效——即使阻挫极其微弱。因此，需要一种不依赖于构造簇、但能同样克服临界减速的替代方案。

Method: 采用小波条件重整化群（WCRG）采样方法，通过能量基模型（energy-based model）学习阻挫二维软自旋模型中集体涨落（collective fluctuations）的概率分布；随后从粗尺度到细尺度递归生成构型，在每一尺度上采样条件小波分布（conditional wavelet distribution）。

Result: WCRG方法能够重现系统在不同相中的主要统计性质（包括局域场分布和结构因子）。在伊辛型临界点处，各尺度的条件动力学在O(1)次扫掠内即保持去相关，整体采样复杂度为O(log2 L)，远优于标准局部MCMC方法。同时，不同可观测量（observable）的采样精度研究表明，快速采样方案的精度取决于所用能量基模型表达小波条件分布的能力。

Conclusion: 学习的多尺度采样（learned multiscale sampling）能够克服阻挫系统中传统聚类算法无法应对的临界减速问题。WCRG方法的主要权衡在于：其采样精度受限于用于估计小波条件分布的能量基模型的表达能力（expressiveness）。

Abstract: Cluster algorithms, such as the Swendsen--Wang and Wolff methods, are among the most successful MCMC methods for mitigating critical slowing down in statistical systems. These constructive cluster algorithms, however, fail in the presence of even extremely weak frustration. Here, we sidestep this fundamental limitation by learning rather than constructing the relevant clusters. Specifically, we use the wavelet conditional renormalization group (WCRG) sampling method to learn the probability distribution of collective fluctuations of a frustrated two-dimensional soft-spin model. Configurations are then generated recursively from coarse to fine scales by sampling conditional wavelet distributions. The WCRG method reproduces the main statistical properties of the system across different phases, including the local-field distribution and the structure factor. At an Ising-like critical point, the conditional dynamics remains decorrelated within $\mathcal{O}(1)$ sweeps at each scale, yielding an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\log_2 L)$, thus making WCRG much more efficient than standard local MCMC methods. These results show that learned multiscale sampling can overcome critical slowing down in frustrated systems for which conventional cluster algorithms fail. By assessing the sampling accuracy of different observables, we also clarify the main tradeoff of the WCRG method: the accuracy of the fast sampling scheme depends on the expressiveness of the energy-based model used to estimate the wavelet conditional distributions.

</details>

### [245] [Local-nuclear-density dependent calculation of nucleon electromagnetic form factor ratios in finite nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29358)
*G. Ramalho,K. Tsushima,Myung-Ki Cheoun*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文在协变旁观者-QMC（covariant spectator-QMC）框架下，通过引入有限核的空间核密度分布（spatial nuclear density profile），计算了束缚在原子核内的核子的电磁形状因子（electromagnetic form factors），并对$^{12}$C、$^{16}$O和$^{40}$Ca三种核的电与磁形状因子之比$G_E^*/G_M^*$进行了预言，以服务于未来极化转移实验（polarization-transfer experiments）中的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望改进先前基于常密度近似（constant-density approximation）的计算，将有限核的密度分布效应纳入考量，从而更真实地描述介质效应对束缚核子电磁形状因子的影响。同时，针对即将开展的$(\vec{e} A, e'\! A' \vec{p})$极化转移实验，为束缚质子（bound protons）的电磁双比率（electromagnetic double ratio）提供理论预言。

Method: 作者在协变旁观者-QMC框架内扩展了此前基于常密度近似的计算，引入有限核的空间核密度分布函数$\rho(r)$，计算束缚在核内的核子的电形状因子$G_E^*$和磁形状因子$G_M^*$作为四动量转移平方$Q^2$的函数，并定义了核内与自由空间比值的双比率以消除部分系统性不确定度。研究涉及$^{12}$C、$^{16}$O和$^{40}$Ca三种原子核，并对束缚中子的相应比率也做了预言。

Result: 结果表明，使用核密度分布函数$\rho(r)$计算的形状因子受抑制程度低于此前使用平均核密度（average nuclear density）得到的结果。低密度表面区域（low-density surface region）的相对重要性随$Q^2$增大而增加，导致比平均密度近似所预言的抑制更弱。

Conclusion: 采用实际核密度分布计算的介质效应比平均密度近似更弱，形状因子的抑制效应集中在核内部高密度区域，表面低密度区域在高$Q^2$下对形状因子的贡献相对增大。作者为束缚质子和中子相关的双比率提供了面向未来实验测量的理论预言。

Abstract: We calculate the electromagnetic form factors of nucleons bound in finite nuclei and make predictions to the ratio between the electric ($G_E^*$) and magnetic ($G_M^*$) form factors in terms of the square of the four-momentum transfer $Q^2$. We extend our previous constant-density calculations within the covariant spectator-QMC framework by incorporating the spatial nuclear density profiles of finite nuclei and quantify the deviations from the average-density approximation used in our previous applications. The impact of the medium effects can then be observed considering the ratio between $G_E^*/G_M^*$ and the ratio in free space $G_E/G_M$, that define the proton electromagnetic double ratio associated with a given nucleus. The double ratio is expected to remove some systematic uncertainties. There is the expectation that ratios $G_E^*/G_M^*$ associated with the bound protons inside the nucleus will be measured in the near future in polarization-transfer experiments $(\vec{e} A, e'\! A' \vec{p})$. Anticipating future measurements on the subject, we make predictions for the double ratios, to be compared with the average measurements on the energy states of protons bound to nuclei. We consider the nuclei $^{12}$C, $^{16}$O and $^{40}$Ca. We conclude that the form factors calculated using nuclear density profile function $ρ(r)$ are less suppressed than in the case of the results calculated using the average nuclear density. The results indicate that the relative importance of the low-density surface region increases with $Q^2$, leading to a weaker suppression than that predicted by the average-density approximation. We also make predictions for the ratios associated with neutrons bound to nuclei.

</details>


### [246] [Strongly coupled quark matter in neutron stars and their mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28879)
*Konstantin Maslov,Ralf Rapp,Joaquin Grefa,Veronica Dexheimer,Claudia Ratti*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文基于自洽T矩阵（self-consistent T-matrix）量子多体理论，研究了高温强耦合夸克-胶子等离子体（sQGP）在高中子密度和低温条件下的表现——即中子星及其合并过程中的物态。通过量子色动力学（QCD）重子数敏感性约束夸克相互作用核的化学势依赖性，发现超导相被限制在极小温度，而强耦合二夸克液体（diquark liquid）主导了热力学；结果与中子星物态方程观测约束一致，支持强耦合夸克物质存在于中子星内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能重离子碰撞中发现的sQGP展现出在强耦合极限下接近量子场论所预言的输运系数的非凡物态性质，但该相在高中子密度和低温区域的体现尚不清楚。作者希望探索sQGP在如中子星及其合并事件所对应的极高重子密度、较低温度条件下的具体表现形式，以加深对夸克物质相结构在不同物理环境下的理解。

Method: 采用基于自洽T矩阵（self-consistent T-matrix）方法的量子多体理论，研究高温sQGP在高重子密度和低温下的物态。利用QCD的重子数敏感性（baryon-number susceptibilities）这一第一性原理信息，约束夸克相互作用核（quark interaction kernel）的化学势依赖性。

Result: 研究发现即使在很大密度下，巨大的碰撞宽度依然持续存在，将超导相（superconducting phases）限制在相当小的温度区间；在对应中子星合并的热力学条件下，强耦合二夸克液体（diquark liquid）占据主导地位。在较低密度下，二夸克贡献超越单夸克贡献，揭示了通往强子化的路径。

Conclusion: 研究结果与中子星物态方程（equation of state）的观测约束一致，支持中子星内部存在强耦合夸克物质的论断，同时二夸克液体相的发现为夸克物质向强子物质的过渡提供了一条新的可能路径。

Abstract: The discovery of the strongly-coupled quark-gluon plasma (sQGP) in high-energy heavy-ion collisions has revealed remarkable properties of matter at high temperature, with transport coefficients close to conjectured bounds from quantum field theory at strong coupling. The high sQGP collision rates imply very large energy uncertainties and the melting of quasiparticle structures. Deploying quantum many-body theory based on the self-consistent $T$-matrix approach for the sQGP at high temperature, we investigate its manifestation at high baryon density and low temperature, as present in neutron stars and their mergers. We constrain the chemical-potential dependence of the quark interaction kernel using first-principles information from Quantum Chromodynamics on baryon-number susceptibilities. Very large collisional widths persist at large density and are found to relegate superconducting phases to rather small temperatures. Instead, a strongly coupled diquark liquid prevails in the thermodynamics under the conditions relevant to neutron-star mergers. At lower densities, the diquarks take over from the single-quark contributions, suggesting a pathway toward hadronization. Our results are consistent with observational constraints on the equation of state of neutron stars, corroborating the presence of strongly coupled quark matter in the interior of these objects.

</details>

### [247] [Localization in microcavities revealed by phase-space non-Hermitian skin effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29795)
*Jung-Wan Ryu,Yong-Hoon Lee,Muhan Choi,Chang-Hwan Yi,Martina Hentschel*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文揭示了开放螺旋形介电微腔中临界线局域化（critical-line localization）现象背后的物理机制——广义非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）。作者建立了非均匀损耗Hatano-Nelson模型与螺旋微腔介电相空间之间的物理对应关系，表明几何诱导的动量漂移与折射逃逸相结合，在相空间动量中产生了广义非厄米趋肤效应，从而解释了共振在临界线附近的局域化现象，并将趋肤效应的概念从非互易晶格拓展到相空间和开放混沌波动系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与完全混沌系统的半经典预期相反，开放微腔中共振的局域化是一种常见现象，但尽管经过大量研究，其物理机制长期以来悬而未决。本文旨在解决这一长期未解问题，揭示螺旋形介电微腔中共振在临界线附近大量局域化的深层物理根源。

Method: 作者建立了非均匀损耗Hatano-Nelson模型与螺旋微腔介电相空间之间的物理对应关系，通过分析几何诱导的动量漂移与折射逃逸的协同作用，在相空间动量中推导出广义非厄米趋肤效应。

Result: 研究结果表明，几何诱导的动量漂移与折射逃逸的结合产生了相空间内的广义非厄米趋肤效应，这一机制被认定为开放混沌螺旋微腔中临界线局域化的起源。

Conclusion: 该工作将非厄米趋肤效应的概念从非互易晶格推广到相空间，并拓展至开放混沌波动系统，为理解混沌开放系统中的共振局域化现象提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Contrary to the semiclassical expectation for fully chaotic systems, localization of resonances is found to be a common feature in open microcavities. In spiral-shaped dielectric microcavities, a substantial fraction of resonances localize on polygonal patterns in real space, are chiral, and their momentum distributions accumulate near the critical line for total internal reflection. Despite the extensive investigation, the physical mechanism responsible for their remarkable abundance has remained a long-standing question. Addressing this, we reveal a physical correspondence between an inhomogeneous-loss Hatano-Nelson model and the dielectric phase space of a spiral microcavity. We show that the combination of geometry-induced momentum drift and refractive escape yields a generalized non-Hermitian skin effect in the phase space momentum. We identify this mechanism as the origin of the critical-line localization of resonances in open chaotic spiral microcavities, extending the skin-effect concept beyond nonreciprocal lattices to phase space and to open chaotic wave systems.

</details>


### [248] [Controlling the Coupling Strengths in Nanophotonic Networks using Modified Yagi-Uda Nanoplasmonic Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29837)
*Vidar Flodgren,Anders Mikkelsen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出并模拟了一种基于纳米等离子体（nanoplasmonic）金属结构与III-V族纳米线发射器/接收器组合的光学神经网络方案，利用自由空间广播和亚波长纳米光子学调控光场分布，以替代传统波导实现片上通信，从而缩小系统尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学神经网络中的片上通信通常依赖波导传输，导致系统占用面积大。作者旨在寻找一种紧凑替代方案，通过自由空间光信号广播和纳米结构调控光场来实现节点间权重分配，以缩减器件尺寸。

Method: 采用有限差分时域法（Finite Difference Time Domain, FDTD）进行模拟，研究由实验中已验证的纳米线光电子器件与纳米等离子体结构组成的系统，并利用Yagi-Uda天线及其非对称导向组件调制光信号的定向传播。结构可通过与纳米线供电金属触点相同的制造步骤制备。

Result: 研究表明Yagi-Uda天线可显著改变纳米线节点的定向通信能力，添加非对称导向组件后可同时向多个不同角度方向引导光。在由七个节点组成的两层网络中，通过组合天线几何形状可实现对连接权重的复杂且高度可变的分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的紧凑纳米线节点层可支撑具有复杂连接性的多种神经网络实现，且该方法可推广至其他类型的纳米尺度发射器/接收器系统。

Abstract: On-chip communication in optical neural networks is commonly done via waveguides which results in large system footprints. A compact alternative is to broadcast light signals between nano-optoelectronic components in free space and tailor the light field distribution using sub-wavelength nanophotonics. We propose and simulate nanoplasmonic metal structures in combination with III-V nanowire emitters and receivers to create an optical network in which the shape and position of these nanostructures create varying weights between the nano-optoelectronic nodes. Using Finite Difference Time Domain modelling, we investigate systems of experimentally verified nanowire optoelectronics combined with nanoplasmonic structures that can be made in the same fabrication step as electrical contacts powering the nanowires. We investigate both individual nanowire/antenna devices as well as networks corresponding to two layers in a neural network. We show that directed communication from a nanowire node can be significantly altered using Yagi-Uda antennas. Modifying the antenna with an asymmetric director component enables the directing of light several different angular directions simultaneously. We find that highly variable complex weight distribution between the connections in two seven node layers can be achieved depending on the combined geometry of the antenna components. The possible weight distributions in compact layers of nanowire nodes could be used for creating a variety of neural networks with complex connectivity. The concepts can be generalized to other types of nanoscale emitter/receiver systems.

</details>


### [249] [Quantum Dot Colloidosomes as Triggerable Microlasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30919)
*Cristian Gonzalez,Saranya Subramanian,Marco Reale,Giuseppe Soligno,Ilia Geints,Siyuan Yin,Claire Y. Kang,Ricky Ronquillo,Gary Chen,Marco Cannas,Cherie R. Kagan,Alice Sciortino,Michael Engel,Fabrizio Messina,Christopher B. Murray,Emanuele Marino*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究报道了量子点（quantum dot, QD）胶体囊（colloidosome）——一种液芯/固壳超粒子——作为可重构微激光器平台，兼具回音壁模式（whispering-gallery-mode）光学反馈与刺激触发的结构塌缩能力。完整胶体囊展现出腔限域激射现象（阈值约2.8 mJ/cm²，线宽2.2–3.7 nm），而多孔壳层则为宽带发射；通过多种触发途径诱导囊体破裂可将其光学响应从腔限域激射重构为宽带发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点超粒子激光器有望成为微尺度光源和光子标记的平台，但其光学性质在组装过程中即被固定，缺乏可调性，因此需要开发能够动态重构光学响应的超粒子结构。

Method: 通过构建包含实心超粒子、胶体囊和微孔空心壳层的形态连续体，系统比较不同壳层连续性与完整性对回音壁模式反馈和激射行为的影响；同时利用弯月面驱动的毛细失效、均匀加热和局部近红外激活等途径触发胶体囊破裂。

Result: 完整胶体囊可实现腔限域激射，其泵浦阈值约为2.8 mJ/cm²，发射线宽为2.2–3.7 nm；壳层连续性被确认为回音壁反馈与激射的关键决定因素，完整囊壳支持窄腔模式，而多孔壳层即使在高温泵浦下仍保持宽带发射。可触发的破裂过程将光学响应从腔限域激射重构为宽带发射。

Conclusion: 量子点胶体囊被确立为可重构微激光器，能够在单一自组装平台上实现光学状态切换与触发放送的耦合功能。

Abstract: Quantum dot (QD) supraparticle lasers are promising platforms for microscale light sources and photonic labeling, yet their optical properties are set during assembly. Here we introduce QD colloidosomes, liquid-core/solid-shell supraparticles that combine whispering-gallery-mode optical feedback with stimulus-triggered structural collapse. Intact QD colloidosomes show cavity-defined lasing with fluence thresholds of ~2.8 mJ/cm2 and linewidths of 2.2-3.7 nm, demonstrating efficient light trapping without a solid core. We identify shell continuity as a critical determinant for whispering-gallery feedback and lasing by exploring a morphological continuum of suprastructures comprising solid supraparticles, colloidosomes, and microporous hollow shells. Intact colloidosomes support narrow cavity modes, whereas porous shells remain broadband even at high pump fluence. We show that colloidosomes can be driven to rupture and release payload through well-defined pathways, including meniscus-driven capillary failure, uniform heating, and localized near-infrared activation, thereby reconfiguring their optical response from cavity-defined lasing to broadband emission. These results establish QD colloidosomes as reconfigurable microlasers that couple optical-state switching and triggered release within a single self-assembled platform.

</details>

### [250] [Non-Elliptic Quadratic $\mathcal{PT}$-Symmetric Operators and Similarity to Self-Adjoint Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28821)
*Stepan Malkov*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在满足动力学平均条件的情形下，具有实谱的非自伴非椭圆二次 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称算子可（弱）相似于一个自伴算子，其充要条件为相应二次型的基本矩阵可对角化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自伴（non-self-adjoint）算子在许多物理与数学问题中广泛出现，其中 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称（$\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric）算子具有实谱但不等价于自伴算子，需要厘清其与自伴算子之间的相似性关系。

Method: 通过引入动力学平均（dynamical averaging）条件，将非椭圆二次 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称算子与二次型的基本矩阵（fundamental matrix）的代数性质建立联系，进而证明弱相似（weak similarity）成立的充要条件。

Result: 证明了此类算子弱相似于自伴算子，当且仅当其关联二次型的基本矩阵是可对角化的（diagonalizable）。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画了在满足动力学平均条件下，$\mathcal{PT}$ 对称非自伴算子可约化为自伴结构的精确代数判据，为理解这类算子的谱性质与相似性分类提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove a similarity result for non-self-adjoint non-elliptic quadratic $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric operators with real spectrum satisfying a dynamical averaging condition. In particular, we show that such an operator is weakly similar to a self-adjoint operator precisely when the fundamental matrix of the associated quadratic form is diagonalizable.

</details>


### [251] [Band edges of periodic Schrödinger operators are generically isolated and nondegenerate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30189)
*Zhongkai Tao,Mengxuan Yang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对d≥2维实空间上周期的薛定谔算子（periodic Schrödinger operators）$H_V=-Δ+V$，证明了对于一般势能，每个谱隙（spectral gap）的端点仅由单个布洛赫带（Bloch band）在有限个准动量（quasimomenta）处达到，且在各到达点处的海森矩阵（Hessian）非退化，从而证明了周期薛定谔算子的谱边猜想（Spectral Edge Conjecture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱隙端点的达到性质与布洛赫带的结构是周期算子谱理论中的基本问题。谱边猜想长期以来作为开放问题，关系到能带几何、有效质量张量的非退化性以及微扰理论等方向，本工作旨在d≥2维一般势能情形下给出完整证明。

Method: 文章对d≥2维实值有界势能作用下的周期薛定谔算子进行谱分析，采用一般势能（generic potentials）框架下的论证方法，通过分析布洛赫带在准动量空间中的临界点行为来考察谱隙端点。

Result: 证明了对一般势能，每一个谱隙的端点仅由单个布洛赫带在有限个准动量处达到，且所有到达点处谱隙端点对应的海森矩阵非退化。这一结果直接确认了周期薛定谔算子的谱边猜想成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为周期薛定谔算子谱结构的精细几何性质提供了确定性的理论回答，验证了谱边猜想的正确性，对理解周期介质中电子的能带行为具有基础性意义。

Abstract: For periodic Schrödinger operators $H_V=-Δ+V$ with bounded real-valued potentials on $\mathbb R^d$ with $d\ge2$, we show that for generic potentials, each endpoint of every spectral gap is attained by a single Bloch band at only finitely many quasimomenta, and has a nondegenerate Hessian at every attaining point. This proves the Spectral Edge Conjecture for periodic Schrödinger operators.

</details>


### [252] [On the discrete spectrum of Dirac operators with Lorentz-scalar $δ$-shell interactions supported on unbounded curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30651)
*Markus Holzmann,Vladimir Lotoreichik,Marco Vogel*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究平面上带质量的大质量狄拉克算子（Dirac operator），其受到沿光滑曲线Σ支撑的吸引型洛伦兹标量δ-壳（Lorentz-scalar δ-shell）相互作用的作用。研究证明该算子在谱隙中的离散特征值数量有限，并在Σ围成区域之一为凸的假设下，证明当相互作用强度τ的绝对值足够小或足够大时，离散谱非空且由几何曲率诱导产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解奇异相互作用（singular interaction）下狄拉克算子的谱性质，特别是几何形状（如折线的局部变形）如何影响离散谱的存在性，是数学物理中的重要问题。这一研究为几何诱导的束缚态（geometrically induced bound states）现象提供理论支撑。

Method: 在C^∞光滑曲线Σ上通过施加合适的透射条件（transmission condition）定义吸引型洛伦兹标量δ-壳相互作用，构造自伴算子。利用谱分析工具证明本质谱中存在间隙，并通过凸性假设与相互作用强度τ的小参数或大参数展开，分析离散谱的存在性与有限性。

Result: 证明该狄拉克算子在谱隙中的离散特征值数量有限；当Σ围成的某个区域为凸集时，若|τ|足够小或足够大，离散谱非空；该结果适用于任意开角的折线的任意局部扰动，且离散谱纯由几何（非直线）诱导——因为支撑在直线上的同类相互作用对应离散谱为空。

Conclusion: 几何曲率本身足以在狄拉克方程的谱隙中诱导出束缚态，即使不存在直线情形下的离散谱。这为理解奇异相互作用系统中的几何效应提供了重要范例。

Abstract: We consider the massive Dirac operator (with positive mass) in the plane with an attractive Lorentz-scalar $δ$-shell interaction of strength $τ\in(-\infty,0)\setminus\{-2\}$ supported on a $C^\infty$-smooth curve $Σ\subset\mathbb{R}^2$ being a local deformation of the broken line. This singular interaction is defined by imposing a suitable transmission condition on the curve $Σ$ in the operator domain. Such a Dirac operator is self-adjoint and has a gap in the essential spectrum, whose size is explicit and depends on the mass and the interaction strength. We show that the number of discrete eigenvalues in the gap is finite. Under the assumption that one of the domains bounded by $Σ$ is convex, we prove that the corresponding Dirac operator has a non-empty discrete spectrum, provided that $τ$ is either sufficiently small or sufficiently large in absolute value. The result holds for any perturbation of a broken line of any opening angle as described above, and this discrete spectrum is induced by the geometry, since for the same type of a singular interaction supported on the straight line the discrete spectrum is empty.

</details>

### [253] [Fractal Confinement and Magnetic Self-sabotage of Current Flow: Electrons Near the Metal-insulator Crossover in a 2D $δ$-layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28739)
*Xinghai Zhang,Matthew S. Foster,Markus Mueller*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用大规模 Hartree-Fock 计算研究二维 δ 掺杂半导体中的低温输运与巡游磁性行为，发现在临界密度附近存在两个输运区间：温度低于 U₀/10 时电导率 σ(T) 随降温而增大，但在 T~U₀/100 时，少量磁矩的形成引发绝缘性下坠，分形波函数局域放大磁性相互作用，电流先聚集为支状细丝，最终因动态 SU(2) 对称性破缺而被阻断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维 δ 掺杂半导体在低温下的输运性质与巡游磁性（itinerant magnetism）之间的关联，特别是磁性杂质涌现如何影响导电行为。

Method: 采用含随机跳跃（random hoppings）与库仑排斥 U₀ 的大规模 Hartree-Fock 计算方法，在临界密度附近系统考察低温电导率 σ(T) 的温度依赖关系。

Result: 发现两个截然不同的输运区间：在 T 低于 U₀/10 时，σ(T) 随温度降低而升高；而在 T~U₀/100 处，低密度磁矩开始形成，导致绝缘性下降转折；分形波函数放大局域磁性作用，电流先集中于支状细丝，最终因动态 SU(2) 对称性破缺被完全阻断。

Conclusion: 二维 δ 掺杂半导体中低温输运受磁性涨落与分形态波函数协同调控，磁矩的形成与动态 SU(2) 对称性破缺是驱动绝缘性转变的关键机制。

Abstract: We study low-$T$ transport and itinerant magnetism in 2D $δ$-doped semiconductors. Large-scale Hartree-Fock calculations with random hoppings and repulsion $U_0$ reveal two transport regimes near a critical density. The conductivity $σ(T)$ initially increases as $T$ drops below $U_0 / 10$. However, at $T \sim U_0/100$, a tiny density of magnetic moments forms, driving an insulating downturn. Fractal wavefunctions locally amplify magnetic interactions, initially concentrating currents into branched filaments, but ultimately choking them off via dynamic SU(2) symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [254] [Pseudo Entropy in Quantum Spin Chains: from Integrability to Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29810)
*Tara Bahadur Rana,Yadav Raj Dahal,Kiran Adhikari*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子自旋链（海森堡XXZ模型和混合场伊辛模型）中的赝熵（pseudo entropy）与谱动力学，验证其作为量子混沌诊断工具的有效性，并推导出赝熵与谱形状因子、费雪零点及谱复杂度之间的精确联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子混沌的可靠诊断是量子多体物理中的核心问题，传统诊断工具在区分可积与混沌系统的能力上仍有局限。作者旨在探索赝熵是否能够作为量子混沌的新型诊断工具，并揭示其与谱动力学之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者在两种量子自旋链模型（海森堡XXZ模型与混合场伊辛模型）中系统地研究热赝熵与空间赝熵。首先推导热赝熵实部与谱形状因子（spectral form factor）之间的精确关系，随后分别分析赝熵虚部的费雪零点（Fisher zeros）信息、XXZ链中空间赝熵的对数临界标度行为，并推导赝熵、相对赝熵与谱复杂度（spectral complexity）之间的精确联系。

Result: 结果表明热赝熵实部表现出典型的下凹（dip）、对数斜坡（logarithmic ramp）与平台（plateau）特征，精确反映量子混沌的谱学行为；赝熵虚部虽不诊断混沌，却能提供费雪零点的关键信息。空间赝熵在可积与混沌区域均保持对数临界标度，且非正性猜想（non-positivity conjecture）成立。此外，赝熵与相对赝熵、谱复杂度之间存在精确联系，并可能关联全息原理（holography）。

Conclusion: 赝熵可作为量子混沌的有效诊断工具，其实部通过谱形状因子精确刻画混沌特征，虚部补充费雪零点信息；空间赝熵的普适标度行为独立于可积/混沌性质。赝熵与谱复杂度及全息原理之间建立的联系为进一步探索量子混沌与量子引力提供了新方向。

Abstract: In this work, we study pseudo entropy and spectral dynamics in quantum spin chains (Heisenberg XXZ model and the mixed-field Ising model) to investigate whether they can serve as diagnostics of quantum chaos. First, we derive an exact relation between the real part of the thermal pseudo entropy and the spectral form factor. This provided evidence that pseudo entropy could exhibit the characteristic dip, logarithmic ramp, and plateau, implying the precise manner in which pseudo entropy probes chaos. We found that the imaginary part, while it does not diagnose chaos, can still provide crucial information about Fisher zeros that is not available in the real counterpart. For spatial pseudo entropy in the XXZ chain, we find that the logarithmic critical scaling persists in both integrable and chaotic regimes, and that the non-positivity conjecture holds. Finally, we derive an exact connection between pseudo entropy, relative pseudo entropy, and spectral complexity with a potential connection to holography.

</details>


### [255] [Hartree Fragmentation and Long-Range Pair Networks in Aperiodic Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30746)
*Yogeshwar Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究缓慢变化非周期势场中费米子体系在随机相互作用下的输运性质，揭示了跨越α-2n临界点时共振系统的重构行为以及两种匹配律的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机相互作用对缓慢变化非周期势中费米子输运的影响，特别是势场如何固定共振供给量及其临界行为。

Method: 通过解析推导分析共振对象系综（resonant-object ensemble）在Hartree碎裂（Hartree fragmentation）作用下的重构过程，考察转向点（turning-point）游程的分裂、孤立翼键（wing bond）的存活以及耦合缩放关系。

Result: 跨越α-2n时，转向点游程分裂为簇（clusters），其内禀匹配律消除对数奇异性；孤立翼键以有限密度存活并恢复缩减的对数项。配对尺度满足x^{4-2n-α}_{pair} ln{x_{pair}} ∝ h^2/(V t0)不变关系。随机系数涨落维持R^{-α}耦合，而均匀系数则抵消至R^{-α-2}。

Conclusion: Hartree碎裂诱导的共振系统重构在临界点α=2n处产生两种不同的对数行为，且耦合的缩放性质强烈依赖于系数涨落的统计特性。

Abstract: For fermions in a slowly varying aperiodic potential with random interactions $V_{ij}/|i-j|^α$, the potential fixes the resonance supply $N_0 \propto L^{2-n}/h$. On crossing $α-2n$, wherever Hartree fragmentation is operative, the resonant-object ensemble reconstructs: turning-point runs fragment into clusters whose intrinsic matching law resolves no logarithmic singularity, while isolated wing bonds survive at finite density and restore a reduced logarithm. The scaling $x^{4-2n-α}_{pair} ln{x_{pair}} \propto h^2/(V t0)$ is unchanged. Random coefficient fluctuations sustain the $R^{-α}$ coupling; uniform ones cancel to $R^{-α-2}$.

</details>


### [256] [Resonance statistics, Fock-space branching, and long-range pair networks in slowly varying interacting chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30761)
*Yogeshwar Prasad*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文在缓变非周期势与随机幂律密度相互作用体系中，发展了共振对象的微观共振理论，推导了精确的相位平均共振统计量，并通过精确构造共振Fock空间图揭示了前向分支尺度与连通性的不等价性，最终划分了碎片化区域并识别出三个具有不同含义的长程阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作中发现共振对象系综在α=2n处发生转变，但缺乏系统的微观共振理论及其适用域。本文旨在建立该微观理论，推导精确的相位平均共振统计量，并澄清各长程阈值（α=1/2、α=2n、α=2）的物理含义及其与局域化相变的关系。

Method: 本文采用解析推导与数值构造相结合的方法：推导精确的相位平均共振统计量（包括供应量N₀、共同相位梳和闭式Hartree方差）；在L≤18的规模下精确构造共振Fock空间图；分离固定配对匹配律与壳平均的q ln(1/q)律，并将梳结构发展为介观壳层理论；处理α=2边缘情形中壳层与匹配边缘性复合产生的双对数行为。

Result: 主要结果包括：证明相互作用不改变共振供应的主导幂律（N₀∝L^{2-n}/h）；发现前向分支尺度h_FB∝L^{2-n}不携带巨分量，一步分支与连通性不等价；识别出三个具有不同物理含义的长程阈值——α=1/2（随机Fock空间能量的精确方差阈值及主导LIOM修身的平方可和性边界）、α=2n（局域共振对象的改变）和α=2（长程壳层和的边缘性），三者均非局域化相变。

Conclusion: 本文建立了缓变非周期势与随机幂律密度相互作用体系中共振现象的微观理论框架，厘清了各阈值之间的层级关系，并指出这些阈值本身不构成局域化相变，为理解Hartree碎片化、共振对象的系综行为及长程相互作用的边缘性提供了系统的理论基础。

Abstract: In a slowly varying aperiodic potential $h_i=h\cos(2πβi^n+φ)$ with random power-law density interactions $V_{ij}/|i-j|^α$, the resonant-object ensemble changes across $α=2n$, wherever Hartree fragmentation is operative, from isolated two-level bonds to a mixture of fragmented multi-site clusters and isolated wing bonds that survive at finite density, while the pair-resonance scaling $x_{\rm pair}^{4-2n-α}\ln x_{\rm pair}\propto h^2/(Vt_0)$ is unchanged~\cite{letter}. Here we develop the microscopic resonance theory underlying these results, together with its domain of validity. We derive the exact phase-averaged resonance statistics --- the supply $N_0\propto L^{2-n}/h$, the correlated common-phase comb, and the closed-form Hartree variance --- proving that the interaction leaves the leading supply law unchanged. Exact construction of the resonant Fock-space graph at $L\le18$ shows that the order-one forward-branching scale $h_{\rm FB}\propto L^{2-n}$ carries no giant component: one-step branching and connectivity are inequivalent. We separate the fixed-pair matching law from the shell-averaged $q\ln(1/q)$ law of the bond ensemble and develop the comb into a mesoscopic shell theory; we map the fragmentation domain, with its support threshold $V_*(α)$ and the boundary-healing recursion; and we treat the marginal case $α=2$, where shell and matching marginalities compound into a double logarithm. Three long-range thresholds emerge with distinct meanings --- $α=1/2$ (the exact variance threshold of the random Fock-space energy and the square-summability boundary of the leading LIOM-dressing estimate), $α=2n$ (change of the local resonant objects), and $α=2$ (marginality of the long-range shell sum) --- none of which is, by itself, a localization transition.

</details>


### [257] [Learning rules for complex-valued patterns in networks of oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30800)
*Federico Sbravati,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将学习规则从实数二元自旋扩展至复数值自旋，从而实现对灰度模式（grayscale patterns）的稳健自然表示，提出了优于标准Hebbian学习的编码规则，并证明了复杂值自旋公式下Diederich和Opper结果的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是将学习规则推广到复数值自旋，以支持灰度模式的自然表示和存储，并证明在复合值自旋框架下Diederich与Opper关于局部增量学习规则结果的推广。

Method: 将自旋从实数二元推广至复数域，使自旋表现为多态神经元（multi-state neurons），并将信息存储在复数值权重矩阵中；提出一种优于Hebbian学习的编码规则，用于振荡器网络的模式检索。

Result: 所提出的规则在模式检索中优于标准方法（如Hebbian学习）；在复数值自旋公式下推广了Diederich和Opper的局部增量学习结果；并在不同条件下测试了振荡器系统的关联记忆行为，涵盖实数及复数的相关和随机模式。

Conclusion: 复数值自旋公式为灰度模式提供了稳健的表示，且所提出的学习规则在性能上优于传统方法，同时保持了神经网络的局部性和增量性要求。

Abstract: In this article, we extend learning rules from real binary to complex-valued spins. This formulation allows for a robust and natural representation of grayscale patterns, where spins behave as multi-state neurons and can be stored in a complex-valued weight matrix. We describe a rule that performs better than standard methods, such as Hebbian learning, to encode information in a suitable form for pattern retrieval with networks of oscillators. Since in neural networks it is of interest to have local and incremental learning rules, we prove the extension of a result by Diederich and Opper with our complex-valued spin formulation. We then test the behavior of the associative memory for the system of oscillators under different circumstances for both real-valued, as well as complex-valued correlated and random patterns.

</details>

### [258] [A Pythia8 Tune for Open Charm and Beauty Production in Fixed-Target Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29076)
*Matei Climescu,Didar Dobur,Kirill Skovpen*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出FTFT（Fixed-Target Fragmentation Tune）参数组，这是一组针对固定靶碰撞中粲与底夸克强子产生的Pythia8调谐参数，用于质心能量约20-42 GeV的固定靶实验，旨在精确预测束流束实验（如SHiP）中的中微子通量与隐藏粒子产额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在该能区准确描述重味产生是预测束流束实验中微子通量和隐藏粒子产额的关键。现有LHC调谐参数在固定靶能区可能不适用，需要针对该碰撞条件下的粲和底强子产生进行专门调谐。

Method: 调谐分两步进行：首先，将D介子在Feynman-$x$（$x_F$）、横动量平方（$p_T^2$）和电荷或领头粒子产生不对称性上的微分分布与来自π介子和质子束的固定靶实验数据进行拟合；其次，利用包含粲产生总截面（inclusive charm cross-section）的测量数据，为每种束流提取K因子（K-factors），以将模拟截面标定到实测数据。

Result: 拟合得到的粲碎裂（charm-fragmentation）参数和多部分子相互作用（multiparton-interaction）参数偏离了标准LHC调谐默认值，这一偏离与早期固定靶研究的结论一致。

Conclusion: FTFT调谐为固定靶碰撞中的重味产生提供了更准确的Pythia8参数集，有助于提升SHiP等束流束实验物理预测的可靠性。

Abstract: We present FTFT (Fixed-Target Fragmentation Tune), a set of Pythia8 parameters optimised for the production of charm and beauty hadrons in fixed-target collisions at centre-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s} \simeq 20-42$ GeV. Accurate heavy-flavour production in this regime is needed to predict neutrino fluxes and hidden-particle yields at beam-dump experiments such as SHiP. The tune is derived in two stages. First, differential distributions in Feynman-$x$ ($x_F$), transverse momentum squared ($p_T^2$), and charge or leading-particle production asymmetry of $D$ mesons are fitted to data from pion- and proton-beam fixed-target experiments. Second, $K$-factors for each beam are extracted from inclusive charm cross-section measurements to scale the simulated cross section to the measured data. The fitted charm-fragmentation and multiparton-interaction parameters depart from standard LHC-tuned defaults, in line with earlier fixed-target studies.

</details>


### [259] [Sensitivity of a Missing-Mass Search for a Light Dark Photon with a Positron Beam on a Hydrogen Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29159)
*Weizhi Xiong,Ashot Gasparian,Bogdan Wojtsekhowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文基于Geant4模拟研究了利用正电子束入射薄液氢靶并通过前向电磁量能器重建末态光子的方法，探测轻暗光子（light dark photon，A'）的灵敏度。该方法采用飞行中湮灭过程e⁺e⁻→γA'的丢失质量技术，可独立于暗光子衰变模式实现探测，在500 MeV束流下对A'质量7-19 MeV范围内，m_A'=19 MeV时ε²的预期灵敏度可达4×10⁻⁸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于Jefferson Lab正在进行的低能正电子装置研发工作，旨在通过模拟研究评估探测轻暗光子（light dark photon）的可行性，为后续实验设计提供依据。

Method: 采用基于Geant4的蒙特卡洛模拟，使用正电子束入射薄液氢靶，配合高分辨率前向电磁量能器（forward electromagnetic calorimeter）实现末态光子重建。利用飞行中湮灭（annihilation-in-flight）过程e⁺e⁻→γA'的丢失质量技术（missing-mass technique），在独立于暗光子衰变模式的前提下实现探测灵敏度评估，并计算标准QED湮灭和轫致辐射（bremsstrahlung）过程的本底贡献。

Result: 实验采用500 MeV束流，可覆盖A'质量范围为7-19 MeV；在m_A'=19 MeV处，对动力学混合参数ε²的投影灵敏度达到4×10⁻⁸。

Conclusion: 模拟结果表明，该实验方案能够对轻暗光子提供有意义的探测灵敏度，为Jefferson Lab低能正电子装置上的暗光子搜索实验提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: We present a Geant4-based simulation study of the sensitivity to a light dark photon, $A'$, using a positron beam incident on a thin liquid-hydrogen target and a high-resolution forward electromagnetic calorimeter for final-state photon reconstruction. This study is motivated by ongoing research and development toward a low-energy positron facility at Jefferson Lab. The proposed search employs the missing-mass technique in the annihilation-in-flight process $e^+e^- \to γA'$, providing sensitivity to the $A'$ independently of its decay mode. We evaluate the expected backgrounds from standard QED annihilation and bremsstrahlung processes and present the projected sensitivity to the kinetic-mixing parameter $ε^2$ as a function of the $A'$ mass. The experiment with 500~MeV beam will provide the data for $A'$ mass range 7-19 MeV. The projected sensitivity on $ε^2$ is $4\times10^{-8}$ at $m_{A'}=19$~MeV.

</details>


### [260] [Evidence for the dead-cone effect in bottom quark initiated jets produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29412)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本论文基于CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞下采集的59.8 fb⁻¹数据，测量了底夸克喷注的发射密度随去簇化子喷注间角距离的分布，并以4.3个标准差的显著性验证了死锥效应（dead-cone effect）的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量夸克对胶子共线辐射的抑制，即死锥效应，是部分子簇射（parton shower）中夸克质量效应的最主要体现。此前缺乏对其在高能对撞实验中直接、精细的检验，本研究旨在提供该效应的直接微分测量证据。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在√s=13 TeV下记录的质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度59.8 fb⁻¹），对源自顶夸克对产生的底夸克喷注（横动量40–200 GeV）进行迭代去簇化（iterative declustering），测量发射密度作为子喷注间角分离的函数。

Result: 实验数据与包含死锥效应的部分子簇射蒙特卡洛模型一致；相比之下，不含死锥效应的模型与数据不符，其显著性达4.3个标准差。

Conclusion: 该微分测量为量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics）中夸克质量效应——尤其是死锥效应——提供了实验确信的验证，并确立了包含该效应的部分子簇射模型在描述底夸克喷注动力学上的优越性。

Abstract: The suppression of collinear gluon emissions from a massive quark, or the ``dead-cone effect,'' is the primary manifestation of quark mass effects in parton showers. A proton-proton collision data set recorded by the CMS experiment at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV with an integrated luminosity of 59.8 fb$^{-1}$ is analyzed. A differential measurement is presented of the emission density of bottom quark jets, studied as a function of the angular separation between iteratively declustered subjets. The measurement is performed in bottom quark initiated jets with transverse momenta between 40 and 200 GeV originating from top quark pair production. Compared to data, a parton shower Monte Carlo model that includes the dead-cone effect is favored, while a model without the dead-cone effect is disfavored with a significance of 4.3 standard deviations.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: XENONnT实验通过弹性中微子-电子散射首次在暗物质探测器中测量太阳低能中微子，建立了迄今为止最低的中微子探测能量阈值（探测低至17 keV的太阳中微子），并以5.0σ的统计显著性排除了仅含背景的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳低能中微子的精确测量对理解太阳内部核反应和核物理具有重要意义，且此前尚未有暗物质探测器实现如此低能量阈值的中微子探测；该工作旨在探索液氙探测器在keV尺度中微子物理中的应用潜力。

Method: 利用XENONnT前两次科学运行数据，对应2.46吨·年的曝光量，通过弹性中微子-电子散射（elastic neutrino-electron scattering）测量1 keV至140 keV的电子反冲能量，实现了对低至17 keV太阳中微子的灵敏探测。

Result: 以5.0σ的统计显著性否定了仅含背景的假设，测得太阳pp中微子通量为(10.2 ± 2.0) × 10^10 cm^-2 s^-1，该结果较Borexino之前测量值偏大，但在1.9σ统计水平下与之一致。

Conclusion: 本工作连同XENONnT及其他液氙时间投影室（time projection chamber）对^8B太阳中微子的相干弹性中微子-核散射观测，共同展示了液氙探测器在keV尺度中微子物理中的日益增长潜力，是迈向下一代多功能观测站的重要里程碑。

Abstract: We report on the first measurement of low-energy solar neutrinos through elastic neutrino-electron scattering in a dark matter experiment, establishing the lowest energy threshold for any neutrino detection to date. The measurement utilizes data from the first two science runs of XENONnT, corresponding to an exposure of 2.46 t $\cdot$ y, and covers electron recoil energies between 1 keV and 140 keV, providing sensitivity to solar neutrinos with energies down to 17 keV. We reject the background-only hypothesis with a statistical significance of $5.0σ$ and measure a solar $pp$ neutrino flux of $(10.2 \pm 2.0) \times 10^{10}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$. The result is larger, but statistically consistent with the previous measurement by Borexino at $1.9σ$. Together with recent observations of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering of $^8$B solar neutrinos in XENONnT and other liquid-xenon time projection chambers, these results demonstrate the growing potential of liquid xenon detectors for neutrino physics down to the keV-scale and represent an important milestone towards a next-generation multipurpose observatory.

</details>


### [262] [From a Long-standing Prediction to a New Family of Hadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29502)
*Xining Wang,Kai Yi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了全粲四夸克（fully charmed tetraquark）从早期理论预言到大强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）实验突破的发展历程，重点介绍了南京师范大学和清华大学团队在CMS实验中的贡献，包括全粲四夸克家族的发现、共振干涉机制的建立以及量子数（quantum numbers）的首次确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全粲四夸克已从长期存在的理论预言演变为强子谱学（hadron spectroscopy）中最活跃的前沿方向之一，因此有必要对其发展历程、关键发现与理论意义进行系统梳理与回顾。

Method: 本文采用综述（review）的方式，回顾全粲四夸克从早期理论研究与实验搜寻到LHC近期突破的完整发展过程，并重点突出南京师范大学（Nanjing Normal University, NNU）和清华大学（Tsinghua University, THU）团队在CMS实验中的具体贡献。

Result: 综述总结了全粲四夸克家族的成功发现，确认了共振间干涉（interference）效应的存在，并首次确定了这些共振态的量子数。

Conclusion: 这些进展将原本对单一奇异共振态的搜寻转变为对新型强子家族的系统研究，为探索量子色动力学（Quantum Chromodynamics, QCD）的非微扰动力学开辟了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Fully charmed tetraquarks have evolved from a long-standing theoretical prediction into one of the most active frontiers in hadron spectroscopy. This article reviews their development from early theoretical studies and experimental searches to the recent breakthroughs at the Large Hadron Collider, with particular emphasis on the contributions of the Nanjing Normal University (NNU) and Tsinghua University (THU) teams at the CMS experiment. We summarize the discovery of the fully charmed tetraquark family, the establishment of interference among resonances, and the first determination of their quantum numbers. These advances have transformed the search for a single exotic resonance into the study of a new family of hadrons and opened a promising avenue for exploring the nonperturbative dynamics of Quantum Chromodynamics.

</details>


### [263] [Low-energy Muon-Nucleon scattering experiment: LUNE (White Paper)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29509)
*Chenlei An,Dong Bai,Ziyu Bai,Kai Chen,Liangwen Chen,Xiang Chen,Jianqiao Deng,Yanxin Dou,Yicheng Feng,Zekai Feng,Lu Gao,Chang Gong,Aiqiang Guo,Liang Han,Qundong Han,Defu Hou,Ruiwen Hou,Huigang Hu,Chen Ji,Xiangdong Ji,Vijay Kumar,Dikai Li,Jiuzhao Li,Liang Li,Qite Li,Xin-Qiang Li,Yuan Li,Qiming Liang,Yutie Liang,Dong Liu,Duanqing Liu,Langtian Liu,Weijie Liu,Zejia Lu,Maowu Nie,Ziwen Pan,Hua Pei,Jinkang Peng,Shusu Shi,Qintao Song,Xiaocheng Song,Xiangming Sun,Yichen Sun,Zhiyu Sun,Enke Wang,Fei Wang,Hulin Wang,Jike Wang,Xiang-Peng Wang,Xiao Wang,Xin-Nian Wang,Yangxi Wang,Yaping Wang,Zeren Simon Wang,Yanbing Wei,Yuehong Xie,Weizhi Xiong,Nu Xu,Yu Xu,Siqi Yang,Yadong Yang,Hang Yin,Xing-Bo Yuan,Dongliang Zhang,Ranyu Zhang,Ruitian Zhang,Xueheng Zhang,Yu Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Zihan Zhao,Shuai Zhou,XiaoKang Zhou,Xiaoyu Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LUNE（基于HIAF缪子源构建的实验合作组）的白皮书，阐述其利用高亮度、高品质缪子束（动量为0.5至7.5 GeV/c）进行精密缪子散射实验的科学目标、探测器概念与预期性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIAF提供的缪子束能量范围独特，介于低能电子装置与未来高能轻子-离子对撞机之间，为精密缪子散射实验填补了空白，可回答质子电荷半径之谜、核子电磁结构等核物理与粒子物理的基础问题。

Method: 实验分两个阶段推进：从弹性散射出发，逐步扩展到核子结构研究及超越标准模型（beyond-Standard-Model）的新物理搜寻；使用正、负缪子束进行弹性缪子-质子散射，并系统测量质子电磁形状因子，同时开展TMD、引力形状因子和核电荷半径等非弹性散射测量。

Result: 预计可将质子电荷半径的测定精度提升至约1.0%，通过正负缪子束的系统测量可精确研究双光子交换效应，并对量子电动力学进行严格检验；此外还将在3D核子/核结构研究中提供新见解。

Conclusion: LUNE实验将补充现有电子散射装置（如JLab、EicC和EIC），覆盖Coulomb畸变修正、核介质效应及超越标准模型迹象等重要课题，其白皮书概述了科学动机、探测器概念、预期性能与长期战略。

Abstract: The HIAF will provide high-intensity, high-quality muon beams with momenta from 0.5 to 7.5 GeV/c. This energy range is uniquely suited for precision muon scattering, bridging the gap between low-energy electron facilities and future high-energy lepton-ion colliders. In particular, HIAF will enable precision measurements with both positive and negative muon beams over a broad kinematic range, complementing existing electron-scattering facilities such as JLab, EicC and EIC.
  Based on HIAF muon source, the LUNE Collaboration has been established to address several fundamental questions in nuclear and particle physics, including the proton charge radius puzzle, nucleon electromagnetic structure, and the dynamics of quantum electrodynamics and hadronic interactions. The program proceeds in two phases, from elastic scattering to nucleon structure and beyond-Standard-Model searches.
  The experiment is expected to determine the proton charge radius with a precision of approximately 1.0\% using elastic muon-proton scattering. It will also perform systematic measurements of the proton electromagnetic form factors with both $μ^+$ and $μ^-$ beams, enabling precise studies of two-photon exchange effects and stringent tests of quantum electrodynamics. Beyond elastic scattering, LUNE will investigate TMD, gravitational form factors, and nuclear charge radii, providing new insights into the 3D structure of nucleons and nuclei. The experiment will further address important topics including Coulomb-distortion corrections, nuclear medium effects, and possible signatures of physics beyond the Standard Model.
  This white paper presents the scientific motivation, detector concept, expected performance, and long-term strategy of LUNE.

</details>
