Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究利用IceCube中微子天文台10.7年的上行缪子中微子数据（0.5–100 TeV），通过中微子在传播过程中与地球物质的相互作用效应，对低能标引力的理论场景加以限制，给出最大额外维度紧致化半径的上限以及多重标准模型副本数量的下限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级问题（hierarchy problem）仍是粒子物理学中悬而未决的问题，而若干解决该问题的理论会降低引力基本能标，从而在中微子扇区产生可观测的后果。作者旨在利用高能中微子观测数据对这些低能标引力场景进行实验约束。

Method: 基于IceCube中微子天文台10.7年的上行缪子中微子数据，能量范围为0.5至100 TeV。在此能区，低能标引力理论预言中微子穿过地球时会因物质效应产生特征性的谱畸变，以此作为鉴别信号进行统计分析。

Result: 在大额外维度（large extra dimension）模型背景下，对最大额外维度的紧致化半径给出R ≲ 0.17 μm（90%置信水平），该约束同时适用于正常和中微子质量反序（inverted ordering）。对多重标准模型副本（multiple Standard Model copies）场景，根据最轻中微子质量的取值，得到副本数下限最高可达N ≳ O(400)。部分参数空间为目前文献中最强约束，其他区域则探测到此前未被探索的参数空间。

Conclusion: IceCube数据对低能标引力场景提供了有效限制，在部分参数区域构成了现有文献所记载的最强约束，并在其他区域首次探索了此前未曾覆盖的参数空间。

Abstract: The hierarchy problem remains an open question in particle physics. A number of theories that address this problem lower the fundamental scale of gravity, resulting in observable consequences in the neutrino sector. In this work, we place constraints on low-scale gravity scenarios using high-energy neutrinos observed with the IceCube Neutrino Observatory. The analysis is based on 10.7 years of upward-going muon neutrino data in the energy range from 0.5 to 100 TeV. In this energy range, the theories predict characteristic spectral distortions arising from matter effects when neutrinos propagate through Earth. In the context of large extra dimension models, we constrain the compactification radius of the largest extra dimension to $R \lesssim 0.17\,μ\mathrm{m}$ at $90\%$ confidence level for both normal and inverted neutrino mass ordering. For scenarios with multiple Standard Model copies, we obtain lower limits of up to $N \gtrsim \mathcal{O}(400)$, depending on the value of the lightest neutrino mass. In parts of the parameter space, these results constitute the strongest constraints in the literature to our knowledge, while in other regions they probe previously unexplored parameter space.

</details>


### [265] [ConCA: Concentration-Aware Channel Attention for Fine-Grained Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30183)
*Yu-Sheng Liu,Yu-Chen Tung*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为浓度感知通道注意力（Concentration-Aware Channel Attention, ConCA）的轻量级通道注意力机制，通过将全局平均池化（GAP）与平移不变的负输入熵（NegEnt）配对形成双描述符，同时编码通道激活的幅度与空间集中度，并使用参数量随通道数线性增长的深度可分离一维卷积多层感知机（MLP）映射为逐通道权重。在六个细粒度视觉识别（FGVR）基准上，ConCA优于无注意力、SE-Net和ECA-Net基线以及四个基于更丰富描述符的模块，并在iNat2021-mini上跨八个骨干网络验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级通道注意力机制在图像分类中广泛应用，但在细粒度视觉识别（fine-grained visual recognition, FGVR）中的效果仍有限。现有模块大多通过全局平均池化（global average pooling, GAP）汇总每个通道，只捕捉激活幅度而忽略空间集中度，导致空间分布不同但均值相同的通道获得相同描述符，这是制约轻量级通道注意力在细粒度识别中表现的重要盲点。

Method: ConCA将每个通道的均值与平移不变的负输入熵（negative-input entropy, NegEnt）配对——NegEnt通过对取负的激活值进行softmax计算——形成同时编码幅度与集中度的双描述符。随后使用参数量随通道数线性增长的深度可分离一维卷积多层感知机（MLP）将描述符映射为逐通道注意力权重。

Result: 在六个细粒度基准数据集上，ConCA在受控的从头训练（from-scratch）协议下优于无注意力、SE-Net和ECA-Net基线，以及四个基于更丰富描述符的模块。在iNat2021-mini上，ConCA在八个骨干网络（backbone）上均表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果说明，在细粒度视觉识别中，通道描述符（channel descriptor）以及将描述符映射为注意力权重的逐通道门控（per-channel gating）机制，是轻量级通道注意力中重要但尚未充分探索的方面。

Abstract: Lightweight channel attention mechanisms are widely used in image classification, yet their effectiveness in fine-grained visual recognition (FGVR) remains limited. Most modules summarize each channel by global average pooling (GAP), which captures activation magnitude but ignores spatial concentration, so channels with different spatial distributions but identical means receive the same descriptor. We propose Concentration-Aware Channel Attention (ConCA), which pairs the mean with a shift-invariant negative-input entropy (NegEnt), computed via a softmax over the negated activations, forming a dual descriptor that jointly encodes magnitude and concentration. A depthwise 1-D convolutional multi-layer perceptron (MLP), whose parameter count is linear in the number of channels, maps the pair to a per-channel weight. On six fine-grained benchmarks, ConCA improves over attention-free, SE-Net, and ECA-Net baselines as well as four richer descriptor-based modules under a controlled from-scratch protocol, and it generalizes across eight backbones on iNat2021-mini. These results indicate that the channel descriptor, together with the per-channel gating that maps it to attention weights, is an important but underexplored aspect of lightweight channel attention in FGVR.

</details>


### [266] [Search for proton decay into a single charged antilepton and a massless invisible particle using the full pure water data set of Super-Kamiokande](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30361)

Girgus,P. Govindaraj,M. Posiadala-Zezula,Y. S. Prabhu,S. B. Boyd,R. Edwards,D. Hadley,M. O'Flaherty,B. Richards,A. Ali,B. Jamieson,C. Bronner,D. Horiguchi,A. Minamino,Y. Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于Super-Kamiokande探测器401千吨·年的纯水相曝光数据，搜索质子衰变为正电荷轻子加不可见中性粒子的过程（p→l⁺+X），未观察到超出大气中微子背景的显著信号，并将质子部分寿命下限分别提高到1.72×10³³年（p→e⁺+X）和0.61×10³³年（p→μ⁺+X），置信水平为90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子衰变（proton decay）是超出标准模型（beyond Standard Model）的许多物理理论（如大统一理论）的重要预言，对其进行实验搜索是检验这些理论的关键途径。本研究旨在利用更大的曝光量（401千吨·年）覆盖Super-Kamiokande完整的纯水运行阶段，从而提升对这一衰变模式的灵敏度。

Method: 实验利用Super-Kamiokande探测器全部纯水阶段的401千吨·年曝光数据，搜索质子衰变为带正电荷轻子（正电子或正μ子）与不可见、无质量、中性粒子（invisible, massless, neutral particle）的衰变通道，并对预期的（预期的大气中微子背景）进行扣除与统计比较。

Result: 未在超过预期大气中微子背景（atmospheric neutrino background）的水平上观察到质子衰变的显著迹象。在90%置信水平下，设置p→e⁺+X的部分寿命下限为1.72×10³³年，p→μ⁺+X的部分寿命下限为0.61×10³³年，分别较此前结果改善2倍和1.5倍。

Conclusion: 本搜索未发现质子衰变证据，并给出了更严格的质子部分寿命下限，显著提升了对此类衰变模式的实验约束。改进后的限制结果对相关超标准模型理论（如大统一理论）的检验具有重要意义。

Abstract: A search for proton decay via $p\rightarrow l^{+}+X$, where $l^{+}$ is a positively charged lepton and $X$ is an invisible, massless, neutral particle, was performed using a 401~kton$\cdot$years exposure representing the entire pure water phase of Super-Kamiokande. No significant indication of a proton decay was observed beyond the expected atmospheric neutrino background. Lower limits on the partial lifetime of the proton were set to at $1.72\times10^{33}$ years for $p\rightarrow e^{+}+X$ and $0.61\times10^{33}$ years for $p\rightarrow μ^{+}+X$ at the $90\%$ confidence level. These results improve on previous limits by factors of 2 and 1.5, respectively.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在LHCb实验的固定靶模式下，测量了质子-氩（pAr）和质子-质子（pp）碰撞中ψ(2S)与J/ψ产生的截面比，并发现pAr系统中出现了核效应（nuclear effects）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对粲偶素（charmonium）态在核介质中产生的抑制是理解量子色动力学（QCD）非微扰效应的重要探针，测量ψ(2S)与J/ψ的截面比有助于揭示原子核环境对粲偶素产生的影响。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在SMOG2存储单元中进行氩气和氢气气体注入，分别获得pAr和pp碰撞数据，在固定靶构型下（质心能量√sNN=113 GeV）测量ψ(2S)-to-J/ψ产生截面比，并研究其作为粲偶素横动量pT与质心系快度y*的函数的行为。

Result: 在-2.3<y*<0.0和0<pT<8 GeV/c范围内，pAr碰撞中的ψ(2S)/J/ψ比率与pp碰撞中的比值之比为0.90±0.04±0.02，表明pAr体系中出现了核效应。

Conclusion: 该测量为LHCb实验未来在更大系统（larger systems）中开展研究的物理解释提供了基线参考。

Abstract: A measurement of the $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ production cross-section ratio is performed in proton-argon ($p\mathrm{Ar}$) and proton-proton ($pp$) collisions in fixed-target mode at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=113\,\mathrm{GeV}$. Data samples were collected by the LHCb experiment during argon and hydrogen gas injections in the SMOG2 storage cell, resulting in $p\mathrm{Ar}$ and $pp$ collisions, respectively. The $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ production cross-section ratio is measured as a function of the charmonium transverse momentum, $p_{\mathrm{T}}$, and rapidity in the centre-of-mass system, $y^{*}$. The $ψ(2S)$-to-$J/ψ$ ratio in $p\mathrm{Ar}$ collisions over that in $pp$ collisions is measured to be $0.90 \pm 0.04 \pm 0.02$ for $-2.3<y^{*}<0.0$ and $0<p_{\mathrm{T}}<8\mathrm{GeV}/c$, indicating the emergence of nuclear effects in the $p\mathrm{Ar}$ system. This study acts as a baseline for the interpretation of future measurements with larger systems accessible by the LHCb experiment.

</details>

### [268] [Global Existence of classical solutions to 3D nonlinear Klein-Gordon equations with low-regularity initial data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29250)
*Wei Xu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维空间中非线性Klein-Gordon方程柯西问题（Cauchy problem）的全局存在性（global existence），在经典局部存在性的正则性范围内严格成立，通过高阶与低阶能量估计完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在经典局部存在性（classical local existence）的正则性区间内证明非线性Klein-Gordon方程在三维空间中的全局解存在性问题，填补该正则性范围内全局存在性证明的空白。

Method: 证明依赖于两个关键工具：（1）运用Georgiev和Popivanov的工作，将二次非线性项约化为三次项加上可由ghost能量控制（ghost-energy-controllable）的项，从而获得低阶估计；（2）建立了一个不含缩放算子（scaling operator）的精细Klainerman-Sobolev型不等式，为二阶导数提供足够的时间衰减，进而通过分部积分（integration by parts）在高阶估计中控制导数损失项。

Result: 论文在经典局部存在性的正则性范围内建立了三维非线性Klein-Gordon方程Cauchy问题的全局存在性，并给出了相应的高阶和低阶能量估计。

Conclusion: 通过结合二次非线性约化技巧与新的Klainerman-Sobolev型不等式，成功将该类方程的全局存在性证明推广到正则性临界区间，扩展了已有的全局存在性结果。

Abstract: This paper studies global existence for the Cauchy problem of nonlinear Klein-Gordon equations in three space dimensions, strictly within the regularity regime of classical local existence. We prove it via higher-order and lower-order energy estimates. The proof relies on two key ingredients. The first is due to the work of Georgiev and Popivanov, which reduces quadratic nonlinearities to cubic terms plus ghost-energy-controllable terms, securing lower-order estimates. The second is a sharp Klainerman-Sobolev-type inequality without the scaling operator, established herein, which yields enough time decay for derivatives up to second order; integration by parts then controls derivative loss terms in the higher-order estimates.

</details>


### [269] [Time-modulated refocusing in inhomogeneous medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29542)
*Hongyu Liu,Wei Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀耗散介质中瞬时时间镜像（instantaneous time mirror）无法产生平衡点聚焦的问题，提出通过微局域选择的反向传播横磁（transverse-magnetic）分量，利用半经典模式和归一化方向能量设计出光滑、非负、物理可实现的调制，使点源场在焦点尺度上产生与方向衰减无关的前沿分布，且空间位移和半振幅面积均为O(h²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀耗散介质中，不同射线经历不同的往返衰减，导致瞬时时间镜像在回溯射线时无法形成平衡的点聚焦。解决这一物理限制，实现可用的时间反演聚焦是该研究的基本动机。

Method: 采用麦克斯韦-德拜（Maxwell–Debye）场方程，对薄壳的短脉冲调制产生微局域选择的反向传播横磁分量；通过精确分解隔离脉冲产生的返回场，运用半经典模式约化（semiclassical mode reduction）得到标量返回算子，其主符号分离了时间脉冲响应、壳调制和累积材料损耗。

Result: 分析表明几何畸变在出射与返回两支中相互抵消，而物理衰减保留。基于无损反向传播子计算的归一化方向能量与返回符号的领先衰减因子相同，以其倒数定义的调制可使选定源侧增益均衡至O(h)相对误差。在该调制下，点源场在焦点尺度上具有独立于方向衰减的前沿分布，位移和半振幅面积均为O(h²)。

Conclusion: 在不依赖完整衰减模型的前提下，即可构造可实现的空间调制使瞬时时间镜像在耗散介质中达到近似最优的聚焦效果，聚焦误差以O(h²)阶受控。

Abstract: In an inhomogeneous dissipative medium, different rays experience different round-trip attenuation, so an instantaneous time mirror may retrace rays without producing a balanced point focus. We solve this problem for a microlocally selected back-propagating transverse-magnetic component generated by a short pulsating modulation of a thin shell surrounding an inhomogeneous Debye medium with Ohmic conductivity. An exact decomposition of the Maxwell--Debye field isolates the pulse-generated return, and a semiclassical mode reduction yields a scalar returned operator whose principal symbol separates the temporal pulse response, shell modulation, and accumulated material loss. Our analysis shows that geometric distortion and amplitude attenuation are both involved in wave propagation, but the geometric distortion cancels between the outgoing and returning branches, whereas physical attenuation remains. A normalized directional energy, computed from pre-modulation measurements using the known lossless backward propagator, contains the same leading attenuation factor as the returned symbol. Its reciprocal defines a smooth, nonnegative, physically admissible modulation that equalizes the selected source-side gain up to a relative $O(h)$ error without requiring a complete attenuation model. With such a modulation, the field generated by a point source has a focal-scale leading profile independent of directional attenuation, with a unique maximum at the source and coherent return time. In every fixed focal window, the maximum is displaced by at most $O(h^2)$ in space and time, and the spatial half-amplitude area is $O(h^2)$.

</details>


### [270] [Endpoint Mapping Properties of Wave Operators for Two-Dimensional Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30602)
*Han Cheng,Changxing Miao,Xiaohua Yao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了二维薛定谔算子波算子（wave operator）在端点空间$L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)$和$L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$上的尖锐映射性质，结合已知的非端点$L^p$理论，完整分类了二维波算子的$L^p$映射行为。结果显示，在端点$p=1$和$p=\infty$处出现出乎意料的阈值范式反转：当零是正则点时，波算子虽非$L^1$和$L^\infty$有界，但满足Cauchy主值类型的Calderón–Zygmund替代估计；而当零能量出现s波共振（s-wave resonance）时，波算子在两个端点空间上反而有界，这与维度$n\ge3$的情形形成鲜明对比。文章还确定了其余零能谱配置（p波共振、零特征值）下的端点行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维薛定谔波算子的端点$L^p$映射性质此前尚不明确，而端点行为对于理解波算子的完整映射谱至关重要。已有非端点$L^p$理论已建立，但端点$p=1$和$p=\infty$处的行为特别是零能量阈值奇异性如何影响端点映射，仍是未解决的关键问题。

Method: 本研究利用谱理论（spectral theory）和调和分析工具，对二维薛定谔算子分析零能量的各谱配置（正则点、s波共振、p波共振、零特征值）下波算子的端点映射性质进行系统分类。方法包括建立尖锐的端点估计，并借助已有的非端点$L^p$理论进行完整性补充。

Result: 主要结果包括：当零为正则点时，波算子满足$L^1\to L^{1,\infty}$、$\mathcal{H}^1\to L^1$和$L^\infty\to \mathrm{BMO}$的Calderón–Zygmund型估计；当零为s波共振（且无其他阈值障碍）时，波算子在$L^1$和$L^\infty$上均有界；p波共振阻碍$L^1$和$L^\infty$两个端点空间的有界性；零特征值情形下，端点有界性由s波和p波共振是否存在以及零能量本征函数满足的显式二阶谐波矩消减条件决定。

Conclusion: 二维波算子端点行为的完整分类已获得，揭示了一个重要且反直觉的现象：在二维中s波共振改善而非恶化了波算子的端点行为，这与三维及以上维数的情况（仅正则情形有利）截然相反。这为二维散射理论（scattering theory）的端点映射研究提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We establish sharp endpoint mapping properties for the wave operators $W_\pm(H,-Δ)$ of two-dimensional Schrödinger operators $H=-Δ+V$ with real-valued decaying potentials $V$. Together with the known non-endpoint $L^p$ theory, our results give a complete classification of the $L^p$ mapping properties of the two-dimensional wave operators, and reveal an unexpected reversal of the usual threshold paradigm at the endpoints $p=1$ and $p=\infty$.
  When zero is a regular point of $H$, the wave operators fail to be bounded on $L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)$ and on $L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$, but they satisfy the atural substitute estimates of Calderón--Zygmund type: $$ L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)\longrightarrow L^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{R}^2),\ \ \ \mathcal{H}^1(\mathbb{R}^2)\longrightarrow L^1(\mathbb{R}^2),\ \ \ L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)\longrightarrow \mathrm{BMO}(\mathbb{R}^2). $$ When zero is instead a threshold singularity of the first kind---an s-wave resonance with no other threshold obstruction, the wave operators are bounded on both endpoint spaces $L^1(\mathbb{R}^2)$ and $L^\infty(\mathbb{R}^2)$. Thus, in dimension two, an s-wave resonance improves the endpoint behavior of the wave operators, in sharp contrast with dimensions $n\ge3$, where the only regular case is the favorable one.
  We also determine the endpoint behavior in the remaining zero-energy spectral configurations of $H$. A p-wave resonance obstructs both the $L^1$- and the $L^\infty$-boundedness of the wave operators, while in the zero-eigenvalue case we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions for endpoint boundedness, expressed in terms of the presence of s- and p-wave resonances and of explicit second-order harmonic moment cancellations satisfied by the zero-energy eigenfunctions.

</details>


### [271] [Higher-order Approximate Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31098)
*Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将Fushchych–Shtelen（FS）近似对称方法推广到任意阶，建立了与Baikov–Gazizov–Ibragimov（BGI）方法的对应关系，并对扰动三次波方程进行了对称分类、约化和长期有效性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fushchych和Shtelen最初通过小参数展开扰动微分方程解来定义近似对称，但这一构造此前仅停留在低阶。本文旨在将其推广至任意阶，并系统比较FS方法与BGI方法之间的异同。

Method: 采用系数映射（coefficient-map）表述推导出阶数为p的FS系统，并在显式假设下建立其与BGI方法的对应关系。对扰动三次波方程，通过等价分类非线性项，分析FS膨胀（dilation）对称；利用Lorentz代数与继承的FS膨胀进行联合约化，并通过周期行波检验FS展开的长期有效性，引入相位重整化（phase renormalization）得到有界且阶一致的近似。

Result: 分类得到一般幂族（generic power family）和在例外指数处的对数分支；两类方法相关但不互换，BGI延续方法在特定条件下选取FS分支的真子族。联合约化整合了所有FS正规形，并恢复了1989年FS信件中的一阶解。在两种框架公共部分，对数修正源于指数受扰动依赖的幂律展开。周期行波分析得到一阶校正和二阶长期结构，相位重整化产生有界近似，且修正波数与对称分类所得标度权重一致。

Conclusion: FS方法与BGI方法在任意阶下存在明确对应但不可互换，FS框架可完整约化扰动波方程并通过相位重整化维持长期有效性，其解析结果与对称分类的标度预测一致。

Abstract: Fushchych and Shtelen defined approximate symmetry by expanding the solution of a perturbed differential equation in the small parameter, replacing the original equation by a triangular system for the expansion coefficients. We extend this construction to arbitrary order. A coefficient-map formulation yields the order-$p$ Fushchych--Shtelen (FS) system and establishes, under explicit hypotheses, a correspondence with the Baikov--Gazizov--Ibragimov (BGI) method.
  The two frameworks are compared for a perturbed cubic wave equation. Up to equivalences, we classify the nonlinearities admitting an FS dilation through second order. The classification consists of a generic power family and logarithmic branches at exceptional exponents. Among perturbations nontrivial at first order, vertical BGI continuations select a proper subfamily of one FS branch, and a second common branch arises when the perturbation first enters at second order; the two methods are related but not interchangeable. Joint reduction by the Lorentz algebra and the inherited FS dilation integrates all resulting FS normal forms; the explicit solutions of the 1989 Fushchych--Shtelen letter are recovered as the first-order members of this reduction. In the part common to both frameworks, the logarithmic corrections arise from the expansion of a power law with a perturbation-dependent exponent.
  Finally, periodic travelling waves are used to examine the long-scale validity of the FS expansion. The first correction is obtained by quadrature, the second-order secular structure is isolated, and phase renormalization produces bounded order-consistent approximations. For integer-power members of the generic family, the amplitude dependence of the corrected wavenumber agrees with the scaling weights obtained from the symmetry classification.

</details>


<div id='math.QA'></div>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [272] [Poisson bialgebras by deformations-to-quasiclassical limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29782)
*Siyuan Chen,Chengming Bai*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在双代数（bialgebra）层面推广了Poisson代数作为结合代数形变拟经典极限的过程，推导出Poisson双代数作为交换余交换反对称无穷小双代数形变的拟经典极限，并将其刻画为Lie双代数量子化过程的"无穷小"版本。该过程等价地通过匹配对（matched pair）结合代数形变和标准Manin三元组（Manin triple）结合代数形变加以描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poisson代数是交换结合代数的结合代数形变的拟经典极限，但这一过程此前未在双代数层面建立。本文旨在将该形变-拟经典极限过程推广到双代数范畴，填补该理论空白。

Method: 作者从反对称无穷小双代数（antisymmetric infinitesimal bialgebra）出发，考察其结合代数形变，在交换余交换情形下推导出Poisson双代数的拟经典极限。同时引入了匹配对结合代数的形变和标准Manin三元组的形变概念，证明其分别与上述过程等价，并利用相干导子（coherent derivations）加以说明。

Result: 建立了Poisson双代数作为交换余交换反对称无穷小双代数形变拟经典极限的框架；给出了匹配对Poisson代数与标准Manin三元组Poisson代数的对应拟经典极限；并展示了这些等价过程的相干导子刻画。

Conclusion: 该形变-拟经典极限过程可视为Lie双代数在Hopf代数框架下量子化过程的"无穷小"版本，为双代数层面的量子化与经典化研究提供了新的等价框架。

Abstract: Poisson algebras are the quasiclassical limits of associative algebra deformations of commutative associative algebras.
  This paper extends this process to the level of bialgebras. We derive Poisson bialgebras as the quasiclassical limits of antisymmetric infinitesimal bialgebra deformations of commutative and cocommutative antisymmetric
  infinitesimal bialgebras. It might be regarded as the ``infinitesimal" version of the
  quantization process of Lie bialgebras in terms of Hopf algebras. Such deformations-quasiclassical limits
  process for a Poisson bialgebra is equivalently characterized in
  terms of the introduced notions of
  deformations of a matched pair of associative algebras as well as a standard Manin triple of associative
  algebras, whose corresponding quasiclassical limits
  are a matched pair of Poisson algebras and a standard Manin triple of Poisson algebras,
  respectively. We illustrate these equivalent deformations-quasiclassical
  limits processes via coherent derivations.

</details>

### [273] [Active wetting transition in cell aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28912)
*MJ Franco-Oñate,Hanno I. Hennighausen,Ricard Alert*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本研究将细胞聚集体建模为活性极性液体液滴，预测了组织铺展过程中从部分润湿到完全润湿的不连续活性润湿转变，该转变源于组织表面张力与活性牵引力之间的竞争，并通过活性Young-Dupré方程加以描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物学过程中细胞聚集体在基质上铺展是一个活性润湿（active wetting）过程，但现有组织铺展模型未能显式描述细胞聚集体达到非零接触角的部分润湿（partial wetting）状态。

Method: 将细胞聚集体建模为活性极性液体液滴（active polar liquid droplets），通过推导包含活性牵引力贡献的活性Young-Dupré方程，分析表面张力与活性力竞争下的润湿转变行为。

Result: 预测了部分润湿与完全润湿之间的不连续相变，存在部分润湿和完全润湿并存的参数区间；与被动润湿不同，该转变依赖于组织黏度和基底摩擦等动力学参数；收缩性细胞内活性应力会提高接触角甚至导致完全去润湿（dewetting）。

Conclusion: 该工作预测了组织的活性润湿转变，并能够刻画实验中常观察到的部分润湿状态。

Abstract: During many biological processes, cell aggregates spread on substrates in a process of active wetting. The existing models of tissue spreading, however, do not explicitly capture the state of partial wetting, in which the cell aggregate reaches a non-zero contact angle. Here, modeling cell aggregates as active polar liquid droplets, we predict a transition between partial and complete wetting. This transition emerges from the competition between tissue surface tension and active traction forces, which is captured in an active Young-Dupré equation. The active wetting transition is discontinuous, and it features a region of parameters in which both partial and complete wetting are possible. Unlike in passive wetting, the transition depends on dynamical parameters such as the tissue viscosity and substrate friction. Finally, adding contractile intercellular active stresses leads to higher contact angles and even full dewetting. Overall, our work predicts an active wetting transition for tissues and it captures the partial wetting states often seen in experiments.

</details>
