# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [Dynamical backaction in nanoscale superfluid electromechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29405)
*Marek Talíř,Filip Novotný,Balázs Szalai,Nasiruddin Mondal,Emil Varga*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种机电系统，将超流$^4$He纳米流体声学谐振器中的第4声（4th sound）声学谐振模式与超导LC谐振电路在边带解析（sideband-resolved）区域耦合，实现了机电诱导透明（EMIT）、光学弹簧效应以及边带阻尼与放大，并通过旋转低温恒温器展示了量子化涡旋（quantized vortices）的探测灵敏度及相关雪崩式俘获/释放过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流$^4$He纳米流体声学谐振器可用于研究量子化涡旋，从二维超流湍流中的集体行为到由旋转产生的少数甚至单个涡旋。为达到探测单个量子化涡旋所需的灵敏度，需要发展以光机（optomechanical）或类光机方式为灵感的读出机制。

Method: 构建了一个机电一体化系统，将第4声声学谐振与超导LC谐振电路在边带解析区相耦合；利用旋转低温恒温器实现对量子化涡旋的探测。

Result: 实验演示了机电诱导透明（EMIT）、光学弹簧效应（optical spring effect）以及光机边带阻尼与放大现象；通过旋转低温恒温器，展示了系统对量子化涡旋的灵敏度，涡旋可在纳米流体腔体内以雪崩式过程被俘获和释放。

Conclusion: 该机电读出机制为实现探测单个量子化涡旋所需的灵敏度水平提供了可行路径，并为研究超流$^4$He中二维湍流的集体行为至单涡旋尺度的物理现象提供了实验平台。

Abstract: Nanofluidic acoustic resonators employing superfluid $^4$He can be used to study quantized vortices from collective behavior in two-dimensional superfluid turbulence down to few individual vortices created by rotation. In order to improve sensitivity to the level needed for probing individual quantized vortices, readout mechanisms employing optomechanical or optomechanics-inspired approaches seem to be promising in this regard. In this work, we develop an electromechanical system, which couples a 4$^\mathrm{th}$ sound acoustic resonance to a superconducting LC tank circuit in a sideband-resolved regime. Using this system, we demonstrate electromechanically induced transparency (EMIT), optical spring effect and optomechanical sideband damping and amplification. Furthermore, by rotating the cryostat, we demonstrate sensitivity to quantized vortices, which can become trapped and released in avalanche-like process inside the nanofluidic volume.

</details>


### [275] [Chiral-symmetry breaking and degeneracy in Koch fractal geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30007)
*L. L. Lage,A. Latgé*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Koch曲线上构建的Su–Schrieffer–Heeger（SSH）模型，发现三角形几何引入的同子格跃迁会破坏手征对称性并改变传统拓扑行为；局部破坏性干涉产生高度简并的平带能级，而其简并度与谱权重由Koch曲线的自相似连接性控制，揭示了局部干涉与全局分形连接在调控电子性质中的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分形几何（fractal geometry）为调控量子干涉、拓扑和局域化提供了独特平台，但传统SSH链的拓扑性质在分形几何修饰下如何改变尚不清楚。

Method: 在Koch曲线上构造SSH模型，引入三角形几何单元中的同子格跃迁，并通过实空间局域标记（real-space local marker）分析其拓扑行为与电子谱结构。

Result: 三角形单元内的破坏性干涉在E=-t处产生高度简并的平带能级；该能级的简并度与谱权重受Koch曲线自相似连接性控制，层级结构减少了独立平带态的数量并将能谱重组为精细的能级分布。

Conclusion: 局部三角形干涉与全局分形连接性在塑造几何修饰SSH链的电子性质中发挥互补作用。

Abstract: Fractal geometries provide a distinctive platform for controlling quantum interference, topology, and localization. We investigate the Su--Schrieffer--Heeger (SSH) model constructed on the Koch curve, where a triangular geometry enables additional same-sublattice hoppings that break chiral symmetry and modify the conventional topological behavior captured by a real-space local marker. Destructive interference within the triangular units produces a highly degenerate flat level at $E=-t$. Although this level originates from the local geometry, its degeneracy and spectral weight are controlled by the self-similar connectivity of the Koch curve. The hierarchy reduces the number of independent flat states and reorganizes the spectrum into an intricate distribution of energy levels. Our results reveal complementary roles for local triangular interference and global fractal connectivity in shaping the electronic properties of geometrically modified SSH chains.

</details>


### [276] [Temperature Dependence of the Refractive Index for AlAsGaSb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30547)
*Helena Janowska,Wojciech Charaszkiewicz,Maja Wasiluk,Markus Peil,Teemu Taskinen,Joonas Hilska,Abhiroop Chellu,Teemu Hakkarainen,Anna Musiał*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文通过实验测量与数值拟合，提取了两种AlGaAsSb合金在11.5 K至300 K温度范围内的折射率温度依赖关系，为工作在第三电信窗口、晶格匹配于GaSb衬底的分布式布拉格反射镜（distributed Bragg reflector, DBR）的低温设计提供了关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子多层结构（如分布式布拉格反射镜）的精确设计与优化需要材料光学常数，尤其是折射率的温度依赖数据。尽管常用半导体的这些参数已有充分研究，但新兴材料（如锑化物）尤其在新的光谱区间内往往仅有室温数据，限制了低温量子光子学器件的可靠设计。

Method: 研究者制备了晶格匹配于GaSb衬底、设计用于第三电信窗口的DBR结构，在11.5 K至300 K温度范围内测量反射光谱，并采用转移矩阵法（transfer matrix method, TMM）结合专用递归数值拟合算法对光谱进行拟合。初始参数包括由扫描电子显微镜（scanning electron microscopy, SEM）确定的层厚以及文献中室温折射率值。

Result: 该拟合方法成功提取了两种适用于1.5 μm波长DBR反射镜的AlGaAsSb合金的温度依赖折射率数据，覆盖了11.5 K至300 K的低温范围。

Conclusion: 所得结果可为DBR设计提供关键输入参数，确保在低温工作条件下（量子发射器高效运行所需）实现正确的停止带定位和高反射率。

Abstract: Accurate design and optimization of photonic multilayer structures like distributed Bragg reflectors (DBRs) require precise knowledge of material optical constants, particularly the temperature dependence of the refractive index. While these parameters are well established for widely used semiconductors, for emerging materials such as antimonides they are often limited to room-temperature data, especially for new spectral ranges of interest. Antimonide compounds, in particular GaSb-based alloys, are promising for quantum photonics applications. In this work, we investigated DBRs lattice-matched to GaSb and designed for operation in the third telecommunication window. Reflectivity spectra were measured in the temperature range from 11.5 K to 300 K, and then fitted using the transfer matrix method (TMM), combined with a dedicated recursive numerical fitting algorithm. Initial parameters included layer thicknesses determined by scanning electron microscopy (SEM) and literature values of refractive indices at room temperature. This approach enabled extraction of the temperature-dependent refractive indices of two AlGaAsSb alloys suitable for forming DBR mirrors for 1.5 um wavelengths. The obtained results provide essential input for reliable DBR design, ensuring proper stopband positioning and high reflectivity under cryogenic operating conditions required for efficient quantum emitter performance.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [Statistical Properties of a Fluctuation-Driven Nanomechanical Duffing Resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28731)
*Monan Ma,Nathan Welles,Johnathon Barbish,Ismet I. Kaya,M. Selim Hanay,Oleksiy Svitelskiy,Mark R. Paul,Kamil L. Ekinci*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了纳米力学双端固支梁谐振器中多个模式的非线性波动动力学，通过引入基于模式涨落幅度的有效温度（effective temperature），观察到随非线性增强而出现的有效热能降低以及响应统计从高斯分布（Gaussian distribution）向钝峰分布（platykurtic distribution）的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解纳米机械谐振器在高激励幅度下从谐振态向非线性Duffing态的过渡动力学，为表征多模式非线性振动行为提供定量框架。

Method: 对双端固支梁谐振器的多个机械模式施加中心频率位于谐振位置的高斯力噪声，在逐步增大力幅度的同时监测模式响应；基于模式涨落幅度定义有效温度以表征动力学状态。

Result: 在非线性效应于大振幅下凸显时，观察到模式有效热能降低，响应统计由高斯分布转变为钝峰分布，与Duffing势中的预期动力学一致。

Conclusion: 有效温度和响应统计的交叉行为与Duffing非线性的理论预期相符，提供了一种在实验上表征非线性纳米机械模式动力学的手段。

Abstract: We investigate the fluctuating nonlinear dynamics of multiple modes of a nanomechanical doubly clamped beam resonator. Each mode is driven by Gaussian force noise centered around the resonance; the mode response is monitored while the force magnitude is increased, inducing a transition from harmonic to the nonlinear Duffing regime. To characterize the dynamics, we introduce an effective temperature based on the mode fluctuation amplitude. As the nonlinearity becomes prominent at large amplitudes, we observe a reduction in the effective thermal energy and a crossover in response statistics from Gaussian to platykurtic, consistent with the dynamics expected in a Duffing potential.

</details>


### [278] [Domain walls with alternating magnetic order in a model with dipolar coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28831)
*G. M. Wysin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文分析了一维链状拉长纳米级磁性岛（其中偶极相互作用）具有逐位交替序的静态畴壁性质。在横向外加磁场下，假设仅考虑最近邻（NN）偶极相互作用，通过数值弛豫模拟和双子格连续介质理论，发现当单轴各向异性常数K1逼近NN偶极耦合常数D时，畴壁宽度无限增长；外加场引起磁偶极轻微倾斜，且两个子格上的偶极在沿格子扫描时沿相反方向旋转，导致畴壁具有大的纵向磁矩和一个依赖于链长奇偶性的拓扑横向磁矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维拉长纳米磁性岛链中由偶极相互作用驱动的交错（交替）磁序及其静态畴壁的性质，以深入理解这类人造自旋系统中的拓扑和磁学行为。

Method: 在横向外加磁场下，将纳米磁性岛的磁偶极表示为单位长度固定的宏自旋（macrospin），假设仅考虑最近邻偶极相互作用，采用数值弛豫模拟（numerical relaxation simulation）和双子格连续介质理论（two-sublattice continuum theory）对连接两个简并交替态的畴壁进行分析。

Result: 畴壁宽度在单轴各向异性常数K1接近NN偶极耦合常数D时无限增大，且大x极限下连续解与格子数值解高度一致。外加场使偶极向场方向轻微倾斜（在畴壁中心最大）；两个子格上的偶极沿晶格扫描时向相反方向旋转，产生显著的纵向磁矩；较小的横向磁矩具有依赖于链长奇偶性的拓扑贡献。

Conclusion: 该一维交替磁序系统中的畴壁展现了随各向异性与偶极耦合比值变化的临界展宽行为，以及由子格反向旋转导致的显著纵向磁矩和与链长奇偶性相关的拓扑横向磁矩，为理解人造纳米磁性系统的拓扑畴壁性质提供了定量框架。

Abstract: A model for a one-dimensional chain of elongated nano-scale magnetic islands with dipole interactions is analyzed here for the properties of its static domain walls with site-by-site alternating order. The anisotropic magnetic islands on a nonmagnetic substrate have their longer axes oriented transverse ($y$-direction) to the chain direction ($x$-direction), in a transverse applied magnetic field. The islands' magnetic dipoles $\vecμ_n$ are represented as macrospins of fixed length $μ$. The nearest-neighbor (NN) dipole interactions drive transverse alternating order, allowing for doubly-degenerate, uniform, static, $y$-alternating states, where the dipoles alternately point transverse to the chain direction, as in $\vecμ_n = \pm(-1)^n μ\hat{y}$. Assuming only NN interactions, the domain walls connecting these two alternating states are found with numerical relaxation simulations and analyzed in a two-sublattice continuum theory. As the uniaxial anisotropy constant $K_1$ descends from larger values until it closely approaches the NN dipolar coupling constant $D$, the domain wall width grows indefinitely, and the large-$x$ continuum solutions closely approach the lattice numerical solutions. The applied field produces a very slight canting of the dipoles towards the field, maximum in the center of the domain wall. The dipoles on the two sublattices are found to rotate in {\em opposite senses} as one scans along the lattice, resulting in a large longitudinal magnetic moment of the domain wall. A much smaller transverse magnetic moment has a topological contribution that depends on whether the chain length is odd or even.

</details>


### [279] [Electrochemical impedance spectroscopy of graphene nanogaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28836)
*Patrick A. McKee,Chris S. DeMellier,Robin N. Schipper,Henk W. Ch. Postma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了石墨烯纳米间隙（graphene nanogap）在水性环境中的界面电化学行为。通过在惰性气氛下可控电击穿制备纳米间隙，利用电化学阻抗谱（electrochemical impedance spectroscopy）揭示了界面临界超薄导电膜中的Warburg扩散行为，并通过等效电路模型提取了有效纳米间隙长度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯纳米间隙是纳米尺度电化学与传感器件的新兴平台，有望用于下一代生物分子测序，但其在水性环境中的界面行为，尤其是频率相关的阻抗和石墨烯边缘的电荷传输机制，仍缺乏充分表征。

Method: 在惰性气氛下通过可控电击穿（controlled electrical breakdown）制备石墨烯纳米间隙，暴露于环境条件后，利用电化学阻抗谱研究其频率响应，并结合等效电路模型（equivalent-circuit model）对阻抗数据进行定量拟合。

Result: 发现表面污染层在石墨烯边缘间吸附的超薄导电膜中支撑电化学活性；阻抗谱表现出与Warburg元件（扩散过程）一致的频率依赖响应；阻抗随液体pH系统性地变化，反映了电化学反应-扩散过程的改变；等效电路模型可从阻抗谱中提取有效纳米间隙长度尺度。

Conclusion: 石墨烯纳米间隙在含水环境中的阻抗行为可由Warburg扩散主导的等效电路模型定量描述，其提取的有效间隙长度尺度提供了与隧穿测量（tunneling measurement）互补的界面结构信息。

Abstract: Graphene nanogaps represent an emerging platform for nanoscale electrochemical and sensing devices, with potential applications in next-generation biomolecular sequencing. However, their interfacial behavior in aqueous environments remains poorly characterized, particularly with respect to frequency-dependent impedance and charge transport mechanisms at the graphene edge. We fabricate graphene nanogaps by controlled electrical breakdown in an inert atmosphere and study their electrochemical response. Upon exposure to ambient conditions, a surface contamination layer supports electrochemical activity within an adsorbed ultrathin conductive film between the graphene edges. Electrochemical impedance spectroscopy reveals distinct frequency-dependent responses consistent with a Warburg element associated with diffusion in this confined interfacial film. The impedance evolves systematically with liquid $\mathrm{p}H$, reflecting changes in electrochemical reaction-diffusion processes at the graphene edges. A quantitative equivalent-circuit model captures these effects and enables extraction of an effective nanogap length scale from impedance spectra, providing information complementary to that obtained from tunneling measurements.

</details>


### [280] [Design and Modeling of the Charge Readout of a SiMOS Quantum Dot with a Single Electron Transistor and CryoCMOS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28906)
*Adam Quinn,Troy England,Andrew Li,Sharmila Mustari Nandita*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于硅MOS（SiMOS）量子点的单电子自旋量子比特读出头，提出了将单电子晶体管（SET）与定制的低温CMOS放大和数字化链路相结合的优化接口方案，并设计了Quandarum项目下的首款低温读出专用集成电路（ASIC）——QNDR1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于与成熟CMOS制造工艺的兼容性，硅MOS量子点中的单电子自旋量子比特有望扩展至千或百万量子比特系统，但现有读出头依赖笨重射频组件或室温互连，难以满足大规模集成需求。

Method: 提出将单电子晶体管与定制的低温CMOS放大和数字化链路相结合，避免使用笨重射频组件和室温互连，并在此基础上设计优化了量子比特-SET低温CMOS接口，最终实现QNDR1专用集成电路的设计。

Result: 完成了QNDR1 ASIC的设计，这是Quandarum项目下首款低温读出专用集成电路，同时给出了接口优化的设计技术和仿真结果。

Conclusion: QNDR1 ASIC的目标是开发用于高能物理的多通道自旋量子比特探测器件，该方案具有无需射频组件和室温互连的优势，为大规模量子比特系统的读出头提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Single electron spin qubits trapped in SiMOS quantum dots are a promising technology for scaling to thousand- or million-qubit systems due to their compatibility with mature CMOS manufacturing processes. A readout system that combines a single electron transistor with a custom cryogenic CMOS amplification and digitization chain offers key advantages by avoiding the use of bulky RF components or room-temperature interconnects. We present design techniques and simulation results for an optimized qubit-SET cryoCMOS interface, culminating in the design of the QNDR1 ASIC, the first cryogenic readout ASIC designed under the Quandarum project, which targets the development of a many-channel spin qubit based detector for use in high energy physics.

</details>


### [281] [Chiral Tubular Magnonic Crystals with Periodic Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28928)
*P. Contreras-Gallardo,J. Flores-Farias,B. Mimica-Figari,V. Puliafito,P. Landeros,R. A. Gallardo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由铁磁纳米管构成的手性管状磁子晶体，其界面Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）沿管长周期性调制。通过适应圆柱几何的平面波形式计算自旋波能带，发现管状几何将部分界面DMI转化为类似各向异性的有效内场，产生符号依赖的频率景观，导致平坦弱色散能带出现，为弯曲结构中非互易自旋波和局域模式的控制提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面手性磁子晶体中周期DMI的作用已被研究，但管状（圆柱）几何对周期DMI角色的影响尚不明确；探索弯曲架构中非互易自旋波和局域模式的调控手段是该研究的主要动机。

Method: 采用适应圆柱几何（cylindrical geometry）的平面波形式（plane-wave formalism）方法，计算包含交换作用、非局域偶极耦合（nonlocal dipolar coupling）和界面DMI的自旋波能带结构。

Result: 管状几何定性地改变周期DMI的作用：除产生通常的非互易频移外，曲率将部分界面DMI转化为类似各向异性的有效内场，形成符号依赖的频率景观，使DMI覆盖区域可成为最低频模式的势阱或势垒；在Damon-Eshbach类和背向体波类构型中均出现平坦弱色散能带，后者在平面系统中没有直接对应。

Conclusion: 周期性工程化的DMI可作为在弯曲架构中控制非互易自旋波、局域模式和平坦磁子能带的有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce a chiral tubular magnonic crystal formed by a ferromagnetic nanotube with a periodically modulated interfacial Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction (DMI). Using a plane-wave formalism adapted to the cylindrical geometry, we calculate the spin-wave band structure including exchange, nonlocal dipolar coupling, and interfacial DMI. We show that the tubular geometry qualitatively modifies the role of periodic DMI compared with planar chiral magnonic crystals. Besides producing the usual nonreciprocal frequency shift, curvature converts part of the interfacial DMI into an effective anisotropy-like internal field. This creates a sign-dependent frequency landscape along the tube, so that DMI-covered regions can act either as potential wells or barriers for the lowest-frequency modes. As a result, flat and weakly dispersive bands appear in both Damon--Eshbach-like and backward-volume-like configurations, with the latter having no direct counterpart in planar systems. Our results identify periodically engineered DMI in nanotubes as a route for controlling nonreciprocal spin waves, localized modes, and flat magnonic bands in curved architectures.

</details>


### [282] [Coherent phononic frequency combs in ferroelectric CMOS oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28956)
*Jinghan Gao,Shruti Mishra,Yilin Kou,S M Enamul Hoque Yousuf,Hanna Cho,Roozbeh Tabrizian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究报道了基于铁电氧化铪-氧化锆纳米机电谐振器中实现的宽带声子频率梳，通过光刻定义的失谐选择生成机制，在0.44–2.1 GHz范围内产生超过170–200条谱线，并建立了机制层和材料层的设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电子系统需要数十个时钟和载波频率，每个频率由独立锁相环（phase-locked loop）从共享参考合成，随着域增加给布线、功耗和同步带来沉重负担。光频梳已在光子学中解决了这一问题，但电子学领域在其原生射频（radiofrequency）域一直缺乏等效的梳状频率源。

Method: 利用互补金属氧化物半导体（CMOS）氧化物构建的铁电氧化铪-氧化锆纳米机电谐振器，通过光刻定义的2:1内共振（internal resonance）失谐来选择梳产生机制。双音种子波混频产生170余条覆盖两个倍频程的谱线，自持Hopf路径则通过环面（torus）和周期倍增（period-doubling）动力学产生200余条谱线。采用修正艾伦偏差（MDEV）的零差测量分析建立双时间尺度律，并结合几何缩放和慢流分岔理论进行验证。

Result: 种子梳继承了泵浦的白相位噪声标度，短期稳定性由机制决定，一秒MDEV从1e-11增至1e-8；自持梳则表现出自由振荡器的相位扩散。长期稳定性由材料决定：无源温控条件下，温度补偿叠层抑制了未补偿谐振器中主导的随机游走漂移。几何缩放使梳生成扩展至0.44 GHz到2.1 GHz频段。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了机制层和材料层的设计规则，使单一谐振器可作为片上尺度的频率基础设施运行，涵盖多时钟产生到射频并行处理等应用场景。

Abstract: Modern electronic systems require tens of clock and carrier frequencies, each synthesized by a dedicated phase-locked loop from a shared reference, imposing routing, power and synchronization burdens that grow with every domain. Optical frequency combs solved this problem in photonics, whereas electronics has lacked an equivalent source in its native radiofrequency domain. Here we report broadband phononic frequency combs in ferroelectric hafnia-zirconia nanoelectromechanical resonators built from complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) oxides. Lithographically defined detuning of a 2:1 internal resonance selects the generation mechanism: two-tone-seeded wave mixing yields more than 170 lines distributed over two octaves, with mutual coherence verified for representative pump and generated lines, whereas an autonomous Hopf route yields hierarchical combs of more than 200 lines through torus and period-doubling dynamics, in agreement with slow-flow bifurcation theory. Geometric scaling extends comb generation across 0.44 GHz to 2.1 GHz. Heterodyne measurements, analyzed using the modified Allan deviation (MDEV), establish a two-timescale law. The mechanism governs short-term stability: seeded combs inherit the white-phase-noise scaling of their pumps, whereas autonomous combs acquire the phase diffusion of a free-running oscillator, with the one-second MDEV increasing from 1e-11 to 1e-8. The material governs long-term stability: in air and without active thermal control, the temperature-compensated stack suppresses the random-walk drift that dominates uncompensated resonators. These results establish mechanism- and material-level design rules for operating a single resonator as chip-scale frequency infrastructure, from multi-clock generation to radiofrequency parallel processing.

</details>


### [283] [Surface-mediated frequency aging beyond quality-factor saturation in an AlScN-on-silicon resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28959)
*S M Enamul Hoque Yousuf,Mahmudul Hasan,Shruti Mishra,Sourav Mukherjee,Jinghan Gao,Roozbeh Tabrizian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过将64.21 MHz的Al₀.₇Sc₀.₃N-on-silicon交叉截面Lamé模态谐振器置于0.01至760 Torr的压力范围内，解耦了品质因数Q与谐振频率f₀对压力的响应，发现Q在约1 Torr以下可逆地达到压力无关的天花板8.5×10⁴，而f₀在低至1e-5 Torr仍表现出历史依赖的频率老化，表明气体阻尼平衡与频率平衡是两个不同状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微纳机电谐振器的真空封装完整性常通过品质因数Q来评估，但Q反映的是残余气体阻尼而非表面态演化，后者才是频率老化（frequency aging）的主导因素。作者旨在解耦Q与谐振频率f₀的压力响应，验证Q饱和是否足以鉴定真空封装质量。

Method: 在0.01至760 Torr的压力范围内，对64.21 MHz Al₀.₇Sc₀.₃N-on-silicon截面Lamé模态（cross-sectional Lamé mode）谐振器进行测量；分别于25°C及68.8°C频率转折点（frequency turnover，一阶热灵敏度被抑制处）采集数据，并对压力阶跃后的Q与f₀瞬态进行拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）动力学拟合，辅以十小时锁相（phase-locked）时间偏差测量。

Result: Q在每次压力阶跃后于压力控制时间尺度内达到可逆、与历史无关的稳态值，在约1 Torr以下趋近拟合得到的压力无关上限8.5×10⁴；而f₀在1e-5 Torr仍可检测到响应，具有历史依赖性且需数小时弛豫，压力阶跃产生的分数频率偏移最大达1.9 ppm。十小时测量显示0.01至100 Torr范围内短时时间偏差（time deviation）接近1e-10 s，而760 Torr下气体阻尼通过降低Q削弱短时跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 气体阻尼平衡与频率平衡是两个截然不同的状态，Q饱和本身不足以鉴定精密机械频率参考的真空封装质量；封装规范还必须约束表面介导的频率老化。

Abstract: Vacuum package integrity in micro- and nanoelectromechanical resonators is commonly assessed through the quality factor Q, although Q probes residual-gas damping rather than the surface-state evolution that can govern frequency aging. Here, we disentangle the pressure responses of Q and the resonance frequency f0 in a 64.21 MHz Al0.7Sc0.3N-on-silicon cross-sectional Lamé-mode resonator between 0.01 to 760 Torr. Measurements at 25 °C and at the 68.8°C frequency turnover, where first-order thermal sensitivity is suppressed, reveal widely separated equilibration timescales. Following each pressure step, Q reaches a reversible, history-independent steady value on the pressure-control timescale and approaches a fitted pressure-independent ceiling of 8.5e4 below approximately 1 Torr. By contrast, f0 responds measurably down to 1e-5 Torr and remains history-dependent, relaxing for hours at fixed pressure. The transients follow stretched-exponential kinetics, consistent with a broad distribution of surface relaxation rates, and individual pressure steps produce fractional frequency shifts as large as 1.9 ppm. Ten-hour phase-locked measurements show a common short-term time-deviation floor near 1e-10 s from 0.01 to 100 Torr, whereas residual deterministic relaxation dominates at long averaging times; at 760 Torr, gas damping degrades short-term tracking through the reduced Q. These results establish gas-damping equilibrium and frequency equilibrium as distinct states. Quality-factor saturation alone is therefore insufficient to qualify vacuum packaging for precision mechanical frequency references; package specifications must also constrain surface-mediated frequency aging.

</details>


### [284] [Fractional Impurity Entropy from Potential Scattering in a Free Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29007)
*Syeda Neha Zaidi,Jukka I. Väyrynen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明，在环上自由电子系统与排斥性点状杂质的散射过程中，当杂质强度增大时，体系会发生 $k_B\ln\sqrt 2$ 的分数熵变（fractional entropy change），且该效应不需要强电子关联或非阿贝尔任意子（non-Abelian anyons）等拓扑条件即可出现，其根源在于杂质导致允许的单粒子动量从整数量子化（integer quantization）转变为半整数量子化（half-integer quantization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，体系熵的分数变化常被视为强电子关联或拓扑系统中非阿贝尔任意子的标志性特征。本工作旨在检验这一关联是否具有必然性，即是否必须满足这些条件才能出现分数熵变。

Method: 作者考虑一个环上的自由电子系统，并引入排斥性点状杂质（repulsive point-like impurity）。通过分析单粒子动量量子化条件从整数到半整数的转变，推导出熵的变化量，并进一步考察不同单粒子色散关系（single-particle dispersion）下该效应的普适性。

Result: 研究发现，随着杂质强度增大，体系的熵变化为 $k_B\ln\sqrt 2$，该分数熵变源于单粒子允许动量从整数量子化转变为半整数量子化。进一步分析表明，该效应在广泛类别的单粒子色散关系中均具有普适性。

Conclusion: 分数熵变并非强相关或拓扑序（topological order）的必要信号，其可以在完全不涉及强关联或拓扑结构的自由电子系统中自然出现。这一结论对以分数熵变作为量子相变或拓扑相判据的实验与理论工作具有重要的警示意义。

Abstract: Fractional changes in entropy are often associated with strong electron correlations or non-Abelian anyons in topological systems. We show that neither condition is necessary -- a system of free electrons on a ring scattering off a repulsive point-like impurity undergoes a $k_B\ln\sqrt 2$ entropy change as the impurity strength is increased. This effect arises from a shift of allowed single-particle momenta from integer quantization to half-integer quantization, induced by the impurity. We show that this fractional entropy change is universal across a broad class of single-particle dispersions, demonstrating that such signatures can arise without strong correlations or topological order.

</details>


### [285] [Probing spin order via magnon transmission across quantum Hall ferromagnet heterojunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29015)
*Seung Hwan Lee,Shaowen Chen,Andrew T. Pierce,Patrick R. Forrester,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Amir Yacoby*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次在单层-双层石墨烯量子霍尔铁磁体（quantum Hall ferromagnet, QHFM）异质结中实现了磁振子（magnon）传输，以单层石墨烯（monolayer graphene, MLG）为磁振子源，通过非局域电压测量探测双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene, BLG）中对称破缺量子霍尔态的自旋序，建立了异质结磁振子传输作为一种模块化的磁有序探测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料平台涌现出从非常规超导到分数量子霍尔绝缘体等多种奇异关联相，探测其中磁有序对理解其底层物理至关重要，但自旋密度稀薄使传统磁探针失效。此前量子霍尔铁磁体中的磁振子探测仅局限于单一材料内的同质结构，缺乏跨异质结界面的磁序探测能力。

Method: 构建单层-双层石墨烯量子霍尔铁磁体异质结，在单层石墨烯的ν=1态产生磁振子，通过非局域电压测量检测磁振子穿过双层石墨烯的传输信号。通过分析传输信号随朗道能级填充因子（Landau level filling）的变化，以及其在塞曼能量（Zeeman energy）处的尖锐起始特征，探测BLG对称破缺量子霍尔态中的自旋序。

Result: 在单层-双层石墨烯异质结中成功观测到磁振子穿越界面的传输，传输信号呈现典型磁振子特征：在塞曼能量处出现尖锐起始，并随朗道能级填充因子系统变化，且在自旋极化消失的ν=4和ν=8处信号被抑制。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了异质结磁振子传输作为一种强大的模块化磁有序探针，能够以单一材料为磁振子源来探测不同材料中的磁有序，为研究快速扩展的二维材料家族中各类奇异量子态开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional material platforms now host a remarkable array of exotic correlated phases, from unconventional superconductivity to fractional Chern insulators. Probing magnetic order in these systems is essential for understanding their underlying physics, yet dilute spin densities render conventional magnetic probes ineffective. Spin waves, or magnons, in quantum Hall ferromagnets (QHFM) have proven effective for probing the magnetic order in various symmetry-broken quantum Hall (QH) phases in graphene systems, but previous works have been limited to homojunction configurations within a single material. Here, we demonstrate magnon transmission across a monolayer-bilayer graphene quantum Hall ferromagnet heterojunction - the first magnon transmission across quantum Hall ferromagnet heterojunctions, using one material as a magnon source to probe magnetic order in a distinct material. Generating magnons in monolayer graphene (MLG) at $ν$ = 1, we detect their transmission through bilayer graphene (BLG) via nonlocal voltage measurements, revealing spin order in BLG symmetry-broken quantum Hall states. The transmission exhibits hallmark magnon signatures: a sharp onset at the Zeeman energy and systematic variation with Landau level filling, including suppression at $ν$ = 4 and 8 where spin polarization vanishes. Our findings establish heterojunction magnon transmission as a powerful, modular probe of magnetic order, opening new avenues for investigating exotic quantum states across the rapidly expanding family of two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [286] [Quantitative Disentanglement of Terahertz Spin and Orbital Pumping in 3d Ferromagnetic Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29165)
*Tongyang Guan,Jiahao Liu,Yuxiao Mo,Liangliang Zhu,Yizheng Wu,Zhensheng Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过结合自旋太赫兹发射谱学（spintronic terahertz emission spectroscopy, STES）与楔形样品厚度控制，在自旋霍尔和轨道霍尔角符号相反的非磁性层（Ta、W、Nb）异质结构中定量分离了超快自旋泵浦和轨道泵浦贡献，发现轨道泵浦占比从Fe到Ni逐步增至自旋电流贡献的数十个百分点，远超理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观理论预测轨道泵浦效率在3d系列中从Fe到Ni递增，但这一预测在超快激发条件下是否成立尚不清楚；同时自旋与轨道电流同时产生并加性地贡献于同一太赫兹发射信号，无法定量分离，这是研究的核心难题。

Method: 将STES与楔形样品厚度控制相结合，选用非磁性层（Ta、W、Nb）具有相反符号自旋霍尔角与轨道霍尔角的异质结构，使两个角动量通道呈现不同发射极性和厚度依赖关系，从而实现其定量分解；对Fe、Co、Ni异质结构进行系统测量。

Result: 系统测量揭示轨道泵浦贡献从Fe到Ni逐渐增加，最高达到自旋电流贡献的数十个百分点，远超理论预测；即使Fe也产生不可忽略的轨道电流，在薄非磁性层区域尤为关键；提取的轨道扩散长度始终短于自旋扩散长度，并随非磁性层的自旋轨道耦合强度减弱而增大。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁性异质结构中的超快自旋与轨道泵浦建立了定量分析框架，表明轨道泵浦在实际材料体系中的贡献显著高于理论预期，对理解超快角动量输运具有重要意义。

Abstract: Spin and orbital pumping - the injection of spin and orbital angular momentum from a driven ferromagnet into an adjacent nonmagnetic layer - are fundamental processes underlying angular-momentum generation and transport in magnetic heterostructures. Femtosecond optical excitation extends these phenomena into the ultrafast regime, where spintronic terahertz emission spectroscopy (STES) detects picosecond angular-momentum currents in a contact-free manner through spin-to-charge and orbital-to-charge conversion. Microscopic theory predicts that orbital-pumping efficiency increases from Fe to Ni across the 3d series, yet whether these predictions hold under ultrafast excitation remains unclear. A central challenge is that spin and orbital currents are generated simultaneously and contribute additively to the same terahertz emission, preventing their quantitative separation. Here, we overcome this limitation by combining STES with wedge-sample thickness control in heterostructures whose nonmagnetic layers (Ta, W, and Nb) have spin Hall and orbital Hall angles of opposite signs. The two angular-momentum channels therefore exhibit distinct emission polarities and thickness dependences, enabling their quantitative decomposition. Systematic measurements on Fe, Co, and Ni heterostructures reveal that the orbital-pumping contribution increases progressively toward Ni, reaching several tens of percent of the spin-current contribution - far exceeding theoretical predictions. Even Fe generates a non-negligible orbital current that becomes essential in the thin-nonmagnetic-layer regime. The extracted orbital diffusion lengths are consistently shorter than spin diffusion lengths and increase with decreasing spin-orbit coupling strength of the nonmagnetic layer. These results establish a quantitative framework for ultrafast spin and orbital pumping in magnetic heterostructures.

</details>


### [287] [Heavy-Hole--Light-Hole Mixing and Spin--Photon Coupling in Germanium Flopping-Mode Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29325)
*Jose Reina-Gálvez,Guido Burkard*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用多能带Luttinger–Kohn框架研究锗（germanium）双量子点（double quantum dots, DQDs）中轻空穴（light-hole, LH）与重空穴（heavy-hole, HH）混合对自旋-光子耦合（spin–photon coupling）的增强作用，发现HH–LH混合可提供比传统HH–HH跃迁更优的自旋-光子品质因子（figure of merit），为电路量子电动力学（circuit-QED）架构拓展了可调控的耦合通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锗中固有的自旋-轨道相互作用（spin–orbit interaction）可提供电学激活的翻转模量子比特（flopping-mode qubits），无需外加磁场梯度即可产生自旋-电荷杂化（spin–charge hybridization）。为充分开发利用该机制，需定量研究不同空穴子带之间的混合如何影响自旋-光子耦合强度，以扩展量子比特-腔耦合的调控手段。

Method: 采用多能带Luttinger–Kohn框架对双量子点进行建模，将重空穴（HH）与轻空穴（LH）态等权保留。沿生长方向使用有限限制势（finite confinement potential）进行垂直约束建模，使部分选定的子带进入显著的HH–LH混合区域。

Result: HH–LH混合通过LH–LH和LH–HH跃迁激活了电偶极自旋耦合（electric-dipole spin-coupling），补足了常规的HH–HH赝自旋翻转共振（pseudospin-flip resonance）通道。在所考察的参数范围内，这些额外通道可比HH–HH跃迁产生更大的自旋-光子品质因子。

Conclusion: HH–LH混合为锗空穴自旋量子比特提供了可调控的比特-腔耦合（qubit–cavity coupling）要素，并拓展了电路量子电动力学架构的可用工作区间。

Abstract: Intrinsic spin--orbit interaction in germanium provides electrically active flopping-mode qubits without requiring magnetic-field gradients to generate spin--charge hybridization. We study double quantum dots (DQDs) with a multiband Luttinger--Kohn framework that retains heavy-hole (HH) and light-hole (LH) states on equal footing. Modeling vertical confinement with a finite confinement potential along the growth direction brings selected subbands into a regime of appreciable HH--LH mixing. This admixture activates electric-dipole spin-coupling through LH--LH and LH--HH transitions, complementing the conventional HH--HH pseudospin-flip resonance. For the parameter sets considered, these additional channels can produce larger spin--photon figures of merit than the HH--HH transition. HH--LH mixing thus supplies a tunable ingredient for qubit--cavity coupling in germanium hole-spin qubits and extends the operating regimes available to circuit-QED architectures.

</details>


### [288] [Extreme Polarization of the Optical Gap and High-Energy Exciton Landscape in CrSBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29566)
*Sayantan Patra,Sourabh Jain,Bhumika Chauhan,Marie-Christin Heißenbüttel,Abhisek Saidarsan,Ranjuna M. K.,Kseniia Mosina,Zdeněk Sofer,Michael Rohlfing,Thorsten Deilmann,Ashish Arora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用光学吸收光谱和GW-贝特-萨尔皮特方程（GW-Bethe-Salpeter equation）从头计算，揭示了单层和体相CrSBr中强烈各向异性的激子（exciton）景观，测量得到面内光学带隙各向异性为470±15 meV，这是近红外至可见光谱区已知材料中的最高值。研究还识别出多个强极化激子和一个耦合的三子（trion）/激子对，并将CrSBr确立为强偏振选择性激子体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维磁性半导体CrSBr中光学激子性质的各向异性，旨在发现具有大光学带隙各向异性、可用于偏振选择性光电子器件的材料体系。

Method: 采用光学吸收光谱实验结合GW-贝特-萨尔皮特方程从头计算（ab initio calculations），对单层及体相CrSBr进行偏振分辨的宽带介电函数测量与理论验证。

Result: 在两条面内偏振本征轴上直接确定了最低亮光学跃迁激子X₀ᵃ和X₀ᵇ，测得面内光学带隙各向异性为470±15 meV，为近红外至可见光谱区已知材料最高值；在1.25 eV至3.1 eV范围内识别出多个沿两条正交轴选择性排列的强极化激子；发现位于X₀ᵇ下方24 meV的X⁻共振，其振子强度逐步转移至X₀ᵇ，行为与耦合三子（费米极化子）/激子对一致。

Conclusion: CrSBr被确立为强偏振选择性激子系统，其巨大的光学带隙各向异性凸显了其在偏振选择性光电子学中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We reveal a strongly anisotropic excitonic landscape in monolayer and bulk-like CrSBr using optical absorption spectroscopy and $GW$-Bethe-Salpeter equation $\textit{ab initio}$ calculations. The direct absorptive determination of the lowest bright optical onsets i.e. $X_0^a$ and $X_0^b$ excitons for the two in-plane polarization eigenaxes yield an in-plane optical gap anisotropy of $470 \pm 15$ meV. This is the highest observed value for any material in the near-infrared-to-visible spectral region to the best of our knowledge. Energetically above, we identify multiple strongly polarized excitons spanning $1.25$ eV to $3.1$ eV selectively aligned along the two orthogonal axes. A resonance $X^-$, located $24$ meV below the $X_0^b$ progressively transfers oscillator strength to $X_b^0$, a behavior consistent with a coupled trion (Fermi-polarion)/exciton pair. Our experiments also provide polarization-resolved broadband dielectric functions of CrSBr. These results establish CrSBr as a strongly polarization-selective excitonic system and highlight its potential for polarization-selective optoelectronics enabled with its large optical-gap anisotropy.

</details>


### [289] [Harnessing the skyrmion Hall effect for low-power skyrmion transport in a tubular synthetic antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29724)
*Ivan P. Miranda,Grzegorz J. Kwiatkowski,Jacob J. Mankenberg,Cecilia M. Holmqvist,Igor S. Lobanov,Valery M. Uzdin,Pavel F. Bessarab*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由反铁磁耦合斯格明子对构成的管状几何系统中，如何利用最优控制将斯格明子霍尔效应从典型的不利横向运动来源转变为降低欧姆损耗的机制，从而实现比无耦合极限更低的功耗输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯格明子霍尔效应通常导致斯格明子在输运过程中产生不期望的横向运动，被视为一种不利因素。作者试图探索能否将该效应转化为一种可操控的自由度，以降低斯格明子输运中的欧姆损耗（Ohmic losses）。

Method: 考虑一对反铁磁耦合的斯格明子被限制在管状几何体中，其相对横向位移构成周期性内部坐标；斯格明子霍尔效应驱动该坐标，而有限层间耦合使得施加电流与纵向速度之间的关系呈现非线性，从而允许不同电流协议产生相同的预定平均速度。作者通过确定使焦耳加热（Joule heating）最小化的最优电流协议来实现控制。

Result: 依层间耦合与自旋转移力矩（spin-transfer torque）参数而定，最优电流要么是恒定的（斯格明子对保持固定相对位置），要么是随时间变化的（斯格明子对绕管做周期性相对运动）。两种情形下，斯格明子霍尔效应与层间耦合创造的额外内部自由度均使输运功耗低于无耦合极限。

Conclusion: 斯格明子霍尔效应与层间耦合相结合所引入的内部自由度，能够实现比无耦合情形更低的功耗斯格明子输运，为该效应的利用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We show that optimal control can turn the skyrmion Hall effect from a source of typically unwanted transverse motion into a mechanism for reducing Ohmic losses in skyrmion transport. We consider a pair of antiferromagnetically coupled skyrmions confined to a tubular geometry, where their relative transverse displacement becomes a periodic internal coordinate. The skyrmion Hall effect drives this coordinate, while finite interlayer coupling makes the resulting relation between applied current and longitudinal velocity nonlinear, allowing different current protocols to produce the same prescribed average velocity. We determine which of these protocols minimizes Joule heating. Depending on the interlayer coupling and spin-transfer-torque parameters, the optimal current is either constant, with the skyrmion pair maintaining a fixed relative position, or time dependent, with the pair undergoing periodic relative motion around the tube. In either case, the additional internal degree of freedom created by the skyrmion Hall effect and interlayer coupling enables skyrmion transport at a lower power than in the uncoupled limit.

</details>


### [290] [Localized orbitals and tunnel couplings from general confinement potentials in gate-defined quantum-dot arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29766)
*Bohdan Khromets,William Chow,Jonathan Baugh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数值局域化方法，将单粒子有效轨道哈密顿量的本征基旋转为与各量子点关联的局域轨道波函数壳层（s、p、d壳层），并直接从哈密顿矩阵元计算成对隧穿耦合。该方法在二维三角形Si-MOS三量子点阵列上验证，获得了隧穿耦合的电压依赖性和无序存在下的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密门控定义的多量子点阵列的高效模拟需要准确且可扩展的建模方法，同时要兼容真实电压控制限制势中的不对称性和缺陷。

Method: 提出一种数值局域化（localization）方法，将一般单粒子有效轨道哈密顿量的本征基旋转为与各量子点关联的局域轨道波函数壳层；成对隧穿耦合（tunnel coupling）直接作为哈密顿量的矩阵元计算。

Result: 在二维三角形Si-MOS三量子点阵列上，获得了隧穿耦合随电压的依赖关系及其在无序（disorder）存在下的分布。

Conclusion: 讨论了该评估方法对多体计算（many-body calculations）的影响，并将其与实验隧穿耦合测量相关联。

Abstract: Efficient simulation of dense gate-defined multi-quantum-dot arrays requires accurate and scalable modeling methods, compatible with asymmetries and imperfections of realistic voltage-controlled confinement potentials. We present a numerical localization procedure that rotates the eigenbasis of a general one-particle effective orbital Hamiltonian into $s$-, $p$-, $d$-, $\ldots$-shells of localized orbital wavefunctions associated with individual quantum dots. The pairwise tunnel couplings between such states are computed directly as matrix elements of the Hamiltonian. We demonstrate this procedure on a 2D triangular Si-MOS triple-quantum-dot array by obtaining the voltage dependencies of the tunnel couplings and their distributions in the presence of disorder. We discuss the implications of this evaluation method on the many-body calculations, and relate it to the experimental tunnel coupling measurements.

</details>


### [291] [Josephson-Phase Reversal of Non-Bloch Andreev Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29787)
*X. Z. Zhang,Hechen Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究证明在自旋-轨道耦合平面约瑟夫森结中，仅改变约瑟夫森相位即可反转低能安德烈夫带的非布洛赫传播方向，并在结的两条边缘之间切换其边界积累。相位通过重塑能带的旋量和电子-空穴组成，使固定耗散库对反向传播模式产生不对称衰减，从而提供了一种在超导平台中实现可重构非厄米输运的原位途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态体系中非厄米控（non-Hermitian control）因耗散固定而难以实现，作者旨在寻找一种仅通过可调外部参数即可实现非厄米输运可逆调控的方法，突破传统固态器件中耗散不可调的局限。

Method: 作者在自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit-coupled）平面约瑟夫森结（planar Josephson junction）中，通过改变约瑟夫森相位来调控低能安德烈夫带（Andreev band）的旋量和电子-空穴组成，进而利用固定的耗散库对正反向传播模式进行不等衰减；并结合弱耗散理论（weak-loss theory）将这种相位控制的损耗失衡与边界选择的复动量（complex momenta）联系起来。

Result: 仅改变约瑟夫森相位即能反转非布洛赫传播方向，并能在结的两个边缘之间切换边界积累（boundary accumulation）。相位对能带组分的重塑使固定耗散库对反向传播模式产生不均等的衰减效果，实现了相位控制的损耗失衡。

Conclusion: 弱耗散理论表明，相位控制的损耗失衡可映射为边界选择的复动量，这为在超导平台中实现对可重构非厄米输运的原位调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian control is difficult in solid-state systems due to fixed dissipation. Here, we show that in a spin-orbit-coupled planar Josephson junction, changing only the Josephson phase reverses the non-Bloch propagation of a low-energy Andreev band and switches its boundary accumulation between junction edges. The phase reshapes the band's spin and electron-hole composition, causing a fixed reservoir to unequally attenuate counterpropagating modes. Weak-loss theory links this phase-controlled loss imbalance to boundary-selected complex momenta, offering an in situ route to reconfigurable non-Hermitian transport in superconducting platforms.

</details>


### [292] [Spin-textured orbitals in altermagnetic artificial atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29861)
*Yue Mao,Yu-Chen Zhuang,Cheng-Ming Miao,Yu-Fei Sun,Qing-Feng Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了交错磁性人工原子（altermagnetic artificial atoms）的概念，通过在具有动量依赖性自旋劈裂的体系中约束电子，将常规受限轨道重构为自旋织构轨道（spin-textured orbitals），并展示了其对称性保护的二重简并、高阶自旋织构以及应变调控自旋极化态的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工原子为工程化类原子轨道提供了灵活平台，但自旋在其轨道结构中通常仅作为被动自由度。作者希望通过引入交错磁性（altermagnetism）来主动调控自旋与轨道的耦合，从而为量子受限系统中的自旋依赖轨道工程开辟新途径。

Method: 通过将电子约束在具有动量依赖自旋劈裂（momentum-dependent spin splitting）的体系中形成交错磁性人工原子，系统性地分析受限轨道被重构为自旋织构轨道的过程，并研究其谱学性质、对称性保护及对外部应变（strain）的响应。

Result: 研究表明，交错磁性将常规受限轨道重构为自旋织构轨道，其中相反自旋分量在空间中呈现明显不同的分布；受限谱保持由 C₄z𝒯 联合对称性保护的二重简并。高阶态中自旋织构持续存在，并与径向结构结合形成特征角向自旋模式；应变可解除简并产生自旋极化态并连续调谐其能量劈裂。

Conclusion: 研究确立了交错磁性人工原子作为在量子受限系统中工程设计自旋依赖轨道结构的可行性路线，为自旋电子学与人工原子领域提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Artificial atoms provide a versatile platform for engineering atomic-like orbitals, yet spin generally remains a passive degree of freedom in their orbital structure. Here, we introduce the concept of altermagnetic artificial atoms formed by confining electrons with momentum-dependent spin splitting. We show that altermagnetism reconstructs conventional confined orbitals into spin-textured orbitals, with spatially distinct distributions of opposite spin components. The resulting confined spectrum retains a twofold degeneracy protected by the combined $C_{4z}\mathcal{T}$ symmetry. These spin textures persist in higher-energy states, where additional radial structures combine with the characteristic angular spin pattern. Furthermore, strain resolves the degenerate orbital pairs into spin-polarized states, and continuously tunes their energy splitting. Our results establish altermagnetic artificial atoms as a route to engineering spin-dependent orbital structures in quantum-confined systems.

</details>


### [293] [Chiral superconductors and competing states across a Lifshitz transition in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29873)
*Chuanqi Zheng,Cheng Xu,Chushan Li,Chenyu Zhang,Zijing Zhang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Hao Yang,Dandan Guan,Liang Liu,Shiyong Wang,Yaoyi Li,Hao Zheng,Canhua Liu,Jinfeng Jia,Shengwei Jiang,Zhiwen Shi,Guorui Chen,Fengcheng Wu,Yang Zhang,Tingxin Li,Xiaoxue Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过量子振荡测量，绘制了菱面体五层石墨烯中手性超导附近的正常态费米学，揭示了由电场控制的Lifshitz转变，并确认费米面拓扑是调控该体系中手性超导电性的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱面体多层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene）中强库仑相互作用与非平凡量子几何交织产生了奇特量子态，近期实验在自旋-谷极化区间观测到电子掺杂体系中的手性超导（chiral superconductivity）迹象，但该超导区域附近的正常态费米学尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 利用量子振荡（quantum oscillation）测量，系统绘制了菱面体五层石墨烯在手性超导相附近的正常态费米面随电场和垂直磁场的变化，并结合理论计算分析两种费米面上的成对倾向与轨道Zeeman对破缺尺度。

Result: 发现电控的Lifshitz转变，介于圆形四分之一金属（quarter-metal）费米面与环形四分之一金属费米面之间；Lifshitz边界随垂直磁场移动，与低能带强动量依赖的轨道磁矩一致。靠近转变两侧时电子有效质量显著增强，形成近乎无色散的能带底部。两种手性超导区域位于Lifshitz边界两侧，具有相近转变温度，但环形侧状态被显著更小的垂直磁场压制。计算表明两种母体费米面具有可比的手性配对倾向，但环形态具有更低的轨道-Zeeman对破缺尺度及额外的有限动量配对倾向。

Conclusion: 费米面拓扑（Fermi-surface topology）被确认为菱面体石墨烯中调控手性超导电性的关键参数，为理解该体系中超导与维格纳晶体及可重入量子霍尔态的竞争提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Rhombohedral multilayer graphene hosts a distinctive low-energy electronic structure in which strong Coulomb interactions and nontrivial quantum geometry intertwine to generate exotic quantum states. Recent experiments reported signatures of chiral superconductivity in electron-doped rhombohedral multilayer graphene within the spin- and valley-polarized regime. Here we map the normal-state fermiology surrounding chiral superconductivity in rhombohedral pentalayer graphene. Quantum oscillation measurements reveal an electrically controlled Lifshitz transition between a simply-connected circular quarter-metal Fermi surface and an annular quarter-metal Fermi surface. The Lifshitz boundary itself shifts with perpendicular magnetic field, consistent with the strongly momentum-dependent orbital magnetic moment of the low-energy band. Approaching the transition from either side, the electron effective mass becomes strongly enhanced, implying the formation of a nearly dispersionless band bottom and a strongly reduced kinetic-energy scale. This singular electronic structure produces a regime of exceptionally strong instability in which chiral superconductivity competes with Wigner crystalline phases and reentrant quantum Hall states. In particular, two superconducting regions with signatures of orbital time-reversal-symmetry breaking lie on opposite sides of the Lifshitz boundary and have comparable transition temperatures, yet the annular-side state is suppressed by a substantially smaller perpendicular magnetic field. Our calculation finds comparable chiral pairing tendencies on the two parent Fermi surfaces while producing a much lower orbital-Zeeman pair-breaking scale and an additional finite-momentum pairing tendency for the annular state. These results identify Fermi-surface topology as a key control parameter for chiral superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene.

</details>


### [294] [Universal tuning of Förster resonance energy transfer in gate-programmable conductor-dielectric-conductor heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30150)
*Alexis J. Agosto,Daniel Gunlycke,Michael N. Leuenberger*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics, QED）理论框架，用于在材料无关的导体-介电体-导体异质结构中普适调控自发辐射和Förster共振能量转移（FRET）。平台由厚度为W的介电间隔层和两个栅极可调二维导体构成，唯一输入的微观信息是两个导体片的横磁/横电反射振幅。通过推导推迟Maxwell并矢、真空/Hadamard场传播子和时序Feynman传播子，揭示了不同反射边界条件下FRET速率从体材料的x_ρ^{-6}定律到指数衰减或准二维对数增强的丰富行为，并在石墨烯-铒体系中展示了耗散与色散耦合的可分离调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自发辐射和FRET调控方案多数依赖特定材料和复杂纳米结构设计，缺乏一个材料无关的普适理论框架。本文旨在建立基于导体-介电体-导体（conductor-dielectric-conductor）异质结构的统一QED理论，以栅极电压为控制旋钮实现可编程的发射和能量转移调控。

Method: 从QED光子传播子出发，在导体-介电体-导体几何中推导推迟Maxwell并矢、真空/Hadamard场传播子和时序Feynman传播子。自发辐射速率由局域真空谱密度控制，FRET速率由非局域推迟/超前格林张量乘积决定。通过分析透明极限(r_TM→0)、Dirichlet/PEC分支(r_TM→-1)和Neumann/PMC类分支(r_TM→1^-)三种极限情形，刻画不同反射边界条件下的耦合行为。

Result: 在透明极限下恢复体材料的x_ρ^{-6}近场FRET定律；在Dirichlet/PEC分支下FRET速率获得Bessel-K包络，大横向分离时呈指数衰减Γ_{D→A}∝exp(-2πx_ρ/W)；在Neumann/PMC类分支下准无隙横模存活，产生宽准二维对数传播子，将非局域耦合增强至x_{ρ,*}∼W/(1-r_TM)的栅极可编程范围。在石墨烯-铒体系中，推迟格林张量将耗散型Er到石墨烯衰变与色散型虚等离激元介导的Er-Er耦合分离，等离子体带隙（plasmonic band gap）可抑制前者而保留后者。

Conclusion: 该理论为材料无关的栅极可调控自发辐射和FRET平台提供了统一的QED描述，展示了通过控制二维导体反射相位即可实现从指数屏蔽到长程增强的FRET行为转换，并指出在钣激发体系中等离激元带隙可用于选择性地抑制耗散通道同时保留色散耦合。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-electrodynamical theory for universal tuning of spontaneous emission and Förster resonance energy transfer (FRET) in a material-agnostic conductor-dielectric-conductor heterostructure. The platform consists of a dielectric spacer of thickness $W$ bounded by two gate-tunable two-dimensional conductors. The only microscopic input from the surrounding materials is the transverse-magnetic and transverse-electric reflection amplitudes $r_{\TM/\TE}(q_ρ,ω)$ of the two sheets. Starting from the QED photon propagator, we derive the retarded Maxwell dyadic, the vacuum/Hadamard field propagator, and the time-ordered Feynman propagator in the same geometry. The spontaneous-emission rate is controlled by the local vacuum spectral density, while FRET is controlled by the nonlocal retarded/advanced product $\obG_{\text R}(\bx_D,\bx_A;ω_D)\Im\,\balpha_A(ω_D)\obG_{\text A}(\bx_A,\bx_D;ω_D)$. In the transparent limit $r_{\TM}\to 0$, the near-field FRET rate recovers the bulk $x_ρ^{-6}$ law. In the Dirichlet/PEC branch $r_{\TM}\to -1$, the gapless transverse mode is removed and the donor-acceptor coupling acquires a Bessel-$K$ envelope, giving an exponentially screened FRET rate $Γ_{D\to A}\propto\exp(-2πx_ρ/W)$ at large lateral separation. In the opposite Neumann/PMC-like branch $r_{\TM}\to 1^{-}$, a nearly gapless transverse mode survives and produces a wide quasi-two-dimensional logarithmic propagator, enhancing the nonlocal electromagnetic coupling over a gate-programmable range $x_{ρ,*}\sim W/(1-r_{\TM})$. For graphene-Er implementations, the same retarded Green tensor also separates dissipative on-shell Er-to-graphene decay, governed by its absorptive part, from dispersive virtual-plasmon-mediated Er-Er coupling, governed by its reactive part; a plasmonic band gap can suppress the former while retaining the latter.

</details>


### [295] [Switchable Magnetoelectric Transport in Graphene via a Van der Waals Multiferroic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30170)
*Miuko Tanaka,Shunta Aoki,Ikoi Sato,Hao Ou,Itishree Pradhan,Ngoc Han Tu,Yangsong Chen,Tomohiro Ishii,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Michihisa Yamamoto,Masayuki Hashisaka,Jiang Pu,Naoki Ogawa,Toshiya Ideue*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文构建了由石墨烯（graphene）与多铁性材料CuCrP₂S₆组成的范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructure），首次实现了栅极可切换的界面磁电输运（magnetoelectric transport），并展示了磁电效应与多铁性畴控制的直接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在原子界面上对输运性质进行电学与磁性调控是下一代电子学与自旋电子学（spintronics）发展的核心。范德华多铁材料（van der Waals multiferroics）在单层极限下同时具有介电序与磁序，为这类界面调控提供了有前景的平台，但此前利用其独特属性实现电子功能一直难以达成。

Method: 研究者构建了石墨烯与多铁性材料CuCrP₂S₆组成的范德华异质结，通过测量电荷中性点（charge-neutrality）电阻峰的变化来表征多铁层的极化翻转对石墨烯输运的影响；同时施加面内磁场以探测磁电效应，并通过在电场下冷却器件来实现多铁性畴（domain）控制。

Result: 石墨烯的电荷中性电阻峰因多铁态的极化翻转而呈现明显的磁滞（hysteresis）；施加面内磁场使该峰发生与极化方向相关的位移，直接揭示了磁场诱导的极化调制，即磁电效应（magnetoelectric effect）的特征。此外，在电场下冷却器件可实现多铁性畴的取向控制，从而可逆地切换界面磁电输运行为。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在范德华异质结中展示了界面磁电输运，为利用二维范德华界面进行功能性器件设计工程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Electric and magnetic control of transport properties at atomic interfaces is central to the development of next generation electronics and spintronics. Van der Waals multiferroics materials that simultaneously host dielectric and magnetic orders down to the monolayer limit offer a promising platform for such interfacial control, yet the realization of electronic functionalities that exploit the unique attributes of van der Waals multiferroics has largely remained elusive. Here, we realize a van der Waals heterostructure comprising graphene and the multiferroic CuCrP2S6, enabling gate-switchable magnetoelectric transport in graphene, mediated by the multiferroic layer. The charge-neutrality resistance peak of graphene exhibits pronounced hysteresis arising from polarization flip in the multiferroic state. Application of an in-plane magnetic field shifts this peak in a polarization-dependent manner, revealing magnetic-field-induced polarization modulation a direct signature of the magnetoelectric effect. Furthermore, cooling the device under an applied electric field enables domain control of the multiferroic order, allowing reversible switching of the interfacial magnetoelectric transport. These results provide the first demonstration of interfacial magnetoelectric transport in a vdW heterostructure, and establish a pathway for engineering two-dimensional van der Waals interfaces for functional device applications.

</details>


### [296] [Singlet-doublet transitions and Josephson currents in a superconducting ring with a quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30306)
*Guo-Hui Ding,Fei Ye,Bing Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用变分波函数方法研究嵌入量子点（quantum dot）的超导环基态性质，建立了有限U安德森杂质与超导环耦合的理论公式，证明了在混合价态区间基态发生单重态-双重态转变，并发现超电流随外磁通振荡且在该相变点处发生突变跳变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子点与超导环耦合系统中基态的性质，特别是理解有限U安德森杂质（finite-U Anderson impurity）与超导环相互作用的物理行为。

Method: 采用变分波函数方法（variational wave-function approach）研究系统基态，提出了处理有限U安德森杂质与超导环耦合的理论公式。

Result: 在混合价态（mixed valence regime）区间，系统基态表现出单重态-双重态转变（singlet-doublet transitions）；超导环中的超电流随外磁通周期性振荡，并在单重态-双重态相变点处出现突变跳变。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了量子点嵌入超导环系统中基态相变与超电流输运之间的内在联系，为理解此类关联介观系统的物理性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the ground state properties of a superconducting ring embedded with a quantum dot (QD) by using a variational wave-function approach. A theoretical formulation for the treatment of the finite-U Anderson impurity coupled with a superconducting ring are presented. We demonstrate singlet-doublet transitions of the ground state for this system with the QD in the mixed valence regime. It is shown that the supercurrent in the superconductor ring shows oscillations with the external enclosed magnetic flux and exhibits abrupt jumps at the singlet-doublet phase transition points.

</details>


### [297] [Scalar Spin Chirality from Dissipative Pumping and Lamb Shift Precession](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30324)
*YuanDong Wang,JianHua Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了三角三重量子点（triangular triple quantum dots）中环流电流（circulating current）在零磁通与实数哈密顿量条件下随偏压反复反转的物理起源，指出耗散隧穿制备轨道赝自旋（orbital pseudospin），而虚电荷涨落产生非共线Lamb场使其向手性方向旋转，从而形成由储库诱导的轨道Hanle效应（orbital Hanle effect）。分层运动方程（hierarchical equations of motion）计算表明该反转在弱耦合描述之外依然成立，并确认其对应进动（precession）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角三重量子点中环流电流在零磁通、实数哈密顿量下仍随偏压反复反转，该现象起源长期以来不明确。作者旨在揭示这一异常输运行为的物理机制，并探索开放量子系统中时间反演破缺响应（time-reversal-odd response）的普适起源。

Method: 作者提出耗散隧穿（dissipative tunneling）制备轨道赝自旋，同时虚电荷涨落（virtual charge fluctuations）产生非共线Lamb场（noncollinear Lamb field）使其向手性方向旋转的理论框架，并通过精确基多重态恒等式（exact ground-multiplet identity）将非手性轨道极化与标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality）联系到环流电流。采用分层运动方程数值方法验证了低偏压反转行为。

Result: 偏压改变轨道赝自旋与Lamb场的相对取向，从而导致环流电流反转。这种由储库诱导的轨道Hanle效应将非手性轨道极化转换为标量自旋手性。分层运动方程计算表明，低偏压反转超出了二阶弱耦合（weak-coupling）描述依然成立；突然切换电压后手性的二次增长（quadratic growth）确认了进动机制。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统中，耗散态制备与相干进动（coherent precession）的相互作用可以产生时间反演奇（time-reversal-odd）响应。该发现不仅解释了三角三重量子点中的反常电流反转现象，还揭示了储库诱导进动在更广泛的量子输运体系中的潜在作用。

Abstract: Circulating currents in triangular triple quantum dots reverse repeatedly with bias even at zero magnetic flux and for a real Hamiltonian, a phenomenon whose origin has remained unclear. We show that dissipative tunneling prepares an orbital pseudospin, while virtual charge fluctuations generate a noncollinear Lamb field that rotates it toward the chiral direction. Bias changes their relative orientation and thereby reverses the current. This reservoir-induced orbital Hanle effect converts a nonchiral orbital polarization into scalar spin chirality, which an exact ground-multiplet identity links to the circulating current. Hierarchical-equations-of-motion calculations show that the low-bias reversal survives beyond the second-order weak-coupling description, while the quadratic growth of chirality after a sudden voltage switch identifies precession. More broadly, time-reversal-odd responses can emerge in open quantum systems from the interplay between dissipative state preparation and coherent precession.

</details>


### [298] [Evanescent-mode Casimir-Josephson force and gate-controlled resonances in ballistic graphene Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30438)
*Shahrukh Salim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种微观散射理论（microscopic scattering theory），用于描述弹道超导体-石墨烯-超导体约瑟夫森结（superconductor-graphene-superconductor Josephson junction）的相位依赖平衡力学响应。计算基于嵌入归一化Matsubara行列式中的能量依赖Dirac散射矩阵，完整包含了理想模型的Bogoliubov-de Gennes谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将此前基于超导电性诱导的力学力方案推广到狄拉克材料（Dirac material）体系，为弹道约瑟夫森结提供完整的相位依赖机械响应理论。

Method: 采用能量依赖的Dirac散射矩阵嵌入归一化Matsubara行列式进行计算，完整包含Bogoliubov-de Gennes谱。在电荷中性点推导出闭式倏逝模（evanescent-mode）结果，并通过精确实能量亚间隙极点（subgap poles）区分带间与带内Andreev过程以及倏逝与传播通道的交叉。

Result: 在电荷中性零温短结极限下，相位差为π时力修正达到2ln2·ΔW/(πL²)，正比于ΔW/L²。栅极掺杂引入传播通道和Fabry-Pérot结构，导致相位依赖力修正δF=F(φ)-F(0)出现大幅振荡和符号反转。区分了由|μ|/Δ控制的带间-带内Andreev交叉和由|μ|L/(ℏv_F)控制的倏逝-传播交叉，并量化了完整行列式结果与冻结散射（frozen-scattering）短结近似之间的差异。

Conclusion: 得到的栅极和相位依赖机械信号提供了超导电性诱导机械力提议的狄拉克材料扩展，为弹道约瑟夫森结的力学响应提供了系统完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic scattering theory of the phase-dependent equilibrium mechanical response of a ballistic superconductor-graphene-superconductor Josephson junction. The calculation is based on an energy-dependent Dirac scattering matrix embedded in a normalized Matsubara determinant, so the reported force correction contains the complete Bogoliubov-de Gennes spectrum of the stated ideal model. At charge neutrality the full determinant approaches a closed-form evanescent-mode result proportional to $ΔW/L^2$, reaching $2\ln 2\,ΔW/(πL^2)$ at phase difference $π$ in the zero-temperature short-junction limit. Gate doping produces propagating channels and Fabry-Pérot structure, causing large oscillations and sign reversals of the phase-dependent force correction $δF=F(φ)-F(0)$. We distinguish the interband-to-intraband Andreev crossover, controlled by $|μ|/Δ$, from the evanescent-to-propagating crossover, controlled by $|μ|L/(\hbar v_F)$. Exact real-energy subgap poles obtained from the same energy-dependent scattering matrix are used to diagnose specular/interband and retro/intraband character; these labels are not treated as separately measurable thermodynamic forces in the mixed regime. We also quantify the difference between the complete determinant result and the frozen-scattering short-junction approximation without identifying that difference with a pure continuum force. The resulting gate- and phase-dependent mechanical signal provides a Dirac-material extension of earlier superconductivity-induced mechanical-force proposals.

</details>


### [299] [Dynamics of a nanoscale ferromagnetic vortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30511)
*Jun Seok Seo,Se Kwon Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由界面Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用（iDMI）稳定化的铁磁涡旋结构，并通过理论分析与微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）证实其可在半径小于5纳米的纳米级铁磁盘中稳定存在，远小于传统约1微米半径的纳米点涡旋。该纳米涡旋兼具传统微米级涡旋的陀螺振荡（gyrotropic oscillation）等性质，同时表现出强非谐势、非线性振荡和Duffing振荡器式响应等非传统行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁涡旋（magnetic vortex）在铁磁纳米点中稳定存在需要约微米级尺度的碟片，这限制了其在小尺度器件中的应用。本文旨在探索能否借助界面Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用（iDMI）在远小于传统尺度的纳米级铁磁盘中实现稳定的涡旋态，从而拓展磁涡旋在纳米尺度下的应用潜力。

Method: 本研究结合理论分析和微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）两种方法。通过解析求解描述非谐涡旋振荡的非线性运动方程，分析涡旋的动力学稳定性；同时通过数值模拟验证所提出iDMI稳定化涡旋在纳米铁磁盘中的稳定性及其响应特性。

Result: 研究结果表明，iDMI稳定化的涡旋可在半径小于5纳米（far smaller than conventional~1μm radius nanodot vortices）的铁磁纳米盘中保持稳定。通过解析求解非线性运动方程，辨识出决定驱动振荡稳定性的临界频率（critical frequency）。该纳米涡旋在面外磁场下表现出陀螺振荡和共振频率位移（resonance frequency shift）等传统性质，同时也展现出强非谐势、非线性振荡以及外施AC偏置下的Duffing振荡器类响应等非常规行为。

Conclusion: 本文提出的iDMI稳定化铁磁涡旋可在纳米尺度下稳定存在，显著突破了传统磁涡旋的尺寸下限，同时保留传统涡旋的陀螺动力学特性，并展现非谐非线性振荡等新物理行为，为纳米磁器件设计和应用提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: We propose a ferromagnetic vortex stabilized by the interfacial Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction (iDMI) and investigate its properties through theoretical analysis and micromagnetic simulations. Our results demonstrate that this vortex can remain stable even in nanoscale ferromagnetic disks with radii below $5\,\text{nm}$---far smaller than those of conventional nanodot vortices having about $1\,μ\text{m}$ radius. We analytically solve the nonlinear equation of motion describing the anharmonic vortex oscillation, and identify the critical frequency that determines the stability of the driven oscillation of the vortex. This nanoscale vortex exhibits conventional properties of microscale vortices, including gyrotropic oscillation and resonance frequency shift under an out-of-plane magnetic field. It also exhibits unconventional behaviors, such as a strongly anharmonic potential, nonlinear oscillations, and a Duffing-oscillator-like response under the external AC bias.

</details>


### [300] [Moiré-induced lattice reconstruction at buried atomic interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30531)
*Nicholas Clark,Frederick Allars,Isaac Soltero,Wendong Wang,David Hopkinson,Hugo de Latour,James G. McHugh,William Talbott,Sam Sullivan-Allsop,Rongsheng Cai,Astrid Weston,Xiao Li,Gareth Tainton,Casey K. Cheung,Francisco Selles,Alex Summerfield,Andrey Kretinin,Christopher S. Allen,Vladimir Falko,Sarah J. Haigh,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用多切片电子叠层衍射成像（multi-slice electron ptychography）非侵入式地重建了厚过渡金属二硫化物（transition-metal dichalcogenide）晶体之间边缘扭曲菱方界面的三维原子结构，实现了约3 pm的位置精度和约1 nm的深度分辨率，揭示了埋藏界面处的位错网络和逐层晶格重构场，并观察到超出三维模型预测的强面内界面重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转型二维界面的原子重构（atomic reconstruction）主导着多种涌现性质（包括滑动铁电性），但此前研究主要集中于悬浮双层体系，多层晶体之间范德华异质界面的结构变化仍很少被探索。

Method: 采用多切片电子叠层衍射成像技术对厚过渡金属二硫化物晶体之间边缘扭曲的菱方界面进行非侵入式三维原子结构恢复，并结合三维建模对比实验观测结果。

Result: 获得了约3 pm的位置精度和约1 nm的深度分辨率，逐层解析了扭曲诱导的晶格重构场及埋藏界面处产生的位错网络（dislocation network）；发现尽管体系为块体，因外层对出平面弯曲（out-of-plane bending）的抑制，面内界面重构显著增强，超出三维模型的预测；同时提取了重构衰减进入块体过程中的应变张量（strain tensor）演化。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解多层莫尔（moiré）体系提供了结构基础，表明块体界面处因弯曲受限而表现出的强面内重构行为需要更完善的理论模型来解释。

Abstract: Atomic reconstruction at twisted two-dimensional interfaces governs many of their emergent optical, electronic, and mechanical properties, including sliding ferroelectricity. Despite recent progress in understanding lattice reconstruction in suspended twisted bilayers, structural changes at van der Waals heterointerfaces between multilayer crystals remain largely unexplored. Here we use multi-slice electron ptychography to non-invasively recover the three-dimensional atomic structure at marginally twisted rhombohedral interfaces between thick transition-metal dichalcogenide crystals. With a position precision of ~3 pm and a depth resolution ~1 nm, we resolve the twist-induced lattice reconstruction field per layer, and the resulting dislocation network at the buried interface. Despite the bulk nature, we observe markedly strong in-plane interfacial reconstruction due to suppression of the out-of-plane bending by outer layers, exceeding predictions from our three-dimensional modelling. Furthermore, we extract the strain tensor evolution during the decay of the reconstruction into the bulk, providing a structural foundation for understanding multi-layer moiré systems.

</details>


### [301] [Exact fluctuation relations in voltage- and temperature-biased Laughlin-edge constrictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30535)
*Gu Zhang,Gabriele Campagnano,Domenico Giuliano,Igor Gornyi,In`es Safi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了非平衡涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation-dissipation relations），证明在劳克林（Laughlin）边缘态通过量子点接触（QPC）耦合时，可实验测量的手性电流自相关与互相关可精确重建隧穿电流噪声与电导，且该关系在任意隧穿强度、电压偏置和边缘态温度下均严格成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平衡条件下，涨落-耗散关系能否在量子霍尔边缘态体系中成立并获得可实验验证的形式，是该领域此前尚未完全解答的问题。作者旨在建立连接实验可测的手性电流相关函数与隧穿噪声之间的严格关系，为表征非平衡输运提供可靠途径。

Method: 作者针对两个处于不同温度和电压的劳克林（Laughlin）手性边缘态，通过量子点接触（QPC）耦合的体系展开综合理论分析。研究将涨落-耗散关系进一步推广至空间扩展的量子点接触以及具有显式电压依赖性的隧穿振幅，并讨论了这些推广成立的适用条件与限制。

Result: 结果表明，所考察的非平衡涨落-耗散关系对任意隧穿强度、电压偏置及边缘态温度均保持精确成立。此外，基于下游测量的自相关与互相关函数，可可靠重构出量子点接触处产生的局域隧穿电流噪声。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了一种通过实验可及的手性电流相关函数来表征量子点接触非平衡输运的稳健路径，为手性边缘态体系中非平衡输运的导体表征提供了理论与实验基础。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of non-equilibrium fluctuation-dissipation relations connect- ing experimentally accessible chiral-current auto- and cross-correlations to the tunneling-current noise and conductance in Laughlin edge states coupled through a quantum point contact (QPC). We examine their validity for two chiral Laughlin edges held at different temperatures and voltages and show that the relations remain exact for arbitrary tunneling strength, voltage bias, and edge- state temperatures. We further generalize them to spatially extended QPCs and to tunneling am- plitudes with an explicit voltage dependence, and discuss the conditions and limitations associated with these generalizations. Our results establish that the local tunneling-current noise generated at the QPC can be reliably reconstructed from experimentally accessible auto- and cross-correlations measured downstream, providing a robust route to characterize non-equilibrium transport in chiral edge states.

</details>


### [302] [Predictive wavelength tailoring of uniform GaSb-based quantum dots for emission at 1.55 um](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30628)
*Markus Peil,Maja Wasiluk,Ziemowit Olinkiewicz,Tymon Przychodni,Robert Matysiak,Teemu Taskinen,Joona Salonen,Abhiroop Chellu,Metin Patli,Joonas Hilska,Anna Musiał,Michał Gawełczyk,Mircea Guina,Teemu Hakkarainen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于GaSb的液滴刻蚀（droplet-etched）纳米孔洞填充形成的量子点（QD），通过独立调控量子点组分与尺寸，可将发射波长从1.48 μm调谐至电信C波段中心（1.55 μm），并展示了极佳的均匀性、窄激子发射线宽和低精细结构劈裂，确立了其作为可调谐量子光子平台的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GaSb的低截止能（low-defect-etching，LDE）量子点具有覆盖电信C波段的潜力，但此前缺乏对发射波长可调谐性的系统研究。本文旨在通过系统研究组分与尺寸对发射波长的独立调控能力，评估该体系作为量子光子（quantum photonics）可扩展平台的可行性。

Method: 采用液滴刻蚀（droplet etching）技术在AlGaSb衬底上制备纳米孔洞，再以InGaSb填充形成量子点；分别独立改变In组分与填充层数（monolayers, ML）来调控发射波长；利用光致发光（photoluminescence）测量光学跃迁能量，并结合考虑均匀In组分、低互扩散及微观测量的QD几何结构的模拟进行对比验证。

Result: 实验发现光学跃迁能量随In组分线性移动，速率约为-4.4 meV/In百分比；随填充层数线性移动，速率为-2.0 meV/ML，与模拟结果（-4.3 meV/In百分比和-2.1 meV/ML）高度一致。所有样品的非均匀展宽（inhomogeneous broadening）远低于7 meV，单量子点激子发射线宽为13.8±6.7 μeV，精细结构劈裂（fine-structure splitting）低于10 μeV。

Conclusion: GaSb基LDE量子点具备从1.48 μm到1.55 μm的波长可调谐能力、优异的系综均匀性与光学质量，可作为面向长距离光通信光纤网络的可调谐电信平台，用于扩展量子光子应用。

Abstract: A detailed study of emission wavelength tailoring of GaSb-based QDs formed by InGaSb-filling of droplet-etched nanoholes in AlGaSb is presented. The study shows that the emission wavelength can be modified from 1.48 um to the center of the telecom C-band at 1.55 mm by independently varying the QD composition and size. More specifically, the optical transition energy shifts linearly as a function of In-content of the QD material at a rate of -4.4 meV/In-percentage, and with the number of monolayers (ML) of material used for filling the nanoholes, at -2.0 meV/ML. These experimentally observed energy shifts are well predicted by simulations yielding rates of -4.3 meV/In-percentage and -2.1 meV/ML, respectively. For the simulation, a uniform In composition, low intermixing, and microscopically measured QD geometry is considered. Additionally, excellent ensemble QD uniformity, with unprecedented inhomogeneous broadening well-below 7 meV across all samples is demonstrated. Finally, photoluminescence of single-QDs reveals narrow excitonic emission lines of 13.8+/-6.7 ueV and low fine-structure splitting values reaching <10 ueV. These results identify GaSb-based LDE QDs as a tunable telecom platform for scaling quantum-photonic applications over long-haul optical fiber networks.

</details>


### [303] [Coexistence of polariton bound states in the continuum and 2D radiative excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30691)
*Simone Zanotti,Marco Liscidini,Dario Gerace,Lucio C. Andreani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明，包含激子辐射损失的非厄米霍普菲尔德（non-Hermitian Hopfield）形式体系能够正确描述连续谱束缚态（bound states in the continuum, BICs），捕捉波矢$k=0$处具有发散辐射寿命的真实极化激元BIC，为辐射激子和极化激元BIC提供了统一的微观理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续谱束缚态（BICs）允许光学模式在辐射连续谱内具有理想无限辐射寿命，但二维激子在光锥内存在固有的、依赖波矢的辐射衰变，霍普菲尔德（Hopfield）形式体系能否在保留极化激元BIC的同时一致地描述这一过程，一直是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 通过将对角化非厄米算符（即霍普菲尔德矩阵，Hopfield matrix）的量子力学框架加以推广，将独立的光子损耗和激子损耗纳入极化激元态，并纳入激子辐射损失，以检验形式体系对BIC的描述能力。

Result: 研究表明，纳入激子辐射损失的非厄米霍普菲尔德形式体系能够正确捕获$k=0$处具有发散辐射寿命的真实的极化激元BIC的存在。

Conclusion: 该理论为辐射激子与极化激元BIC提供了统一的微观框架，并成为设计耦合至量子阱、过渡金属二硫化物及其他二维激子材料的光子晶体平台的高效预测工具。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) enable optical modes with ideally infinite radiative lifetimes despite lying within the radiation continuum. Radiation-matter interaction in periodically patterned planar waveguides embedding two-dimensional (2D) optically active excitations can be described quantum mechanically by diagonalizing a non-Hermitian operator, known as the Hopfield matrix, which can be generalized to incorporate independent photonic and excitonic losses into the polaritonic states. However, since 2D excitons undergo intrinsic wavevector-dependent radiative decay within the light cone, whether the Hopfield formalism can consistently account for this process while preserving polariton BICs has remained an open question. Here we show that a non-Hermitian Hopfield formalism incorporating excitonic radiative losses correctly captures the existence of genuine $k=0$ polariton BICs with diverging radiative lifetimes. The theory provides a unified microscopic framework for radiative excitons and polariton BICs, and an efficient predictive tool for designing photonic-crystal platforms coupled to quantum wells, transition-metal dichalcogenides, and other 2D excitonic materials.

</details>


### [304] [Supermoiré Reconstruction and Topological Mosaics in Twisted Trilayer WSe$_2$ and MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30813)
*Hai Meng,Yang Xu,Fengcheng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习力场（machine-learning force fields）和大尺度从头计算（large-scale ab initio calculations），研究了螺旋与交替堆叠的三层扭转WSe₂和MoTe₂中的晶格弛豫和能带结构，发现超摩尔（supermoiré）晶格弛豫后重构为由少数畴域组成的马赛克结构，各畴域因缺乏C₂z对称性而在能量和拓扑上彼此不等价，且具有畴域依赖的谷陈数（valley Chern numbers）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越双层扭转体系的新物理平台。与已有较多研究的扭转三层石墨烯不同，过渡金属二硫化物体系缺乏C₂z对称性，可能带来能量和拓扑性质上的独特差异，值得系统研究。

Method: 采用机器学习力场结合大规模从头计算方法，系统计算螺旋和交替两种堆叠构型下三层扭转WSe₂和MoTe₂的晶格弛豫行为与电子能带结构，并分析畴域类型及相应拓扑性质。

Result: 双层摩尔（moiré）晶格之间的干涉产生超摩尔晶格，弛豫后重构为若干主导畴域类型；由于体系缺乏C₂z对称性，原本由该对称性关联的畴域在能量和拓扑上变得不等价。能带计算显示最顶层价带来自K（K'）谷，并携带畴域依赖的谷陈数。

Conclusion: 该超摩尔晶格承载由拓扑不等价畴域构成的马赛克结构，为探索关联物理和拓扑物理提供了一个新的材料平台。

Abstract: We investigate lattice relaxation and band structures of helical and alternating twisted trilayer WSe$_2$ and MoTe$_2$ using machine-learning force fields and large-scale ab initio calculations. Interference between the two bilayer moiré lattices generates a supermoiré lattice that, upon relaxation, reconstructs into a few dominant domain types with locally commensurate bilayer moiré lattices. Because the systems lack $C_{2z}$ symmetry, domains otherwise related by this symmetry become energetically and topologically distinct, unlike in twisted trilayer graphene. Band structure calculations show that the topmost valence bands originate from the $K$ ($K'$) valleys and carry domain-dependent valley Chern numbers. The resulting supermoiré lattice hosts a mosaic of topologically inequivalent domains, offering a platform for exploring correlated and topological physics.

</details>


### [305] [Local phase-space Berry curvature and Hall transport in textured twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30882)
*Tohid Farajollahpour*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）中慢扭转角纹理与异质应变纹理如何产生相空间贝里几何（phase-space Berry geometry）。作者证明纯几何四元组/位移扇区（tetrad/shift sector）在自旋联络一致处理时不会产生混合贝里曲率，而投影后的纹理迷你狄拉克锥则会产生真实的混合相空间曲率。在一维纹理和M_x镜面对称下的局域霍尔棒极限中，几何响应（赝规范梯度导致的纵向电导率重整化和非线性霍尔响应）均在相同的局域填充μ_loc=√2 m处达到峰值，为该共同起源提供了可通过栅极调谐的特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层石墨烯中的空间缓慢变化纹理为探索相空间贝里几何提供了自然途径，但目前缺乏对几何扇区与纹理诱导效应的清晰分离，以及预测可观测输运响应（如非线性霍尔响应）的系统框架。

Method: 作者在自旋联络一致处理的条件下，分析纯几何四元组/位移扇区与投影纹理迷你狄拉克锥的混合贝里曲率差异；在一维纹理及M_x镜面对称约束下建立局域霍尔棒极限模型，并从θ=1.3°的异质应变Bistritzer-MacDonald模型中提取所有局域锥参数及其纹理磁化率。

Result: 纯几何四元组/位移扇区不产生混合贝里曲率，而投影的纹理迷你狄拉克锥会产生真实的混合相空间曲率；赝规范梯度仅重整化沿纹理方向的纵向电导率，非线性霍尔响应仅在额外的谷奇倾斜产生贝里曲率偶极子时出现；两种几何响应在同一局域填充处达到峰值，所涉倾斜对应的异质应变仅为0.03%-0.2%，低于器件中常规观测的数值。

Conclusion: 该工作为纹理化莫尔狄拉克材料提供了局域解析框架，清晰分离了几何、纹理诱导与输运层次的物理要素，对实际TBG器件具有直接适用性。

Abstract: Slow twist-angle and heterostrain textures in twisted bilayer graphene provide a natural route to phase-space Berry geometry. We show that a purely geometric tetrad/shift sector does not generate mixed Berry curvature once the spin connection is treated consistently. By contrast, a projected textured mini-Dirac cone in twisted bilayer graphene acquires genuine mixed phase-space curvature. We analyze a controlled local Hall-bar limit with a one-dimensional texture and mirror $M_x$. In that limit the pseudogauge gradient renormalizes only the longitudinal conductivity along the textured direction, while a dc nonlinear Hall response appears only when an additional valley-odd tilt generates a Berry-curvature dipole. Both geometric responses peak at the same local filling, $μ_{\rm loc}=\sqrt{2}\,m$, providing a gate-tunable fingerprint of their common origin. All local-cone parameters and their texture susceptibilities are extracted from a heterostrained Bistritzer-MacDonald model at $θ=1.3^\circ$. The tilt invoked in the transport estimates corresponds to heterostrain of only $0.03\%-0.2\%$, below values routinely imaged in devices. The resulting theory provides a local analytic framework for textured moiré Dirac materials and cleanly separates geometric, texture-induced, and transport-level ingredients relevant to realistic TBG.

</details>


### [306] [Signatures of inter-sideband coherence in the resonance fluorescence spectrum of an acoustically-modulated quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30886)
*Rafał A. Bogaczewicz,Hubert J. Krenner,Paweł Machnikowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究通过滤波场形式论（filtered-field formalism）在三相干涉（Mach-Zehnder）构型下理论探究声学调制量子点（quantum dot）共振荧光谱中的带间相位相干性（inter-sideband phase coherence）。结果表明干涉图的几何倾斜可作为对环境白噪声（white noise）稳健的相位相干性指示器，为频域编码（frequency-bin encoding）和可扩展量子网络提供了验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固体量子系统中，环境噪声引起的频谱扩散（spectral diffusion）会破坏单光子相干性，阻碍频域编码和量子网络的实际部署，因此需要寻找对噪声稳健的相干性检测方案。

Method: 采用滤波场形式论构建声学调制量子点在Mach-Zehnder构型下的共振荧光谱模型，理论分析干涉图中几何倾斜（geometric slant）与噪声间的相互作用关系。

Result: 噪声诱导的频谱扩散会降低全局条纹强度（global fringe intensity），但干涉图的特征倾斜角度严格保持不变，证明该几何指标具有对白噪声的稳健性（resilience）。

Conclusion: 本工作建立了在真实、有噪声的固态架构中验证谱带间单光子相干性的理论框架，为频率编码和可扩展量子网络的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the inter-sideband phase coherence within the resonance fluorescence spectrum of an acoustically modulated quantum dot using a filtered-field formalism for a Mach-Zehnder configuration. We demonstrate that geometric slant of the interferograms provides an indicator of phase coherence that is resilient to environmental white noise. Specifically, noise-induced spectral diffusion reduces the global fringe intensity, while leaving the characteristic inclination strictly invariant. Our findings establish a framework for verifying single-photon coherence between spectral sidebands, essential for frequency-bin encoding and scalable quantum networking in realistic, noisy solid-state architectures.

</details>


### [307] [Breakdown of Charge-Conjugation Symmetry of Disclinations in 2D Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30921)
*Ruslan Yamaletdinov,Mikhail I. Katsnelson,Oleg V. Yazyev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过原子模拟（atomistic simulation）研究了自由悬浮石墨烯中向错（disclination）的弹性性质，发现负拓扑荷向错的能量呈亚线性标度、平衡形态偏离传统鞍面假设，并展现出长程吸引相互作用，颠覆了正负向错之间的电荷共轭对称性，揭示了负曲率缺陷迥异的能量景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向错是二维晶体膜中的基本拓扑缺陷，但其弹性性质此前鲜有研究。理解向错特别是负向错的能量与相互作用规律，是阐明石墨烯等二维膜稳定性、自折叠行为和缺陷驱动形貌的基础。

Method: 采用原子模拟（atomistic simulation）方法，以自由悬浮石墨烯作为典型的二维晶体模型，系统考察不同拓扑荷向错的能量（energetics）、形貌（morphology）和相互作用（interactions），并进一步研究自粘附（self-adhesion）折叠膜中这些效应的放大情况。

Result: 正向错（正拓扑荷）行为符合预期；而负向错能量随电荷呈亚线性标度（sublinear energy scaling），其平衡形状显著偏离传统鞍面（saddle ansatz）假设。电荷共轭对称性的破坏导致定性不同的相互作用：正向错相互排斥，负向错则呈现稳健的长程吸引。这些趋势在自粘附折叠膜中进一步放大。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了负曲率缺陷（negative curvature defects）所对应的根本不同的能量景观，为理解石墨烯及其他二维膜材料的稳定性、自折叠行为和缺陷驱动的形貌提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Disclinations are elementary topological defects in two-dimensional (2D) crystalline membranes, yet their elastic properties remain largely unexplored. Using atomistic simulations, we address the energetics, morphology, and interactions of disclinations in free-standing graphene, a prototypical 2D crystal. We find that while disclinations with positive topological charges follow an expected behavior, negative disclinations exhibit sublinear energy scaling with charge as well as equilibrium shape that deviates sharply from the conventional saddle ansatz. This breakdown of charge-conjugation symmetry leads to qualitatively distinct interactions: positive disclinations repel, whereas negative disclinations display a robust long-range attraction. These trends are shown to be further amplified by self-adhesion in folded membranes. Our results uncover a fundamentally different energetic landscape for negative curvature defects and provide a basis for understanding the stability, self-folding behavior, and defect-driven morphology of graphene and other 2D membranes.

</details>


### [308] [Interfacial Spin-to-Charge Conversion in Sputtered MoTe2 Heterostructures Probed by Spin Pumping and Spin-Torque Ferromagnetic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30953)
*J. L. Costa,E. Santos,E. L. T. França,J. B. S. Mendes,A. Azevedo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过自旋泵浦与自旋力矩铁磁共振（SP-FMR和ST-FMR）实验，系统研究了MoTe2/MoTe2异质结构中的自旋-电荷互转换机制，发现其转效率与MoTe2厚度无关，表明转换主要由Py/MoTe2界面处的Rashba-Edelstein效应主导，而非体自旋输运，这一发现为低功耗自旋电子器件提供了新的设计思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属二硫族化物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）及其Weyl半金属相（Weyl semimetal phases，如MoTe2）因高效的电荷-自旋转换而备受关注，但其转换主要源于体效应还是界面效应尚不清楚，这一核心争议有待阐明。

Method: 采用自旋泵浦铁磁共振（spin-pumping FMR）和自旋力矩铁磁共振（spin-torque FMR）两种实验手段，对MoTe2进行厚度依赖的系统测量，并通过双向SP-FMR和界面分离测量进行辅助验证。

Result: 实验发现MoTe2中的自旋-电荷转换效率和自旋力矩效率在大范围厚度下基本不变，与Pt异质结构中呈现的显著厚度依赖形成鲜明对比，表明转换主要由Py/MoTe2界面的Rashba-Edelstein效应主导。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Py/MoTe2界面在实现高效自旋-电荷转换和自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torque）中的主导作用，表明溅射生长的MoTe2/Py异质结构有望用于低功耗自旋电子应用，包括磁存储和逻辑器件。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) and their Weyl semimetal phases, such as MoTe$_2$, have attracted significant attention for spin-orbit torque applications due to their efficient charge-to-spin conversion. However, whether this conversion originates predominantly from the bulk or the interface remains unclear. Here, we investigate spin-charge interconversion in MoTe$_2$ using spin-pumping and spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance (SP-FMR and ST-FMR). Thickness-dependent measurements reveal large spin-to-charge conversion and spin-torque efficiencies that are essentially independent of MoTe$_2$ thickness, indicating that the conversion is predominantly governed by the Rashba-Edelstein effect at the Py/MoTe$_2$ interface rather than by bulk spin transport. This behavior contrasts with the characteristic thickness dependence observed in Pt heterostructures and is further supported by bidirectional SP-FMR and interface-separation measurements. Our results highlight the dominant role of the Py/MoTe$_2$ interface in enabling efficient spin-charge conversion and spin-orbit torques in TMD-based spintronic devices. These findings highlight the potential of sputtered MoTe$_2$/Py heterostructures for low-power spintronic applications, including magnetic memory and logic devices.

</details>


### [309] [Device characterization of Si$/$SiGe double quantum dots using exchange oscillations in Earth's magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31093)
*Holly G. Stemp,Harry Hanlim Kang,Chih Hwan Yang,Gabriel D. Cutter,Frederike Brockmeyer,Patrick J. Strohbeen,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,William D. Oliver*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文利用同位素富集Si/SiGe中残余核自旋产生的本征磁场梯度，在零外加磁场条件下实现两个量子点之间的交换振荡，作为一种无需微磁体、动态核极化或多轴标定的简单诊断工具，并通过CPMG序列将单重态相干时间从T2*=1.17 μs延长至T2^CPMG=74.8 μs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于交换（exchange-based）的半导体量子比特多种编码方式可在零外加磁场下运行，但其表征通常依赖多轴控制标定或人为设计的磁场梯度。对于混合半导体-超导体系统，磁场会降级超导组件，因此零场诊断方案尤为重要。

Method: 利用同位素富集Si/SiGe中残余核自旋产生的内在磁场梯度，实现两量子点间的交换振荡；采用Carr-Purcell-Meiboom-Gill（CPMG）交换序列以70个重聚焦脉冲延长单重态相干时间，并通过振荡相位解析残余交换的数值及其在(1,1)电荷胞内的分布。

Result: CPMG交换序列将单重态相干时间从T2*=1.17±0.02 μs延长至T2^CPMG=74.8±1.8 μs（N=70个重聚焦脉冲）；振荡相位可解析数十kHz量级的残余交换，并实现其在(1,1)电荷胞上的映射。

Conclusion: 本征梯度交换振荡可作为交换型及相关的半导体量子比特编码简单且更贴近实际工况的零场诊断工具，适用于快速高通量的器件表征。

Abstract: Exchange-based semiconductor qubits encompass a broad family of encodings constructed from singlet- and triplet-like spin states, several of which are compatible with operation at zero applied magnetic field. Their reliable operation requires characterization of environmental noise, residual idle interactions, and exchange-dependent decay, but this characterization often relies on multi-axis control calibration or deliberately engineered magnetic-field gradients. A simpler zero-applied-field diagnostic is particularly valuable for hybrid semiconductor-superconductor systems, in which magnetic fields can degrade superconducting components. Here, we use the intrinsic magnetic-field gradient produced by residual nuclear spins in isotopically enriched Si/SiGe to implement exchange oscillations between two quantum dots as a characterization tool without a micromagnet, dynamic nuclear polarization, or prior multi-axis calibration. Using Carr-Purcell-Meiboom-Gill exchange sequences, we extend the singlet coherence from $T_2^*=1.17\pm0.02~μ$s to $T_2^{\mathrm{CPMG}}=74.8\pm1.8~μ$s with $N=70$ refocusing pulses. The oscillation phase resolves residual exchange in the tens-of-kilohertz regime and enables it to be mapped across the $(1,1)$ charge cell. These results establish intrinsic-gradient exchange oscillations as a simple, more relevant zero-field diagnostic for exchange-only and related semiconductor qubit encodings that is amenable to rapid, high-throughput device characterization.

</details>
