### [310] [Analysis of the Principal Components of Correlation Matrices of S&P 500 Financial Data from an Econophysics Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26128)
*Javier Gómez Morales*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本论文从经济物理学（econophysics）视角，将标普500指数视为复杂系统，基于430家公司的日对数收益率构建滑窗皮尔逊相关矩阵，通过主特征值、主特征向量平方分量及截断矩阵重构等谱量，采用k-Means聚类识别市场状态（Market States），并分析其稳定性、转移矩阵及参与度分布。COVID-19期间被识别为异常状态，需多个主成分才能重构，危机期资产参与度更加分散。该研究是描述性而非预测性的，为资产组合设计与交易策略提供概念和方法论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从复杂系统视角分析金融市场的集体动力学（collective dynamics），探索如何利用相关矩阵的谱结构（spectral structure）揭示市场状态的动态演化，并量化资产参与度的分布规律。

Method: 以430家公司的日对数收益率为基础，在滑窗内构建时变Pearson相关矩阵；利用最大特征值（市场状态）、主特征向量平方项（各股的相对参与度）以及由最大l个特征对重构并归一化的截断矩阵C^l作为特征，通过k-Means聚类识别市场状态；进一步计算状态转移矩阵（transition matrix）及平稳向量，并使用逆参与比（inverse participation ratio）量化参与度在资产间的分散程度。

Result: COVID-19在完整相关矩阵中呈现为异常状态，需超过一个主成分方可重构，且C^2和C^3构造在窗长q=40、聚类数k=5条件下可复现该状态；危机期间相对参与度在更多资产间分散；2017-2018区间对动力学有贡献但未表现为独立状态。

Conclusion: 该研究为描述性而非预测性分析，提供了适用于资产组合设计与交易策略的概念和方法论工具，并自然可扩展至其他国际市场。

Abstract: This thesis analyzes the collective dynamics of the S&P 500 from an econophysics perspective, treating the financial market as a complex system. Using daily logarithmic returns of 430 companies, time-dependent Pearson correlation matrices are constructed over sliding windows and their spectral structure is studied through the leading eigenvalues and eigenvectors. Market States (MS) are identified by applying k-Means clustering to several spectral quantities: the largest eigenvalue (interpreted as the State of the Market), the squared entries of the dominant eigenvector (read as a relative participation of each stock), and truncated matrix reconstructions C^l built from the l largest eigenpairs and normalized to retain a correlation-matrix interpretation.
  The stability of the resulting clusters is assessed, transition matrices and their stationary vectors are computed, and the inverse participation ratio is used to quantify how participation spreads across assets. The COVID-19 episode emerges as an atypical state in the full correlation matrices; reconstructing it requires more than one principal component, and the C^2 and C^3 constructions reproduce it, particularly for window length q=40 and k=5. During crisis periods the relative participation distributes over a larger number of assets, while the 2017-2018 interval contributes to the dynamics without appearing as an isolated regime. The analysis is descriptive rather than predictive, and provides conceptual and methodological tools relevant to portfolio design and trading strategies, with natural extensions to other international exchanges.

</details>

### [311] [Local connectivity balance shapes population dynamics in random recurrent networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30008)
*Shotaro Takasu,Richard Gast,Ann Kennedy*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文从解析上研究了具有不同程度局部连通性平衡（local connectivity balance）的随机递归网络，发现该平衡虽不改变连接谱（connectivity spectrum），却通过抑制自生成反馈输入（self-generated feedback input）显著重塑集体动力学：在线性及以上激活函数下稳定网络，在次线性或饱和激活函数下驱动网络进入混沌，且对常见的奇对称激活函数这些效应完全消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经回路中兴奋性与抑制性权重汇聚到每个单元时近似相消的局部连通性平衡普遍存在，但其对集体网络动力学的影响此前尚不明确，需要在随机递归网络框架下进行系统解析。

Method: 对具有不同局部平衡程度、包含单单元非线性激活函数的随机连通递归网络进行解析分析，考察连接谱变化与动力学校稳性/混沌行为，并揭示统一机制。

Result: 局部平衡不改变连接谱，却剧烈重塑动力学：激活函数为线性或更快增长时抑制网络状态无界增长并稳定动力学，次线性或饱和激活函数时驱动网络进入混沌；对奇激活函数效应消失；饱和非线性下有效维度随平衡程度非单调变化；所有现象源于局部平衡对自生成反馈输入的抑制。

Conclusion: 局部连通性平衡被识别为真实无序网络中集体动力学的一个此前被忽视的控制参数（control parameter），为理解神经网络稳定性与混沌行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Disordered dynamical systems comprising many interacting units, from ecological communities to neural circuits, are ubiquitous, and understanding how connectivity shapes their collective behavior is a central theoretical challenge. One long-recognized feature of neural circuits is local connectivity balance, in which the excitatory and inhibitory weights converging onto each unit approximately cancel. Although local connectivity balance has been proposed to serve functions such as gating incoming signals, its effect on collective network dynamics remains unclear. Here we analytically study randomly connected recurrent networks with varying degrees of local connectivity balance. We show that this balance leaves the connectivity spectrum unchanged yet drastically reshapes the dynamics in a manner that depends critically on the single-unit nonlinearity. Local balance suppresses unbounded growth of the network state and stabilizes network dynamics when the activation function scales linearly or faster, whereas it drives the network into chaos when the activation function is sub-linear or saturating. Importantly, these effects vanish for odd activation functions, which are commonly assumed in previous work. We further find that, for saturating nonlinearities, the effective dimension of the dynamics varies nonmonotonically with the degree of balance. We show that all these phenomena arise from a unifying mechanism: the suppression of a self-generated feedback input by local connectivity balance. Our results identify local connectivity balance as a previously overlooked control parameter for collective dynamics in realistic disordered networks.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

### [312] [ENPINN: Energy-Norm-Guided Gradient-Enhanced PINNs for Generalized Transport Problems with Sharp Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29499)
*Subhendu Maity,Pratibhamoy Das,Arihant Patawari,Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出能量范数增强型物理信息神经网络（energy-norm-enhanced PINN, ENPINN），通过在损失函数中引入梯度信息和变分结构，提高对具有陡峭梯度的边界层和内部层解的分辨能力。作者先分析了弱损失PINN（WLPINN）和梯度增强PINN（gPINN）两种相关公式的局限性，进而系统构建ENPINN，并从理论上证明能量范数误差与ENPINN损失之间的关系，以及神经网络的逼近存在性和导数界。数值实验涵盖燃烧模型、耦合多尺度系统、二维Burgers方程和三维含时问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural networks, PINNs）作为求解偏微分方程（partial differential equations, PDEs）的无网格替代方法已广泛使用，但其捕获陡峭梯度的能力有限，在边界层和内部层的求解中会产生显著误差，亟需改进。

Method: 首先分析两种相关公式——在传统PINN残差中引入测试函数的弱损失PINN（weak-loss PINNs, WLPINNs）和用PDE残差的空间导数增强损失的梯度增强PINN（gradient-enhanced PINNs, gPINNs），据此识别其局限性。进而提出ENPINN，在损失函数中整合梯度信息和变分结构，并建立能量范数误差与损失的关系。理论上证明具有任意小能量误差的神经网络逼近的存在性，并推导相应的导数界。

Result: 理论分析表明，合适的修正残差导数项（residual-derivative term）对准确捕获边界层至关重要；同时证明了具有任意小能量误差的神经网络近似解的存在性。数值实验显示，在对流-扩散-反应问题（convection-diffusion-reaction problems）中，ENPINN对陡峭梯度的分辨能力优于现有PINN变体，并在燃烧模型、耦合多尺度系统、二维Burgers方程及三维含时问题中得到验证。

Conclusion: ENPINN通过与能量范数相关的损失构造，有效改善了层主导解的求解精度，为PINN在具有陡峭梯度的PDE问题中的应用提供了理论支撑与实践验证。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a meshless alternative to conventional numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, their limited ability to capture sharp gradients can lead to substantial errors when resolving boundary and interior layers. Here, we introduce an energy-norm-enhanced PINN (ENPINN) that incorporates gradient information and variational structure into the loss function to improve the resolution of layer-dominated solutions. We first examine two related formulations: weak-loss PINNs (WLPINNs), which incorporate test functions into the conventional PINN residual, and gradient-enhanced PINNs (gPINNs), which augment the loss with spatial derivatives of the PDE residual. By analyzing these formulations, we identify their limitations in resolving steep solution gradients and motivate the systematic construction of ENPINN. We establish theoretically how the energy-norm error depends on the ENPINN loss and show that a suitably modified residual-derivative term is essential for accurately capturing boundary layers. We further establish the existence of neural-network approximations with arbitrarily small energy error and derive corresponding derivative bounds, providing a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework. The performance of ENPINN is assessed through systematic comparisons with existing PINN variants for convection-diffusion-reaction problems exhibiting steep gradients. Numerical experiments include a combustion model, a coupled multi-scale system, a two-dimensional Burgers equation with an interior layer, and a three-dimensional time-dependent problem.

</details>


### [313] [Entropy-Stable and Physical-Constraint-Preserving DGSEM for Symmetry-Reduced General-Relativistic Hydrodynamics on Stationary Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29229)
*Guosheng Fu,Jian-Guo Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于对称约化广义相对论流体力学（symmetry-reduced general-relativistic hydrodynamics）的熵稳定且保持物理约束的不连续伽辽金谱元方法（DGSEM）。通过在仿射张量积网格上运用局部正交变换，将流体变量转化为与空间度量无关的形式，使几何度规仅通过静态系数进入系统，从而结合熵守恒的特殊相对论通量与几何源项的相容离散。数值实验覆盖光滑流、强激波、轴对称射流、Michel吸积、Schwarzschild Bondi–Hoyle流及四种Kerr吸积情形，验证了高阶精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论流体力学（general-relativistic hydrodynamics）在弯曲静态时空中的数值模拟面临熵稳定性与物理约束保持的双重挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾两者。作者旨在构建一种在仿射张量积网格上既保守又满足半离散熵不等式的方法，同时保持物理约束（如密度、压力非负）的凸性框架。

Method: 方法核心是引入局部正交变换（local orthonormal transformation），使流体变量的相对论流体力学代数结构与可容许集独立于空间度量，而时空几何仅通过静态系数进入。基于此分离，将熵守恒的特殊相对论通量与几何源项的相容离散结合；在仿射张量积网格上，DGSEM满足保守性与半离散熵不等式。稳定化采用仅依赖几何的因果速度，用于经典局部Lax–Friedrichs熵耗散和物理约束保持的Lax–Friedrichs分裂；全离散格式结合SSP Runge–Kutta时间推进、振荡消除及保守局部正交态缩放。

Result: 数值实验涵盖光滑与强激波特殊相对论流动、轴对称射流、静止Michel吸积、Schwarzschild Bondi–Hoyle流以及四种Kerr吸积情形。结果表明该方法在光滑区域达到设计的高阶精度，并在弯曲静态背景上的高要求相对论流动中表现稳健。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将熵稳定性与物理约束保持统一在仿射张量积网格的DGSEM框架内，为对称约化广义相对论流体力学在预设静态时空上的数值模拟提供了稳健且精确的工具。

Abstract: We develop an entropy-stable and physical-constraint-preserving discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method for symmetry-reduced general-relativistic hydrodynamics on prescribed stationary spacetimes. Using a local orthonormal transformation, the fluid variables are expressed in a form for which the relativistic hydrodynamic algebra and the admissible set are independent of the spatial metric, while the spacetime geometry enters through stationary coefficients. This separation allows entropy-conservative special-relativistic fluxes to be combined with a compatible discretization of the geometric source terms. On affine tensor-product meshes, the resulting DGSEM is conservative and satisfies a semidiscrete entropy inequality, while the transformed variables provide a convex framework for physical-constraint preservation.
  For practical stabilization, we use a geometry-only causal speed that is sufficient for both classical local Lax--Friedrichs entropy dissipation and the physical-constraint-preserving Lax--Friedrichs splitting. The fully discrete method combines this stabilization with SSP Runge--Kutta time stepping, oscillation elimination, and conservative local-orthonormal-state scaling. Numerical experiments cover smooth and strongly shocked special-relativistic flows, an axisymmetric jet, stationary Michel accretion, Schwarzschild Bondi--Hoyle flow, and four Kerr accretion cases. The results demonstrate the designed high-order accuracy in smooth regimes and robust performance for demanding relativistic flows on curved stationary backgrounds.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [314] [The $\mathbb{Z}_2$-Index of a Pair of Pure States and the Topology of Interacting 1D Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28796)
*Anna Mazhar,Jacob Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在含单位元的C*-代数上为局域可比的宇称不变纯态定义了一个相对Z₂指标，并证明了其良定义性、可乘性、宇称保持自同构不变性及范数拓扑下的局部常值性。作者将该构造应用于一维自对偶CAR代数的半链自同构，定义了多体马约拉纳数（many-body Majorana number），证明其在准自由希尔伯特-施密特（Hilbert–Schmidt）区域与经典单体马约拉纳数一致，并据此完全分类了对称局部自同构路径的连通分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳数（Majorana number）是拓扑超导体分类中的关键不变量，但传统定义局限于单粒子（准自由）体系。本文旨在将这一概念推广到一般（非准自由）的多体相互作用体系，从而为更广泛的一维拓扑相分类提供严格的数学框架。

Method: 作者在含单位元C*-代数（unital C*-algebra）上，对局域可比（locally comparable）且宇称不变（parity-invariant）的纯态定义相对Z₂指标（relative Z₂-index）；通过半链自同构（half-chain automorphism）σ构造多体马约拉纳数；并引入对称局部自同构路径（symmetric local automorphism paths）来定义等价关系，证明指标对其构成完整分类不变量。

Result: 该指标满足良定义性、可乘性、宇称保持自同构不变性及范数拓扑局部常值性；在准自由希尔伯特-施密特区域内，多体马约拉纳数与单体马约拉纳数一致，验证了构造与既有理论的兼容性；该指标完全分类了对称局部自同构路径的连通分量，但该等价关系可能丢失相对零维宇称障碍（relative zero-dimensional parity obstruction）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个适用于一般多体态的Z₂拓扑不变量，将马约拉纳数的适用范围从准自由体系推广至强相互作用体系。所定义的自动态路径等价关系虽保留体马约拉纳数，但无法区分可能存在的相对零维宇称障碍，这一局限性为后续工作提供了研究方向。

Abstract: We define a relative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-index $\mathcal{N}(ω_1,ω_2)$ for locally-comparable, parity-invariant pure states on a unital $C^*$-algebra. We prove that the index is well-defined, multiplicative, invariant under parity-preserving automorphisms, and locally constant in the norm topology. For the one-dimensional self-dual CAR algebra, we apply this construction to the half-chain automorphism $σ$ and define the many-body Majorana number $\mathcal{N}σ(ω):=\mathcal{N}(ω,ω\circσ)$ for parity-invariant $σ$-local pure states. In the quasi-free Hilbert--Schmidt regime, this index agrees with the usual single-body Majorana number. We then introduce symmetric local automorphism paths and prove that $\mathcal{N}_σ$ completely classifies the resulting automorphic-path-components. This automorphic-path equivalence retains the bulk Majorana number but may forget a relative zero-dimensional parity obstruction.

</details>


### [315] [Pseudorandomness and Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28917)
*David Damanik,Nicolae Strungaru*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个形如 V(n)=λcos(2π(x₁+nx₂+n(n-1)α/2)) 的拟随机结构（pseudorandom structure）在衍射（diffraction）意义上具有拟随机性，即其衍射测度（diffraction measure）对几乎所有参数组合都是纯绝对连续的，这一性质与随机势的情形一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟随机结构（pseudorandom structures）是行为类似于随机但自身不必是随机的结构。已有著名猜想认为上述势函数在薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operator）视角下是拟随机的（即表现出安德森局域化，Anderson localization），本文旨在通过衍射性质为这一猜想提供进一步证据，并阐明薛定谔谱测度与衍射测度之间表现出的二元性。

Method: 作者从衍射理论的角度对该势函数进行分析，研究其关联的衍射测度是否为纯绝对连续（purely absolutely continuous），并针对所有非零 λ、所有无理数 α 以及所有 x₁, x₂ 的参数范围进行论证。

Result: 对所有 λ≠0、所有无理数 α 及所有 x₁, x₂，该势函数 V 所关联的衍射测度均为纯绝对连续的，这恰好与随机势情形下的衍射特征一致，从而证明了 V 在衍射性质上具有拟随机性。

Conclusion: 该结果对薛定谔算子情形下的拟随机性猜想提供了进一步证据，并揭示了薛定谔谱测度（Schrödinger spectral measure）与衍射测度之间明显的二元对偶行为（dual behavior）。

Abstract: Pseudorandom structures are structures that behave like random ones, without necessarily being random themselves. It is a prominent, and evidently very difficult, conjecture that $$ V(n) = λ\cos \Big( 2 π\Big( x_1 + n x_2 + \frac{n(n-1)}{2} α\Big) \Big) $$ is pseudorandom from a Schrödinger operator perspective in that the Schrödinger operator in $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z})$ with potential $V$ displays Anderson localization, that is, pure point spectrum with exponentially decaying eigenfunctions for almost all parameter values --- the same spectral features as those produced by random potentials. We show that $V$ is pseudorandom in terms of its diffraction properties, that is, the associated diffraction measure is purely absolutely continuous for all $λ\not= 0$, all irrational $α$, and all $x_1,x_2$ --- which is the case as well for the random case. Our result gives further evidence for the conjecture in the Schrödinger case and it elucidates the apparent dual behavior of Schrödinger spectral measures and diffraction measures.

</details>


### [316] [Simulating passive scalar advection in rough Kraichnan flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29321)
*Long Li,André L. P. Considera,Simon Thalabard*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对粗糙二维Kraichnan流中的被动标量（passive scalar）输运进行了系统的欧拉（Eulerian）数值研究，覆盖了速度粗糙度指数h∈(0,1)的全范围，通过高达2048²分辨率的伪谱法（pseudo-spectral method）模拟，验证了标量与流场之间的对偶关系，测量了四阶平坦度反常（flatness anomaly），并提供了可靠的模拟参数选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多在拉格朗日（Lagrangian）框架下预测Kraichnan流中被动标量的标度律和非高斯统计，但缺乏系统的欧拉数值验证；同时，如何在不同粗糙度指数h下正确设置模拟参数（尤其是分子扩散系数）尚无定论，这阻碍了该模型的可靠数值研究及其向更复杂扩展模型的推广。

Method: 采用伪谱法直接在欧拉框架下积分平流-扩散（advection-diffusion）方程，分辨率高达2048×2048网格点；使用冻结噪声龙格-库塔（frozen-noise Runge–Kutta）格式，与载波流（carrier flow）的白噪声时间构造保持一致。通过分析二阶统计量、四阶平坦度、标量增量概率密度函数（PDF）及高阶统计量来评估标度行为。

Result: 模拟重现了"载波越平滑、标量越粗糙"的对偶关系，并验证了第二阶统计量的理论标度律；在正确识别标度区间后，测量了h全范围内的四阶平坦度反常，与Frisch等人（1999）的拉格朗日估计及Bernard等人（1998）和Pumir等人（1997）的微扰预测一致。标量增量的PDF及高阶统计呈现系统性偏离高斯和对数正态（log-normality）行为，在中间h值处偏差最显著。此外，系统阐述了模拟参数（尤其分子扩散系数）随h的调整方法，并证明残余偏差可由载波流的有限谱表示定量解释。

Conclusion: 本文为粗糙Kraichnan流中被动标量的欧拉模拟提供了系统的方法论基线和数值基准，明确了参数设置规则，并验证了非高斯统计的普适特征；该工作可作为模拟流场与高斯乘性混沌（Gaussian multiplicative chaos）耦合的更现实扩展模型的数值基础，在这些扩展中理论进展有限。

Abstract: We present a systematic Eulerian study of passive scalar advection in rough two-dimensional Kraichnan flows, covering the full range of velocity roughness exponent $h\in(0,1)$. The advection--diffusion equation is integrated directly using a pseudo-spectral method at resolutions up to $2048^2$ grid points, with a frozen-noise Runge--Kutta scheme consistent with the white-in-time construction of the carrier flow. The simulations recover the duality between advected scalar and advecting flow---the smoother the carrier, the rougher the scalar---together with the predicted scaling laws for the second-order statistics. Once the scaling range is properly identified, the fourth-order flatness anomaly is measured across the whole range of $h$, in agreement with the Lagrangian estimates of Frisch \textit{et al.} (1999) and with the perturbative predictions of Bernard \textit{et al.} (1998) and Pumir \textit{et al.} (1997). Benefiting from the fact that our Eulerian simulations give direct access to the full scalar field, we also examine the probability density functions of scalar increments and the corresponding higher-order statistics, which show systematic departures from Gaussianity and from log-normality, with the strongest deviations manifesting for intermediate values of $h$. A central outcome of this work is a systematic account of how the simulation parameters, in particular the molecular diffusivity, must be adjusted with $h$, providing practical guidelines for reliable simulations; we further show that the residual deviations from the theoretical scaling laws are quantitatively accounted for by the finite spectral representation of the carrier flow. Our analysis also serve as a numerical baseline for simulating more realistic extensions of the Kraichnan model, where the carrier flow is coupled with a Gaussian multiplicative chaos and theoretical developments are limited.

</details>


### [317] [Sharp Logarithmic Quantum Dynamics for Quasiperiodic Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29339)
*Wencai Liu,Xueyin Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类具有偶势能的一频率准周期薛定谔算子（quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators）在正李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）区域中，动力学局域化（dynamical localization）的相位一致（phase-uniform）位置矩上界在时间和矩阶数两方面的对数标度均为最优（sharp）。核心工具是基于反射对称性构造的"半均匀局域化本征函数"（semi-uniformly localized eigenfunctions），利用完全共振相位（completely resonant phase）下的两个局域中心获得沿时间序列匹配的对数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学局域化要求量子波包的所有位置矩随时间有界，但准周期薛定谔算子的界一般不随相位一致成立。在正李雅普诺夫指数区域，已知的相位一致估计以对数标度增长，本文旨在证明该对数标度及矩阶数依赖对偶势能一类算子均为不可改进的最优结果。

Method: 提出一种反射版的半均匀局域化本征函数方法，针对完全共振相位下出现的两个局域化中心进行适配；利用该方法构造沿特定时间序列的匹配对数下界，与已知的上界估计对照从而证明紧性（sharpness）。

Result: 证明了对于一类具有偶势能的一频率准周期薛定谔算子，在正李雅普诺夫指数区域中，相位一致的位置矩界在对数标度上是最优的；同时矩阶数对界的影响也是不可改进的，沿时间序列可获得匹配的对数下界。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了此类算子在完全共振相位条件下动力学局域化相位一致估计的最优性，表明已知的对数增长上界不能一般性地改进，揭示了偶势能与共振条件对动力学局域化行为的深层影响。

Abstract: Dynamical localization requires all position moments of a quantum wavepacket to remain bounded in time, but for quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators such bounds are generally not uniform in phase. In the positive Lyapunov exponent regime, the best known phase-uniform estimates instead grow on a logarithmic scale. We prove that both the logarithmic scale and the dependence on the moment order are sharp for a class of one-frequency quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with even potentials. Our main ingredient is a reflective version of semi-uniformly localized eigenfunctions, adapted to the two localization centers forced by a completely resonant phase, from which we obtain matching logarithmic lower bounds along sequences of times.

</details>


### [318] [Simultaneous Reconstructions of (x,t) -Dependent Coefficients and Initial Conditions of Parabolic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29377)
*Michael V. Klibanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了二阶一般线性抛物方程(parabolic equation)中同时重构未知的(x,t)依赖系数与未知初始条件的反问题(inverse problem)，输入数据依赖(n+1)个变量从而形式上确定(formally determined)。文章获得了该反问题的唯一性定理与稳定性估计，并首次推出了时间反转数据下抛物方程初始条件的对数稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于形式上确定输入数据的抛物型方程反问题研究均假设未知系数仅依赖x或仅依赖t，且初始条件通常假定已知，仅有两篇已引用文献例外。本文旨在填补同时重构(x,t)依赖系数与未知初始条件这一更一般情形的理论空白。

Method: 研究涉及二阶一般线性抛物方程中未知系数与初始条件的同步重构，输入数据依赖(n+1)个变量，属于形式上确定的反问题。通过建立唯一性定理与稳定性估计对其可解性进行刻画，并借助时间反转数据技术推导初始条件的对数稳定性估计(logarithmic stability estimates)。

Result: 获得了该反问题的唯一性定理与稳定性估计。作为副产品，首次得到了时间反转数据下抛物方程初始条件的对数稳定性估计及相应不等式。

Conclusion: 该反问题具有两类潜在应用：一是均值场博弈（Mean Field Games）理论框架下的公众舆论预测，二是流行病时空爆发的追踪。

Abstract: This is the first publication, in which an inverse problem of the simultaneous reconstruction of an unknown (x,t) - dependent coefficient and an unknown initial condition in a general linear parabolic equation of the second order is considered. The input data depend on (n+1) variables and are, therefore, formally determined ones. Stability estimates and uniqueness theorem are obtained for this inverse problem. As a by-product, logarithmic stability estimates for initial conditions of parabolic equations and inequalities with time reversed data are obtained for the first time.
  All known publications about inverse problems for parabolic equations with formally determined input data and unknown coefficients assume that those coefficients depend either only on x or only on t. In addition, the initial condition is assumed to be known, except of two publications cited in the text.
  The inverse problem of this paper has two potential applications. The first one is in forecasting of public opinions in the framework of the Mean Field Games theory. The second one is in tracking spatiotemporal outbreaks of epidemics.

</details>


### [319] [Kubo Formulas and Bulk-Edge Correspondence for Curved Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29524)
*Aren Martinian,Tom Stoiber*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用Roe代数与非可分C*-代数适用的KK-理论变体，证明了在任意边界下一般位置空间Dirac算子的指标配对（index pairing）满足广义体-边对应（bulk-edge correspondence），并证明了该配对与标准对偶Dirac算子所得结果在乘积意义下相等，同时给出了广义Kubo公式的实空间表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强拓扑绝缘体（strong topological insulators）由整数不变量分类，这些不变量存在多种实空间（real-space）表达形式。受近期关于弯曲边界空间中这些不变量的研究启发，作者希望严格考察各种表达形式（包括Fredholm指标配对与涉及半空间投影的Kubo公式）之间的等价性。

Method: 作者使用Roe代数和适用于非可分C*-代数的KK-理论变体作为主要工具，对带任意边界情形下、与一般位置空间Dirac算子（position-space Dirac operator）的指标配对，证明了一种普遍的体-边对应形式。对于粗等价于R^d的空间，通过符号函数（symbol function）的拓扑度（topological degree）显式给出配对的多重性关系。

Result: 证明了对粗等价于R^d的空间，一般指标配对与标准对偶Dirac算子（dual Dirac operator）得到的配对在多重性意义下相等，多重性由符号函数的拓扑度显式给出。此外，还证明了广义Kubo公式（generalized Kubo formula），该公式通过实空间表达式计算一般的指标配对。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了强拓扑绝缘体中多种实空间整数不变量的表达形式，建立了适用于任意边界的广义体-边对应，并推广了Kubo公式的适用范围，为拓扑不变量在不同几何条件下的等价性提供了严格的数学证明。

Abstract: Strong topological insulators are classified by integer invariants which admit different real-space expressions. Inspired by recent work studying these invariants for spaces with curved boundaries, we revisit the problem of equivalence of the various expressions, including Fredholm index pairings and Kubo formulas involving half-space projections. Using Roe algebras and a variant of KK-theory suitable for non-separable $C^*$-algebras, we prove a general form of bulk-edge correspondence for the index pairing with a general position-space Dirac operator in the presence of arbitrary boundaries. For spaces coarsely equivalent to $\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that up to multiplicity, these pairings are equal to those obtained with the standard dual Dirac operator, with the multiplicity explicitly given as the topological degree of the symbol function. In addition, we prove a generalized Kubo formula which computes the general index pairing by a real-space formula.

</details>


### [320] [A Morse-Family Integrator for Hamilton--Jacobi Dynamics Across Caustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29645)
*F. Jiménez Alburquerque,M. Leok,C. Sardón,X. Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个基于离散Morse族（Morse families）、Lagrangian关系（Lagrangian relations）和Tulczyjew三重（Tulczyjew's triple）离散类比的几何框架，将定义离散动力学的Lagrangian子流形（Lagrangian submanifold）视为基本几何对象，而非显式辛演化映射，从而自然容纳隐式、约束和退化离散系统。在此框架下建立了II型离散Hamilton–Jacobi理论，并通过与Morse族的复合实现Lagrangian子流形在奇异投影下的传播，最终应用于光波前穿过折叠焦散（fold caustics）的传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统显式离散Hamilton系统无法自然处理隐式、约束和退化情形，需要一种更一般的几何框架。该框架应将Lagrangian子流形视为基本对象，并为无单值生成函数的情形提供正则的传播描述。

Method: 基于离散Morse族、Lagrangian关系及Tulczyjew三重离散类比建立几何框架；提出II型离散Hamilton–Jacobi理论，通过相邻离散步间Lagrangian子流形的传播实现演化；当投影奇异时，将II型离散动力学与Morse族复合从而得到生成族（generating family）。

Result: 当Lagrangian子流形由精确一次形式dW_k和dW_{k+1}局部表示时，得到相邻生成函数之间的离散Hamilton–Jacobi关系；投影奇异时，通过Type-II动力学与Morse族的复合获得其传播生成族，无需动力学表示为图（graph）。将这一框架应用于光波前通过折叠焦散的传播，获得了辛射线积分器（symplectic ray integrator）与多值波前（multivalued wavefront）的正则表示。

Conclusion: 同一几何构造同时提供了离散Hamilton积分器和多值Hamilton–Jacobi解的正则表示，表明该框架能在奇异点处统一处理离散动力学与Hamilton–Jacobi理论。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for implicit discrete Hamiltonian systems based on discrete Morse families, Lagrangian relations, and discrete analogues of Tulczyjew's triple. The main idea is to regard the Lagrangian submanifold defining the discrete dynamics, rather than an explicit symplectic evolution map, as the fundamental geometric object. This viewpoint naturally accommodates implicit, constrained, and degenerate discrete systems.
  Within this framework, we formulate a Type--II discrete Hamilton--Jacobi theory in terms of the propagation of Lagrangian submanifolds between consecutive discrete steps. When these submanifolds are locally represented by exact one-forms $dW_k$ and $dW_{k+1}$, the resulting equations provide a discrete Hamilton--Jacobi relation between consecutive generating functions. More generally, when the projection onto configuration space becomes singular and a single-valued generating function ceases to exist, we show that the evolution can be described by the composition of Type--II discrete dynamics with Morse families. This yields a generating family for the propagated Lagrangian submanifold without requiring the dynamics to be represented as a graph.
  As an application, we consider the propagation of optical wavefronts through fold caustics. A Type--II discrete Hamiltonian yields a symplectic ray integrator, while a Morse family represents the multivalued wavefront near the caustic. Their composition provides a discrete propagation rule for the complete Lagrangian manifold across the singularity. In this way, the same geometric construction simultaneously provides a discrete Hamiltonian integrator and a regular representation of multivalued Hamilton--Jacobi solutions.

</details>


### [321] [Homogeneous attractive Bose-Einstein condensates with repulsive three-body interactions: the one-dimensional case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29874)
*Dinh-Thi Nguyen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由N个全同玻色子组成的一维均匀玻色气体，其具有吸引性二体相互作用和排斥性三体相互作用。通过中间Hartree理论，作者严格推导了均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔泛函（cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger functional），将其作为多体哈密顿量的平均场极限（mean-field limit），并特别关注排斥性三体项对基态能量估计和质量集中性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解含三体相互作用的一维量子多体玻色系统的平均场极限行为，以及排斥性三体相互作用如何修正仅含二体吸引作用的经典结果。

Method: 采用中间Hartree理论（intermediate Hartree theory），对包含吸引性二体和排斥性三体相互作用的均匀一维玻色气体多体哈密顿量，严格推导其平均场极限，得到均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔泛函。

Result: 成功推导出均匀三次-五次非线性薛定谔泛函作为多体哈密顿量的平均场极限，并分析了排斥性三体项对基态能量估计和质量集中性质的影响。

Conclusion: 排斥性三体相互作用在吸引性二体系统的平均场描述中起到关键调节作用，其对基态能量和质量集中性质的影响值得深入研究。

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of a homogeneous one-dimensional Bose gas composed of $N$ identical bosons experiencing attractive two-body and repulsive three-body interactions. Utilizing intermediate Hartree theory, we rigorously derive the homogeneous cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger functional as the mean-field limit of the many-body Hamiltonian. Particular attention is given to the impact of the repulsive three-body term on ground state energy estimates and mass concentration properties

</details>


### [322] [Homogeneous attractive Bose-Einstein condensates with repulsive three-body interactions: the two-dimensional case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29875)
*Dinh-Thi Nguyen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究占据无限二维空间的均匀玻色气体系统，该系统同时具有吸引性二体相互作用和排斥性三体相互作用。通过Hartree中间理论，作者严格推导出该模型的平均场极限为齐次三次-五次非线性薛定諤泛函（homogeneous cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger functional），并重点考察系统相对于二体相互作用的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于理解由N个全同玻色子组成的均匀气体在无限二维空间中，当存在吸引性二体相互作用与排斥性三体相互作用竞争时的系统性行为。

Method: 采用中间Hartree理论（intermediate Hartree theory）作为技术工具，严格推导系统的平均场极限（mean-field limit），将多体量子问题约化为齐次三次-五次非线性薛定諤泛函。

Result: 成功推导出系统在平均场极限下的齐次三次-五次非线性薛定諤泛函，该泛函同时编码了吸引性二体相互作用与排斥性三体相互作用的效应。

Conclusion: 该平均场模型为研究中二体相互作用条件下系统行为提供了严格的数学基础，为理解二维玻色气体中吸引与排斥相互作用之间的竞争提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We consider a homogeneous Bose gas composed of $N$ identical bosons occupying an infinite two-dimensional space. This gas exhibits both an attractive two-body interaction and a repulsive three-body interaction. Via the intermediate Hartree theory, we rigorously derive the homogeneous cubic-quintic nonlinear Schroedinger functional as the mean-field limit of the model. Our investigation focuses on the system's behavior in relation to the two-body interaction.

</details>


### [323] [Local Density Approximation and Other Limit Regimes for a Homogeneous Bose Gas with Repulsive Three-Body Interactions in Low-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29876)
*Thi Anh Thu Doan,Dinh-Thi Nguyen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在低空间维数（d≤2）中研究具有吸引三次非线性和排斥五次非线性的能量泛函极小值问题，证明了固定有效统计参数下极小值的存在性，并在大统计参数极限下推导出均匀玻色气体的有效托马斯-费米型模型，同时考察了其他极限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究描述具有两体吸引和三体排斥的量子玻色气体的能量泛函极小值，理解低维系统中有效统计参数对大范围内玻色气体行为的影响。

Method: 在低空间维数（d ≤ 2）的整个空间中，研究含吸引三次非线性与排斥五次非线性的能量泛函，在固定有效统计参数下证明极小值的存在性（existence of minimizers），并在质量临界非线性（mass-critical nonlinearity）相对动能的大有效统计参数极限下推导托马斯-费米型（Thomas-Fermi-like）有效模型。

Result: 证明了在低维空间中固定有效统计参数下极小值的存在性；在大有效统计参数极限下，得到了均匀玻色气体的有效托马斯-费米型模型；此外还考虑了依赖于质量临界非线性的其他极限机制。

Conclusion: 低维玻色气体系统的能量极小值存在性得到确立，其在大统计参数极限下的行为可由托马斯-费米型模型有效描述，揭示了质量临界非线性对极限机制选择的关键作用。

Abstract: In the whole space of low spatial dimensions, namely $d \leq 2$, we study the minimizers of an energy functional with an attractive cubic nonlinearity and a repulsive quintic nonlinearity, which describes a quantum Bose gas with a two-body attraction and a three-body repulsion. We prove the existence of minimizers at fixed effective statistics parameter. In the limit of a large effective statistics parameter of the mass-critical nonlinearity (with respect to the kinetic energy), we derive an effective Thomas-Fermi-like model for the homogeneous Bose gas. We also consider other limit regimes depending on the mass-critical nonlinearity.

</details>


### [324] [Multiple Nonlinear Waves by (Quantum) Neural Networks: Checking the AI supremacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29894)
*Luigi Martina,Riccardo Caricato,Riccardo Della Torre*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文旨在检验量子物理信息神经网络（quantum physics-informed neural network, QPINN）技术在求解演化偏微分方程方面的潜力，以具有周期边界条件的Korteweg-de Vries（KdV）方程中弱非线性介质内的多波传播问题作为测试平台，通过一组性能指标和多个数值实验评估该方法是否足够成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管该问题存在解析解，但高亏格（high-genus）解对数值方法而言是相当具有挑战性的测试基准，因此作者希望评估QPINN是否具备在该场景中的应用成熟度。

Method: 选取Korteweg-de Vries方程（具有周期边界条件的弱非线性介质中的多波传播问题）作为测试问题，设计一组性能指标（indicators of merit），并通过开展多个数值实验来系统检验QPINN的能力。

Result: 共进行了多个数值实验，以评估QPINN在该测试问题上的表现；具体实验数值结果在摘要中未详述。

Conclusion: 研究旨在判断QPINN技术在当前发展阶段是否已足够成熟，能够应用于演化型偏微分方程的求解场景。具体结论需结合实验数据进一步说明，摘要中未提供明确结论。

Abstract: To test the potential of so-called quantum physics-informed neural network (QPINN) technology for solving evolutionary partial differential equations, we consider the problem of multiple wave propagation in a weakly nonlinear medium described by the Korteweg-de Vries equation with periodic boundary conditions. Although this problem is solvable analytically, high-genus solutions could represent a rather challenging testbed for numerical methods. Therefore, our idea is to test whether a QPINN is sufficiently mature for application in this context by providing a set of indicators of merit and performing several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [325] [Uniqueness of universal quantum dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30012)
*M. Y. Avetisyan,R. L. Mkrtchyan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在若干假设下，证明了普遍量子维度公式（universal quantum dimension formulae）的唯一性：首先证明经典代数的量子维度具有一种可由Vogel参数统一表示的特殊形式，进而推导出简单的唯一性判据，据此所有已知的普遍量子维度均唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子维度在量子群表示论中扮演核心角色，而普遍量子维度公式是否为唯一这一根本问题此前尚未解决。明确其唯一性是理解该类公式理论地位的关键一步。

Method: 论文首先证明经典代数的量子维度可表示为形如 aα+bβ+cγ（其中 c=0,1,2，a、b为整数）的 q-维度因子（q-dimension factor）之积/比，这一形式在Vogel意义上是普遍可表示的。在此基础上，推导出简单的唯一性判据（uniqueness criteria），用于判定普遍量子维度公式的唯一性。

Result: 建立了普遍量子维度公式的唯一性判据，证明在所述假设条件下，所有已知的普遍量子维度公式都是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该论文在给定假设下完整回答了普遍量子维度公式的唯一性问题，为量子群表示理论中此类公式的系统化应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove, under some assumptions, the uniqueness of universal quantum dimension formulae. We first show that the quantum dimensions of classical algebras have a specific form that can be represented universally, in Vogel's sense, as the product/ratio of q-dimension factors with arguments $aα+bβ+cγ$ where $c=0, 1, 2$ and $a, b$ are integers. On this basis, we derive simple uniqueness criteria, according to which all known universal quantum dimensions are unique.

</details>


### [326] [On the equivalence between the Polchinski flow and the Connes-Kreimer approaches to perturbative renormalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31049)
*Yvain Bruned,Paul Laubie,Aurélien Minguella*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了扰动重整化（perturbative renormalisation）中 Polchinski 流与 Connes-Kreimer 方法之间的对等关系，表明基于装饰图（decorated graphs/Feynman 图）的带组合重整化程序的 ansatz 可以求解 Polchinski 方程，并据此推导出文献中最一般的重整化势形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Polchinski 流方法与 Connes-Kreimer 图论重整化方法是两个独立的扰动重整化框架，本文旨在建立两者之间的严格数学对应关系，以统一理解重整化机制，并推广到一般欧几里得量子场论。

Method: 论文采用 ansatz 方法，即基于装饰图（即 Feynman 图）构造解，并结合组合重整化（combinatorial renormalisation）程序求解 Polchinski 方程。证明的核心工具包括重整化下的多重态射性质（multiple morphism properties）以及一个关于装饰图森林（forests of decorated graphs）的新型对偶公式（duality formula）。

Result: 证明了 Polchinski 流与 Connes-Kreimer 方法产生相同的重整化结果，该结论适用于非常一般的欧几里得量子场论（Euclidean quantum field theories）。由此导出了重整化势（renormalised potential）的显式形式，据作者所述，这是文献中出现的最一般形式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了两个主要重整化框架之间的严格数学联系，并为一般性欧几里得量子场论中的重整化势提供了最通用的显式表达。

Abstract: We prove a correspondence between the Polchinski flow and the Connes-Kreimer approaches to perturbative renormalisation, in the sense that the first yields the same renormalisation as the latter. More precisely, we show that an ansatz based on decorated graphs (Feynman diagrams),with a combinatorial renormalisation procedure solves the Polchinski equation. This result holds for very general Euclidean quantum field theories. We are able to derive from this the form of the renormalised potential which is, to the best of our knowledge, the most general one in the literature. The main ingredients of the proof are multiple morphism properties with respect to the renormalisation, as well as a novel duality formula for forests of decorated graphs.

</details>


### [327] [Boundary Quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov Equations and Integrability of Quantum Field Theories with Time-Dependent Bulk and Boundary Coupling Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31080)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义Bethe ansatz框架从周期边界条件推广到具有显式含时边界条件的开放边界情形，证明该框架可将含时Schrödinger方程约化为边界量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov（BqKZ）方程，并揭示静态模型的重整化群不变量与含时模型的动力学不变量之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的广义Bethe ansatz框架仅适用于周期边界条件下的含时耦合强度量子多体系统，尚缺乏对开放边界条件的推广。开放边界条件且边界显式含时的情形在物理上具有广泛意义，需要建立统一的解析求解框架。

Method: 将文献中提出的广义Bethe ansatz框架扩展至开放边界条件情形，其中除体相互作用含时外，边界条件本身也显式含时。对于可积的含时体耦合强度，利用该框架确定与可积性相容的含时边界条件，并将含时Schrödinger方程约化为矩阵差分方程（即边界量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程）。

Result: 求解BqKZ方程即可获得精确波函数的显式形式。此外，静态模型中的重整化群（RG）不变量被证明等同于含时模型中的动力学不变量。

Conclusion: 广义Bethe ansatz框架可自然推广至开放且边界含时的可积多体系统，为这类系统提供统一的精确解构造路径，同时建立了静态与含时模型之间不变量结构的深刻联系。

Abstract: The generalized Bethe ansatz framework formulated in [P. R. Pasnoori, Phys. Rev. B 112, L060409 (2025)] provides a unified framework to find exact solutions to quantum many-body systems with time-dependent coupling strengths with periodic boundary conditions. In this work we extend this framework to the case of open boundary conditions where in addition to the time-dependent interactions in the bulk, the boundary conditions are explicitly time-dependent. We show that for integrable time-dependent bulk coupling strengths, the generalized Bethe ansatz framework provides the time-dependent boundary conditions compatible with integrability and reduces the time-dependent Schrodinger equation to a set of matrix difference equations called the boundary quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (BqKZ) equations. The solution to the BqKZ equations provides the explicit form of the exact wavefunction. We further show that the RG invariants of the corresponding static model identify with the dynamical invariants in the time-dependent model.

</details>


### [328] [Rotating-wave approximation for spin-boson models with structured fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31090)
*Aitor Balmaseda,Davide Lonigro,Juan Manuel Pérez-Pardo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了由无界哈密顿量（unbounded Hamiltonian）生成的两次量子演化之间的状态依赖上界，主要工具是在椭圆（sesquilinear）形式层面而非算子层面进行第二次分部积分，从而无需共同的算子不变域。作为应用，文中给出了自旋-玻色模型（spin-boson model）旋转波近似（rotating-wave approximation）的显式定量界，该界在高频极限下精确成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子两时间演化之间的差异估计通常依赖于两个生成元具有共同的不变算子域，这一限制对具有无界哈密顿量的物理系统而言往往过强。作者希望放宽这一技术条件，并得到对快振荡项平均效应敏感的估计，从而能够量化旋转波近似的有效性。

Method: 核心技术手段是在双线性形式（sesquilinear form）层面而非算子层面执行第二次分部积分，以消除对共同不变算子域的需求。所得估计涉及两个生成元时间积分差的范数而非其范数的时间积分，从而能捕捉快振荡项引起的平均效应。证明还包含对高频标度（high-frequency scaling）的细致分析。

Result: 得到的状态依赖上界对一般无界哈密顿量成立，并将之用于一般测度空间上带任意大质量色散关系（massive dispersion relation）的旋转波近似，得到完全显式的定量界。该界在状态的稠密子空间上成立，所有常数仅依赖于模型参数而与频率尺度无关，因此在频率趋于无穷时近似精确成立。

Conclusion: 本文建立的界比传统形式对快振荡项更敏感，并成功解决了无界哈密顿量下旋转波近似的定量证明问题；其显式且与频率无关的常数结构保证了在大频率极限下近似的精确性。

Abstract: We derive state-dependent bounds on the difference between two quantum evolutions generated by unbounded Hamiltonians sharing a common form domain. The main technical tool is a second integration by parts, performed at the level of sesquilinear forms rather than at the operator level, which removes the need for a common invariant operator domain. The resulting estimate involves the norm of the time-integrated difference of the two generators, rather than the integral of its norm, and is therefore sensitive to the averaging effects produced by fast-oscillating terms. As an application we prove a quantitative bound on the rotating-wave approximation for spin-boson models with a structured boson field, described by an arbitrary massive dispersion relation on a general measure space and by a suitable class of form factors. The proof involves a careful analysis of the high-frequency scaling. The bound holds on a dense subspace of states, is fully explicit, and all the constants entering it depend only on the parameters of the model and not on the frequency scale, so that the approximation becomes exact in the limit of large frequency.

</details>


### [329] [The Dunkl two-photon/Schrödinger algebras and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31104)
*Francisco J. Herranz,Danilo Latini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入了 Dunkl 双光子代数（Dunkl two-photon algebra）与 Dunkl-Schrödinger 代数，作为标准六维双光子代数和 Schrödinger 代数的反射扩展对应物。作者推导了这两种同构代数的三次多项式 Casimir 不变量，识别了若干重要子代数并计算其二次 Casimir 不变量，还在 Fock 空间与 Dunkl-Fock-Bargmann 表示中构造了表示，最终通过 Dunkl 空间导数给出向量场实现，导出一族六个非平凡的 Dunkl 微分-差分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在将经典的六维双光子代数和 Schrödinger 代数推广到包含反射算符（reflection operator）的扩展结构，从而引入 Dunkl 型代数的扩展框架，并为进一步研究相应的微分方程和动力学系统提供代数基础。

Method: 通过引入一个对合的（involutive）反射生成元对标准六维双光子代数和 Schrödinger 代数进行扩展，构造 Dunkl 型代数结构；推导同构关系、三次多项式 Casimir 不变量及子代数的二次 Casimir 不变量；在一维 Dunkl 谐振子的 Fock 空间上构造表示，并转化为 Dunkl-Fock-Bargmann 全纯实现；利用 Dunkl 空间导数给出显式向量场实现，并将 Casimir 不变量作用于该实现得到微分-差分方程。

Result: 证明了 Dunkl 双光子代数与 Dunkl-Schrödinger 代数同构，并得到其三阶多项式 Casimir 不变量；识别了多个结构重要子代数及其二次 Casimir 不变量；在 Dunkl 谐振子 Fock 空间上构造了表示并给出全纯函数实现；通过向量场实现导出一族 1+1 维的六个非平凡 Dunkl 微分-差分方程，其中包含著名的热-薛定谔方程（heat-Schrödinger equation）的 Dunkl 版本。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了 Dunkl 型反射扩展代数的完整代数结构，包括同构性、不变量和表示理论，并展示了其在实际微分方程中的直接应用，为 Dunkl 型量子系统与微分方程理论之间的桥梁提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: The work introduces the novel Dunkl two-photon and Dunkl-Schrödinger algebras as reflection-extended counterparts of the standard six-dimensional two-photon and Schrödinger algebras. These extensions are realized through the presence of an involutive generator representing a reflection, and they reduce to the undeformed two-photon and Schrödinger algebras in the absence of this additional generator in an appropriate limit. For both algebraic structures, which are shown to be isomorphic, we derive the associated cubic polynomial Casimir invariant. Furthermore, we identify several structurally significant subalgebras and compute their corresponding quadratic Casimir invariants. Representations of the Dunkl two-photon algebra are constructed on the Fock space of the one-dimensional Dunkl harmonic oscillator and subsequently translated into holomorphic realizations within the associated Dunkl-Fock-Bargmann representation. Finally, for the Dunkl-Schrödinger algebra, we present an explicit vector field realization in terms of the Dunkl spatial derivative, while keeping time continuous. When applied to the Casimir invariants of the aforementioned subalgebras, this realization yields a family of six nontrivial Dunkl differential-difference equations in $1+1$ dimensions, including, in particular, a Dunkl version of the well-known heat-Schrödinger equation.

</details>


### [330] [Exactly flat bands beyond the chiral limit in Bistritzer--MacDonald Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31147)
*Zhen Huang,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究扭曲双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene）的 Bistritzer–MacDonald（BM）模型在手征极限（chiral limit）之外的精确平带（exactly flat bands）问题。作者构造了一族非手征隧穿势（nonchiral tunnelling potentials），使哈密顿量在每个Bloch动量处至少有两个零能态，从而给出Zworski开放问题3的反例族，并证明了该性质的完整刻画。同时表明标准BM模型在第一谐波隧穿势下不存在精确平带（除离散耦合强度集合外），为此引入了显式可逆性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手征极限下BM模型在幻角（magic angles）处具有精确平带，但该性质在手征极限之外是否成立尚未明确。本文旨在回答Zworski综述中提出的开放问题3，即是否存在非手征隧穿势使精确平带性质得以保持。

Method: 作者构造一族非手征隧穿势，使哈密顿量在每个Bloch动量处至少有两个零能态。通过反证与完全刻画证明该族即为所有具有此性质的容许势。对标准BM模型，引入显式可逆性判据：要求投影微扰（projected perturbation）在某个Bloch动量处具有非零标量系数，从而阻碍精确平带；对构造的势族，投影微扰处处消失，判据失效。标准BM势的判据验证采用局部泰勒展开（local Taylor expansion）论证。

Result: 构造出一族非手征隧穿势，使哈密顿量对任意实数非手征耦合强度在每个Bloch动量处具有至少两个零能态，构成Zworski开放问题3的反例族。反证表明该族完整刻画了所有具有此性质的容许势。标准BM模型在第一谐波隧穿势下，除无有限聚点的离散非手征耦合强度集合外，不承认幻角处的精确平带。

Conclusion: 精确平带可以突破手征极限而存在，但仅限于特定构造的势族；标准BM模型则几乎不承认非手征情形下的精确平带。该可逆性判据为判定一般非手征隧穿势是否支持精确平带提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The chiral limit of the Bistritzer--MacDonald model for twisted bilayer graphene has exactly flat bands at magic angles. We show that exact flatness can persist beyond the chiral limit with nonchiral tunnelling potentials in the usual symmetry class. We construct a family of nonchiral tunnelling potentials for which the Hamiltonian has at least two zero-energy states at every Bloch momentum and for every real value of the nonchiral coupling. This gives a family of counterexamples to Open Problem~3 of Zworski's survey \cite{ZworskiSurvey}. Conversely, we prove that every admissible potential with this property belongs to this family, thereby obtaining a complete characterization. By contrast, we show that the standard BM model, namely, BM Hamiltonians with first harmonic tunnelling potentials, does not admit exactly flat bands at the magic angle except possibly at a discrete set of nonchiral coupling strengths, with no finite accumulation point. To prove this, we introduce an explicit invertibility criterion. The criterion requires the projected perturbation to have a nonzero scalar coefficient at some Bloch momentum, obstructing exact flatness for general nonchiral tunnelling. A local Taylor expansion argument verifies the criterion for the standard BM potentials. For the family constructed above, however, the projected perturbation vanishes at every Bloch momentum, so the invertibility criterion fails.

</details>
